## AST_WAVELENGTHS_1_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AAST_WAVELENGTHS_1_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetName]小行星正在发射受控脉冲辐射。是不是有什么人，还是说有什么东西，想要和我们交谈？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:10`

### English

The asteroid [Root.GetName] is emitting some form of radiation in controlled bursts. Is someone - something - trying to communicate with us?

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:14`

## anomaly.75.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.75.name&lang=en)

### 中文

石脑

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:96`

### English

Rock Brain

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:102`

## anomaly.75.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.75.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

对巨型小行星[From.GetName]的扫描得出了有趣的结果。这块岩石的核心里含有携带微弱电流的导电晶体网络。[Root.GetName]上的成员辨认出这片晶体网络与原始神经网络具有相似性，认为就算该天体并不具有完全智能，也能进行某种程度的思考。他们请求将核心取出，并带到最近的轨道站以进行更仔细的研究。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:97`

### English

A scan of the particularly massive asteroid [From.GetName] yields fascinating results. The rock's core is shot through with a network of conductive crystals carrying a weak electric charge. The crew on the [Root.GetName] recognize this matrix as a primitive neural lattice and believe the celestial body to be thinking in some capacity, if not fully sapient. They seek permission to excavate the core and transport it to the nearest orbital station for closer study.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:103`

## anomaly.75.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.75.a&lang=en)

### 中文

就这么办。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:98`

### English

Make it so.

## anomaly.75.b

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:99`

### English

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:105`

## anomaly.75.c

## anomaly.75.c.tooltip

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:102`

### English

## IS_LITHOID_EMPIRE_ICON_TIP

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AIS_LITHOID_EMPIRE_ICON_TIP&lang=en)

### 中文

由于§H[This.Owner.GetName]§!是§Y石质§!国家，该选项可用

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:103`

### English

This option is available due to the §H[This.Owner.GetName]§! being a §YLithoid§! empire.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:109`

## anomaly.76.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.76.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我是[rock_scientist.GetName]。这项任务很有挑战性，但我认为我们已经成功将这些内容解析为类似于……也许不是语言，而是概念，或者想法。我们可以将类似的东西反馈给小行星，或许可以进行交流。\n\n我们将显示一些 §Y<过滤后的输出>§!，我将尽我所能对原始数据进行§G“§!更完整的解释§G”§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:104`

### English

[rock_scientist.GetName] here. It was a challenge, but I think we've managed to parse the readings into something like... not language perhaps, but concepts. Ideas. And we can feed something similar back to the asteroid, to maybe communicate.\n\nWe'll display some of the §Y<filtered output>§!, and I'll do my best to give a more §G"§!complete interpretation§G"§! of the raw data.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:110`

## anomaly.76.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.76.a&lang=en)

### 中文

转念一想，这似乎不明智。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:105`

### English

On second thought, this seems ill-advised.

放弃直接与[rock_brain.GetName]的交流，保持距离进行研究吧。

### English

Abort any attempts at contacting [rock_brain.GetName] directly, and study it from afar

## anomaly.76.b

就这么办。

### English

Patch us through.

## anomaly.77.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.77.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

\n§Y<新><想法>。<原因>。<原因><令人困惑>。§!\n\n\n§G“§!多说一句，[rock_brain.GetName]没有传统意义上的感觉器官。它将我们的输入认为是它自己的想法。至少某种程度上是这样的。§G”§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:108`

### English

\n§Y<new> <thoughts>. <reason>. <reason> <perplexing>.§!\n\n\n§G"§!To provide some context, [rock_brain.GetName] has no sensory organs in the traditional sense. It is perceiving our input as its own thoughts. To an extent.§G"§!

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:114`

## anomaly.77.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.77.a&lang=en)

### 中文

<你还不明白我们不是你吗？>

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:109`

### English

<Do you understand that we are not you?>

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:115`

## anomaly.77.a.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.77.a.response&lang=en)

### 中文

\n§Y<想法>-<不同><协调>-<相同><分歧>-<不同>.§! \n\n\n§G“§!我怀疑石脑认为我们只是短暂认知偏差，但它过去确实曾经有过这样的想法。我们是转瞬即逝的消遣。§G”§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:110`

### English

\n§Y<thought>-<different> <reconciliation>-<same> <divergence>-<different>.§!\n\n\n§G"§!It is unconvinced that we are anything but a cerebral blip, but it has entertained such cognitive ghosts in the past. We are a fleeting diversion.§G"§!

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:116`

## anomaly.77.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.77.b&lang=en)

### 中文

<你们在[rock_sun.GetName]的轨道上漂流多久了？>

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:111`

### English

<How long have you been drifting in orbit of [rock_sun.GetName]?>

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:117`

## anomaly.77.b.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.77.b.response&lang=en)

### 中文

\n§Y<不是>……<刚才><不是>。<不是>。§!\n\n\n§G“§![rock_brain.GetName]不认识，甚至未曾感知过[rock_sun.GetName]的存在。它除了对连续发生的想法的有有限掌握之外，它还缺乏时间的概念。§G”§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:112`

### English

\n§Y<not>... <previous> <not>. <not>.§!\n\n\n§G"§![rock_brain.GetName] does not recognize nor even perceive the existence of the star [rock_sun.GetName]. It also lacks a concept of time, outside of a limited grasp of the successive occurrence of thought.§G"§!

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:118`

## anomaly.77.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.77.c&lang=en)

### 中文

“我们从中得到什么有用的东西了吗？”

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:113`

### English

"Are we getting anything useful out of this?"

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:119`

## anomaly.77.c.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.77.c.response&lang=en)

### 中文

\n§Y<……>§!\n\n\n§G“§!哦，有的，这种交流正在以难以置信的速度发展我们的模糊解码器网络。我很惊讶它的效果就像原生编码一样好，但我也收到了一些警告的读数……§G”§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:114`

### English

\n§Y<...>§!\n\n\n§G"§!Oh yes, this exchange is evolving our fuzzy decoder nets at an incredible rate. I am amazed this is working as well as it is, but I am picking up some alarming readings...§G"§!

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:120`

## anomaly.77.d

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.77.d&lang=en)

### 中文

<让我们聊点别的……>

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:115`

### English

<Let us talk about something else...>

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:121`

## anomaly.78.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.78.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

\n§Y<查--。<查询>§!\n\n\n§G“§!正如我担心的一样。我们的探测器正在让驱动[rock_brain.GetName]意识的能量流失。[rock_brain.GetName]本身也不是一个完美的闭环系统，所以这种事迟早会发生的——尽管这需要成千上万年，但至少不是现在…§G”§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:116`

### English

\n§Y<quer--. <query>§!\n\n\n§G"§!As I feared. Our probes are bleeding [rock_brain.GetName] of the energy that drives its cognition. [rock_brain.GetName] is not a perfectly closed system so this would have happened eventually - though in tens of thousands of years, rather than now...§G"§!

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:122`

## anomaly.78.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.78.a&lang=en)

### 中文

<我们有什么可以帮到你们的吗？>

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:117`

### English

<Is there anything we can do to help you?>

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:123`

## anomaly.78.a.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.78.a.response&lang=en)

### 中文

\n§Y<是>。§!\n\n\n§G“§!它说它很好，但事实并非如此。我们曾尝试将一些能量反回神经网络，但这只会产生噪音更大的脑电波，而不是更强的脑电波。我们已经超过了阈值，无论我们做什么，剩下的一点能量很快就会消失。它没有办法检测到它的认知在减慢，也无法知晓这意味着它的终结。更不知道死亡意味着什么……§G”§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:118`

### English

\n§Y<am>.§!\n\n\n§G"§!It says it's fine, but it's not fine. We have tried feeding some energy back into the neural lattice but that only produces noisier brain waves, not stronger ones. We've already crossed the threshold, what little energy remains will soon peter out no matter what we do. It has no means of detecting its cognition slowing down, nor any reason to predict that this spells its end. Nor any idea about what it means to die...§G"§!

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:124`

## anomaly.78.b1

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.78.b1&lang=en)

### 中文

<有什么遗言吗？>

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:119`

### English

<Any last words?>

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:125`

## anomaly.78.b1.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.78.b1.response&lang=en)

### 中文

\n§Y<重复><以> - <感知>。<[rock_fallen_empire.GetRealName]>。§!\n\n\n§G“§!它会再次想起我们，或者是现在，再次，通过它的思想具象化我们？恐怕信号正在迅速衰减。我们也不确定如何理解“[rock_fallen_empire.GetRealName]”——它似乎是我们从[rock_brain.GetName]得到的最接近传统语言的语言，但它的含义仍然模糊不清。也许只是我们的解析算法因为目标神经网络失效而崩溃。§G”§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:120`

### English

\n§Y<perceive>-<within> <repeat>. <[rock_fallen_empire.GetRealName]>.§!\n\n\n§G"§!It will think of us again, or it is now, again, embodying us through its thoughts? I'm afraid the signal is fading rapidly. We are also not sure what to make of "[rock_fallen_empire.GetRealName]" - it appears to be the closest to traditional language we've gotten from [rock_brain.GetName], but its meaning remains obscure. Maybe our interpretation algorithms are simply breaking down as the subject's neural lattice fails.§G"§!

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:126`

## anomaly.78.b2

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.78.b2&lang=en)

$anomaly.78.b1$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:121`

### English

$anomaly.78.b1$

## anomaly.78.b2.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.78.b2.response&lang=en)

### 中文

\n§Y<重复><以> - <感知>。§!\n\n\n§G“§!它会再次想起我们，或者是现在，再次，通过它的思想具象化我们？恐怕信号正在迅速衰减——也许只是我们的解析算法因为目标神经网络失效而崩溃。§G”§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:122`

### English

\n§Y<perceive>-<within> <repeat>.§!\n\n\n§G"§!It will think of us again. Rather, it is now once again embodying us through its thoughts? I'm afraid the signal is fading rapidly - or maybe our interpretation algorithms are simply breaking down as the subject's neural lattice fails.§G"§!

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:128`

## anomaly.78.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.78.c&lang=en)

### 中文

“是时候说再见了。”

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:123`

### English

"Time to say goodbye."

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:129`

## anomaly.79.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.79.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[rock_brain.GetName]沉寂了。科学专员[rock_scientist.GetName]尝试给这个小行星的矿物网络重新充能的所有方案均以失败告终；所有曾经激发其粗糙神经元的智慧火花都消失了，但我们从这个遥远的进化表亲那里学到了很多东西。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:124`

### English

[rock_brain.GetName] is silent. All attempts by Science Officer [rock_scientist.GetName] to recharge the mineral lattice within the asteroid have failed; whatever spark of sapience once animated its rough-hewn neurons is gone, but we have learned much from this distant evolutionary cousin.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:130`

## anomaly.79.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.79.a&lang=en)

### 中文

这是这个“物种”的最后一个，还是第一个？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:125`

### English

Was it the last of its kind, or the first?

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:131`

## anomaly.80.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.80.name&lang=en)

### 中文

岩石偏头痛

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:126`

### English

Rock Migraine

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:132`

## anomaly.80.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.80.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.From.GetName]的核心刚从小行星外壳内部被小心取出，就对太空的真空环境产生了不良反应。在这块石疙瘩缓慢但不可逆地开裂之前，[Root.GetName]的设备检测到了一个突如其来的能量峰值。\n\n我们随后回收到了大量的导电性矿物，但曾赋予它们生命的神秘电荷却不复存在了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:127`

### English

The core of [From.From.GetName], once coaxed from beneath the crust of the asteroid, does not respond well to the vacuum of space. The [Root.GetName]'s instruments read a sudden energy spike moments before the rocky lump gently, but decisively, fractures.\n\nLarge chunks of the conductive mineral are salvaged, but whatever spectral charge animated them is now gone.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:133`

## anomaly.80.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.80.a&lang=en)

### 中文

这些水晶本身还是有价值的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:128`

### English

The crystals are still valuable in their own right.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:134`

## anomaly.81.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.81.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的死灵法师已经在小行星脆弱的矿物层上留下了新的痕迹，在上面刻下了粗糙的咒文，并充满了脉冲电流。然而，曾为[rock_brain.GetName]的意识并没有回来。\n\n现在它成了另一个东西，沉眠于一个邪恶的梦境中——被装上方向推进器，等待这我们控制。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:129`

### English

Our necromancers have scored new traces across the asteroid's delicate mineral seams, crude invocations hewn in stone and suffused with a pulsing current. Yet, the consciousness that was [rock_brain.GetName] remains lost to us.\n\nIt is something different now, suspended in a sinister dream - fitted with directional thrusters and awaiting our command.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:135`

## anomaly.85.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.85.name&lang=en)

### 中文

窃语的行星

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:130`

### English

Gossiping Planets

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:136`

## anomaly.85.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.85.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.GetName]和它附近行星不规则的电磁信号引起了[Root.GetName]成员的注意。进一步研究表明这些信号是些内容十分平淡无奇的加密通讯。随后似乎察觉到了窃听一样，电磁活动突然中止了，但在此之前，[Root.GetName]已经纪录下[From.GetName]和其星系内其他行星的状态。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:131`

### English

Irregular electromagnetic bursts from [From.GetName] and its neighboring planets intrigue the crew of the [Root.GetName]. Further study reveals that the bursts are coded communications, though the contents are fairly banal. The electromagnetic activity suddenly ceases, as if in response to the eavesdropping, but not before the [Root.GetName] has cataloged the self-reported statuses of [From.GetName] and every other planet in the system.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:137`

## anomaly.85.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.85.a&lang=en)

### 中文

使用这些数据进行进一步的研究远距离通讯。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:132`

### English

Use the data to further research long-range communications.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:138`

## anomaly.85.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.85.b&lang=en)

### 中文

将数据匹配到该星系内的所有行星。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:133`

### English

Map the status data to every planet in the system.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:139`

## anomaly.90.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.90.name&lang=en)

### 中文

藤蔓缠绕

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:134`

### English

Grasping Vines

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:140`

## anomaly.90.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.90.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.GetName]上浓密的青草在接触[Root.GetName]的探针后，异常凶猛地将其撕碎。这些强壮的藤蔓努力地伸向远方的飞船，给了这颗行星缺少动物的情况合理的解释。\n\n已发起§Y特殊项目§!来进一步调查这个行星上的植物生命。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:135`

### English

The dense grasses of [From.GetName] respond to the [Root.GetName]'s exploratory probes by disassembling them in a spectacularly violent manner. As sinewy vines reach for the ship far above, the relative lack of animal life on the planet starts making sense.\n\nA §YSpecial Project§! has been issued for further study of the planet's plant life.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:141`

## anomaly.90.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.90.a&lang=en)

### 中文

这种食人植物是个威胁。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:136`

### English

A triffidian menace.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:142`

## anomaly.91.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.91.name&lang=en)

### 中文

青草军团

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:137`

### English

Grassy Militia

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:143`

## anomaly.91.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.91.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

缜密的检查揭示了[From.From.GetName]上的青草和藤蔓的剧烈行为并非无意识的自卫反应。\n\n这些植物有着复杂的内部社会秩序。不同地区不同种类的植物有着不同的使命，其中最重要的一种植物似乎是负责驱赶当地的食草动物和来自天空的入侵者的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:138`

### English

Careful examination reveals that the violent behavior of the vines and grasses of [From.From.GetName] is no dumb self-defense reflex.\n\nThe plants have a complex internal social order. Depending on geographical region, different phyla assume a number of different tasks, the most important one seemingly being staving off native herbivores and would-be invaders from the skies.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:144`

## anomaly.91.a.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.91.a.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

已向[From.From.GetName]添加§Y狡猾的植物§!修正：\n£society£§G+6§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:139`

### English

Adds the §YCunning Flora§! modifier to [From.From.GetName]:\n£society£ §G+6§!

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:145`

## anomaly.95.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.95.name&lang=en)

### 中文

他方之宝

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:140`

### English

Important to Someone Else

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:146`

## anomaly.95.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.95.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一块铝板被发现于[From.GetName]周围漂浮的碎石中，它的阳极化表面使它从周围的金属残骸中脱颖而出。它上面绘有两只模样滑稽的哺乳动物，还有一些无趣的几何形状。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:141`

### English

An aluminum plaque is found drifting among the detritus around [From.GetName], its anodized surface standing out from the metal debris surrounding it. It depicts two ridiculous-looking mammalian figures and some rather tasteless geometric shapes.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:147`

## anomaly.95.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.95.a&lang=en)

### 中文

也许可以当我酒杯的杯垫。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:142`

### English

It might make a good coaster for my drink.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:148`

## anomaly.95.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.95.b&lang=en)

### 中文

解析这块铝板。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:143`

### English

Decipher the plaque.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:149`

## anomaly.95.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.95.c&lang=en)

### 中文

肯定有人愿意出价购买。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:144`

### English

I'm sure someone would be willing to pay for it.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:150`

## anomaly.95.b.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.95.b.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

将太阳系坐标加入情报日志。\n

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:145`

### English

Adds the coordinates of the Sol system to the Situation Log.\n

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:151`

## anomaly.96.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.96.name&lang=en)

### 中文

地球大陆

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:146`

### English

Terra Firma

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:152`

## anomaly.96.desc.neutral

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.96.desc.neutral&lang=en)

### 中文

铝板上雕刻的坐标大体准确。\n\n这是太阳系，而这块铝板显然来自被我们宇航员标记为太阳系-III的那颗行星。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:147`

### English

The directions inferred from the inscribed plaque were largely accurate.\n\nThis is the system of Sol, with the plaque apparently originating from the planet our astronomers have labeled Sol III.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:153`

## anomaly.96.desc.phobe

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.96.desc.phobe&lang=en)

### 中文

我们已经找到了那些可疑破旧金属废料的源头。太阳系是一个以一颗黄矮星为中心的小星系，一个和[Root.Owner.GetName]的辉煌毫不相称的穷乡僻壤。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:148`

### English

We have found the alleged source of the tacky metal scrap we recovered. The system of Sol is a pinprick in space centered on a pitiful yellow dwarf, a backwater completely eclipsed by the splendor of the [Root.Owner.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:154`

## anomaly.96.desc.phile

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.96.desc.phile&lang=en)

### 中文

我们最终到达了太阳系，那里的人在沥尽心血地用极其原始的方式与外界交流。铝板所标记的源头似乎是从恒星往外数第三块岩石，应该是一颗大陆行星。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:149`

### English

We have finally reached the system of Sol, a destination some being went to great pains to communicate through a very primitive medium. The plaque's point of origin is seemingly the third rock from the sun, which should be a continental planet.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:155`

## anomaly.96.a.neutral

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.96.a.neutral&lang=en)

### 中文

真是路途遥远啊。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:150`

### English

Far shores indeed.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:156`

## anomaly.96.a.phobe

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.96.a.phobe&lang=en)

### 中文

发表声明。然后把铝板丢到他们的恒星里去。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:151`

### English

Make a statement. Jettison the plaque into their sun.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:157`

## anomaly.96.a.phile

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.96.a.phile&lang=en)

### 中文

你们向虚空呼唤，我们便来了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:152`

### English

You shouted into the void, and we came.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:158`

## anomaly.100.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.100.name&lang=en)

### 中文

超越先驱者

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:153`

### English

Catching up with a Pioneer

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:159`

## anomaly.100.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.100.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一块阳极镀金的铝板被发现于[From.GetName]的表面。在进行任何分析和数据对比之前，[Root.GetName]上的所有成员就已经认识到了它的来源。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:154`

### English

An aluminum plate, anodized gold, is recovered from the surface of [From.GetName]. Everyone aboard the [Root.GetName] recognize it long before analysis and database cross-referencing confirm its origins.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:160`

## anomaly.100.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.100.a&lang=en)

### 中文

铝板来自……先驱者11号。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:155`

### English

The plaque from Pioneer... 11.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:161`

## anomaly.105.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.105.name&lang=en)

### 中文

旧日之曲

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:156`

### English

Old-Timey Tunes

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:162`

## anomaly.105.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.105.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetName]在调查[From.GetName]微弱引力场时发现了一张金色碟片。碟片已被收回，初步分析表明其表面的细槽可能是由某种外星文明所创作的经过加密的音频和视频数据。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:157`

### English

A golden disc strikes the forward viewport of the [Root.GetName] as it is surveying the weak gravitational field of [From.GetName]. The object is recovered, and cursory analysis indicates that the minuscule grooves on the disc's surface might be encoded audio and video data, authored by some alien civilization.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:163`

## anomaly.105.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.105.a&lang=en)

### 中文

也许可以当大酒杯的杯垫。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:158`

### English

It might make a good coaster for a very big drink.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:164`

## anomaly.105.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.105.b&lang=en)

### 中文

进行解析。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:159`

### English

Decode it.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:165`

外星音乐爱好者肯定会出大价钱。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:160`

### English

Xeno-audiophiles would pay a lot for this.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:166`

地球大陆

### English

Terra Firma

## anomaly.106.desc.neutral

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.106.desc.neutral&lang=en)

### 中文

碟片上刻录的坐标大体准确。\n\n这是太阳系，而这块碟片显然来自被我们宇航员标记为太阳系-III的那颗行星。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:163`

### English

The directions inferred from the audiovisual record were largely accurate.\n\nThis is the system of Sol, with the disc apparently originating from the planet our astronomers have labeled Sol III.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:169`

## anomaly.106.desc.phobe

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.106.desc.phobe&lang=en)

### 中文

我们找到了那张被我们修复的破旧的光盘的可能的来源。只是这个星系在太空实在是太过于渺小了，以一颗可怜的黄矮星为中心。在壮丽的[Root.Owner.GetName]面前，这只是一个根本不值得一提的蛮荒之地。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:164`

### English

We have found the alleged source of the tacky disc we recovered. The system of Sol is a pinprick in space centered on a pitiful yellow dwarf, a backwater completely eclipsed by the splendor of the [Root.Owner.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:170`

## anomaly.106.desc.phile

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.106.desc.phile&lang=en)

### 中文

我们最终到达了太阳系，一些生物通过很原始的媒介艰难的通讯的地方。这块徽章的来源看起来是太阳的第三颗行星，那是一个大陆行星。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:165`

### English

We have finally reached the system of Sol, a destination some being went to great pains to communicate through a very primitive medium. The disc's point of origin is seemingly the third rock from the sun, which should be a continental planet.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:171`

### English

Far shores indeed.

### English

Make a statement. Jettison the record into their sun.

## anomaly.110.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.110.name&lang=en)

### 中文

往日之声

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:169`

### English

Voices From the Past

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:175`

## anomaly.110.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.110.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在[From.GetName]表面一座被早已逝去的[Owner.GetPreFTLLowerPlural]民族所抛弃的堡垒内，一张金色碟片静躺在一座石台上，被厚厚的孢子尘埃覆盖着。\n\n起初它被认为是一件微不足道的外星制品，可能有着某种宗教意义，但一台档案计算机很快辨识出它是当初旅行者号探测器发射时携带的镀金唱片。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:170`

### English

In an abandoned fortress on the surface of [From.GetName], evidently the stronghold of some long-dead race of [Owner.GetPreFTLLowerPlural], a golden disc rests on a pedestal beneath a thick covering of spore-dust.\n\nFirst designated to be stowed away as an alien artifact of minor, possibly xeno-religious significance, an archival computer soon identifies it as the golden phonograph record originally launched with the Voyager probe.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:176`

## anomaly.110.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.110.a&lang=en)

### 中文

马上回放。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:171`

### English

Play it back. Now.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:177`

## anomaly.115.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.115.name&lang=en)

### 中文

细胞仓库

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:172`

### English

Cell Cells

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:178`

## anomaly.115.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.115.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.GetName]部分中空。其内部是密集的蜂窝结构，由某种未知的合金打造。其中存放着许多储存外星基因物质的容器。这显然是一座生命档案馆，记录着一个失落的文明的基因资料。多数遗传因子都已严重损毁无法复原，但我们仍能从中学到许多。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:173`

### English

[From.GetName] is partially hollow. Within its interior spaces sprawls a dense honeycomb structure crafted of some unknown alloy, housing containers of alien genetic material. By all appearances this is an archive of life, the genetic record of some earlier civilization come and gone. Most of the DNA is hopelessly degraded, but there is still much to learn here.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:179`

## anomaly.115.b.evopred

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.115.b.evopred&lang=en)

### 中文

真是体贴！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:174`

### English

How considerate!

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:180`

## anomaly.120.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.120.name&lang=en)

### 中文

埃迪克巨石

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:175`

### English

Eddic, Monolithic

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:181`

## anomaly.120.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.120.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.GetName]无人居住，也确实无法居住，但却并非无人造访过。它的表面散布着许多高耸的尖碑，由某种非本星球的矿物雕刻，显然是由某种热衷艺术的太空文明所置放于此。这些碑塔流动的曲线雕琢出了一段如此如梦似幻的历史，其一定是虚构的。 \n\n一定是。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:176`

### English

[From.GetName] is uninhabited and indeed uninhabitable, but not unvisited. Its surface is littered with tall cenotaphs carved from some mineral not native to the planet; evidently placed here by some artistically-inclined spacefaring race. The monoliths' flowing lines deftly chart a history so fantastical it must surely be fictional.\n\nSurely.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:182`

## anomaly.120.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.120.a&lang=en)

### 中文

拍摄后存入档案。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:177`

### English

Image them for the archives.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:183`

## anomaly.120.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.120.b&lang=en)

### 中文

它们……好美。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:178`

### English

They are... beautiful.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:184`

## anomaly.120.b.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.120.b.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

将这些碑文的数字版复制品广泛投放到我们的§Y帝国§!中去。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:179`

### English

Make digital reproductions of the monoliths widely available in our §YEmpire§!.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:185`

## anomaly.121.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.121.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这些来自[From.From.GetName]的碑塔图像的注解版在[Root.GetName]广受欢迎。\n\n许多人表示这些美妙的史册完全改变了他们对外星生物的看法。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:180`

### English

The annotated reproductions of the monolithic imagery found on [From.From.GetName] prove immensely popular in the [Root.GetName].\n\nMany citizens state that the fantastical annals have completely changed how they feel about aliens.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:186`

## anomaly.121.a.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.121.a.tooltip&lang=en)

约§Y600§!£pop£§H人口§!获得§Y亲外主义§!思潮。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:181`

### English

Approximately §Y600§! £pop£ §HPops§! gain the §YXenophile§! Ethic.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:187`

## anomaly.121.b.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.121.b.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

约§Y300§!£pop£§H人口§!获得§Y亲外主义§!思潮。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:182`

### English

Approximately §Y300§! £pop£ §HPops§! gain the §YXenophile§! Ethic.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:188`

## anomaly.125.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.125.name&lang=en)

### 中文

来路不明

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:183`

### English

Unsolicited Mapping

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:189`

## anomaly.125.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.125.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetName]截获求救信号后，向[From.GetName]那不稳定的表面派送了探测器。似乎有一艘未注册的货船最近曾撞毁在这颗行星上。无论它当时运了什么东西，皆已化为尘土。不过其导航电脑中的该星系的调查数据仍然可以恢复。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:184`

### English

Intercepting the distress signal, the [Root.GetName] sends a probe down to the volatile surface of [From.GetName]. It seems an unregistered freighter recently crashed on the planet; whatever it was carrying has been reduced an ashen sludge, but some data from the navigation computer can be recovered and survey data for this system reconstructed.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:190`

## anomaly.125.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.125.a&lang=en)

### 中文

就这么办。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:185`

### English

Make it so.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:191`

## anomaly.130.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.130.name&lang=en)

### 中文

引力坠机

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:186`

### English

Gravity Crush

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:192`

## anomaly.130.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.130.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetName]对小行星的调查暂时停止，因为一架探测器被突如其来的引力异常捕获。\n\n在被极度巨大的力量摧毁之前，这架探测器成功传回了一些数据。而这股力量很快就消失不见了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:187`

### English

The [Root.GetName]'s survey of the asteroid is temporarily halted as a probe is caught by an errant gravitational anomaly.\n\nThe probe manages to transmit some data before it is brutally crushed by the extreme forces - forces that completely dissipate soon after.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:193`

## anomaly.130.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.130.a&lang=en)

### 中文

至少没人受伤。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:188`

### English

At least no one got hurt.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:194`

## anomaly.135.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.135.name&lang=en)

### 中文

废弃游乐场

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:189`

### English

Abandoned Amusement Park

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:195`

## anomaly.135.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.135.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.GetName]上的建筑并没有我们最初想的那么古老。它似乎是某种游乐场。科学专员[Root.GetLeaderName]表示，这些奇妙的装置异常复杂，我们能从中学到很多。在它们建造者的视角中，这可能是尖端的传感阵列，也可能是巨大的艺术设施。\n\n然而，在我们[Root.Owner.GetSpeciesNamePlural]看来，这看起来更像是你会带着小孩子去玩的地方。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:190`

### English

The structures on [From.GetName] are not as old as we first believed. It is a playground or amusement park of some sort. Science Officer [Root.GetLeaderName] notes that many of the contraptions are highly complex creations that we can learn much from and that, from the builders' alien point of view, this might have been a cutting-edge sensor array or even a gigantic art installation.\n\nRegardless, to us [Root.Owner.GetSpeciesNamePlural], it looks mostly like a place where you would take your young and let them amuse themselves.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:196`

## anomaly.140.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.140.name&lang=en)

### 中文

熟悉的古龛

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:191`

### English

Familiar Shrine

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:198`

## anomaly.140.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.140.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

小行星的大部分地方都被激光精细雕刻过，看上去像小型的[Root.Owner.GetSpeciesName]庙宇：这是一座供奉着§Y未叙尊者§!的古龛，祂是诸多[Root.Owner.GetSpeciesAdj]古旧信仰中专司毁灭的神祇。\n\n这座古龛已经在太空中盘旋了许久，比我们掌握太空航行技术的时间还要更加久远——这个发现让我们很多的研究者们百思不得其解。\n\n由于发现的这一物体仍正体不明，科学专员[Root.GetLeaderName]建议我们寻找更多与这个宇宙古龛有相似特征的天体。[Root.leader.GetHeSheCap][Root.leader.GetHasHave]准备了一份附近恒星系中可能的候选列表。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:192`

### English

Most of the asteroid has been laser-chiseled with great precision into the likeness of a small [Root.Owner.GetSpeciesName] temple: a shrine to §Ythe Untold§!, great destroyer-deity of several old [Root.Owner.GetSpeciesAdj] faiths.\n\nThe temple has been tumbling through the quiet of space for longer than we have been capable of space-flight - a finding which disturbs more than a few of our researchers.\n\nDespite the unsettling nature of this discovery, Science Officer [Root.GetLeaderName] has suggested we search for more objects matching the characteristics of this space temple. [Root.leader.GetHeSheCap] [Root.leader.GetHasHave] prepared a list of likely candidates in nearby star systems.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:199`

## anomaly.140.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.140.a&lang=en)

### 中文

不去管这些东西也许是最好的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:193`

### English

Perhaps it would be best if we did not.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:200`

## anomaly.140.a.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.140.a.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

§L就让沉睡的神灵继续沉睡着吧。§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:194`

### English

§LLet sleeping gods lie.§!

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:201`

## anomaly.140.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.140.b&lang=en)

### 中文

我们看看就走。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:195`

### English

Then a-looking we shall go.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:202`

## anomaly.140.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.140.c&lang=en)

### 中文

我们不会受这种异端的影响。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:196`

### English

We will not suffer this heresy.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:203`

## anomaly.140.c.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.140.c.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

命令[Root.GetName]把神殿夷为平地。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:197`

### English

Order the [Root.GetName] to raze the shrine.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:204`

## anomaly.142.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.142.name&lang=en)

### 中文

古神：接近恩赐女神

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:198`

### English

Old Gods: Approaching the Benefactress

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:205`

## anomaly.142.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.142.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetName]已经进入了[Root.GetStarName]星系。船员报告指出那颗恒星周围确实环行着另一个与未叙尊者的神龛别无二致的物体。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:199`

### English

The [Root.GetName] has entered the [Root.GetStarName] system. Its crew reports the star is indeed orbited by another object not dissimilar to the shrine of the Untold.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:206`

## anomaly.142.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.142.a&lang=en)

### 中文

开始调查。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:200`

### English

Let us investigate.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:207`

## anomaly.143.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.143.name&lang=en)

### 中文

古神：接近勇气之神

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:201`

### English

Old Gods: Approaching the Undaunted

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:208`

## anomaly.143.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.143.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetName]已经进入了[Root.GetStarName]星系。船员报告指出另一个在轨道上的物体与未叙尊者的古龛有很高的相似性。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:202`

### English

The [Root.GetName] has entered the [Root.GetStarName] system, where its crew report another object in orbit bearing strong resemblance to a shrine to the Untold.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:209`

## anomaly.143.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.143.a&lang=en)

### 中文

干得好。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:203`

### English

Good work.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:210`

## anomaly.144.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.144.name&lang=en)

### 中文

古神：接近洞察女神

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:204`

### English

Old Gods: Approaching the Percipient

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:211`

## anomaly.144.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.144.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetName]的船员们本以为搜寻洞察女神的神殿会更费功夫一些，没想到刚一进入[Root.GetStarName]就看到了非常明显的指引。这个星系中的星体上被雕刻着人工矿物的图案，纷纷指向了一片中央区域。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:205`

### English

Expecting perhaps for a more difficult search when scanning for the shrine to the Percipient, the crew of [Root.GetName] were not ready to see it obviously signposted when entering [Root.GetStarName]. Artificial mineral patterns have been carved around on stellar bodies in the systems pointing to a central location.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:212`

## anomaly.144.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.144.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我知道了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:206`

### English

I see.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:213`

## anomaly.145.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.145.name&lang=en)

### 中文

所奏为何

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:207`

### English

What Music They Make

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:215`

## anomaly.145.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.145.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.GetName]上的动物——不论是捕食者还是猎物——都会在夜幕降临的时候聚集在一起，然后……唱歌。\n\n嚎叫、咔哒声、叽喳声、咆哮、嘘声混在一起，初听来呕哑嘲哳难为听，但其细听却能发现潜藏着一种可以辨认的旋律。[Root.GetName]上的船员也说那种音乐甚至还挺好听的。\n\n我们认为必然有外来影响干涉才能创造出这种非智慧生物的合唱。我们无法放理解其原因，但至少我们可以研究他们是如何做到的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:208`

### English

The animals of [From.GetName] - predator and prey alike - gather together as night falls and... sing.\n\nThe howls, clicks, chirps, roars and hisses seem discordant at first, but underneath lies a recognizable melody. The crew of the [Root.GetName] suggest it's even rather pleasant to hear.\n\nWe believe an external influence must have interfered in order to develop this non-sapient choir. We cannot fathom as to why, however we may be able to figure out how they did it.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:216`

## anomaly.145.b.evopred

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.145.b.evopred&lang=en)

### 中文

还是通过化学来交流比较好。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:209`

### English

Better communication through chemistry.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:217`

## anomaly.150.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.150.name&lang=en)

### 中文

泽珞之下

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:210`

### English

Below Zro

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:218`

## anomaly.150.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.150.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.GetName]不是一颗寻常的冰巨行星，这颗冰巨行星由极其罕见的冰构成。冰内部包裹着许多泽珞，在行星大气层的上层便可以展开开采。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:211`

### English

[From.GetName] is not only an ice giant, but an ice giant made up of highly unusual solids. There are pockets of Zro trapped in the within reach of the upper levels of the planet's atmosphere.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:219`

## anomaly.150.a.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.150.a.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

发现§Y泽珞冰晶§!矿藏。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:212`

### English

§YZro Ice§! deposit found.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:220`

## anomaly.155.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.155.name&lang=en)

### 中文

不法勾当

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:213`

### English

Illicit Matter

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:221`

## anomaly.155.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.155.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetName]在小行星表面的坑洞中发现了令人不安的隐藏科技。\n\n显然有人——用简单粗略的方法——在此处稳定创造了一个单向虫洞。科学专员[Root.GetLeaderName]得出结论，这颗小行星是一个出口。虫洞的另一端是一块距离某个黑洞几光年远的未知区域，并有少量暗物质——因为受到虹吸——从虫洞中泄漏。\n\n不管是谁建立虫洞，他们似乎已经放弃了此项行动。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:214`

### English

The [Root.GetName] finds a disturbing tangle of technology hidden in a deep crater on the asteroid's surface.\n\nEvidently someone has - by rather simplistic means - managed to stabilize a one-way wormhole. Science Officer [Root.GetLeaderName] suggests that the asteroid is an exit point. The other end opens up somewhere in uncharted space some light-years away from a black hole, and small quantities of dark matter are leaking - being siphoned - through the wormhole.\n\nWhoever set this up seems to have abandoned the operation.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:222`

## anomaly.155.a.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.155.a.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

发现§Y暗物质§!矿藏。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:215`

### English

§YDark Matter§! deposit found.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:223`

## anomaly.160.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.160.name&lang=en)

### 中文

紫雨

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:216`

### English

Purple Rain

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:224`

## anomaly.160.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.160.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

由[Root.GetName]的地表探测器拍摄的[From.GetName]上迷人紫雨的图像，辗转流传到了[Root.Owner.Capital.GetName]的文化精英手中。\n\n虽然这个发现毫无科研价值，但令人浮想联翩的斑斓降雨图像在[Root.Owner.GetSpeciesName]的艺术领域依然擦出了一点复兴的火花，这一事件也许多年后才会开花结果。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:217`

### English

Images capturing [From.GetName]'s mesmerizing purple-colored rain, taken by the [Root.GetName]'s surface exploration probes, somehow find their way into the hands of the cultural elite on [Root.Owner.Capital.GetName].\n\nWhile the discovery is of no scientific value, the evocative imagery of the colorful precipitation sparks a minor renaissance in the [Root.Owner.GetSpeciesName] art world, the positive effects of which shall be felt for years to come.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:225`

## anomaly.165.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.165.name&lang=en)

### 中文

古神：恩赐女神的神殿

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:218`

### English

Old Gods: Shrine to the Benefactress

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:227`

## anomaly.165.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.165.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

破解恩赐女神神殿中的象形文字要比预计的顺利许多。负责该项目的语言学家们称，这些文本莫名地令人亲切：能轻易地“触动”他们的心弦，尽管其中的语法古老又冗杂。\n\n然而，他们指出，这些信息暗示了一些非常晦涩难懂的主题和上下文。我们除非与关于这位四位一体的神的其他文本结合起来，否则很难完全理解他们的信息。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:219`

### English

Deciphering the pictograms in the Benefactress' shrine went more smoothly than anticipated. The linguists assigned to the project describe these texts as somehow familiar: "clicking" easily for them, despite the grammar's age and complexity.\n\nThey note, however, these written messages allude to some truly obscure themes and contexts. We cannot fully understand their messages until they are combined with other texts associated with this quadrumvirate godhead.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:228`

## anomaly.166.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.166.name&lang=en)

### 中文

古神：勇气之神的神殿

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:220`

### English

Old Gods: Shrine to the Undaunted

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:229`

## anomaly.166.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.166.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在经过了连日的仔细、甚至好几次令人崩溃的工作之后，科学专员[Root.GetLeaderName]取回了勇气之神的宗卷：一个普通的织物卷轴用用坚固塑料膜封存。这个卷轴被带回[Root.GetName]进行进一步研究。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:221`

### English

After days of careful, and at times excruciating effort, Science Officer [Root.GetLeaderName] has retrieved the Doctrine of the Undaunted: a humble textile scroll coated with a sturdy plastic polymer. The scroll has been brought back to the [Root.GetName] for further analysis.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:230`

## anomaly.167.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.167.name&lang=en)

### 中文

古神：洞察女神的神殿

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:222`

### English

Old Gods: Shrine to the Percipient

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:231`

## anomaly.167.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.167.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

科学专员[Root.GetLeaderName]汇报了有关洞察女神神殿的情况。\n\n它的墙壁上镶嵌着表语文字，被一层层的保护漆严重地扭曲了。工作人员试图初步破译这种文字，但是失败了；它的语法与我们遇到的其他语言体系截然不同。[Root.GetName]的船员试图去除保护漆，希望能看得更加清楚，但没有成功。\n\n在任务即将宣布失败的时候——在恼怒即将爆发的边缘——[Root.GetLeaderName]突然开始感知到一段段的连贯文本。\n\n船员们没有花很长时间就利用这些线索编成了索引。很快，他们就把天花板、墙壁和地板描绘成了一个螺旋形的图案：这是一幅更广泛的图画的一部分，我们相信可以用其他神殿的文字来完成。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:223`

### English

Science Officer [Root.GetLeaderName] has reported back from the Percipient's shrine.\n\nIts walls are inlaid with logographic script, heavily distorted by layers of a protective lacquer. Initial attempts to decipher this text failed; its grammar is too distinct from other linguistic groups we've encountered. The crew of the [Root.GetName] tried removing the protective lacquer for a clearer view, but to no avail.\n\nThe mission was about to be declared a failure when - on the cusp of an irritated outburst - [Root.GetLeaderName] suddenly began to perceive discrete lines of coherent text.\n\nIt did not take the crew long to utilize these insights as a codex. Soon they had the ceiling, walls and floor imaged in a spiral pattern: part of a broader picture which we believe may be completed using texts from the other shrines.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:232`

## anomaly.168.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.168.name&lang=en)

### 中文

古神：揭示

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:224`

### English

Old Gods: Revelation

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:233`

## anomaly.168.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.168.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

完整的众神教条——因为我们认为被恢复的文本就应该被这样视作——对我们具有启示意义，它对于我们古老宗教赖以建立的那些幻想基础是具有毁灭性的。\n\n当放在以[Root.GetSpeciesAdj]为中心的情景中时，这些文本清楚地表明，我们注定要在一位宗教领袖强有力的手段的引导下实现伟大的功业。我们注定要成为神，但我们的过去充斥着流产的预言和因无知而产生的罪恶。救赎是短暂的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:225`

### English

The complete doctrine of the gods - for that is how we believe the recovered texts should be regarded - is as revelatory to us as it is devastating to the fantasies upon which our ancient religions were founded.\n\nWhen placed within a [Root.GetSpeciesAdj]-centered context, these texts make it clear that we were meant for greatness, guided by the strong hand of a religious leader. We are destined for godhood, but our past is littered with unfulfilled prophecies and sin born of ignorance. Redemption is ephemeral.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:234`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.170.name&lang=en)

### 中文

流亡者

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:239`

### English

The Exile

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:249`

## anomaly.170.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.170.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在[FromFrom.GetName]表面探测到一个小型研究站发出的信号，但[Root.Owner.GetName]既没有这个研究站的成立记录也没有对其授权记录。里面只有一位[Root.Owner.GetSpeciesAdj]科学家，如果在[FromFrom.GetName]的研究可信的话，平心而论确实是一位颇有才华且成果丰硕的研究者。但其身份存在一个问题，我们无法在帝国正式记录匹配到相关信息，此人也拒绝公开身份，只说自己是一个“流亡者”。\n\n其表达了回到[Root.Owner.Capital.GetName]直接为[Root.Owner.GetName]服务的愿望，条件是[Root.GetName]立刻离开[FromFrom.GetName]，并且任何[Root.Owner.GetSpeciesAdj]飞船都不能返回那里。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:240`

### English

The readings originate from a small research outpost - which the [Root.Owner.GetName] has no records of establishing or indeed authorizing - on the surface of [FromFrom.GetName]. It is staffed by one [Root.Owner.GetSpeciesAdj] scientist, a fairly talented and industrious one if their research on [FromFrom.GetName] is anything to go by. There is a problem with regards to their identity however; we have been unable to match them against official state records, and they refuse to identify themselves save for stating that they are "an exile."\n\nThey have expressed an interest in returning to [Root.Owner.Capital.GetName] and serving the [Root.Owner.GetName] directly, on the condition that the [Root.GetName] immediately depart [FromFrom.GetName] and no [Root.Owner.GetSpeciesAdj] ships ever return there.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:250`

## anomaly.170.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.170.a&lang=en)

想都别想。给我把那个研究站翻个底朝天。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:241`

### English

Out of the question. Search every nook and cranny of that outpost.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:251`

### English

Detain §YThe Exile§! and ransack the unauthorized research outpost.

§Y[Root.GetName]§! was found in a Research Outpost on the [Root.GetHomeWorldBiome] §Y[Root.GetBackgroundPlanet]§!.

## anomaly.171.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.171.name&lang=en)

### 中文

冷清空旷

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:246`

### English

Cold and Empty

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:256`

## anomaly.171.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.171.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

研究站本身没什么特别。它规模很小，只是用来对抗严酷环境的一个临时庇护所，仅能在严酷的环境拖垮之前，进行时间有限的表面级的星球研究。\n\n[From.From.From.GetName]的研究站已经接近生命的尽头，但那颗星球的严寒还未压下最后一根稻草。那些功能正常、廉价且可抛弃的器材得到了充分使用，本地数据库中填满了详尽却极为寻常的行星数据，以及在严酷环境下进行的简单试验。\n\n有趣的是，似乎研究站地基下的矿脉并没有得到深入检查。[Root.GetName]的扫描表明这座大部分被冰雪覆盖的山脉可能是中空的。更令人警觉的是，科学专员[Root.GetLeaderName]报告这座研究站唯一的栖居者，那位神秘的“流亡者”消失不见了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:247`

### English

There is nothing unusual about the research outpost itself. It is little but a temporary shelter erected against the savage elements, allowing surface-level planetary study for a limited time before it wears down and conditions become untenable.\n\nThe outpost on [From.From.From.GetName] is nearing the end of its lifespan, but the planet's intense chill has yet to critically degrade the impermanent structure. The equipment - functional, cheap, intended to be abandoned - is well-used, and the local databanks are filled with detailed but ultimately mundane accounts of the planet as well as the simple experiments that were carried out in its harsh environment.\n\nCuriously, no closer examination seems to have been made of the mineral outcropping the outpost's foundations rest on; scans by the [Root.GetName] indicate that the geological formation, a mountain mostly buried under ice, may be partially hollow. More alarmingly, Science Officer [Root.GetLeaderName] reports that the sole inhabitant of the outpost, the mysterious "exile," has gone missing and is nowhere to be found.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:257`

想不通。

### English

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.172.name&lang=en)

### 中文

深入地下

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:249`

### English

Down Below

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:259`

## anomaly.172.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.172.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.From.From.GetName]上的研究站有点不寻常。建筑本身并无奇怪之处——用来对抗严酷环境的小型临时庇护所，仅能在严酷的环境拖垮之前，进行时间有限的表面级的星球研究——但它恰巧建在突破星球表面冰海的岩石之上。\n\n[Root.GetName]的成员发现，这座山的内部有复杂的洞穴和通道，由研究站内部便可进入。\n\n在一个较大的岩洞里，他们发现了研究站原成员脱水的遗体。这些[Root.Owner.GetSpeciesAdj]研究员原先被派往别处，但显然，他们却来到了这个无人居住的危险行星。\n\n当然，那个所谓的“流亡者”如今已经不见踪影。[Root.GetName]发射了一所逃生舱，将逝者的遗体送回[Root.Owner.Capital.GetName]。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:250`

### English

There is something unusual about the research outpost on [From.From.From.GetName]. The structure itself is commonplace enough - a temporary shelter erected against the harsh elements, allowing surface-level planetary study for a limited time before it wears down and conditions become untenable - but it is situated on top of a rocky formation poking through the sea of ice that covers the planet.\n\nThe crew of the [Root.GetName] found an intricate system of caves and narrow passages leading into this mountain, accessible from inside the research outpost.\n\nIn one of the larger cavern-halls, they found the desiccated remains of the outpost's original staff. This contingent of [Root.Owner.GetSpeciesAdj] researchers had originally been dispatched elsewhere, but evidently diverted to this uninhabited and hostile planet to set up shop.\n\nOf course, the self-proclaimed "exile" is now nowhere to be found. The [Root.GetName] has launched one of its escape pods, transporting the bodies of the deceased back to [Root.Owner.Capital.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:260`

## anomaly.175.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.175.name&lang=en)

### 中文

咸咸的

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:251`

### English

Salty

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:261`

## anomaly.175.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.175.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

科研船[Root.GetName]找不到我们在[From.From.GetName]上探测到的奇怪读数的来源，这些读数似乎只是传感器的回声。不过，科学专员[Root.GetLeaderName]——一位自称的美食家和历史爱好者——声称在那里有了重大发现。\n\n在几块巨大的地表冰层中发现的矿藏似乎与[Root.Owner.GetSpeciesAdj]星盐关系密切：星盐曾经是许多传统[Root.Owner.GetSpeciesAdj]菜肴中不可或缺的调料，但长期以来一直被认为在[Root.Owner.Capital.GetName]上已经枯竭。\n\n烹饪史学家长期以来一直怀疑这种矿物调味品不是我们母星的原产物，而科学专员在[From.From.GetName]上的发现则为这一理论提供了一些支持。至于这种盐的味道是否真的像以前的[Root.Owner.GetSpeciesAdj]星盐，这并不是最重要的：仅仅是用如此相近的调味料恢复传统食品的想法，就一定会让我们的经济和味觉非常兴奋。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:252`

### English

Science ship [Root.GetName] could find no source for the strange readings we detected on [From.From.GetName], which appear to be mere sensor echoes. Nevertheless, Science Officer [Root.GetLeaderName] - a self-proclaimed gourmet chef and history buff - claims to have made a significant discovery there.\n\nA mineral deposit found in several vast patches of surface ice appears closely related to [Root.Owner.GetSpeciesAdj] Stellar Salt: once an essential ingredient in many traditional [Root.Owner.GetSpeciesAdj] dishes, long thought depleted on [Root.Owner.Capital.GetName].\n\nCulinary historians have long suspected that this mineral condiment was not native to our homeworld, and the Science Officer's discovery on [From.From.GetName] lends some credence to this theory. Whether or not the salt actually tastes like the [Root.Owner.GetSpeciesAdj] Stellar Salt of old is ultimately of little consequence: the mere idea of reviving traditional foodstuffs with such closely-matched ingredients is bound to excite our economy and our palates.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:262`

## anomaly.175.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.175.a&lang=en)

### 中文

可口。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:253`

### English

Savory.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:263`

## anomaly.180.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.180.name&lang=en)

### 中文

小家伙

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:254`

### English

Nimkip

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:264`

## anomaly.180.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.180.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.From.GetName]上满是微小且斑斓的生命体，介于植物与动物之间。它们缺乏真正的进化潜能，但与体型相比，它们的力量和组织协调性令人震惊。\n\n科学专员[Root.GetLeaderName]迷上了他们，并承认对那个“红色的小家伙”情有独钟。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:255`

### English

[From.From.GetName] is populated by tiny, colorful lifeforms that straddle the divide between flora and fauna. They lack any serious evolutionary potential, but their group coordination and great strength relative to their size is impressive.\n\nScience Officer [Root.GetLeaderName] has taken a shine to them, admitting a particular soft spot for "the red ones."

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:265`

令人愉悦。

### English

Delightful.

[Root.GetName]的工作人员们进行了详尽迅速的驱除工作，保证这些可爱的外星生物再也不会侵犯[Root.Owner.GetName]。\n

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:258`

### English

A swift extermination campaign carried out by the crew of the [Root.GetName] will ensure that this cutesy alien menace can never rise to threaten the [Root.Owner.GetName].\n

## anomaly.185.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.185.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

信号似乎是从某个类似信号天线的结构中传来的。我方已经从[Root.GetName]发射了探索无人机，希望能够在星球表面发现更多线索。\n\n科学专员[Root.GetLeaderName]所发现的洞穴仓库十分容易地就被撬开。他判定这座仓库为某种早已灭亡的外星种族所用，似乎是其族群最后的一处避难所。该种族的科技水平虽然较为先进，但似乎从未进行太空探索，结果因为某种涉及到整颗星球的自然灾害而消亡了。库房面积广大，其中充斥着成千上万个黑匣子。据[Root.GetName]判定，这些匣子都是数据储存装置。\n\n已发起§Y特殊项目§!来进一步对这些匣子进行解读。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:264`

### English

The signal originates from the remains of an antenna-like structure. Exploratory probes were dispatched from the [Root.GetName] to the surface to investigate the matter more closely.\n\nWhat Science Officer [Root.GetLeaderName] found was a cavernous vault that, while easily pried open, was evidently the last safehouse and refuge of some long-dead race of aliens. While reasonably technologically advanced, it seems they never reached for the stars and perished to some planet-wide natural disaster. The vault is immense, housing thousands upon thousands of black boxes that the [Root.GetName] has determined to be data storage devices.\n\nA §YSpecial Project§! has been prepared to decode their contents.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:274`

## anomaly.190.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.190.name&lang=en)

### 中文

卫星勘测项目

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:265`

### English

Moon Projection

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:275`

## anomaly.190.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.190.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

科学专员[Root.GetLeaderName]汇报说，他们觉得在[Root.GetName]上采集的感应数据一点也不可靠。其中最为可疑的就是[From.From.GetName]的卫星，他们已发起§Y特殊项目§!来进行进一步的调查。\n\n详情请查看情报日志。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:266`

### English

Science Officer [Root.GetLeaderName] reports that they do not trust the sensor readings they've gathered aboard the [Root.GetName], not one bit of it. They are especially suspicious of [From.From.GetName]'s moon, and have outlined a §YSpecial Project§! for further investigation.\n\nConsult the Situation Log for details.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:276`

## anomaly.191.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.191.name&lang=en)

### 中文

卫星勘测结果

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:267`

### English

Moon Projected

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:277`

## anomaly.191.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.191.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在卫星上发现了十分不可思议的装置。其中心为三进制电脑，其程序似乎在不断地根据其收集的行星数据进行新的排列运算。计算后产生的数据经过强力通讯列阵调制后，被发射到卫星所环绕的星球表面。虽然这信号乍一看来似乎只是乱码，但这种调制方式却能让信号进入行星大气层并被反射后变得和普通的感应采集数据没有区别。\n\n虽然我们无法判定这装置的用处，但通过此次调查，我学习了外星生物的工程技术，受益匪浅。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:268`

### English

There is a device on the moon, and it is mystifying. At its core lies a ternary computer, seemingly tasked with endlessly calculating new permutations based on a core set of planetary readings. These values are then passed to a powerful comm array, which modulates the data before blasting it at the surface of its parent planet. The raw signal appears nonsensical at a casual glance, but the careful modulation ensures that it becomes nearly indistinguishable from legitimate sensor readings once the signal is reflected from the parent planet's surface and filtered through its atmosphere.\n\nThe purpose of this set-up is difficult to deduce, but provides a good opportunity to study alien engineering techniques.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:278`

## anomaly.191.a.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.191.a.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

卫星获得了§Y卫星勘测§!修正：\n£engineering£§G+6§!\n\n

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:269`

### English

Adds the §YProjecting Moon§! modifier to the moon:\n£engineering£ §G+6§!\n\n

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:279`

## anomaly.195.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.195.name&lang=en)

### 中文

薄暮海岸

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:270`

### English

Misty Shores

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:280`

## anomaly.195.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.195.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

科学专员[Root.GetLeaderName]报告说，在[From.From.GetName]海域之中有人为制造的大型且柔韧的几何网格，材料主要为某种复合塑料。\n\n这些塑料网格从大气层外的轨道上依然清晰可见，其作用暂时不明，似乎只是用来装饰的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:271`

### English

Science Officer [Root.GetLeaderName] reports that large, flexible geometric grids made up of some plastic polymer - clearly artificial in nature - have been placed in the seas of [From.From.GetName].\n\nVisible from orbit, they seem to serve no purpose other than, possibly, an aesthetic one.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:281`

## anomaly.200.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.200.name&lang=en)

### 中文

地热能源

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:272`

### English

Geothermal

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:282`

## anomaly.200.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.200.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

轨道上监测到的规律性敲击声原来是行星上十分古老的巨大地热采集器，仍然在垂死运行着。很久前就被遗弃的这些机器虽然已经不太坚固，但仍然在源源不断地从星球核心泵出超高温的液体。\n\n虽然其储能单元已经全部破损泄露，但仍然能够从中获取少量能源。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:273`

### English

The measured pounding observed from orbit is the motion of immense and ancient geothermal extractors, breathing their last. Built and then abandoned at some point in the distant past, the vast batteries of disintegrating machinery have been pumping up superheated fluids from the planet's core ever since.\n\nTheir storage capacitors are all broken or leaking, but some energy can still be siphoned from them.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:283`

## anomaly.205.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.205.name&lang=en)

### 中文

一马平川

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:274`

### English

Completely Leveled

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:284`

## anomaly.205.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.205.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在彻底调查[From.From.GetName]的地理状况后，[Root.GetName]并未能找到任何值得注意的现象。\n\n倒不如说，此处的地理状况平淡得让人惊讶……其唯一特征就是一点特征都没有。虽然地势稍有高低之分，但[From.From.GetName]的整个地表都十分不自然地平整。同时，星球上的各种植被也十分平淡，令人过目就忘。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:275`

### English

A thorough geological survey of [From.From.GetName] by the [Root.GetName] reveals nothing of interest.\n\nIn fact, it is extraordinarily uninteresting - its only defining feature is its lack of defining features. There are depressions and elevations, yes, but on the whole the surface of [From.From.GetName] can be regarded as unnaturally flat. Likewise, the native flora and fauna are strikingly docile and unworthy of attention.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:285`

## anomaly.205.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.205.a&lang=en)

### 中文

这算是……好事吧？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:276`

### English

That's... good?

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:286`

## anomaly.205.a.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.205.a.tooltip&lang=en)
