所有£blocker£§Y地块障碍§!已从[From.From.GetName]被移除。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:277`

### English

All £blocker£ §YTile Blockers§! have been cleared from [From.From.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:287`

## anomaly.210.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.210.name&lang=en)

### 中文

维度陷阱

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:278`

### English

Dimensional Pocket

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:288`

## anomaly.210.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.210.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetName]本应前往[From.From.From.GetName]研究其附近的异常状况，但途中却遭遇了不测。包括科学专员[Root.GetLeaderName]及船员在内的整艘舰船在调查太空废墟时突然消失不见了。\n\n而在数分钟后，舰船又重新出现。\n\n据[Root.GetLeaderName]汇报，他们在一瞬之间到达了某个超维度空间。他们从外部观察口看到其附近布满了各式各样的外星舰船，都一动不动地被困在无限的虚空之中。在船员能够判定所在位置之前，船外的景色突然消失，而舰船又重新回到了普通空间之中。\n\n但不幸的是，船上的一半工作人员都消失了——科学专员[Root.GetLeaderName]认为这些船员应该是因为某种不可知的机制而永远地被困在那里了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:279`

### English

The [Root.GetName] never made it all the way to [From.From.From.GetName] to study the anomalies surrounding it. The ship, along with Science Officer [Root.GetLeaderName] and crew, blinked out of existence as it was navigating the debris field.\n\nAnd then reappeared minutes later.\n\n[Root.GetLeaderName] reports that they found themselves, briefly, in some extra-dimensional space. External viewports revealed that they were surrounded by countless ships of alien design, suspended in a seemingly endless void. Before the crew could get their bearings, the vision abruptly faded and they found themselves back in regular space.\n\nHowever, roughly half of the crew are missing - Science Officer [Root.GetLeaderName] speculates that they may have been selectively trapped, by some unknowable mechanism, in that strange space.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:289`

## anomaly.211.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.211.name&lang=en)

### 中文

维度漂移

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:280`

### English

Dimensional Drift

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:290`

## anomaly.211.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.211.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

原本被认为迷失在维度裂缝中的[Root.GetName]失踪船员重新出现了。\n\n所有失踪船员都被放置在一艘与我方科技飞船相似的飞船上，漂浮在[Root.GetName]原本遇到维度陷阱的地方。船员们全数声称，并不记得这期间发生了什么事情，只是隐约觉得自己离开了一段时间。\n\n船员们希望能够回到科学专员[Root.GetLeaderName]手下工作，并将飞船移交给了[Root.Owner.GetSpeciesAdj]帝国。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:281`

### English

The missing members of the [Root.GetName]'s crew, thought lost to the dimensional rift, have reappeared.\n\nThey hailed us from aboard an unknown vessel, not entirely dissimilar to our own science ships, idling close to where the [Root.GetName] originally experienced the dimensional disturbance. They claim to remember nothing of their time outside our dimension, aside from a vague awareness of having been away for some time.\n\nThey wish for nothing but to return to service under Science Officer [Root.GetLeaderName], and intend to surrender their ship to [Root.Owner.GetSpeciesAdj] authorities.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:291`

## anomaly.211.desc.bio

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.211.desc.bio&lang=en)

### 中文

原本被认为迷失在维度裂缝中的[Root.GetName]失踪船员重新出现了。\n\n他们从一艘有机舰船上向我们呼叫，这艘船诡异地与我们的科研船相似，但又微妙地……不同。它的移动方式、脉搏完全不遵循任何已知的基因蓝图，仿佛是某种扭曲的外星意识对我们舰船的怪异理解。它静静的在[Root.GetName]第一次遭遇扰动的坐标附近漂浮、等待着。\n\n船员们什么也不记得。只是有一种模糊的缺席感，一种去了别的地方的感觉，比感受到的时间长得多。然而，它们又没有改变，至少他们自己是这么说的。\n\n船员们希望能够回到科学专员[Root.GetLeaderName]手下工作，并将他们怪异而沉默飞船毫不犹豫地移交给了[Root.Owner.GetSpeciesAdj]帝国。但当我们接收到它们的信息时，一种深沉而陌生的共鸣在通信中嗡嗡作响。船虽然静止不动，但感觉好像在倾听。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:282`

### English

The missing crew of the [Root.GetName], long presumed lost to the dimensional rift, have inexplicably reappeared.\n\nThey hailed us from aboard an unfamiliar organic construct, its form eerily similar to our own science vessels, yet subtly... off. The way it moves, the way it pulses, it follows no known genetic blueprint, as though it were a reflection of our ships seen through a warped, alien mind. It drifted silently near the coordinates where the [Root.GetName] first encountered the disturbance, waiting.\n\nThe crew remembers nothing of their time beyond. Only a vague sensation of absence, of having been somewhere else, for far longer than time should allow. And yet, they are unchanged. Or so they claim.\n\nThey wish to return to service under Science Officer [Root.GetLeaderName], and they offer their strange, silent vessel to [Root.Owner.GetSpeciesAdj] authorities without hesitation. But as we receive their transmission, a deep and unfamiliar resonance hums through the comms. The ship, though still, feels as though it is listening.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:292`

## anomaly.211.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.211.a&lang=en)

### 中文

很好。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:283`

### English

Very well.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:293`

## anomaly.211.a.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.211.a.tooltip&lang=en)

获得一艘科研船。

### English

Gain a Science Ship.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:294`

## anomaly.211.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.211.b&lang=en)

### 中文

为了[Root.Owner.GetSpeciesAdj]人民的安全必须毁灭它。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:285`

### English

The safety of the [Root.Owner.GetSpeciesAdj] people hinges on their destruction.

## anomaly.215.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.215.name&lang=en)

### 中文

丰饶景色

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:288`

### English

Cornucopicturesque

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:298`

## anomaly.215.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.215.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.From.GetName]的表面充斥着许多处过于茂盛的植被，十分不自然。\n\n科学专员[Root.GetLeaderName]仍然无法解释这颗星球的土地为部分地区异常肥沃的机理，但认为此处的植物稍经加工就能够被[Root.Owner.GetSpeciesAdj]正常食用。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:289`

### English

[From.From.GetName]'s surface is dotted with irregular patches of intense greenery.\n\nScience Officer [Root.GetLeaderName] is not yet ready to explain the mechanics behind the soil's localized hyperfertility, but asserts that much of the native flora is suitable for [Root.Owner.GetSpeciesAdj] consumption with only minimal processing needed.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:299`

## anomaly.215.desc.serv

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.215.desc.serv&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.From.GetName]的地表散布着不规则的密集植被。\n\n科学专员[Root.GetLeaderName]尚无法对土地局部性异常肥沃背后的原理作出解释，但声称大多数本地植物适合供[Root.Owner.GetSpeciesAdj]活体陈设食用，而且几乎无需进行加工。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:290`

### English

[From.From.GetName]'s surface is dotted with irregular patches of intense greenery.\n\nScience Officer [Root.GetLeaderName] is not yet ready to explain the mechanics behind the soil's localized hyperfertility, but asserts that much of the native flora is suitable for [Root.Owner.GetSpeciesAdj] Bio-Trophy consumption with only minimal processing needed.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:300`

## anomaly.215.a.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.215.a.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

向[From.GetName]添加§Y丰饶§!修正：\n增加§G食物§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:291`

### English

Adds the §YCornucopia§! modifier to [From.GetName]:\nAdditional §GFood§!

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:301`

## anomaly.220.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.220.name&lang=en)

### 中文

矿产丰富

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:292`

### English

Mineralistic

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:302`

## anomaly.220.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.220.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

科学专员[Root.GetLeaderName]就[From.GetName]的矿藏进行了详细报告。由于独特的地壳构造进程，[Root.GetName]的地表附近聚集了大量的珍贵矿脉。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:293`

### English

Science Officer [Root.GetLeaderName] has provided us with a detailed report on [From.GetName]'s geological riches. Apparently the planet's unique tectonic history has served to concentrate many minerals of interest to the [Root.GetName] near the surface.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:303`

## anomaly.220.a.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.220.a.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

向[From.GetName]添加§Y原矿石§!修正：\n增加§G矿物§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:294`

### English

Adds the §YRaw Materials§! modifier to [From.GetName]:\nAdditional §GMinerals§!

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:304`

## anomaly.225.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.225.name&lang=en)

### 中文

游荡阴影

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:295`

### English

Swirling Shadows

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:305`

## anomaly.225.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.225.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.GetName]的表面总会有大片的阴影掠过。\n\n造成这些阴影的并非是云朵，而是大气层上方漂浮着的一层有机物质，为了便于活动，上面还有关节一般的结构。\n\n科学专员[Root.GetLeaderName]仍然无法判定这些有机物质是植被还是动物，也不能解释其造成的阴影到底有什么作用。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:296`

### English

Immense, ragged planes of shadow drift across [From.GetName]'s face.\n\nThey are cast not by clouds but by sheets of organic material drifting through the upper layers of the atmosphere, hinged - or rather jointed - to allow for a small degree of articulation.\n\nScience Officer [Root.GetLeaderName] is as yet unwilling to say whether these things are flora or fauna, or what possible purpose, if any, their elaborate shadow-casting might serve.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:306`

## anomaly.225.a.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.225.a.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

添加§Y皮影戏§!修正至[From.GetName]：\n£society£ §G+4§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:297`

### English

Adds the §YShadow Play§! modifier to [From.GetName]:\n£society£ §G+4§!

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:307`

## anomaly.230.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.230.name&lang=en)

### 中文

诡变多端

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:298`

### English

Grimacing

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:308`

## anomaly.230.desc.v1

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.230.desc.v1&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.GetName]的地理构造的确是处在变动之中。科学专员[Root.GetLeaderName]认为这是地壳活动造成的。这一地壳活动虽然有趣但是却极其深奥。不过[Root.Owner.GetName]所感兴趣的，是某种可以停止星球这一无尽且无序的自我变构的方法。简单来说，使用此方法转化星球不仅用时极短，而且理论上也有可能让星球变得更加易于居住。\n\n[Root.GetLeaderName]需要额外的200能量币进行地理活动勘测，以证明科学专员所提出的外科手术刀式精准改造方案是否真的可行。当然，如果以后想要使用现有的环境改造科技，也是可以的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:299`

### English

The very geological structure of [From.GetName] is indeed in flux, driven by what Science Officer [Root.GetLeaderName] assures us is a tectonic process as fascinating as it is esoteric. What is of more interest to the [Root.Owner.GetName] is that we may be able to strategically halt the planet's ceaseless, chaotic self-transformation. In short, a near-instant terraforming of the planet into something slightly - if not entirely - more habitable, is theoretically possible.\n\n[Root.GetLeaderName] estimates that an additional 200 Energy Credits will be needed to power a thorough mapping of planetary activity and determine the validity of what the Science Officer has termed "a surgical terraforming strike." Of course, we can always choose to terraform it using existing technologies at a later date.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:309`

## anomaly.230.desc.v2

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.230.desc.v2&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.GetName]的地理构造的确是处在变动之中。科学专员[Root.GetLeaderName]认为这是地壳活动造成的。这一地壳活动虽然有趣但是却极其深奥。不过[Root.Owner.GetName]所感兴趣的，是某种可以停止星球自我变更的步骤。简单来说，使用此步骤转化星球后，不仅用时极短，而且理论上也有可能让星球变得更加易于居住。\n\n[Root.GetLeaderName]需要额外的200能量币进行地理活动勘测，以证明科学专员所提出的方案是否真的可以使用。目前我们还无法进行常规性的环境改造，所以这次真的是千载难逢的机会。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:300`

### English

The very geological structure of [From.GetName] is indeed in flux, driven by what Science Officer [Root.GetLeaderName] assures us is a tectonic process as fascinating as it is esoteric. What is of more interest to the [Root.Owner.GetName] is that we may be able to strategically halt the planet's ceaseless, chaotic self-transformation. In short, a near-instant stabilization of the planet into something slightly - if not entirely - more habitable, is theoretically possible.\n\n[Root.GetLeaderName] estimates that an additional 200 Energy Credits will be needed to power a thorough mapping of planetary activity and determine the validity of what the Science Officer has termed "a surgical terraforming strike." Actual terraforming lies beyond our grasp at present, so this is an exciting opportunity.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:310`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.230.a&lang=en)

仅仅研究其变化的自然过程。

Just study its natural processes.

给予[Root.GetName]200能量币。

The [Root.GetName] will have its 200 credits.

花费能量币，尝试稳定星球并使之更宜居。大约有§Y50%的成功几率§!。\n

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:304`

Spend Energy Credits to attempt to stabilize the planet in a more favorable state, with roughly a §Y50% chance of success§!.\n

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.230.c&lang=en)

我们不想在这种不确定的假设上浪费能量币。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:305`

### English

We have no credits to spend on vague hypotheses.

## anomaly.231.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.231.name&lang=en)

### 中文

战术性环境改造

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:306`

### English

Tactical Terraforming

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:316`

## anomaly.231.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.231.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

科学专员[Root.GetLeaderName]汇报说，[From.From.GetName]的确能够进行改造！在星球的自身转化进入时长几个月的不稳定阶段时，我们有一个非常短暂的时机。[Root.GetLeaderName]认为可以将小型炸药植入星球地壳中，再加上星球大气层的战略性辐照作用，便可以停止[From.From.GetName]永不停歇的自我转化过程，将其转变为更加易于居住的星球。\n\n已经设立了§Y特殊项目§!，但时间十分紧迫。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:307`

### English

Science Officer [Root.GetLeaderName] reports that [From.From.GetName] can indeed be terraformed! A narrow window of opportunity is expected to open shortly, as the planet enters a particularly unstable phase of its cycle lasting a couple of months. [Root.GetLeaderName] believes that miniaturized explosive charges delivered to the planet's crust and strategic irradiation of the atmosphere during this time will halt [From.From.GetName]'s tumultuous changes and stabilize it in a form somewhat more suitable for sustaining life.\n\nA §YSpecial Project§! has been issued, but we do not have much time.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:317`

## anomaly.232.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.232.name&lang=en)

### 中文

战术性环境改造失败

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:308`

### English

Tactical Terraforming Failed

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:318`

## anomaly.232.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.232.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetName]的船员遗憾地汇报：虽然我方成功判定了环境改造的时机，但最终仍然没赶上改造[From.From.GetName]的机会。这样的机会大概几个世纪之内是不会再有了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:309`

### English

The crew of the [Root.GetName] are disappointed to report that the window of opportunity for terraforming [From.From.GetName] has closed, despite their best efforts to identify it in time. Another opportunity is unlikely to arise for another few centuries.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:319`

## anomaly.233.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.233.name&lang=en)

### 中文

战术性环境改造成功

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:310`

### English

Tactical Terraforming Successful

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:320`

## anomaly.233.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.233.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

科学专员[Root.GetLeaderName]十分骄傲地汇报：[From.From.GetName]可以被重新定位为干旱星球，而不再是荒芜星球了。此次试验的成功大大促进了[Root.Owner.GetSpeciesAdj]的科学发展。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:311`

### English

Science Officer [Root.GetLeaderName] is proud to report that [From.From.GetName] can now be re-classified as an Arid rather than Barren planet. This grand experiment has greatly enriched the [Root.Owner.GetSpeciesAdj] sciences.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:321`

## anomaly.233.desc.mach

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.233.desc.mach&lang=en)

### 中文

科学专员[Root.GetLeaderName]自豪地报告称[From.From.GetName]现在已可被重新分类为机械星球而不是荒芜星球。这次大实验极大地丰富了[Root.Owner.GetSpeciesAdj]的科学知识。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:312`

### English

Science Officer [Root.GetLeaderName] is proud to report that [From.From.GetName] can now be re-classified as a Machine planet rather than Barren planet. This grand experiment has greatly enriched the [Root.Owner.GetSpeciesAdj] sciences.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:322`

## anomaly.235.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.235.name&lang=en)

### 中文

光影艺术

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:313`

### English

Lightshow

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:323`

## anomaly.235.desc.v1

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.235.desc.v1&lang=en)

### 中文

科学专员[Root.GetLeaderName]汇报说，覆盖[From.GetName]的冰层下遍布的闪光波浪源于外星科技。\n\n在这颗星球上曾经发生过上古战争，当时有一架外星战机解体后在此处坠落，只有露在船身外的主武器核心没被摧毁。看起来这个外星种族所运用的激光武器与[Root.Owner.GetSpeciesAdj]舰队目前正在使用的相似，其武器核心不定期地向[From.GetName]的冰层表面放射低能射束，但因为行星的气候过于恶劣，激光无法融化冰层，所以就产生了十分美丽的光影艺术。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:314`

### English

Science Officer [Root.GetLeaderName] reports that the luminous waves coursing through the ice that blankets [From.GetName] are of alien origin.\n\nSome ancient battle has deposited scattered wreckage from an alien fighter on the frozen planet, and only the exposed core of its main weapons remains intact. It seems this alien race used lasers similar to the ones currently in use by [Root.Owner.GetSpeciesAdj] fleets, and the fizzling innards periodically discharge low-powered beams into the ice of [From.GetName]. The planet's harsh climate prevents the lasers from actually melting the ice, allowing the chilly matrix to act as a brilliant prism.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:324`

## anomaly.235.desc.v2

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.235.desc.v2&lang=en)

### 中文

科学专员[Root.GetLeaderName]汇报说，覆盖[From.GetName]的冰层下遍布的闪光波浪源于外星科技。\n\n在这颗星球上曾经发生过上古战争，当时有一架外星战机解体后在此处坠落，只有露在船身外的主武器核心没被摧毁。看起来这个外星种族所运用的激光武器比[Root.Owner.GetSpeciesAdj]舰队目前正在使用的先进许多，其武器核心不定期地向[From.GetName]的冰层表面放射低能激光，但因为行星的气候过于恶劣，激光无法融化冰层，所以就产生了十分美丽的光影艺术。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:315`

### English

Science Officer [Root.GetLeaderName] reports that the luminous waves coursing through the ice that blankets [From.GetName] are of alien origin.\n\nSome ancient battle has deposited scattered wreckage from an alien fighter on the frozen planet, and only the exposed core of its main weapons remains intact. It seems this alien race used lasers somewhat more advanced than the ones currently in use by [Root.Owner.GetSpeciesAdj] fleets, and the fizzling innards periodically discharge low-powered lasers into the ice of [From.GetName]. The planet's harsh climate prevents the lasers from actually melting the ice, allowing the chilly matrix to act as a brilliant prism.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:325`

## anomaly.235.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.235.b&lang=en)

### 中文

我们可以利用此科技。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:316`

### English

We could use that technology.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:326`

## anomaly.240.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.240.name&lang=en)

### 中文

虚假的巨行星

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:317`

### English

Deceptive Giant

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:327`

## anomaly.240.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.240.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们曾经以为这颗星球是十分少见的小型气体星球，但事实并非如此。\n\n在大气层上方浮动着的一层厚厚的粉尘欺骗了科学专员们，粉尘层下面掩盖的只不过是一颗贫瘠但十分普通的行星而已。\n\n这一发现让[Root.Owner.Capital.GetName]的天文学家们十分尴尬。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:318`

### English

What we had assumed was an abnormally small gas giant is, in fact, not.\n\nBeneath a deceptive cover of thick dust swirling in the upper atmosphere lies a regular planet. A barren planet, but a planet nonetheless.\n\nThis has proven quite embarrassing to the astronomical community on [Root.Owner.Capital.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:328`

## anomaly.240.a.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.240.a.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.GetName]：被定位为§Y荒芜§!星球。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:319`

### English

[From.GetName]: Revealed to be a §YBarren§! planet.\n

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:329`

## anomaly.245.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.245.name&lang=en)

### 中文

盈乐园

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:320`

### English

Eden Overrun

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:330`

## anomaly.245.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.245.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.GetName]上的确勃勃生机。\n\n只不过这些生物全部都极为残忍。\n\n虽然星球十分适于殖民，但[Root.GetName]的船员警告说，可能会遭遇当地生物的大肆袭击。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:321`

### English

[From.GetName] is indeed teeming with life.\n\nDangerous life.\n\nWhile ripe for colonization, the crew of the [Root.GetName] warn that we are likely to run into problems with the local wildlife.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:331`

## anomaly.250.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.250.name&lang=en)

### 中文

不明气体

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:322`

### English

A Whiff of Something

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:332`

## anomaly.250.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.250.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetName]从[From.GetName]的表面回收了一只长方形的金属匣子。这只匣子的五个面上都有一些小孔，而科学专员[Root.GetLeaderName]也因此判定这并不是装东西用的匣子，而是用来散发气味的某种工具。初试测试表明，这匣子中原本存放的气味现在仍然在缓慢地释放着。\n\n为了查明此匣子的具体功用，已经设立了§Y特殊项目§!。或许外星物种是用味道来传递信息，然后再通过大气中的化学物质接收信息，从而进行交流也不一定呢。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:323`

### English

The [Root.GetName] has recovered an elongated metal box from the surface of [From.GetName]. Clusters of small perforations on five sides lead Science Officer [Root.GetLeaderName] to believe it is not a container, but some sort of aerosol dispersal device. Initial tests seem to confirm their suspicions as trace aromatics still emanate from the object.\n\nA §YSpecial Project§! has been issued to confirm whether this might be an information-carrying device constructed by some alien race communicating primarily through the secretion and reception of atmosphere-borne chemical compounds; smells.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:333`

## anomaly.250.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.250.a&lang=en)

### 中文

臭不可闻。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:324`

### English

Malodorous.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:334`

## anomaly.251.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.251.name&lang=en)

### 中文

不明气体

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:325`

### English

A Whiff of Something

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:335`

## anomaly.251.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.251.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这匣子的确是存放了某种数据。科学专员[Root.GetLeaderName]承认，本来大家都希望匣子中存放的是星球的历史或者某种文化的重要资料，但结果却令人十分失望。\n\n这散发着芳香的匣子中存放的乃是十分短小的几段故事，故事每一章节快到末尾的时候气味都会快速变化，而这一转变似乎是为了惊吓或逗乐读者，也就是笑话了。虽然匣子储存气味的技术值得研究，但其中的笑话却毫无价值。\n\n[Root.GetName]的船员们因为闻了外星生物所产生的某种气味而感到不太舒心。然而，对于匣子为什么会被扔在这了无人烟的荒凉星球上，[Root.GetLeaderName]却不愿进行猜测。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:326`

### English

The box is indeed a document, of a sort. Science Officer [Root.GetLeaderName] admits that they had been hoping for a historical record or some other kind of codex significant to whatever culture left it behind, but they were disappointed.\n\nThe true nature of the aromatic box seems to be a collection of fairly short narratives which, going by the rapid changes in odor towards the end of each sequence, are intended to surprise or be interpreted as comedic. The techniques used to store and reproduce specific smells is of some interest, but the tales it tells are not.\n\nThe crew of the [Root.GetName] are left with the uncomfortable feeling that they have unwittingly become intimately familiar with what certain gaseous byproducts of alien digestion smell like. However, [Root.GetLeaderName] is unwilling to speculate as to why the box was dumped on this frozen hellscape of a planet.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:336`

## anomaly.255.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.255.name&lang=en)

### 中文

过去的世界末日

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:327`

### English

Apocalypse Past

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:337`

## anomaly.255.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.255.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在[From.GetName]上曾经居住着某个智力较为发达的外星物种，或许再过几十年就能够研发出太空旅行，但在那之前他们就已经把自己终结了。虽然星球本身已经从毫无意义的大肆毁灭中恢复了过来，但表面上仍然有十分明显的伤疤。\n\n地表上残存着几处遗迹，其中有座保存得较为良好的石碑。[Root.GetLeaderName]十分轻松地解读了上面的讯息，原来是世界末日的幸存者们为后来之人所刻下的一则警告。其种族灭亡的直接原因便是“终极武器”的发明。这个种族的某种心理学特征导致共同毁灭机制不再有效。\n\n因为最终武器很难被摧毁，那些最终由于不明原因迅速死去的幸存者们在临死前便将其藏在了地底深处，并留下讯息劝告后人不要去寻找它们。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:328`

### English

[From.GetName] was once home to a race of reasonably intelligent beings who destroyed themselves when they were seemingly only decades away from space flight. The planet has recovered from what must have been immense acts of senseless destruction, but it still bears very visible scars.\n\nOne of the few remaining ruins on the surface holds a reasonably well-preserved stone monolith inscribed with a message, quickly decoded by [Root.GetLeaderName]. It is a warning, from the last of the extinct species to whoever they believed would arise after their passing. It was the development of an "ultimate weapon" that was their downfall; some quirk in the psychology of this species evidently rendered deterrence through mutually assured destruction impossible.\n\nSurvivors, before dying of unknown causes soon after, hid this weaponry deep underground as destroying them was difficult, and left this message dissuade us from going looking for them.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:338`

### English

Then dissuaded we shall be.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:339`

有趣的发现

### English

An Interesting Find

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:341`

## anomaly.256.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.256.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

看起来这颗星球上的“最终武器”只不过是我们眼中最为基本的武器科技而已。\n\n他们能运用这些科技导致世界末日，或许只是因为十分爱好杀戮而已吧。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:332`

### English

It seems the people of this planet were experimenting only with what we would consider to be fairly basic weapons technologies.\n\nThe havoc they wrought is more a testament to their incredible, genocidal tenacity than it is to the power of their weaponry.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:342`

## anomaly.257.name

残酷的发现

### English

A Brutal Find

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:348`

## anomaly.257.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.257.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

科学专员[Root.GetLeaderName]认为幸存者们隐藏武器的决定十分正确。在地底洞穴中找到的这些武器虽然与[Root.Owner.GetName]的现役装备原理相同，但更为先进。这些科技一定会对[Root.Owner.GetSpeciesAdj]战争机器大有帮助。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:339`

### English

Science Officer [Root.GetLeaderName] remarks that the previous inhabitants of this place were right to hide these weapons, recovered from a cave beneath the ruins, away. They build on the same basic principles as weapons already in use in the [Root.Owner.GetName], but are far more advanced. These technologies are sure to be a boon to the [Root.Owner.GetSpeciesAdj] military machine.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:349`

搜查无果。不管是怎样强大的武器，现在都没了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:345`

### English

The search proves fruitless. Whatever these mystical weapons were, they are now gone.

## anomaly.260.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.260.name&lang=en)

### 中文

矿物工厂

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:346`

### English

Mineral Factorial

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:356`

## anomaly.260.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.260.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetName]在[From.GetName]上发现了全星球仅有一座的外星建筑。科学专员[Root.GetLeaderName]认为星球上所有的其它建筑都因为某种原因被移到了别处，但不知为何只留下这么一座建筑。\n\n这建筑乃是处理星球上丰富矿脉的加工厂，经过[Root.GetName]初步勘测后，认定可以十分轻松地重新运作。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:347`

### English

The [Root.GetName] has found an alien structure on [From.GetName]. It seems to be the only one of its kind on the planet - Science Officer [Root.GetLeaderName] speculates that all other xenoarchitectural traces may have been moved off-world, with this sole building left behind for reasons unknown.\n\nIt is a processing plant working the planet's abundant mineral resources, and preliminary scans by the [Root.GetName] indicate that it can be reactivated with relative ease.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:357`

## anomaly.260.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.260.a&lang=en)

### 中文

太好了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:348`

### English

Most excellent.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:358`

## anomaly.260.a.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.260.a.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

添加§Y古代工厂§!至[From.GetName]。\n

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:349`

### English

Adds an §YAncient Factory§! to [From.GetName].\n

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:359`

## anomaly.265.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.265.name&lang=en)

### 中文

外星汽车赛

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:350`

### English

Alien Drag Racing

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:360`

## anomaly.265.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.265.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.GetName]浅层松软表面上的坑洞中有着不规则的长条印记。科学专员[Root.GetLeaderName]在印记的附近发现了十分令人惊讶的各种废品：被撞毁的轮式载具。它们看起来并不像是科研用品，而是载人用的。\n\n[Root.GetName]的船员们认为这里应该是外星人用作赛车的地点。在这几乎零重力的地方，外星生物们驾着自己车辆互相对抗，测试车辆的强度与速度，真是激动人心。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:351`

### English

The crater on [From.GetName] exhibits long, irregular marks imprinted into the relatively soft upper layers of the asteroid. Science Officer [Root.GetLeaderName] has recovered some interesting unexpected wreckage from the edges of the depression; wheeled vehicles, seemingly personal transports rather than research craft.\n\nThe crew of the [Root.GetName] speculate that the crater may once have been used for sport, with some alien species pitting their personal locomotion devices against each other in tests of mechanical strength or speed in near-zero-G.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:361`

## anomaly.265.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.265.a&lang=en)

### 中文

真厉害。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:352`

### English

Vroom.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:362`

添加§Y刹车印记§!修正至[From.GetName]：\n£engineering£§G+6§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:353`

### English

Adds the §YSkid Marks§! modifier to [From.GetName]:\n£engineering£ §G+6§!

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:363`

## anomaly.270.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.270.name&lang=en)

### 中文

撞车大事故

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:354`

### English

Fender Bender

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:364`

## anomaly.270.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.270.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

虽然此处的陨石坑应该只是普通的陨石撞击造成的，但陨石坑中复杂的纹路应该是产生于撞击之后。\n\n科学专员[Root.GetLeaderName]认为这些地表的印记应该不是陨石造成的，而是小行星[From.GetName]与某种未知的轨道设施或外星飞船之类的东西撞击后造成的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:355`

### English

While the crater in question was most likely the result of a regular meteorite impact, the complex patterns in the resulting depression originated at a later time.\n\nScience Officer [Root.GetLeaderName] argues that these surface-level patterns are not the result of an impact but a collision, one between asteroid [From.GetName] and some unspecified orbital installation or ship of alien make.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:365`

## anomaly.270.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.270.a&lang=en)

### 中文

真奇怪呢。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:356`

### English

How odd.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:366`

## anomaly.270.a.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.270.a.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

添加§Y死亡赛道§!修正至[From.GetName]：\n£engineering£§G+3§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:357`

### English

Adds the §YCircuits in the Dust§! modifier to [From.GetName]:\n£engineering£ §G+3§!

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:367`

## anomaly.275.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.275.name&lang=en)

### 中文

遗弃的推动装置

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:358`

### English

Abandoned Thrusting

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:368`

## anomaly.275.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.275.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.GetName]的其中一个大坑中心有外星工艺制造的金属机械。\n\n科学专员[Root.GetLeaderName]认为这些大型机械是用于推动整颗星球的。其目的便是将[From.GetName]从原先轨道中推出，但目的地不明。[Root.GetName]正在待命，可以将这些推动装置进行拆卸与分析，或许可以提升我方的推进科技。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:359`

### English

In the center of a particularly large crater on [From.GetName] lies a cluster of metal shapes, doubtlessly of alien origin.\n\nScience Officer [Root.GetLeaderName] is convinced that they are massive, planetoid-mountable propulsion devices, intended to push [From.GetName] from its orbit for purposes and destinations unknown. The [Root.GetName] is on standby, its crew ready to begin disassembly and analysis of the alleged propulsion systems with the hopes of improving our own thruster technology.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:369`

## anomaly.275.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.275.a&lang=en)

### 中文

就这么办。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:360`

### English

Make it so.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:370`

## anomaly.275.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.275.b&lang=en)

### 中文

就这么放着别动了吧。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:361`

### English

Leave the alien devilry be.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:371`

## anomaly.280.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.280.name&lang=en)

### 中文

殖民失败

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:362`

### English

Colonial Failure

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:372`

## anomaly.280.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.280.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在[From.GetName]上曾经有过外星殖民地，至少是一次早期的殖民尝试。科学专员[Root.GetLeaderName]报告说该次殖民明显还不成熟，建立的建筑也绝对无法抵御[From.GetName]的恶劣气候。\n\n[Root.GetName]不想空手而归，便随船带回了一个报废了的大气过滤装置，想着或许能做些研究。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:363`

### English

There was an alien colony on [From.GetName] once, or at least an early attempt at one. Science Officer [Root.GetLeaderName] reports that the effort was obviously premature, the ramshackle structures never standing a chance against the hostile climate of [From.GetName].\n\nNot wanting to leave empty-handed, the [Root.GetName] has recovered a defunct atmospheric filtration unit that may be of some interest.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:373`

## anomaly.280.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.280.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们可以吸取他们的教训。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:364`

### English

We can learn from their mistakes.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:374`

## anomaly.280.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.280.b&lang=en)

### 中文

这东西一点用都没有。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:365`

### English

This thing's useless.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:375`

## anomaly.280.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.280.c&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.Owner.GetName]永远不会犯下这样的错误。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:366`

### English

The [Root.Owner.GetName] would never make such mistakes.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:376`

## anomaly.285.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.285.name&lang=en)

### 中文

不寻常的地壳

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:367`

### English

Unusual Tectonics

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:377`

## anomaly.285.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.285.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetName]在[From.GetName]上找到的并非是建筑，而是十分普通的矿物岩层。科学专员[Root.GetLeaderName]认为这些岩层之所以会露出地面，是因为不同的地壳构造版块互相撞击而形成的，其形状的特殊性是由行星地壳中不同寻常的物理构造造成的。\n\n因为其形状过于工整，看起来某种外星生物似乎曾经对[From.GetName]的地壳进行过改动。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:368`

### English

What the [Root.GetName] found on [From.GetName] were not buildings, but surprisingly regular mineral outcroppings. Science Officer [Root.GetLeaderName] asserts that these angular blocks of rock are the product of interactions between tectonic plates, their shapes the result of the unusual tectonophysics inherent in the planet's crust.\n\nThe perfect angularity of these thrust faults implies a possibility of aliens tampering with [From.GetName]'s crust at some point in the past.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:378`

## anomaly.285.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.285.a&lang=en)

### 中文

还真有意思。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:369`

### English

Most intriguing.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:379`

## anomaly.285.a.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.285.a.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

添加§Y异常地壳§!修正至[From.GetName]：\n£engineering£§G+2§!\n£physics£§G+2§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:370`

### English

Adds the §YUnusual Tectonics§! modifier to [From.GetName]:\n£engineering£ §G+2§!\n£physics£ §G+2§!

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:380`

## anomaly.290.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.290.name&lang=en)

### 中文

潜在矿脉

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:371`

### English

Rock Potential

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:381`

## anomaly.290.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.290.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.GetName]的矿脉资源十分丰富。\n\n不幸的是，其中大部分矿石对我们都没用。然而，科学专员[Root.GetLeaderName]认为[From.GetName]地壳中的许多元素都可以通过正电子轰炸转化成对我方有用的矿石。\n\n[Root.GetName]希望[Root.Owner.GetName]能拨出1000能量币实行此计划。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:372`

### English

[From.GetName] is exceptionally rich in minerals.\n\nMinerals that are, unfortunately, of no use to us. However, Science Officer [Root.GetLeaderName] believes the many elements in the crust of [From.GetName] can be transformed into potentially more useful forms through positron bombardment.\n\nThe [Root.GetName] has requests that the [Root.Owner.GetName] set aside 1000 Energy Credits for the purposes of this project.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:382`

## anomaly.290.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.290.a&lang=en)

### 中文

不行，不批准。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:373`

### English

No. No, we will not.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:383`

## anomaly.290.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.290.b&lang=en)

### 中文

很好，准备正电子轰炸。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:374`

### English

Very well, ready the positron charges.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:384`

## anomaly.292.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.292.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetName]的船员们汇报了试验的成功。[From.From.GetName]上的许多矿脉现在都可以被我们所使用了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:376`

### English

The crew of the [Root.GetName] are pleased to report that the experiment was a success, and a significant number of dense mineral veins on [From.From.GetName] have been transmuted into usable resources.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:386`

## anomaly.295.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.295.name&lang=en)

### 中文

上下颠倒

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:377`

### English

Up is Down

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:387`

## anomaly.295.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.295.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetName]穿过小行星带前往[From.GetName]的路上时，科学专员[Root.GetLeaderName]汇报飞船经历了几次来势突然的重力变化。因为好奇，[Root.GetLeaderName]派遣了无人机前去勘测原因。\n\n结果，无人机发现了几只老旧的外星重力生成器。因为极性反转，电池又不足，这些重力生成器时常会对着虚空放射出反重力波。但其效果不强，无法造成任何实质性的伤害。我们无法将生成器的效果恢复至其原本具有的强度。\n\n科学专员[Root.GetLeaderName]认为这些生成器本来是为小行星带中某颗较大的小行星提供人造重力用的，但因为某种故障，这颗小行星已经四分五裂了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:378`

### English

As the [Root.GetName] passed through the asteroid belt on its way to [From.GetName], Science Officer [Root.GetLeaderName] reported that the ship experienced a number of sudden gravitational shifts. Ever curious, [Root.GetLeaderName] dispatched a number of probes to scan the debris field to discern the cause.\n\nIt turns out that the culprit is a number of gravity generators of old, alien make. Sputtering on dying energy sources and with their polarities inverted, they occasionally spew reverse-gravity into the void, though not with enough force to cause any serious harm. Not anymore.\n\nScience Officer [Root.GetLeaderName] theorizes that the generators once provided artificial gravity for a large asteroid in the belt, but some malfunction eventually tore the rock apart.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:388`

## anomaly.295.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.295.a&lang=en)

### 中文

这主意真是扰人。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:379`

### English

A disturbing thought.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:389`

## anomaly.3000.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3000.name&lang=en)

### 中文

低级错误

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:380`

### English

Careless Pawing

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:391`

## anomaly.3000.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3000.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

据[Root.GetName]的船员汇报，他们的任务失败了。科学专员[Root.GetLeaderName]心血来潮想手动操纵无人机采集矿石样本，结果不小心撞上了矿石，虽然撞击力度并不大，但矿石还是粉碎殆尽了。\n\n船员还提到，科学专员[Root.GetLeaderName]对此十分内疚，并且那些粉碎的矿石还有些价值。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:381`

### English

According to a report from the crew of the [Root.GetName], their mission was a failure. Science Officer [Root.GetLeaderName] allegedly took manual control of the probe sent to retrieve a sample from the cluster, resulting in a collision between drone and mineral cluster that, while mild, resulted in the mineral cluster breaking apart completely.\n\nThe crew is careful to note that the experience has left Science Officer [Root.GetLeaderName] somewhat shaken, and the mineral shards should still be of some value.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:392`

## anomaly.3000.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3000.a&lang=en)

### 中文

碎了的矿石也还是矿石。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:382`

### English

Its parts are equal to the sum of its parts.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:393`

## anomaly.3000.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3000.b&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.Owner.GetName]无法接受这么粗心的行为。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:383`

### English

The [Root.Owner.GetName] finds such carelessness unacceptable.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:394`

## anomaly.3005.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3005.name&lang=en)

### 中文

金色牢笼

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:384`

### English

Gilded Cage

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:395`

## anomaly.3005.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3005.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这块矿物的构造就像是个房间，没有其他更贴切的词能形容了。\n\n构成房间的矿物在[Root.Owner.GetName]十分常用。这块矿石是中空的三十二面体，磁共振扫描显示其内部中空，很可能曾有人居住过。但是矿石内部与其光鲜的外表并不相同，看起来被人破坏的混乱不堪。\n\n科学专员[Root.GetLeaderName]报告称由于这些破坏，我们很难从矿石内部找到有价值的信息。但其建筑的外部结构能够抵御深空压力，其构造技术一定十分先进。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:385`

### English

The mineral construct is, for lack of a better term, a room.\n\nMade out of the same type of minerals in common use in the [Root.Owner.GetName], the construct is a hollow icosidodecahedron. Resonance scans indicate that it is mostly hollow, and was likely inhabited at some point in time. Unlike its pristine exterior, the interior is in complete disarray, possibly the result of an intentional act of sabotage.\n\nScience Officer [Root.GetLeaderName] reports that this makes it difficult to learn anything of value from the construct's insides, but stresses that the engineering techniques involved in creating the outer shell, eminently capable of withstanding the stresses of deep space, must have been tremendously advanced.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:396`

## anomaly.3005.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3005.a&lang=en)

### 中文

想不通。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:386`

### English

Puzzling.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:397`

## anomaly.3010.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3010.name&lang=en)

### 中文

晶体抄本

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:387`

### English

Crystal Codex

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:398`

## anomaly.3010.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3010.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这块矿石是个全息记录仪，外表棱角分明，呈半透明状。其目前被安全地放置于[Root.GetName]的牵引光束中，但解码其内容仍需假以时日。\n\n已经设立加速该进程的§Y特殊项目§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:388`

### English

The mineral cluster is a holographic record, multi-sided, many-edged, and translucent. It remains secure in the [Root.GetName]'s tractor beam, but our experts will need some time to decode its contents.\n\nA §YSpecial Project§! has been issued to expedite this process.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:399`

## anomaly.3010.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3010.a&lang=en)

### 中文

肯定没好事。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:389`

### English

Nothing good would come of it.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:400`

## anomaly.3010.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3010.b&lang=en)

### 中文

很好。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:390`

### English

Very well.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:401`

## anomaly.3012.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3012.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

全息记录仪中的数据文档乃是一份曾经称霸此区域的三个大国之间的贸易协议。其中的内容我们无法理解也毫无意义，但科学专员[Root.GetLeaderName]认为这可以大大提高我们与外星人的沟通能力。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:391`

### English

The holographic data documents the terms and conditions for a trilateral trade treaty between three major alien powers that once dominated this quadrant of the galaxy. Context eludes us and the details are meaningless, but Science Officer [Root.GetLeaderName] feels that this find will prove invaluable to improving our own ability to communicate with alien forces.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:402`

## anomaly.3015.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3015.name&lang=en)

### 中文

外星军营

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:392`

### English

Alien Barracks

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:403`

## anomaly.3015.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3015.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

轨道上探查出的影像的确是建筑。进一步调查后发现建筑显然是用于军事用途。虽然被遗弃了一段时间——从星系尺度上看并不算久——但科学专员[Root.GetLeaderName]认为从尘土下的废弃军营和空旷的军火库中仍然可以找到外星战术秘密。\n\n已经设立了研究废墟的§Y特殊项目§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:393`

### English

The silhouettes discernible from orbit are indeed buildings, and closer inspection reveal them to be of an obvious martial nature. While abandoned for some time - though very recently on a galactic timescale - Science Officer [Root.GetLeaderName] believes that secrets of alien tactics may yet be exhumed from beneath the dust that covers the decaying barracks and looted armories.\n\nA §YSpecial Project§! may be issued to study the remains.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:404`

## anomaly.3015.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3015.a&lang=en)

### 中文

了解。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:394`

### English

Acknowledged.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:405`

## anomaly.3015.b.pacifist

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3015.b.pacifist&lang=en)

### 中文

既然已经化为废墟，那就不要多管了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:395`

### English

That this outpost is dead and buried speaks volumes.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:406`

## anomaly.3017.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3017.name&lang=en)

### 中文

外星军营

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:396`

### English

Alien Barracks

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:407`

## anomaly.3017.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3017.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

科学专员[Root.GetLeaderName]对于[From.From.GetName]进行了详尽的外星历史分析，但最终结果不佳。该兵营早在行星变得贫瘠之前就被建立了，但不知是因为自然原因还是战争原因，才变成了今天这样的废墟。\n\n可以得出的结论是：这些外星人使用的武器与战略并不比我们现在使用的要先进。\n\n不过，科学专员[Root.GetLeaderName]说自己因为这次勘探，获取了不少灵感。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:397`

### English

Science Officer [Root.GetLeaderName]'s thorough xeno-historical analysis of the military installation on [From.From.GetName] has yielded few tangible results. The base was constructed before the planet turned completely barren, but it is unclear if its current inhospitality is the result of armed conflict or some more peaceful or even natural process.\n\nWhat can be deduced of their strategies and tactics in armed conflict does not seem to have been far in advance of our own.\n\nOn the upside, Science Officer [Root.GetLeaderName] claims to have drawn much inspiration from the alien remnants.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:408`

## anomaly.3017.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3017.a&lang=en)

### 中文

还是有用的嘛。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:398`

### English

Always something.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:409`

## anomaly.3020.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3020.name&lang=en)

### 中文

格子

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:399`

### English

Checkered

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:410`

## anomaly.3020.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3020.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.GetName]的特殊拓扑结构整齐地将星球划分为无数个不同的群落，各群落的气候以一种微妙的方式维持着周边环境的平衡。科学专员[Root.GetLeaderName]强烈怀疑这是先进的环境改造的结果，但我们无法解释这是如何做到的。\n\n尽管如此，环境改造的结果对[Root.GetLeaderName]和[Root.GetName]的船员来说显而易见。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:400`

### English

The unique topology of [From.GetName] neatly divides the planet into innumerable distinct biomes, micro-climates that interact only in subtle ways seemingly engineered to sustain neighboring climes. Science Officer [Root.GetLeaderName] strongly suspects advanced terraforming techniques at work, but few clues remain regarding the process itself.\n\nIts effects, however, remain plainly visible to [Root.GetLeaderName] and the [Root.GetName]'s crew.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:411`

## anomaly.3020.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3020.a&lang=en)

### 中文

还真有意思。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:401`

### English

Most intriguing.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:412`

## anomaly.3025.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3025.name&lang=en)

### 中文

远古自动设施

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:402`

### English

Ancient Automata

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:413`

## anomaly.3025.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3025.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在[From.GetName]一处植被非常茂密的地域的中心发现了被植物掩盖的外星建筑，起初我们以为它已被摧毁，但经过[Root.GetName]仔细勘察发现，它还没有被这颗星球上格外茂盛的植物所侵蚀。\n\n已发起§Y特殊项目§!来寻找建筑入口。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:403`

### English

One of the major areas of extreme verdancy on [From.GetName] is centered on an overgrown alien structure. Initially thought ruined, closer observations by the [Root.GetName] reveal that the building has mostly survived the crushing embrace of the planet's flora.\n\nA §YSpecial Project§! has been issued to find a way into the structure.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:414`

## anomaly.3025.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3025.a&lang=en)

### 中文

很有意思。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:404`

### English

Very interesting.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:415`

## anomaly.3027.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3027.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这座杂草丛生的建筑是外星人的矿物加工厂。位于[From.From.GetName]的工厂尽管早已被废弃，但似乎曾基本实现了自动化，工厂的地上还躺着大量全自动机器人的残骸——他们的身体主要由有机化合物组成，体内以高分子内骨骼作为支撑，现在也一并腐烂了。\n\n科学专员[Root.GetLeaderName]认为正是这些有机残余物在液化后渗入了地面，使得土壤变得肥沃，星球上的各种植物也才会如此繁茂坚韧。\n\n工厂可以被重新激活，但是[Root.GetLeaderName]感兴趣的那些迷人的，全自动的工人们却再也不能活过来了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:405`

### English

The overgrown structure - an alien mineral processing plant - on [From.From.GetName] was, prior to its abandonment in the distant past, mostly automated. Husks of artificial workers litter the floor, their organic components - for their bodies were largely of a biological nature, held upright by polymer endoskeletons - long since rotted away.\n\nScience Officer [Root.GetLeaderName] speculates that it is the organic remains of these automatons that, soon liquefied and drained into the ground, provided an intense boost to soil nutrition and allowed the native flora to grow both abundant and resilient.\n\nThe plant can be reactivated but the automatons, fascinating as they may be to [Root.GetLeaderName], will never walk its halls again.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:416`

## anomaly.3027.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3027.a&lang=en)

### 中文

明白了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:406`

### English

Understood.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:417`

## anomaly.3030.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3030.name&lang=en)

临时停泊

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:408`

### English

Hard Shoulder

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:419`

## anomaly.3030.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3030.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在[From.GetName]的平原发现了一艘无人船只，其传感器对星球轨道上的[Root.GetName]做出了回应。\n\n[Root.GetName]的船员未能确认此船何时被弃，也不知道此船是何人所弃。至于原船员的下落更是无从调查。根据科学专员[Root.GetLeaderName]所说，若想将其收编入我方舰队，远程激活这艘轻装飞船应该不成问题。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:409`

### English

There is a ship idling on the plains of [From.GetName], its sensors reacting to the [Root.GetName] in orbit around the planet.\n\nThe [Root.GetName]'s crew have been unable to confirm exactly when the craft was abandoned - for it is abandoned - or who left it there, or where they are now. According to Science Officer [Root.GetLeaderName], remotely activating the lightly armed ship would be trivial, should we wish to add it to our fleets.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:420`

## anomaly.3030.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3030.a&lang=en)

### 中文

当然，反正也没人要。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:410`

### English

No one will miss it, surely.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:421`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3030.a.tooltip&lang=en)

获得一艘§Y护卫舰§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:411`

### English

Gain a §YCorvette§!-class ship.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:422`

## anomaly.3030.b.phobe

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3030.b.phobe&lang=en)

我们既不知道这是哪儿来的，也不知道这玩意要到哪儿去。拆掉吧。

We don't know whence it came or where it is going. Dismantle it.

## anomaly.3035.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3035.name&lang=en)

### 中文

清点存量

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:413`

### English

Taking Stock

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:424`

## anomaly.3035.desc.carefree

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3035.desc.carefree&lang=en)

### 中文

事实证明，荒芜的[From.GetPlanetMoon][From.GetName]毫无有趣之处。\n\n当船员们监视着了无动静的仪器时，科学专员[Root.GetLeaderName]闲来无事查看了[Root.GetName]的能源储备情况。根据他们的报告，[Root.GetName]上次离港时获得的能量补给少于标准量，不过更有可能是[Root.GetLeaderName]对于资源管理的松懈造成了此次窘境。\n\n不管怎么说，这艘船需要更多的能量作为补给。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:414`

### English

The desert [From.GetPlanetMoon] [From.GetName] turned out to be rather unremarkable.\n\nWhile the crew monitored silent instruments, Science Officer [Root.GetLeaderName] was idly eyeing the [Root.GetName]'s energy reserves. According to their report the [Root.GetName] was issued less than the standard amount of energy when it last left port, but is more likely that [Root.GetLeaderName]'s carefree approach to resource management is to blame for the deficit.\n\nRegardless, the ship does need additional Energy Credits.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:425`

## anomaly.3035.desc.meticulous

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3035.desc.meticulous&lang=en)

### 中文

事实证明，荒芜的[From.GetPlanetMoon][From.GetName]毫无有趣之处。\n\n当船员们监视着了无动静的仪器时，科学专员[Root.GetLeaderName]对[Root.GetName]的能源储备进行了认真盘点。根据他们的报告，[Root.GetName]上次离港时获得的能量补给多于标准量，这很可能是书写错误造成的。\n\n[Root.Leader.GetName]已提出要自愿将多余的能量交还给[Root.Owner.GetSpeciesAdj]当局。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:415`

### English

The desert [From.GetPlanetMoon] [From.GetName] turned out to be rather unremarkable.\n\nWhile the crew monitored silent instruments, Science Officer [Root.GetLeaderName] was conscientiously taking inventory of the [Root.GetName]'s energy reserves. According to their report the [Root.GetName] was issued more than the standard amount of energy when it last left port, probably due to some clerical error.\n\n[Root.Leader.GetName] has volunteered to return the excess to [Root.Owner.GetSpeciesAdj] authorities.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:426`

意外的收获。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:416`

### English

Unexpected windfall.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:427`

果然不出所料。

### English

## anomaly.3040.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3040.name&lang=en)

### 中文

被流放的海盗

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:418`

### English

Pirate Exiles

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:429`

## anomaly.3040.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3040.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们误以为是矿物簇的物体实际上是两艘外星护卫舰的金属外壳，它们正悄悄飘过小行星带。\n\n科学专员[Root.GetLeaderName]已经与其建立联系，这两艘船的船员自称他们以前都是海盗，现在被首领放逐了。他们听说[Root.Owner.GetRulerTitle][Root.Owner.GetRulerName]是[Root.Owner.GetName]的统治者，为人亲民，特来寻求庇护。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:419`

### English

What was thought to be a cluster of minerals are the metal hulls of two alien corvettes, stealthily drifting through the asteroid belt.\n\nScience Officer [Root.GetLeaderName] established contact, and the two ships are crewed by alleged former pirates, now cast out from their robber-clan. They have heard whispers that [Root.Owner.GetRulerTitle] [Root.Owner.GetRulerName] of the [Root.Owner.GetName] is a champion of the people, and seek asylum.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:430`

## anomaly.3040.desc.fallback

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3040.desc.fallback&lang=en)

### 中文

我们误以为是矿脉的物体实际上是两艘外星护卫舰的金属外壳，它们正悄悄飘过小行星带。\n\n科学专员[Root.GetLeaderName]已经与其建立联系，这两艘船的船员自称他们以前都是海盗，现在被匪帮首领放逐了。很神奇的是，他们相信我们会妥善对待他们，因此特来寻求庇护。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:420`

### English

What was thought to be a cluster of minerals are the metal hulls of two alien corvettes, stealthily drifting through the asteroid belt.\n\nScience Officer [Root.GetLeaderName] established contact, and the two ships are crewed by alleged former pirates, now cast out from their robber-clan. Curiously, they believe that we will be well-disposed towards them, and are seeking asylum.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:431`

## anomaly.3040.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3040.a&lang=en)

他们可以派上用场。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:421`

### English

They can make themselves useful.

我们必须拒绝他们的避难请求。

We must deny their asylum claim.

## anomaly.3050.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3050.name&lang=en)

### 中文

刺耳格栅

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:427`

### English

Grinding Lattice

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:438`

## anomaly.3050.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3050.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.GetName]遍布着此起彼伏的矿物复合带，用高耸且富有弹性的架子高悬于其本就十分崎岖的表面上。由于经常受到气流的扰动，摆动的架子和层叠的带状物互相摩擦，产生了[Root.Owner.GetSpeciesNamePlural]无法听到的声波。\n\n科研专员[Root.GetLeaderName]报告称将此声波从星球的背景噪音中分离出来时遇到了困难，同时也提出如果有意进行开采，建议将矿物带破坏，从而由[Root.Owner.GetName]进行回收利用。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:428`

### English

[From.GetName] is crisscrossed by overlapping mineral-composite bands that are elevated above the harsh surface by tall, flexible pylons. Constantly agitated by atmospheric winds, these pylons sway and the overlapping bands rub together to produce sonic waves at what are - to [Root.Owner.GetSpeciesNamePlural] - inaudible frequencies.\n\nScience Officer [Root.GetLeaderName] reports some difficulty in isolating this audio from the natural background noise of the planet, but notes that the mineral bands could - should we so choose - be broken down and efficiently recycled by the [Root.Owner.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:439`

将声音分离出来并进行研究。

### English

Isolate and study the alien sounds.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:440`

## anomaly.3055.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3055.name&lang=en)

### 中文

森林国度

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:433`

### English

Wooden Hegemony

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:444`

## anomaly.3055.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3055.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.GetName]是一颗生态丰富的热带星球。对其表面植被的近距离分析却表明其生物异常缺乏多样性，一种特殊的高大树木成为了生态系统的主体部分。\n\n虽说这种树木本身并不会造成什么危害，但其单一的主导地位却会对该星球小型动物的生存带来不利影响，若要进行殖民，想在其表面有效率地培育农作物也会异常困难。\n\n科学专员[Root.GetLeaderName]作为享有盛誉的外星生物学专家，制定了重新平衡此星球生态系统的计划。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:434`

### English

[From.GetName] is a tropical planet with a thriving biosphere. A close analysis of surface flora reveals an unexpected lack of diversity, as one particular family of tall trees dominate the ecosystem.\n\nThough not harmful in and of themselves, their monocultural hegemony appears to have a detrimental effect on the survivability of the planet's small animals, and it would be difficult to efficiently cultivate food crops on the surface should colonization ever be attempted.\n\nScience Officer [Root.GetLeaderName], a xeno-biologist of some repute, has put together a plan for re-balancing this alien biosphere.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:445`

## anomaly.3055.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3055.a&lang=en)

### 中文

同意该计划。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:435`

### English

Approve the plan.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:446`

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:436`

### English

Adds the §YUnbalanced Ecosystem§! modifier to [From.GetName]:\n£food£ §R-20%§!\n

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:447`

还是保持原状吧。

Leave the planet be.

## anomaly.3055.b.tooltip
