Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:438`

### English

Adds the §YUnbalanced Ecosystem§! modifier to [From.GetName]:\n£food£ §R-20%§!

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:449`

## anomaly.3056.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3056.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

平衡已恢复了——或许是首次达到了。[Root.GetName]的放射射线消除了长久以来覆盖了这颗星球的那些多年生木本植物的繁殖能力。还有一些树木被彻底烧成了灰烬，以求尽快达到真正的平衡。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:439`

### English

Balance has been restored - or perhaps achieved for the first time - as large swathes of the dominant perennials have been rendered infertile by targeted radiation emissions from the [Root.GetName]. Additional specimens have been reduced to ash to further hasten the arrival of a truer equilibrium.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:450`

## anomaly.3057.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3057.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

激进地消灭一直以来覆盖着[From.From.GetName]的树木造成了意想不到的后果。\n\n之前被这些树木搞得生计艰难的小动物们数量急剧上升，也越发富有攻击意愿——或者说是变得更加野蛮了。被隔离开来的焦土曾是茂密森林的所在，现在却成了领地争端的战场。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:440`

### English

The inelegant elimination of wide swathes of the dominant trees on [From.From.GetName] have had unintended consequences.\n\nThe small animals that previously suffered under the perennial tyranny of the trees are growing both more plentiful and more aggressive - some would say savage. The isolated ashen fields that used to be home to lush forests now play host to heated territory battles.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:451`

## anomaly.3057.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3057.a&lang=en)

### 中文

也许我们本不该介入的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:441`

### English

Perhaps we should have known better than to meddle.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:452`

## anomaly.3057.a.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3057.a.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

§R危险野生动物§!现已遍布[From.From.GetName]。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:442`

### English

§RDangerous Wildlife§! now roams [From.From.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:453`

## anomaly.3060.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3060.name&lang=en)

### 中文

金砂藏舰

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:443`

### English

Head in the Sand

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:454`

## anomaly.3060.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3060.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

科学专员[Root.GetLeaderName]报告称，我们犯了个愚蠢的错误，在[From.GetName]沙漠看到的沙海涟漪其实是启动的飞船推进器造成的。\n\n有一艘外星飞船头朝下埋没在沙海中，其断断续续工作的推进系统在上方的沙丘激起了阵阵波涛。看来这玩意已经困在这里有段时间了，它在推进器的作用下渐渐插进了沙丘深处。[Root.GetName]没有能够将此船吊出的设备与方法，[Root.GetLeaderName]建议派遣一只飞船到[From.GetName]，以对其进行发掘。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:444`

### English

Science Officer [Root.GetLeaderName] reports that the waves seen rippling across the sandy seas of [From.GetName] are, bizarrely enough, the result of active ship thrusters.\n\nAn alien vessel lies buried in the sand nose-down, its sputtering propulsion systems weakly agitating the dunes above. It appears to have been down there for some time, slowly driving itself deeper into the desert dunes. The [Root.GetName] lacks the means to excavate the struggling ship, and [Root.GetLeaderName] recommends sending a military vessel to [From.GetName] to unearth it.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:455`

## anomaly.3060.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3060.a&lang=en)

### 中文

了解。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:445`

### English

Acknowledged.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:456`

## anomaly.3060.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3060.b&lang=en)

### 中文

这么明显的陷阱，我们可不会上当。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:446`

### English

We will not take the obvious bait.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:457`

## anomaly.3060.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3060.c&lang=en)

### 中文

任何剩余的有机生物都将被消灭。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:447`

### English

If there are any organics left they will be terminated.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:458`

## anomaly.3061.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3061.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetFleetName]终于成功将外星飞船打捞了出来。\n\n但它是空的，或者可以说，被拆的什么都没剩了。\n\n原船员一定是将不是很重要的东西全拆掉后便弃船而去，留下这艘空壳在[From.From.GetName]沉没。[Root.GetFleetName]的工程师忙活了好一阵子，终于将其能量补充完毕并重启了飞船。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:448`

### English

The [Root.GetFleetName] eventually succeeds in raising the submerged alien vessel.\n\nIt is empty. Almost stripped bare, in fact.\n\nIts crew must have abandoned ship before it was left to burrow into the particulate surface of [From.From.GetName], and they took most of the ship's non-critical internals with them. The engineers of the [Root.GetFleetName] have managed to essentially hotwire the vessel, and have replenished its dwindling energy stores.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:459`

## anomaly.3061.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3061.a&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.Owner.GetSpeciesAdj]舰队有了新成员。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:449`

### English

A fresh commission for the [Root.Owner.GetSpeciesAdj] fleet.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:460`

## anomaly.3061.a.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3061.a.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

获得一艘§Y护卫舰§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:450`

### English

Gain a §YCorvette§!-class ship.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:461`

## anomaly.3062.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3062.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetFleetName]使用高强度拉索最终成功打捞起了沉没的外星飞船，却同时也听到了其武器系统启动的轰鸣声！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:451`

### English

The [Root.GetFleetName]'s high-tensile wires eventually managed to raise the submerged alien vessel, only to be met by the recognizable whirring of weapons systems powering up!

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:462`

## anomaly.3062.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3062.a&lang=en)

### 中文

快回避！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:452`

### English

Evasive maneuvers!

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:463`

## anomaly.3062.a.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3062.a.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y“鸵鸟号”§!发起了进攻！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:453`

### English

The §YOstrich§! attacks!

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:464`

## anomaly.3063.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3063.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

回收行动失败了。[Root.GetFleetName]绕在舰船上的高强度拉索在向上提拉时的瞬间就将腐蚀殆尽的舰体切成了数段。\n\n单独的碎块回收起来还相对容易，但整个飞船已变成了废铁。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:454`

### English

The salvage attempt failed. The high-tensile tethers that the [Root.GetFleetName] had wrapped around the burrowing ship effortlessly sliced through the vessel's corroded hull as raising was attempted.\n\nThe individual pieces were then relatively easy to retrieve, but the whole thing is reduced to scrap.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:465`

## anomaly.3063.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3063.a&lang=en)

### 中文

又搞砸了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:455`

### English

Bungled again.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:466`

## anomaly.3065.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3065.name&lang=en)

### 中文

无效通讯

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:456`

### English

Illicit Communications

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:467`

## anomaly.3065.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3065.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一个拼凑的通讯中继器被很外行地绑在了[From.GetName]上。\n\n科学专员[Root.GetLeaderName]认为这是海盗通信网络的一部分。初步分析表明通信内容并不包含什么敏感的战术信息，只是些海盗船船员间的寻常通话。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:457`

### English

A cobbled-together comms repeater has been inexpertly strapped to [From.GetName].\n\nScience Officer [Root.GetLeaderName] is confident that this is part of a relay network for pirate communications. Initial analysis indicates that the messages relayed are not of a particularly strategically sensitive nature, but rather private inter-ship communications between individual crewmembers.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:468`

## anomaly.3065.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3065.a&lang=en)

### 中文

也许我们可以一窥深空的生活。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:458`

### English

Perhaps we can learn something about life in deep space.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:469`

## anomaly.3065.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3065.b&lang=en)

### 中文

也许我们可以一窥深空的生活。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:459`

### English

Perhaps we can learn something useful about life in deep space.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:470`

## anomaly.3070.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3070.name&lang=en)

### 中文

付诸东流

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:460`

### English

Down the Drain

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:471`

## anomaly.3070.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3070.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

读数来自于一个小型金属舱，科学专员[Root.GetLeaderName]判定这是一艘远古外星救生艇。\n\n这东西显然是许久以前就被黑洞外围的引力井困在此处，因为角度问题无法弹射出来——至于这是故意为之还是偶然至此尚不得而知——救生艇的推进器从那时起就一直在竭力逃离黑洞视界。目前它已离黑洞视界太近，无法进行回收了。\n\n[Root.GetLeaderName]的报告突然中断，随后[Root.GetName]船员发来通讯称，该科学专员浑身颤抖，似乎是对此救生艇的悲惨命运感到十分难过。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:461`

### English

The readings originate from a small metal pod; Science Officer [Root.GetLeaderName] has determined it to be a lifeboat of ancient and alien make.\n\nEvidently caught on the outskirts of the black hole's gravity well eons ago, at an angle just short of a slingshot maneuver - whether this was the intent or happenstance is difficult to judge - and the pod's automated thrusters have been fighting its descent to the event horizon ever since. It is now too close to the horizon to salvage.\n\n[Root.GetLeaderName]'s report ends abruptly, and the crew of the [Root.GetName] communicate that the Science Officer appears shaken and deeply troubled by the tragic fate of the lifeboat.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:472`

## anomaly.3070.desc.mach

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3070.desc.mach&lang=en)

### 中文

读数来自于一个小型金属舱，科学专员[Root.GetLeaderName]判定这是一艘远古外星救生艇。\n\n这东西显然是许久以前就被黑洞外围的引力井困在此处，因为角度问题无法弹射出来——至于这是故意为之还是偶然至此尚不得而知——救生艇的推进器从那时起就一直在竭力逃离黑洞视界。目前它已离黑洞视界太近，无法进行回收了。\n\n[Root.GetLeaderName]的报告突然中断，随后[Root.GetName]船员发来通讯称，该科学专员似乎深受这一新数据的影响，因其已显著改变了[Root.GetName]的风险评估统计与生存率预测。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:462`

### English

The readings originate from a small metal pod; Science Officer [Root.GetLeaderName] has determined it to be a lifeboat of ancient and alien make.\n\nEvidently caught on the outskirts of the black hole's gravity well eons ago, at an angle just short of a slingshot maneuver - whether this was the intent or happenstance is difficult to judge - and the pod's automated thrusters have been fighting its descent to the event horizon ever since. It is now too close to the horizon to salvage.\n\n[Root.GetLeaderName]'s report ends abruptly, and the crew of the [Root.GetName] communicate that the Science Officer appears deeply affected by this new data, as it has significantly altered the [Root.GetName]'s risk assessment statistics and survival rate predictions.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:473`

可以理解。

### English

## anomaly.3075.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3075.name&lang=en)

### 中文

零散辐射

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:464`

### English

Peddling Radiation

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:475`

## anomaly.3075.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3075.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

惊人的是，此系统获取的数据居然是来自[From.GetName]本身。\n\n作为黑洞来说，其体积较小，似乎可以解释为何会有辐射从其视界逃逸出来。科学专员[Root.GetLeaderName]认为此黑洞正在崩解，不久便会消失，但很难说这个过程会不会在宇宙的热寂之前完成。\n\n用[Root.GetLeaderName]的话说，黑洞幼年期受到的重力扰动搞得它“动荡不安”，从而进一步加强了其视界流出的能源。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:465`

### English

The readings picked up in the this system come - somewhat surprisingly - from [From.GetName] itself.\n\nAs black holes go it is fairly small, which could account for some of the unexpected radiation leaking from its event horizon. Science Officer [Root.GetLeaderName] believes that the black hole is on its way to "bleeding out" and will eventually disappear entirely - though it is difficult to say whether this process would be complete before the heat death of the universe.\n\nSome gravitational upset in the hole's youth has rendered it - to quote [Root.GetLeaderName] - "wobbly," further increasing the flow of energy from its event horizon.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:476`

## anomaly.3075.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3075.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们可以从中学到很多东西。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:466`

### English

There is much we could learn.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:477`

## anomaly.3080.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3080.name&lang=en)

### 中文

玩具工厂

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:468`

### English

Toy Factory

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:479`

## anomaly.3080.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3080.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

虽说[From.GetName]上的大部分建筑都已成废墟，但还有少数得以留存。[Root.GetName]初次经过该星球时只发现了一座还算完整的建筑，孤零零地耸立在[From.GetName]的废墟之中。\n\n这便是噪音的来源。\n\n该建筑不仅结构完整，据科学专员[Root.GetLeaderName]称，似乎还存在活动迹象。\n\n那是一座生产设施，专门将塑料聚合物制成不明的物体，不过似乎是用于装饰的。其生产原料与最终产品对[Root.Owner.GetName]来说没有研究价值，但我们可以对其自动化的生产过程进行研究。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:469`

### English

There are structures on [From.GetName], though most are less than ruins and more akin to rubble. The [Root.GetName]'s initial pass over the planet reveals only one building that can be considered intact, seemingly spared whatever calamity robbed [From.GetName] of its buildings and builders alike.\n\nThis is the source of the noise.\n\nNot only is the building intact, reports Science Officer [Root.GetLeaderName], but it is active.\n\nIt is a production facility of some sort, turning plasticine polymers into mute objects of uncertain, but possibly ornamental, purpose. The raw materials and end product are largely uninteresting to the [Root.Owner.GetName], but we could doubtlessly stand to learn something from the automated manufacturing process itself.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:480`

## anomaly.3080.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3080.a&lang=en)

### 中文

也许我们应该建立据点并进行观察。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:470`

### English

Perhaps we should establish a foothold and observe.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:481`

### English

Adds an §YAlien Toy Factory§! to [From.GetName].\n

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:482`

## anomaly.3085.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3085.name&lang=en)

### 中文

《君主论》

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:472`

### English

The Prince

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:483`

## anomaly.3085.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3085.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

巨大的[From.GetName]周围的缓轨道上漂浮着一个可移动的数据库。[Root.GetName]将其打捞后发现其中似乎包含某种外星语言写就的内容。其词汇组成并非完全陌生，但其语法则是十分复杂。\n\n科学专员[Root.GetLeaderName]建议对其进行进一步研究，以便解密该文本的内容与起源。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:473`

### English

In gentle orbit around the massive [From.GetName] floats a portable databank. Scooped up by the [Root.GetName], it is found to contain text in some alien language. The vocabulary is not wholly unfamiliar, but the syntax is disconcertingly complex.\n\nScience Officer [Root.GetLeaderName] recommends a more thorough study to decipher the text's contents and origins.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:484`

## anomaly.3085.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3085.a&lang=en)

### 中文

在[Root.Owner.Capital.GetName]上进行分析。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:474`

### English

Analyze it on [Root.Owner.Capital.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:485`

## anomaly.3086.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3086.name&lang=en)

### 中文

《君主论》通读本

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:476`

### English

The Prince, Legible

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:487`

## anomaly.3086.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3086.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

该文本的标题大致可以译为“地区统治者”，通篇似乎是以[book_writer.GetSpeciesAdj]古方言写就，该方言一般使用于[book_writer.GetName]。\n\n内容无疑对他们具有一定历史价值，搞不好还能带来些什么启发。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:477`

### English

The text - its title roughly translates to "Regional Ruler" - is written in a presumably ancient dialect of the [book_writer.GetSpeciesAdj] tongue spoken in the [book_writer.GetName].\n\nThe text is doubtlessly of some historical import to them, and might contain valuable insights.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:488`

### English

Let us divine its secrets - they need not know.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:489`

## anomaly.3087.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3087.name&lang=en)

### 中文

[book_writer.GetName]

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:481`

### English

[book_writer.GetName]

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:492`

## anomaly.3087.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3087.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们听说你找到了《君主论》的最后一份拷贝。这可是我们社会十分重要的历史文化遗产。\n我们曾以为它已在多年前的海盗袭击中被毁掉了，但它如今能落入文明人的手中，我们倍感欣慰。\n\n可以将其交还给我们吗？我们会以能量币作为报酬。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:482`

### English

We understand that you have found the only surviving copy of "The Prince," a text of significant historical and cultural importance to our people.\nWe thought it irrevocably lost to a pirate raid years ago, but we are grateful to see that it found its way into civilized hands.\n\nWill you return it to us? We will gladly transfer some Energy Credits for your trouble.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:493`

## anomaly.3087.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3087.a&lang=en)

当然可以。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:483`

### English

But of course.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:494`

### 中文

我们听说你找到了《君主论》的最后一份拷贝。这可是我们社会十分重要的历史文化遗产。\n\n我们曾以为它已在多年前的海盗袭击中被毁掉了，但它如今能落入文明人的手中，我们倍感欣慰。\n\n可以将其交还给我们吗？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:485`

## anomaly.3090.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3090.name&lang=en)

### 中文

星际绑架者：紧急信标

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:493`

### English

The Ransomeers: Emergency Buoy

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:504`

## anomaly.3090.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3090.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在[From.GetName]和缓的运行轨道上竟漂浮着一枚紧急信标。其外壳已被严重损坏，似乎是被武器火力击中或是被大爆炸波及。\n\n这似乎是来自[Root.Owner.GetSpeciesAdj]的民用飞船专属型号——用于在通讯系统故障时作为紧急措施进行发射——但其受损的严重程度使具体情况都不得而知了。\n\n它一直在重复一个微弱而失真的信号。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:494`

### English

An emergency transmission buoy floats in gentle orbit around [From.GetName]. Its outer casing is heavily damaged, seemingly by glancing weapons fire or a powerful explosion.\n\nIt might be a [Root.Owner.GetSpeciesAdj] model for civilian ships - designed to be ejected as an emergency measure should the comms systems fail - but the extent of the damage makes it impossible to know for certain.\n\nIt is repeating a weak, distorted signal.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:505`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3090.a&lang=en)

### English

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:511`

## anomaly.3091.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3091.b&lang=en)

### 中文

彻底关掉。这件事到此为止。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:501`

### English

Shut it down. No one is to speak of this.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:512`

## anomaly.3092.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3092.name&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetSpeciesAdj]俘虏

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:502`

### English

[Root.GetSpeciesAdj] Captives

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:513`

## anomaly.3092.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3092.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

有、有人吗？这里是[Root.GetSpeciesAdj]民用运输船“卢克西昂号”的舰长。\n\n啊，应该说是前舰长。卢克西昂已经不复存在了。我们被海盗登船，我们在飞船爆炸前就被掳走成了人质。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:503`

### English

He- hello? This is the captain of the Luxion, a [Root.GetSpeciesAdj] civilian transport.\n\nUh, former captain. The Luxion is gone. We were boarded by pirates and taken captive before they blew up the ship.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:514`

## anomaly.3092.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3092.a&lang=en)

### 中文

你们是被谁抓了？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:504`

### English

Who captured you?

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:515`

## anomaly.3092.a.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3092.a.response&lang=en)

### 中文

他们……我、我听他们好像自称星际什么来着？星际绑架者是吧？\n\n他们可不是善茬。如果他们认为不能从[Root.GetName]手中得到些什么来换取我们的性命就会干掉我们。我偷听到他们已经在讨论要开什么条件了……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:505`

### English

They, I- I think they call themselves the ransomers? The Ransomeers?\n\nAt any rate they're a vile bunch, we'd all be dead already if they didn't think they could get something for us from the [Root.GetName]. I've overhead them discussing their demands...

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:516`

## anomaly.3092.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3092.b&lang=en)

### 中文

我们不会救你们的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:506`

### English

We will not rescue you.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:517`

## anomaly.3092.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3092.c&lang=en)

### 中文

我们会来救你们的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:507`

### English

We will rescue you.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:518`

## anomaly.3093.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3093.name&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetSpeciesAdj]俘虏

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:508`

### English

[Root.GetSpeciesAdj] Captives

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:519`

## anomaly.3093.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3093.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

什么？什么？！你们不能见死不救！\n\n要是他们认为不能用我们向[Root.GetName]索取赎金的话，绝对会连眼都不眨就宰了我们的！就连真的付了赎金的话我们的性命也没有保障啊！你们不能——

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:509`

### English

What? What? You can't. You can't not save us!\n\nIf they get it into their heads that they won't be able to ransom us back to the [Root.GetName] they won't hesitate to kill us. They'll probably kill us even if the ransom is paid! You can't--

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:520`

## anomaly.3093.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3093.a&lang=en)

### 中文

永别了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:510`

### English

Farewell.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:521`

## anomaly.3094.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3094.name&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetSpeciesAdj]俘虏

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:511`

### English

[Root.GetSpeciesAdj] Captives

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:522`

## anomaly.3094.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3094.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

哦，真是太感谢了，太感谢了啊！[Root.GetRulerTitle]。我们似乎被关在某个空间站，但具体位置我也不知道。\n\n我这就加强传输频率，你们应该可以通过紧急信标进行三角测量，确认我们被绑架的位置……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:512`

### English

Oh thank you! Thank you, [Root.GetRulerTitle]. We're being held in some space station, but I have no idea where we are beyond that.\n\nI will increase the transmission burst now, it should let you triangulate the location of this prison from the emergency buoy...

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:523`

## anomaly.3094.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3094.a&lang=en)

### 中文

位置已记录于情报日志。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:513`

### English

Position recorded in the Situation Log.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:524`

## anomaly.3095.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3095.name&lang=en)

### 中文

星际绑架者：最佳计划

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:514`

### English

The Ransomeers: Best Laid Plans

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:525`

## anomaly.3095.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3095.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

虽说海盗的基地十分隐蔽，但若知道如何寻找的话就十分明显了。\n\n一支舰队排着奇怪的队形慢慢从重新运行的采矿设施后慢悠悠飘了出来——显然是并没想到会有不速之客。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:515`

### English

The pirate base, while fairly inconspicuous, is obvious when you know what to look for.\n\nA fleet in confused formation slowly drifts out from behind the re-purposed mining complex - they were obviously not expecting company.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:526`

## anomaly.3095.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3095.a&lang=en)

### 中文

打开通讯系统。必须让被囚者做好准备。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:516`

### English

Open comms. The captives need to be ready.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:527`

## anomaly.3096.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3096.name&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetSpeciesAdj]俘虏

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:517`

### English

[Root.GetSpeciesAdj] Captives

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:528`

## anomaly.3096.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3096.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

你好啊，[Root.GetRulerTitle]。看来这一切都是因为[Root.GetSpeciesAdj]的飞船而起的了？\n\n我们好客的主人们似乎即将离开他们当前的位置。登上这地方对各方都不利，但是……如果你直接对基地发起攻击，我觉得我们倒是可以拼一拼，看看能不能弄到一艘救生艇。\n\n我们出来的时候可别把我们给炸飞了！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:518`

### English

Hello again, [Root.GetRulerTitle]. I take it [Root.GetSpeciesAdj] ships are the cause of all this commotion?\n\nOur hosts seem about ready to leave their posts. Boarding this place would probably end badly for everyone involved, but... If you attack the base directly, I think we can make a break for one of the lifeboats.\n\nPlease try not to blow us up on our way out!

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:529`

## anomaly.3096.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3096.a&lang=en)

### 中文

很好。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:519`

### English

Very well.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:530`

## anomaly.3098.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3098.name&lang=en)

### 中文

星际绑架者：尘归尘，土归土

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:520`

### English

The Ransomeers: Dust and Echoes

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:531`

## anomaly.3098.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3098.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

索取赎金的海盗们的基地被摧毁了，[Root.GetSpeciesAdj]的俘虏们也一道消失了。俘虏们似乎成功联系到了发动进攻的外星飞船，却被对方忽视了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:521`

### English

The Ransomeer pirate base has been destroyed, and the [Root.GetSpeciesAdj] prisoners along with it. It appears the captives managed to hail the alien ships that attacked, but were ignored.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:532`

## anomaly.3098.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3098.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们绝不会忘记这个悲剧。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:522`

### English

We will never forget this tragedy.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:533`

## anomaly.3099.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3099.name&lang=en)

### 中文

星际绑架者：意外的救星

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:523`

### English

The Ransomeers: Unexpected Saviors

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:534`

## anomaly.3099.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3099.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.From.GetName]的战斗行动彻底摧毁了星际绑架者的部队。\n\n[Root.GetSpeciesAdj]俘虏在交战中成功逃出，目前已登上一艘救生艇，即将与[Root.GetSpeciesAdj]救援飞船接头。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:524`

### English

Military action by the [From.From.GetName] has destroyed the Ransomeer forces.\n\nThe [Root.GetSpeciesAdj] captives made a successful escape attempt during the attack, and are now safely on board a lifeboat well on its way to be a rendezvous [Root.GetSpeciesAdj] rescue vessels.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:535`

## anomaly.3099.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3099.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们对此感激不尽。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:525`

### English

We are eternally grateful.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:536`

## opinion_live_captives

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aopinion_live_captives&lang=en)

### 中文

生俘

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:526`

### English

Live Captives

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:537`

## anomaly.3100.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3100.name&lang=en)

### 中文

星际绑架者：质疑

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:527`

### English

The Ransomeers: Assailed

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:538`

## anomaly.3100.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3100.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

不知何方发起的战斗行动彻底摧毁了星际绑架者的部队。\n\n[Root.GetSpeciesAdj]俘虏们奇迹般地逃出生天，在攻击行动中偷去了一艘救生艇，即将返回[Root.Capital.GetName]。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:528`

### English

Military action by an unknown force destroyed the Ransomeer pirates.\n\nMiraculously, the [Root.GetSpeciesAdj] captives managed to break out and steal a lifeboat during the attack, and are now well on their way back to [Root.Capital.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:539`

## anomaly.3100.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3100.a&lang=en)

### 中文

真是神秘而偶然的遭遇啊。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:529`

### English

Most fortuitous, and mysterious.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:540`

## anomaly.3101.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3101.name&lang=en)

### 中文

星际绑架者：捡拾碎片

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:530`

### English

The Ransomeers: Picking Up the Pieces

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:541`

## anomaly.3101.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3101.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

海盗的基地在我方飞船造成的连锁效应中缓慢地分崩离析。与此同时，我们开始了对卢克西昂号生还者的搜索。\n\n如果他们真的上了救生艇，现在也该联络我们了……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:531`

### English

As the pirate base slowly tears itself apart in a savage chain reaction instigated by our military vessels, the search for the captive crew of the Luxion begins.\n\nIf they made it to a lifeboat, they should be attempting to hail us by now...

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:542`

## anomaly.3101.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3101.a&lang=en)

### 中文

扫描所有频率。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:532`

### English

Scan all frequencies.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:543`

## anomaly.3102.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3102.name&lang=en)

### 中文

已解救[Root.GetSpeciesAdj]俘虏

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:533`

### English

Freed [Root.GetSpeciesAdj] Captives

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:544`

## anomaly.3102.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3102.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetRulerTitle]，这里是卢西安号的船员。\n\n计划进行得十分顺利。在你们展开对基地的袭击后，卫兵们都跑上船了，于是我们也成功破坏了囚室大门。救生艇无人看守，看来你们给这群海盗造成了不小的麻烦，完全没人注意到我们逃跑了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:534`

### English

[Root.GetRulerTitle], this is the crew of the Luxion.\n\nThe plan went off without a hitch. Once you started bombarding the base directly, the guards took to their ships, and we were able to break down the cell door. The lifeboats were unguarded, and it seems you kept the pirates busy enough that they did not care much about our escape.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:545`

## anomaly.3102.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3102.a&lang=en)

### 中文

欢迎回家，脱狱者。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:535`

### English

Welcome home, jailbreakers.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:546`

星际绑架者：化为灰烬

### English

The Ransomeers: Smoke and Ashes

## anomaly.3104.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3104.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

被关押的[Root.GetSpeciesNamePlural]最终未能逃出海盗基地。我们并未能确认他们到底是如何死亡的——是救生艇未能成功发射，还是他们压根没有逃出囚室——但这不妨碍我们确认他们已死亡的事实。\n\n[Root.GetSpeciesAdj]们一定会十分感激[Root.GetName]的[Root.GetRulerTitle]采取的迅速行动，但感觉他们短时间内也不会遗忘这出惨剧。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:542`

### English

The kidnapped [Root.GetSpeciesNamePlural] never made it off the pirate base. We are unable to determine exactly where they met their end - if the lifeboat failed to launch, or if they never made it out of their holding cell - but fact remains that they are dead.\n\nThe [Root.GetSpeciesAdj] people are sure to appreciate the swift actions undertaken by the [Root.GetRulerTitle] of the [Root.GetName], but they are also unlikely to soon forget this tragedy.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:553`

## anomaly.3104.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3104.a&lang=en)

### 中文

确实如此……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:543`

### English

Indeed...

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:554`

## anomaly.3105.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3105.name&lang=en)

### 中文

星际绑架者：一切都好？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:544`

### English

The Ransomeers: All's Well?

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:555`

## anomaly.3105.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3105.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

卢克西昂号的船员安全的回到了[Root.Capital.GetName]，一场灾难就此避免，[Root.GetName]的[Root.GetRulerTitle]的果断行动让[Root.GetAdj]人民十分自豪。\n\n海盗的绑架勒索事件已经成功划上句点，海盗团也被消灭了，但还有一些事没搞清楚，比如被俘虏的人声称他们在被抓后曾经从飞船窗口中看到了更大型的海盗舰队。从目前情况来看，可以确定威胁比我们实际看到的还要大的多。\n\n进行这次勒索行动的家伙们已经被处理了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:545`

### English

With the crew of the Luxion safely on their way back to [Root.Capital.GetName], disaster has been averted. The [Root.GetAdj] people are sure to glory in the decisive actions of the [Root.GetName]'s [Root.GetRulerTitle].\n\nThe situation with the kidnapping Ransomeer pirates, as well as the pirate band itself, has come to an end. Some questions remain, mainly relating to the much larger piratical navy the captives claim to have seen through portholes shortly after their capture. But, considering the circumstances, it can safely be assumed that the threat appeared to be much greater than it actually was.\n\nThe Ransomeers are over and done with.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:556`

## anomaly.3105.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3105.a&lang=en)

### 中文

发人深省……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:546`

### English

I wonder...

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:557`

## anomaly.3110.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3110.name&lang=en)

### 中文

关于过去的信号

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:547`

### English

Of Transmissions Past

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:558`

## anomaly.3110.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3110.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

不出所料，[Root.GetName]成功定位了[From.GetName]信号的来源。\n\n虽说整颗星球看似完全未被外星生物开发过，不过星球南半球一座山丘顶部却发现了一座光滑的白色矿物柱体。信号正是从该柱体中发出，柱体也明显是为了避免共振而设计。\n\n科学专员[Root.GetLeaderName]已停下手边工作，开始筹备§Y特殊项目§!，以期能够解密该信号携带的信息。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:548`

### English

As expected, the [Root.GetName] has located the source of the transmission emanating from [From.GetName].\n\nWhile the planet is otherwise seemingly untouched by alien hands, a lone monolith of some sleek off-white mineral stands atop a hill in the southern hemisphere. It is from within this monument that the signal emerges, the enclosure clearly built for longevity rather than resonance.\n\nScience Officer [Root.GetLeaderName] has gone out of their way to establish the parameters for a §YSpecial Project§! that would decode the information the signal carries.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:559`

## anomaly.3110.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3110.a&lang=en)

### 中文

不错的提议。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:549`

### English

An appropriate initiative.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:560`

## anomaly.3110.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3110.b&lang=en)

### 中文

死人的话有什么好听的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:550`

### English

We have little to gain by listening to the dead.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:561`

## anomaly.3110.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3110.c&lang=en)

### 中文

拆除柱体，寻找发射器。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:551`

### English

Raze the monument, find the transmitter.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:562`

## anomaly.3111.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3111.name&lang=en)

### 中文

湮灭的信号

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:552`

### English

Of Transmissions Crushed

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:563`

## anomaly.3111.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3111.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们原以为所发现的巨石只是发射器的保护性外壳，但事实并非如此。\n\n在被单独取出后，发射器本身毫无用处。已被摧毁的巨石有某种内部结构，能够使其运转。现在我们已经无法恢复其功能了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:553`

### English

We believed the monolith to be only a protective housing for the transmitter, but we were wrong.\n\nThe transmitter, once isolated, turned out to be useless on its own. Something about the internal structure of the now-razed monolith made it work, and we have no hope of restoring its function.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:564`

## anomaly.3111.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3111.a&lang=en)

### 中文

令人失望。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:554`

### English

Disappointing.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:565`

## anomaly.3112.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3112.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

出人意料，巨石很轻易地就被解体。与最初的猜想相反，其中心的小型发射器为巨石的矿质结构供能，使得信号发射得以进行。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:555`

### English

The monolith disassembles with surprising ease. Contrary to earlier assumptions, the tiny transmitter at its center energizes the mineral structure of the monolith, enabling the transmission.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:566`

## anomaly.3112.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3112.a&lang=en)

### 中文

真是有趣。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:556`

### English

Interesting.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:567`

## anomaly.3112.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3112.b&lang=en)

### 中文

很不幸，这算不上什么新闻。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:557`

### English

Old news, unfortunately.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:568`

## anomaly.3113.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3113.name&lang=en)

### 中文

被解密的信号

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:558`

### English

Of Transmissions Decoded

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:569`

## anomaly.3113.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3113.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

科学专员[From.GetLeaderName]报告称[From.From.GetName]发出的信号已经解密完成了。\n\n这是一段记录性质的信息，记载了千年前一场造成数百人死亡的战斗。一支原本坚定的联盟由于背叛与欺骗最终分崩离析，他们的大型舰队在敌人到来之前就自相残杀殆尽。\n\n看来这些残骸作为墓碑被保留在了这里。敌人精准地命中了舰体的弱点，而船员们在那个时候就被消灭了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:559`

### English

Science Officer [From.GetLeaderName] reports that the signal broadcast by the transmission beacon on [From.From.GetName] has been decoded.\n\nIt is a memorial message, decrying the loss of hundreds of lives in a battle millennia past. A stalwart alliance apparently fell victim to treachery and deceit, and a massive fleet wiped itself out before the enemy had even arrived, as ally turned on ally.\n\nApparently these desiccated hulks were left in place as floating grave-markers, the crews having been destroyed by surgical strikes to weak points in their ships' hulls.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:570`

## anomaly.3113.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3113.a&lang=en)

### 中文

研究这些记录。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:560`

### English

Study these records.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:571`

## anomaly.3113.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3113.b&lang=en)

### 中文

计算舰队残骸的轨道与当前位置。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:561`

### English

Calculate the wrecked fleet's trajectory and current position.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:572`

## anomaly.3114.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3114.name&lang=en)

### 中文

海盗暗涌

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:562`

### English

Of Pirates Current

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:573`

## anomaly.3114.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3114.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

到达[From.GetName]星系后，显而易见，[Root.GetName]来得太迟了。\n\n不久前一支来路不明的海盗舰队发现了这片战场并将其拆除，用所得的材料在附近一颗小行星上建立了基地。\n\n我们的研究宣告结束，看来一场好斗冲突已不可避免。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:563`

### English

Upon arriving in the [From.GetName] system, it becomes clear that the [Root.GetName] is too late.\n\nA pirate fleet of unknown origin discovered the straying battlefield some time ago, and cannibalized it to build a base on a nearby asteroid.\n\nOur search ends, as a violent confrontation seems unavoidable.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:574`

## anomaly.3115.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3115.name&lang=en)

### 中文

漂流的舰队

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:564`

### English

Of Fleets Drifting

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:575`

## anomaly.3115.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3115.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetName]已找到了[From.GetName]星系内漂移的残骸。信息中提到的飞船许多已在长年侵蚀下化为尘埃，但仍有少数保存得相当完整。\n\n其中有六艘可以让工程师进行抢修。\n\n打开§Y情报日志§!以查看详情。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:565`

### English

The [Root.GetName] has located the drifting wrecks in the [From.GetName] system. Many of the ships referenced in the memorial beacon message have been reduced to dust and ashes over the centuries, but a few still remain remarkably intact.\n\nBetween them, six military vessels should have enough engineers to restore the better-preserved hulks to working order.\n\nConsult the §YSituation Log§! for details.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:576`

## anomaly.3115.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3115.a&lang=en)

### 中文

还真有意思。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:566`

### English

Most intriguing.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:577`

## anomaly.3116.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3116.name&lang=en)

### 中文

征用舰队

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:567`

### English

Of Fleets Commandeered

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:578`

## anomaly.3116.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3116.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

部分古代舰队残骸已恢复运转，尽管派去的工程师无法对如何完成这一点达成一致。\n\n舰队已经收编入[Root.GetSpeciesAdj]舰队。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:568`

### English

The ancient rag-tag armada has been partially resurrected, although the dispatched engineers cannot agree on exactly how this feat was accomplished.\n\nThe vessels have been commissioned into the [Root.GetSpeciesAdj] fleets.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:579`

## anomaly.3116.a.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3116.a.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y古代联合舰队§!已修复。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:569`

### English

The §YAncient Alliance fleet§! is restored.\n

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:580`

## anomaly.3117.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3117.name&lang=en)

### 中文

舰队启动

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:570`

### English

Of Fleets Turned

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:581`

## anomaly.3117.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3117.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

工程师们首先将精力放在了大型无畏舰上。根据存储信号显示，这是整场战斗中最先对其它飞船发起攻击的几艘船之一。\n\n然而事实证明这是个错误。\n\n尽管我们已经有意尝试绕过飞船的高级系统，但飞船的电脑最终还是突然启动，并以高频脉冲攻击了我方飞船。由于我方为了增加行动可用带宽而在此之前关闭了战斗防火墙，这段高频脉冲便立即将附近所有我方飞船控制为己有。通讯切断前的最后几篇日志显示，飞船的所有生命支持系统均已失效，飞船被设置为前往[Root.Owner.Capital.GetName]。\n\n据推测，我方船员已全体阵亡。古代作战计算机也瞄准了我们的母星，我们得提前做好最坏的打算。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:571`

### English

The engineering corps focused first on the massive dreadnought, indicated by the memorial signal to have been one of the first ships to turn on the others in that fated battle.\n\nThis turned out to be a mistake.\n\nDespite conscious efforts to bypass the higher-level systems, the ship's computer suddenly came online and blasted our ships with a high-frequency pulse. With combat firewalls disabled to increase bandwidth during the operation, the burst transmission instantly slaved all nearby vessels to the reactivated dreadnought. The final logs before all transmissions were cut shows all life support systems being systematically disabled, and a course plotted for [Root.Owner.Capital.GetName].\n\nWith our crews presumed dead and an ancient combat computer setting its sights on our homeworld, we had best prepare for the worst.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:582`

## anomaly.3118.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3118.name&lang=en)

### 中文

舰队被毁

### English

Of Fleets Destroyed

无畏舰被摧毁，其余飞船已激活，事件结束

### English

dreadnought killed, event ends with remaining ships being activated

舰队被毁

Of Fleets Destroyed

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3119.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

无畏舰被他人摧毁，其余飞船全部被毁

### English

dreadnought killed by others, all remaining ships destroyed

## anomaly.3120.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3120.name&lang=en)

### 中文

剿灭海盗

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:576`

### English

Of Pirates Destroyed

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:587`

## anomaly.3120.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3120.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

威胁已被终结。或许，我们至少能部分利用起被海盗抢走的古老科技。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:577`

### English

The threat is ended. We might, at least, be able to make some use of the ancient technology the pirates plundered.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:588`

## anomaly.3120.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3120.a&lang=en)

### 中文

但疑问尚未解开……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:578`

### English

But questions remain...

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:589`

## anomaly.3120.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3120.b&lang=en)

### 中文

$anomaly.3120.a$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:579`

### English

$anomaly.3120.a$

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:590`

## anomaly.3125.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3125.name&lang=en)

### 中文

古代工厂

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:582`

### English

Ancient Manufactory

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:595`

## anomaly.3125.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3125.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

该轨道站似乎曾作为小行星的矿物开采与加工中心使用，设计上与我们的开采站有些许相似。\n\n科学专员[Root.GetLeaderName]认为派出几位工程师——最好是已经驻扎在飞船上的[Root.Owner.GetSpeciesNamePlural]——也许就可以使其恢复运转。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:583`

### English

The orbital station appears to have once functioned as a mineral extraction and processing center for the asteroid, not wholly dissimilar from our own mining station designs.\n\nScience Officer [Root.GetLeaderName] expects that a handful of engineers - ideally [Root.Owner.GetSpeciesNamePlural] already stationed on military vessels - just might be able to restore the station to working order.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:596`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3125.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们对异种残骸没有兴趣。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:584`

### English

We have no interest in xeno wreckage.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:597`

### English

Let us restore this station.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:598`

## anomaly.3126.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3126.name&lang=en)

### 中文

古代工厂：新开采站

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:588`

### English

Ancient Manufactory: New Mining Station

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:602`

## anomaly.3126.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3126.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

工程师们完成了任务，使用改装过的[Root.Owner.GetSpeciesAdj]部件与控制机制翻修了这座破旧的开采站。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:589`

### English

The engineering corps completed their mission, retrofitting the decrepit mining station with adapted [Root.Owner.GetSpeciesAdj] parts and control mechanisms.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:603`

## anomaly.3127.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3127.name&lang=en)

### 中文

古代工厂

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:591`

### English

Ancient Manufactory

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:606`

## anomaly.3127.desc.1

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3127.desc.1&lang=en)

### 中文

$anomaly.3126.desc$\n\n似乎这个工厂最初是用来开采和精炼贝塞里安石的————现在它已经恢复如初了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:592`

### English

$anomaly.3126.desc$\n\nIt seems the manufactory was originally built for the mining and refining of Betharian Stones - functionality that has now been restored.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:607`

## anomaly.3127.desc.2

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3127.desc.2&lang=en)

### 中文

$anomaly.3126.desc$\n\n对于这个空间站最初的功能我们仍然还有很多不了解的地方。看来这里最初用于开采和精炼某些其它的资源，而非我们所感兴趣的矿物生产。以上推测还需进一步调查才能确认。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:593`

### English

$anomaly.3126.desc$\n\nThere remain some aspects of the station's original function that are not entirely clear to us. It appears the manufactory was originally intended to mine and refine something other than the production minerals we are interested in. This bears further investigation.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:608`

## anomaly.3127.desc.3

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3127.desc.3&lang=en)

### 中文

$anomaly.3126.desc$\n\n我们接到报告，从工厂的加工室中回收到某种未知物质——也许这个开采站曾经开采并精炼过我们从没见过的矿物。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:594`

### English

$anomaly.3126.desc$\n\nWe have received reports of an unknown substance recovered from the manufactory's processing chambers - it is possible that the station once mined and refined something other than the minerals we concern ourselves with.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:609`

### English

There is much we can learn.

## anomaly.3128.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3128.name&lang=en)

### 中文

古代工厂：无人机启动

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:599`

### English

Ancient Manufactory: Drones Activated

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:615`

## anomaly.3128.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3128.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.Owner.GetSpeciesName]工程师们的探索一无所获。这个太空港过于破旧，已无法再次启动，很多建筑结构在尝试启动时就直接崩塌了。\n\n在工厂坍塌的同时，有什么东西从地底下爬了出来——它们用高能激光器切开了一条通路，直勾勾地瞪着[Root.GetFleetName]。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:600`

### English

The [Root.Owner.GetSpeciesName] engineers' prodding and probing has proven fruitless. The station is too dilapidated to bring online, large sections of the structure simply collapsing under the strain of attempted reactivation.\n\nAs the manufactory's structural integrity finally failed, something, some things, emerged from its depths - cutting their way out with high-powered lasers and setting their sights on the [Root.GetFleetName].

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:616`

## anomaly.3129.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3129.name&lang=en)

### 中文

古代工厂：惊动古代巢穴

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:602`

### English

Ancient Manufactory: Nest Disturbed

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:619`

## anomaly.3129.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3129.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

老旧的石层破开，有什么东西涌了出来。\n\n完全不是预想中光洁的矿石，尖利的锯齿状晶体边缘切碎了破旧的轨道站，随后又将注意力转向了[Root.GetFleetName]。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:603`

### English

As the old rock tumblers spin up, something spills out.\n\nFar from the polished mineral ore expected, jagged crystal edges shred the decrepit orbital station before turning their attention to the [Root.GetFleetName].

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:620`

## anomaly.3129.a.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3129.a.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

古代工厂被毁。\n\n[Root.GetFleetName]正遭受攻击！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:604`

### English

The Ancient Manufactory is destroyed.\nThe [Root.GetFleetName] is under attack!

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:621`

## anomaly.3130.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3130.name&lang=en)

### 中文

辩论与探讨

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:605`

### English

Debate and Discussion

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:623`

## anomaly.3130.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3130.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

科学专员[Root.GetLeaderName]报告称[From.GetPlanetMoon][From.GetName]上确实存在过较为发达的文明，但还没来得及飞出[From.GetPlanetMoon]便自行消亡了。通过分析从星球表面取回的古代无线通讯站碎片，[Root.GetName]的船员逐渐还原了整个文明消逝的过程。\n\n在很久以前的某个时间点，该文明的看法分裂为两派，也就是我们今天所说的集体主义与个人主义。虽说这些哲学上的争论与[Root.Owner.GetName]人们的生活并没有什么太大关系，但这个文明却因不知采用何种意识形态和道德标准而最终分崩离析。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:606`

### English

Science Officer [Root.GetLeaderName] reports that the [From.GetPlanetMoon] [From.GetName] was indeed once home to a somewhat advanced civilization, although one that brought about its own end before escaping [From.GetPlanetMoon]bound existence. By analyzing the few fragments that could be recovered from the ancient wireless information depots on the surface, the crew of the [Root.GetName] have pieced together the narrative of the civilization's demise.\n\nAt some point in the distant past, an ideological schism developed over the definitions of the philosophies we today know as collectivism and individualism. While the relevance of these philosophies seems highly questionable to the people of the [Root.Owner.GetName], this civilization evidently tore itself apart over the question of which moral stances should be attributed to which ideology.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:624`

## anomaly.3130.desc.colle

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3130.desc.colle&lang=en)

### 中文

科学专员[Root.GetLeaderName]报告称[From.GetPlanetMoon][From.GetName]上确实存在过较为发达的文明，但还没来得及飞出[From.GetPlanetMoon]便自行死亡了。通过分析从星球表面取回的古代无线通讯站碎片，[Root.GetName]的船员逐渐还原了整个文明消逝的过程。\n\n在很久以前的某个时间点，该文明的看法分为两派，也就是我们今天所说的集体主义与个人主义。虽说集体主义的本质对于[Root.Owner.GetName]的人们来说再明显不过，但这个文明却因不知采用何种看法和心理标准而最终分崩离析。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:607`

### English

Science Officer [Root.GetLeaderName] reports that the [From.GetPlanetMoon] [From.GetName] was indeed once home to a somewhat advanced civilization, although one that brought about its own end before escaping [From.GetPlanetMoon]bound existence. By analyzing the few fragments that could be recovered from the ancient wireless information depots on the surface, the crew of the [Root.GetName] have pieced together the narrative of the civilization's demise.\n\nAt some point in the distant past, an ideological schism developed over the definitions of the philosophies we today know as collectivism and individualism. While the nature of collectivism is blindingly obvious to the [Root.Owner.GetSpeciesNamePlural], this civilization evidently tore itself apart over the question of which moral stances should be attributed to which ideology.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:625`

## anomaly.3130.desc.mater

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3130.desc.mater&lang=en)

### 中文

科学专员[Root.GetLeaderName]报告称[From.GetPlanetMoon][From.GetName]上确实存在过较为发达的文明，但还没来得及飞出[From.GetPlanetMoon]便自行死亡了。通过分析从星球表面取回的古代无线通讯站碎片，[Root.GetName]的船员逐渐还原了整个文明消逝的过程。\n\n在很久以前的某个时间点，该文明的看法分为两派，也就是我们今天所说的集体主义与个人主义。虽说个人主义的本质对于[Root.Owner.GetName]的人们来说再明显不过，但这个文明却因不知采用何种看法和心理标准而最终分崩离析。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:608`

### English

Science Officer [Root.GetLeaderName] reports that the [From.GetPlanetMoon] [From.GetName] was indeed once home to a somewhat advanced civilization, although one that brought about its own end before escaping [From.GetPlanetMoon]bound existence. By analyzing the few fragments that could be recovered from the ancient wireless information depots on the surface, the crew of the [Root.GetName] have pieced together the narrative of the civilization's demise.\n\nAt some point in the distant past, an ideological schism developed over the definitions of the philosophies we today know as collectivism and individualism. While the nature of individualism is blindingly obvious to the people of the [Root.Owner.GetName], this civilization evidently tore itself apart over the question of which moral stances should be attributed to which ideology.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:626`

## anomaly.3135.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3135.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

从荒芜的[From.GetName]星球回来时，科学专员[Root.GetLeaderName]带回来了一张用植物纤维做成的针织物。虽然不清楚其来历和用途，但是实验证明了此物的吸水性极好。虽然此物对科学研究毫无帮助，不过[Root.GetLeaderName]却对它情有独钟，无论去哪里都把该物品随身携带。\n\n“[Root.GetLeaderName]真是个奇人。”船员们私底下评论道。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:612`

### English

Returning from the desolate surface of [From.GetName], Science Officer [Root.GetLeaderName] brings with them a sheet of woven plant fiber. Its exact origin and purpose is unknown, but it has proven quite absorbent in tests. While useless to the sciences, [Root.GetLeaderName] has taken a shine to the thing and now carries it with them wherever they go.\n\n"Officer [Root.GetLeaderName] is a real hoopy frood," the crew whisper.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:630`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.3135.a&lang=en)

那是什么意思？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:613`

### English

What does that even mean?
