## country.999.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acountry.999.name&lang=en)

### 中文

发现彗星

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:619`

### English

Comet Sighted

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:639`

## country.999.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acountry.999.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetSpeciesNamePlural]天生就很有好奇心，而[Root.Capital.GetName]上空划过的彗星在我们的民众中引起了广泛的惊奇之情。\n\n他们坚信这是大事将至的先兆。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:620`

### English

[Root.GetSpeciesNamePlural] are naturally curious, and the appearance of a comet in the sky above [Root.Capital.GetName] has sown a widespread sense of wonder among our people.\n\nThey are convinced that this is a sign of great things to come.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:640`

## country.999.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acountry.999.a&lang=en)

### 中文

这是一个吉兆。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:621`

### English

It is a good omen.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:641`

## country.999.desc.pac

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acountry.999.desc.pac&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetSpeciesNamePlural]天生就很有好奇心，[Root.Capital.GetName]上空划过的彗星在我们的民众中引起了广泛的恭谦之情。\n\n他们热切地希望这样的奇迹终有一天能让全星系的所有生物团结一心。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:622`

### English

[Root.GetSpeciesNamePlural] are naturally curious, and the appearance of a comet in the sky above [Root.Capital.GetName] has sown a widespread sense of humility among our people.\n\nIt is their fervent hope that wonders like this one will someday bring all beings of the galaxy together.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:642`

## country.999.a.pac

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acountry.999.a.pac&lang=en)

### 中文

这是一个吉兆。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:623`

### English

It is a good omen.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:643`

## country.999.desc.mil

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acountry.999.desc.mil&lang=en)

### 中文

一颗彗星从平流层顶部擦过，熊熊燃烧的烈焰之羽炙烤着[Root.Capital.GetName]的苍穹。其天体轨迹展现出的强大力量给首都的人们留下了无比深刻的印象。\n\n他们用这条势不可挡的航迹来比喻[Root.GetName]终将攀上星系无上霸权的必然性。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:624`

### English

A fierce plume of flame sears the heavens of [Root.Capital.GetName] as a comet skirts the upper stratosphere. The sheer power evident in its cosmic trajectory has made a profound impression on the capital's populace.\n\nThey liken the intractability of its path to the inevitability of the [Root.GetName]'s ascension to galactic supremacy.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:644`

## country.999.a.mil

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acountry.999.a.mil&lang=en)

### 中文

愿胜利与我们同在。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:625`

### English

To victory.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:645`

## country.999.desc.spirit

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acountry.999.desc.spirit&lang=en)

### 中文

一道亮光划过[Root.Capital.GetName]的夜空，它柔和的弧线与缓慢运转的繁星们交相辉映，形成鲜明的对比。\n\n[Root.Capital.GetName]的居民将其视作上天认可我们的证明，也是对我们事业的微妙祝福。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:626`

### English

A light was seen moving in the night sky of [Root.Capital.GetName], its gentle arc standing in stark contrast to the slow revolution of the stars.\n\nThe citizens of [Root.Capital.GetName] see it as proof of recognition by the powers that be, and a subtle blessing of our cause.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:646`

## country.999.a.spirit

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acountry.999.a.spirit&lang=en)

### 中文

确实如此。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:627`

### English

Indeed.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:647`

## country.999.desc.material

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acountry.999.desc.material&lang=en)

### 中文

近日，[Root.Capital.GetStarName]星系发现一颗带有气体和尘埃尾部的小型天体。\n\n它的消逝平淡无奇。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:628`

### English

A small celestial object with pronounced gaseous and particulate tails was recently observed in the [Root.Capital.GetStarName] system.\n\nIts passing was uneventful.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:648`

## country.999.a.material

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acountry.999.a.material&lang=en)

### 中文

了解。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:629`

### English

Acknowledged.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:649`

## country.999.desc.phile

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acountry.999.desc.phile&lang=en)

### 中文

一颗彗星带着光亮的尾迹划破了[Root.Capital.GetName]的地平线，宛如来自遥远星星的特使。[Root.GetSpeciesName]民众为之欢欣鼓舞，认为它冥冥之中预示着我们在宇宙中并不孤单。\n\n他们比以往任何时候都更加支持[Root.GetName]的雄心，以涨开双臂迎接整个星系。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:630`

### English

A comet blazes a trail across [Root.Capital.GetName]'s horizon, a bright envoy from distant stars. The [Root.GetSpeciesName] people find great joy in this reminder, vague as it is, that we are not alone in this universe.\n\nThey are more supportive than ever of the [Root.GetName]'s ambition to greet the galaxy with open arms.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:650`

## country.999.a.phile

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acountry.999.a.phile&lang=en)

### 中文

真是天大的好消息。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:631`

### English

Tremendous.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:651`

## country.999.desc.phobe

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acountry.999.desc.phobe&lang=en)

### 中文

一颗骇人的彗星在[Root.Capital.GetName]的天空中游荡。那一道久久未能消散的弧线，残酷地提醒着[Root.GetSpeciesName]，我们难得独善其身。茫茫宇宙中，未知的令人惊骇也许比我们想的更加迫近了。\n\n我们应该将其视作一个信号，并对保持对外星生物毫不松懈的警惕态度。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:632`

### English

A dread comet haunts the skies of [Root.Capital.GetName]. Its implacable arc is a harsh reminder to the [Root.GetSpeciesName] that we are not alone, and that unknowable horrors from beyond the stars may be closer than they appear.\n\nWe must take this as a sign to remain ever vigilant against alien influence.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:652`

## country.999.a.phobe

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acountry.999.a.phobe&lang=en)

### 中文

这是一个凶兆。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:633`

### English

It is an omen.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:653`

## country.999.desc.coll

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acountry.999.desc.coll&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetSpeciesNamePlural]的许多团体都观测到一颗彗星飞快地掠过[Root.Capital.GetName]的天空。这颗孤独又渺小的天体却令许人们感到巨大的不安。[Root.GetSpeciesName]与[Root.GetSpeciesName]互相确认着自己并不孤单以寻求慰藉。\n\n他们相信，这预兆着[Root.GetSpeciesAdj]社会将要面临孤立和离散的命运。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:634`

### English

Groups of [Root.GetSpeciesNamePlural] watched a comet streak across the sky of [Root.Capital.GetName]. The appearance of this lonely stellar object caused seemingly disproportionate distress, and [Root.GetSpeciesName] turned to [Root.GetSpeciesName] for assurance that they are not alone.\n\nThey are convinced that this is a sign of coming isolation and splintering of [Root.GetSpeciesAdj] society.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:654`

## country.999.a.coll

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acountry.999.a.coll&lang=en)

### 中文

令人警醒。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:635`

### English

Alarming.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:655`

## country.999.desc.ind

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acountry.999.desc.ind&lang=en)

### 中文

一颗孤傲的彗星在[Root.Capital.GetStarName]星系中滑行，在[Root.Capital.GetName]上清晰可见。它在宇宙中一路穿行，充满活力，势不可挡。\n\n这正是每位[Root.GetSpeciesNamePlural]都想为之奋斗的理想。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:636`

### English

A lone, proud comet soars through the [Root.Capital.GetStarName] system, clearly visible from [Root.Capital.GetName]. It forges its own path through the cosmos, vibrant and ever undeterred.\n\nThis is the ideal which all [Root.GetSpeciesNamePlural] strive for.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:656`

## country.999.a.ind

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acountry.999.a.ind&lang=en)

### 中文

这是一个吉兆。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:637`

### English

It is a good omen.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:657`

## country.999.desc.mach

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acountry.999.desc.mach&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.Capital.GetName]上的[Root.GetSpeciesNamePlural]单元报告观测到了一个正在气化的小型冰质星系内天体，其赤纬交角为10.3度，赤经2度54分8秒。\n\n经测量，该天体相到恒星的距离大约为3.75个天文单位，其彗发的尺寸及可见度值得注意。这个天体的尺寸经计算大约为5×10×6立方公里。该次观测事件正常结束，没有意外发生。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:638`

### English

Planetside [Root.GetSpeciesNamePlural] units on [Root.Capital.GetName] report the sighting of a small, icy solar system body exhibiting outgassing at two hours, fifty-four minutes, eight seconds right ascension, ten-point-three degrees declination.\n\nThe distance of the body has been triangulated at roughly three-point-seven-five astronomical units to the sun, and the size and visibility of the body's coma have been classified as noteworthy. The body's dimensions are calculated to be approximately five by ten by six kilometers. The sighting event concluded without incident.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:658`

## country.999.a.mach

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acountry.999.a.mach&lang=en)

### 中文

收到。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:639`

### English

Received.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:659`

## country.999.desc.belief

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acountry.999.desc.belief&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.Capital.GetStarName]系最近发现了一颗彗星。\n在§H[Root.Capital.GetName]§!表面清晰可见的这颗彗星在大众心中激起了强烈的反响。\n\n几乎无人能否认，在§H[GetCycleName]§!轮回中出现这样的一个天体毫无疑问是[GetCycleAttitude]的原因。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:640`

### English

A comet was recently detected in the [Root.Capital.GetStarName] system.\nClearly visible from the surface of §H[Root.Capital.GetName]§!, its appearance was met with an intense reaction from the planet's population.\n\nThere is little disagreement that the occurrence of such a celestial event during a §H[GetCycleName]§! is a cause for [GetCycleAttitude].

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:660`

## country.1000.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acountry.1000.name&lang=en)

### 中文

浪迹星涯

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:645`

### English

Wanderlust

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:667`

## country.1000.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acountry.1000.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetName]研究部门的首席科学官失踪了。\n\n他们的私人赞助的科研船试验机也不见了。\n\n[Root.Capital.GetName]科学界一片哗然。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:646`

### English

The Head of the [Root.GetName]'s Research Department has gone missing.\n\nAlong with their privately-funded, highly experimental prototype Science Ship.\n\nThe incident has the scientific community on [Root.Capital.GetName] in an uproar.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:668`

## country.1000.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acountry.1000.a&lang=en)

### 中文

真奇怪呢。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:647`

### English

How odd.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:669`

## country.1001.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acountry.1001.name&lang=en)

### 中文

浪迹星涯：早有谋划

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:648`

### English

Wanderlust: With Purpose

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:670`

## country.1001.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acountry.1001.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

首席科学官——准确来说应该是前首席科学官——还没有找到。但是一些关于科研船试验机以及长官的私人研究的消息却已经传到了我们的耳朵里。虽然有一些太过离谱，但显而易见的是尽快寻回首席关乎着[Root.GetName]的利益。\n\n情报日志已更新。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:649`

### English

The whereabouts of the Head - now technically the former Head of Research - have yet to be ascertained. Certain details regarding the prototype Science Ship and the Head's private research have reached our ears, and while some aspects seem too fantastical to be true, it is clear that it is in the [Root.GetName]'s interest to find and recover the Chair as soon as possible.\n\nThe Situation Log has been updated.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:671`

## country.1001.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acountry.1001.a&lang=en)

### 中文

很好。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:650`

### English

Very well.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:672`

开启“浪迹星涯：叛变科学官”事件链

### English

Begins the Wanderlust: Rogue Scientist Event Chain

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acountry.1003.name&lang=en)

浪迹星涯：死胡同

### English

Wanderlust: Dead End

## country.1003.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acountry.1003.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

首席科学官并不在这个星系之内。\n\n这一趟要不就彻底是白忙活，要不就是线索早已经断了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:653`

### English

The Research Head is not in this system.\n\nEither the lead was a dud, or the trail has gone cold.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:675`

## country.1003.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acountry.1003.a&lang=en)

### 中文

继续寻找。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:654`

### English

We will keep looking.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:676`

## country.1005.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acountry.1005.name&lang=en)

### 中文

浪迹星涯：匆匆一瞥

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:655`

### English

Wanderlust: A Glimpse

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:677`

## country.1005.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acountry.1005.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

科学家的船就在这个星系里——如果没猜错的话。\n\n我们没法通过探测器确定他们的位置，不过他们主动呼叫了我们。把他们的信号接到[Root.Capital.GetName]。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:656`

### English

The Researcher's ship is in this system - we think.\n\nWe are unable to lock in their position on sensors, but they are hailing us. Patching them through to [Root.Capital.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:678`

## country.1005.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acountry.1005.a&lang=en)

### 中文

打开通讯系统。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:657`

### English

Open comms.

## country.1010.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acountry.1010.name&lang=en)

### 中文

首席科学官

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:658`

### English

Head of Research

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:680`

## country.1010.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acountry.1010.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetRulerTitle]，鄙人向您传达我们诚挚的问候。\n\n我估计你不是单纯来寒暄问候的吧？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:659`

### English

Greetings and salutations, [Root.GetRulerTitle].\n\nI take it this is not a social call?

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:681`

## country.1010.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acountry.1010.a&lang=en)

### 中文

这是什么意思？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:660`

### English

What is the meaning of this?

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:682`

## country.1010.a.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acountry.1010.a.response&lang=en)

### 中文

您应该想问我从[Root.GetName]上消失的事。\n\n我只是离开一小会，仅此而已。我在寻找真实之窗。但显然我并没找到。\n\n它也不在这里。我必须继续我的研究了。不过我可不会带着这些找到的无人机。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:661`

### English

I take it you are referring to my disappearance from [Root.GetName] space.\n\nI needed some time away, is all. Well. Rather, I am looking for the Window. And the Window is certainly not there.\n\nIt is not here either. I must continue my search. I will not be bringing these drones I found, though.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:683`

## country.1010.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acountry.1010.b&lang=en)

### 中文

我们可以派船护送你回家。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:662`

### English

We can assign ships to escort you home.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:684`

## country.1010.b.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acountry.1010.b.response&lang=en)

### 中文

我现在不想回家。\n\n但是，为了表示我的诚意，请收下这些无人机吧，作为我送给[Root.GetName]的礼物。这些无人机是我在这个星系的外围发现的。经过彻底修理过后，我才发现它们根本不适合用作研究探测。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:663`

### English

I have no interest in returning home at present.\n\nBut please, as a token of my goodwill, take these drones. A donation to the [Root.GetName]. I found them drifting on the outskirts of this system, and only after extensive repairs did I realize that they are utterly unfit to function as research probes.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:685`

## country.1010.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acountry.1010.c&lang=en)

### 中文

你必须马上返回首都。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:664`

### English

You will return to the capital at once.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:686`

## country.1010.c.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acountry.1010.c.response&lang=en)

### 中文

对不起，我的研究才刚刚开始。使命在召唤我，这是为了科学。\n\n但是，为了表示我的诚意，请收下这些无人机吧，作为我送给[Root.GetName]的礼物。这些无人机是我在这个星系的外围发现的。经过彻底修理过后，我才发现它们根本不适合用作研究探测。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:665`

### English

I think not, this is but the beginning. Duty calls, for science, and so on.\n\nBut please, as a token of my goodwill, take these drones. A donation to the [Root.GetName]. I found them drifting on the outskirts of this system, and only after extensive repairs did I realize that they are utterly unfit to function as research probes.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:687`

## country.1010.d

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acountry.1010.d&lang=en)

### 中文

对话结束了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:666`

### English

This conversation is over.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:688`

## country.1015.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acountry.1015.name&lang=en)

### 中文

浪迹星涯：无人机

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:667`

### English

Wanderlust: The Drones

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:689`

## country.1015.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acountry.1015.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

首席再次神秘地消失了。\n\n不过他们的确留下了一些无人机。这些无人机装备了武器，而且正在启动！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:668`

### English

The Chair has vanished, inexplicably, yet again.\n\nHowever, they did leave a number of drones behind, as promised. The drones are armed, and their weapons are powering up!

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:690`

## country.1020.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acountry.1020.name&lang=en)

### 中文

浪迹星涯：毁坏的无人机

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:669`

### English

Wanderlust: The Drones Destroyed

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:691`

## country.1020.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acountry.1020.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这些无人机的敌我识别系统的模组被拆除了，应该是首席干的。不过它们的威胁评估处理器还完好无损。由于此人的破坏行为，威胁等级有些升高。\n\n首席科学官不像是想让它们攻击我们，但是它们很好地掩护了首席，让其成功逃走了。\n\n查看情报日志的§Y特殊项目§!，以获得首席最后一次出现的位置。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:670`

### English

The drones' IFF modules had been removed, likely a result of the Head Researcher's repairs, but their threat assessment processors remained intact and somewhat stressed by the Head's invasive procedures.\n\nIt is unlikely that the head meant for the drones to attack us, but fact remains that they covered the scientist's escape nicely.\n\nConsult the Situation Log for a §YSpecial Project§! to track the Head from their last known location.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:692`

## country.1022.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acountry.1022.name&lang=en)

### 中文

浪迹星涯：无人机被击毁

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:671`

### English

Wanderlust: The Drones Defeated

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:693`

## country.1022.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acountry.1022.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

科学家留下的无人机似乎被某个人或者是某个东西击毁了。\n\n我们应该抓住这个机会，寻找有关首席的线索。\n\n一个§Y特殊项目§!已经添加到情报日志中。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:672`

### English

It seems the drones left behind by the Researcher have been destroyed, by something or someone.\n\nWe should seize this opportunity to pick up the Head's trail.\n\nA §YSpecial Project§! has been added to the Situation Log.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:694`

## country.1025.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acountry.1025.name&lang=en)

### 中文

浪迹星涯：特殊项目已完成

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:673`

### English

Wanderlust: Special Project Completed

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:695`

## country.1025.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acountry.1025.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们已经破译了首席留下的科研船上的能源信息，可以再度对其展开追踪。\n\n查看情报日志以获得更多细节。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:674`

### English

Having decoded the intricate web of energy signatures left behind by the Chair's elusive Science Ship, we are on their trail once again.\n\nConsult the Situation Log for details.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:696`

## country.1030.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acountry.1030.name&lang=en)

### 中文

浪迹星涯：遇上麻烦了

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:675`

### English

Wanderlust: In Trouble

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:697`

## country.1030.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acountry.1030.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

首席的科研船神出鬼没，侦测起来相当困难，但这还不是最难的。\n\n传感器上显示的数据令人不解，上面显示首席的船两侧各有一艘虎视眈眈的护卫舰。\n\n首席物理学家正在向我们致意。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:676`

### English

The ephemeral Science Ship remains difficult to detect, but apparently not difficult enough.\n\nThe blur of confused sensor readings that we have come to associate with the Head's ship is flanked on both sides by a pair of rather nasty-looking corvettes.\n\nThe Physicist Head is hailing us.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:698`

## country.1035.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acountry.1035.name&lang=en)

### 中文

首席科学官

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:677`

### English

Head of Research

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:699`

## country.1035.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acountry.1035.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetRulerTitle]，您好。\n\n请允许我向[Root.GetSpeciesName]的舰队致以热烈欢迎。恐怕我的突然出现激怒了这个星系的外星生物，现在他们的飞船把我包围了。\n\n如果您能帮忙弥补一下我的失礼行为，那就再好不过了。事情解决后，我会很乐意与你们分享我的探险经历。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:678`

### English

Greetings, [Root.GetRulerTitle].\n\n[Root.GetSpeciesName] ships are a most welcome sight. I fear my own sudden appearance in this system may have angered the alien beings aboard the ships that now surround mine.\n\nAny assistance in rectifying whatever faux pas I may have committed would be most appreciated. I will gladly share with you the nature of my expedition once this matter is settled.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:700`

## country.1035.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acountry.1035.a&lang=en)

### 中文

你为什么要跑？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:679`

### English

Why did you run?

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:701`

## country.1035.a.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acountry.1035.a.response&lang=en)

### 中文

跑？我只是离开而已。\n\n[Root.GetRulerTitle]，我知道你们都不太喜欢我的行为。但我不能让[Root.GetSpeciesName]科学界没完没了的派别斗争危害到我的研究。\n\n而且，有些研究也需要实地考察。一直如此。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:680`

### English

Run? I left.\n\nI realize you are none too pleased with my actions, [Root.GetRulerTitle], but I could not risk my work being mired in the petty politics of the [Root.GetSpeciesName] scientific community.\n\nBesides, I had some research to do in the field. Still do.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:702`

## country.1035.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acountry.1035.b&lang=en)

### 中文

先跟我们讲讲关于真实之窗的事。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:681`

### English

Tell us more about this Window, first.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:703`

## country.1035.b.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acountry.1035.b.response&lang=en)

### 中文

在这种节骨眼上，我不太方便讲述细节。我不太想，嗯，让自己看起来像个傻瓜。万一最后证明我是错的呢？\n\n但是我们也许最后都会变成全息影像。这又是否值得呢？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:682`

### English

I would be loath to go into details at this juncture. Would not, ah, want to look the fool. Should it turn out that I am mistaken.\n\nBut we may all turn out to be holograms in the end. Would it not be worth it?

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:704`

## country.1035.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acountry.1035.c&lang=en)

### 中文

我们会帮你摆平局面的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:683`

### English

We will try to get you out of this mess.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:705`

## country.1040.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acountry.1040.name&lang=en)

### 中文

阳炎海盗

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:684`

### English

Sunblaster Pirates

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:706`

## country.1040.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acountry.1040.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

滚开！是我们先找到这艘破烂幽灵船的。\n\n谁先看见就是谁的，不是谁先登船就是谁的。它是我们的，不是你们的。而且它的舰长还死了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:685`

### English

Get lost! We found the rotten ghost-ship first.\n\nOnce we figure out how to see it proper-like, it's as good as boarded. And ours. Not yours. And its captain dead.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:707`

## country.1040.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acountry.1040.a&lang=en)

### 中文

外星人渣，敢跟我们结梁子，你早晚要后悔。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:686`

### English

You will rue the day you crossed us, alien scum.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:708`

## country.1040.a.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acountry.1040.a.response&lang=en)

### 中文

真是丑人多作怪。野蛮人，这不是我们第一次交锋了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:687`

### English

Big words from someone so ugly. This isn't our first rodeo, bucko.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:709`

## country.1040.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acountry.1040.b&lang=en)

### 中文

用150能量币换取飞船。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:688`

### English

150 Energy Credits for the ship.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:710`

## country.1040.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acountry.1040.c&lang=en)

### 中文

武器预备。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:689`

### English

Ready weapons.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:711`

## country.1040.d

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acountry.1040.d&lang=en)

### 中文

现在滚还能留你一条活路。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:690`

### English

Leave now and you may yet live.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:712`

## country.1040.d.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acountry.1040.d.response&lang=en)

### 中文

虚张声势！丑八怪，这可不是你的领地。\n这些星星是阳炎海盗的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:691`

### English

Empty threats! You are a long way from home, ugly.\nThe Sunblaster Pirates own these stars.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:713`

## country.1041.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acountry.1041.name&lang=en)

### 中文

阳炎海盗

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:692`

### English

Sunblaster Pirates

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:714`

## country.1041.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acountry.1041.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

150能量币？这价格估计都算不上家猪用剩的一点小零头，真狡猾。但是……\n\n哎，这艘混账幽灵船带来的麻烦比它的好处还要多。\n\n弟兄们！我们走！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:693`

### English

150 Energy Credits? Hardly a drop in the domesti-beast's piss-bucket, ugly. But...\n\nBah, the filthy ghost-ship has been more trouble than it's worth anyway.\n\nSkitskur'r! We out!

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:715`

## country.1041.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acountry.1041.a&lang=en)

### 中文

祈祷最好别再遇上我们吧，可怜虫。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:694`

### English

Pray that we do not meet again, degenerates.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:716`

## country.1041.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acountry.1041.b&lang=en)

### 中文

一切都是浮云，物质财富更是如此。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:695`

### English

All things are fleeting, material wealth especially so.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:717`

## country.1041.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acountry.1041.c&lang=en)

### 中文

金钱是万能的。如果做不到，说明你钱不够多。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:696`

### English

Money talks. Sometimes you have to raise your voice.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:718`

## country.1041.d

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acountry.1041.d&lang=en)

### 中文

请离开。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:697`

### English

Please leave.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:719`

## country.1042.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acountry.1042.name&lang=en)

### 中文

阳炎海盗

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:698`

### English

Sunblaster Pirates

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:720`

## country.1042.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acountry.1042.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这些傻子需要好好学学。\n\n弟兄们！拿起武器！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:699`

### English

The fool needs to be taught a lesson.\n\nSkitskur'r! Weapons!

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:721`

## country.1042.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acountry.1042.a&lang=en)

### 中文

你们会见识到[Root.GetSpeciesNamePlural]军团的厉害。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:700`

### English

You will know the military might of the [Root.GetSpeciesNamePlural].

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:722`

## country.1044.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acountry.1044.name&lang=en)

### 中文

首席科学官

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:701`

### English

Head of Research

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:723`

## country.1044.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acountry.1044.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我忍不住听到了谈话中一些只言片语——毕竟他们是在开放频段上广播的。\n\n我想交易进行得还算顺利。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:702`

### English

I couldn't help but catch bits and pieces of that conversation - they were broadcasting on an open channel, after all.\n\nI gather the transaction went smoothly.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:724`

## country.1045.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acountry.1045.name&lang=en)

### 中文

首席科学官

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:703`

### English

Head of Research

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:725`

## country.1045.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acountry.1045.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetRulerTitle]，我看到您一下子就除掉了那些海盗。\n\n作为一名科学家，我痛恨争斗……但我也深知在那种情形下这是必需的。如果我落入那些海盗之手，想必待遇不会太好。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:704`

### English

I see you made short work of the pirates, [Root.GetRulerTitle].\nAs a scientist, I abhor violence... But recognize that it may have been necessary in this case. I strongly suspect I would have been treated less than kindly at the hands of those pirates.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:726`

## country.1045.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acountry.1045.a&lang=en)

### 中文

确实如此。现在有话快说吧。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:705`

### English

Indeed. Now, speak.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:727`

## country.1045.a.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acountry.1045.a.response&lang=en)

### 中文

噢，好的。\n\n就是……我不用细枝末节来烦扰您了，[Root.GetRulerTitle]，但是有一种观念，一切皆为虚妄。或者说我们所认知的真实其实并非如此，至少并非完全如此，甚至比其他事物更接近于虚妄。这也就是说，这一切——包括我，[Root.GetRulerTitle]您自己，以及我们认为是不可动摇的宇宙法则——都只不过是光的把戏而已。这个宇宙不过是镜花水月，是映像，抑或是全息投影，或是棱镜。终究只不过是散射了真正现实的迷人光线罢了。\n\n明白了吗？这些光一定是从某个地方来的。从真实之窗来的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:706`

### English

Oh yes, of course.\nThere is... I will not bore you with the details [Root.GetRulerTitle], but there is this idea, if you will, that nothing is real. Or that what we think is real is not, that it is less real, less so than something else. That this, this is all - I, you yourself [Root.GetRulerTitle], the very laws of nature we consider absolute - nothing but tricks of the light. The universe as a reflection, hologram, or prism, simply breaking the brilliant rays of the truly real.\n\nSee? The light has to come from somewhere. Through the Window.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:728`

## country.1045.b

你必须马上回到[Root.Capital.GetName]。

### English

## country.1046.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acountry.1046.name&lang=en)

### 中文

首席科学官

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:708`

### English

Head of Research

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:730`

## country.1046.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acountry.1046.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetRulerTitle]，您心里很清楚我是不会回去的。\n\n事实上，我也考虑过其他的可能性。我想我短时间内很可能无法回去了。\n\n我手头的所有研究数据，只要我觉得对[Root.GetSpeciesName]的人们有用，我就会上传到[Root.Capital.GetName]的数据库中。但是和真实之窗有关的数据我会自己保留。现在我觉得自己还没有准备好。不过显然，没人准备好。\n\n我得走了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:709`

### English

You know I will not, [Root.GetRulerTitle].\nIn fact, having had some time to consider additional possibilities, I believe there is a significant risk I will not be able to return within a reasonable timeframe.\n\nI have taken the liberty of uploading whatever research data I have that may benefit the [Root.GetSpeciesName] people to the databases on [Root.Capital.GetName]. The data related to the Window stays with me, though. I am starting to think I am not ready; certainly no one else is.\nI will be going now.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:731`

### English

## country.1047.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acountry.1047.name&lang=en)

### 中文

浪迹星涯：瞎子

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:711`

### English

Wanderlust: The Blinds

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:733`

## country.1047.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acountry.1047.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

简单查看了一下，首席的确说到做到。[Root.GetName]的物理学研究整体得到了小幅度的提升。\n\n科学家又一次消失了，但是这一次，这个人的行踪在离开这个星系之前就被我们侦测到了。\n\n如果他们想去寻找这个真实之窗，那么我们也会去。\n查看情报日志以获得更多细节。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:712`

### English

A quick check confirms the Head's claims, and the [Root.GetName] has enjoyed a small but not insignificant boost to our collective body of physics research.\n\nThe Researcher has vanished once again, but this time their course had been divined before they were even out of the system.\n\nIf they are headed for this Window, then so are we.\nConsult the Situation Log for details.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:734`

## country.1050.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acountry.1050.name&lang=en)

### 中文

浪迹星涯：真实之窗开启

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:713`

### English

Wanderlust: The Window Opens

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:735`

## country.1050.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acountry.1050.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

科研船原型机第一次清楚地出现在了我们的传感器上。它以极近的距离围绕着这个星系的恒星运转，不过其他地方并没有什么特别的。\n\n只需简单的光学扫描，这艘船的秘密就暴露无遗。它的船身设计十分普通，没有什么独到之处。上面有许多十字形的金属网格，像是交流电压控制下的电磁铁。只要足够接近恒星，飞船就能稍微扭曲恒星发出的射线，将它们缠绕于自身附近，把自己伪装起来。\n\n这个科技理论上听起来不错，但是实际应用中却有一些明显的问题。由于暴露在恒星的辐射中太久，它的整套设配都已经被烤焦而且变得毫无用处。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:714`

### English

For the first time, the prototype Science Ship is plainly visible on sensors. It is orbiting dangerously close to the system's star, but appears otherwise unremarkable.\n\nA simple optical scan reveals the ship's secret. Its hull is a standard design altered without much subtlety, now crisscrossed by a lattice of metal filaments likely acting as electromagnets at alternating, tightly-controlled voltages. By staying close to a star the ship can weakly warp its stellar emissions, wrapping them around itself as a form of camouflage.\n\nWhile conceptually interesting, the technique has obvious practical drawbacks, as evidenced by the entire set-up now having been fried and rendered useless by prolonged exposure to solar radiation.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:736`

## country.1051.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acountry.1051.name&lang=en)

首席科学官

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:715`

### English

Head of Research

## country.1051.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acountry.1051.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetRulerTitle]，您好。\n\n我有十足的理由相信你们将会继续追随我们。\n\n所以我采取了一些措施。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:716`

### English

Greetings, [Root.GetRulerTitle].\n\nIt seemed entirely reasonable to assume that you would follow, again.\n\nSo I did.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:738`

## country.1051.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acountry.1051.a&lang=en)

### 中文

首席科学官，你想做什么？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:717`

### English

What now, Head Researcher?

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:739`

## country.1051.a.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acountry.1051.a.response&lang=en)

### 中文

我想我已经找到真实之窗了。至少是一扇真实之窗。也许还会有更多。说不定我们的真身就在那里。说不定所有东西的真身就在那里。不得不承认，我脑子里一点概念也没有。我知道我获得的这些知识也没办法造福什么人。但是如果我就此打住的话，那也太不负责任了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:718`

### English

I have found the Window, I think. A window, at least. There may be more. Do our true selves lie beyond? Does anything? I must confess that I haven't the faintest idea. I recognize that no one else may very well benefit from whatever knowledge I gain. But to stop here would be utterly irresponsible.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:740`

## country.1051.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acountry.1051.b&lang=en)

### 中文

你想怎么做？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:719`

### English

What will you do?

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:741`

## country.1051.b.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acountry.1051.b.response&lang=en)

### 中文

我会试着穿过真实之窗，前往真实。显然，来自真实的光不单单只是通过这里。整个宇宙就像是一大块玻璃窗，我们只是其中的一小块扭曲，一小块瑕疵而已。我们的生命来源于我们之上的那种光辉。\n\n这就是真实之窗，我相信它会开启的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:720`

### English

I will attempt to pass through the Window, into reality. The light of the real does not solely shine through here, of course. The entire universe is like a vast pane of glass, and we are the minute warps and imperfections in it, given life by the brilliance beyond.\n\nThis is the Window, because I believe it opens.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:742`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acountry.1051.c&lang=en)

### English

Your actions are harebrained and dangerous.

## country.1052.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acountry.1052.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我的行为带来了诸多麻烦，为此我深感抱歉，[Root.GetRulerTitle]。但是，我不欠[Root.GetName]任何东西，对他们我已经仁至义尽。现在我必须完成我的使命了。您凭着强大的毅力一路跟来，我对您的帮助非常感激，但是从一开始我就已经下定决心，不会改变主意。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:723`

### English

I truly regret all the trouble I have caused, [Root.GetRulerTitle], I do. However, I owe nothing to the [Root.GetName], I have paid my dues in full and of this I am sure. You came after me of your own volition, and I am grateful for your help, but I made my intentions clear at the outset.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:745`

## country.1055.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acountry.1055.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

科研船是空的。\n\n所有用于舱外行动的衣服都好好地放在原处。飞船日志显示气闸从未向外打开过。\n\n飞船还在围绕恒星运行，但现在受到[Root.Owner.GetSpeciesAdj]的控制。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:730`

### English

The Science Ship is empty.\n\nAll suits for extra-vehicular activity remain in their pods, and the ship logs state that the airlock has never been exit-cycled.\n\nThe ship still orbits the star, but is now under [Root.Owner.GetSpeciesAdj] control.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:752`

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:734`

## country.1100.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acountry.1100.name&lang=en)

### 中文

大灭绝

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:736`

### English

Mass Extinction

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:760`

## country.1100.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acountry.1100.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们向§Y[Root.Capital.GetName]§!之上的虚空迈出的第一步并不单单只是受到好奇心或征服欲的驱使，更是一种必要之举。\n\n§Y[Root.Capital.GetName]§!的生物圈经历了剧烈的变化。科学家们警告大灭绝将很有可能发生，这种周期性的生物灭亡是这个宇宙中行星生物的自然规律。\n\n我们制定了一个研究项目，对象是几颗因重大天文灾害而变得不宜居行星。通过还原这些灾难性事件，我们预期可以避免§Y[Root.Capital.GetName]§!和§Y[Root.GetSpeciesName]§!一族重蹈覆辙。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:737`

### English

Our first steps into the void beyond §Y[Root.Capital.GetName]§! were driven not just by curiosity or conquest but by necessity.\n\nThe biosphere of §Y[Root.Capital.GetName]§! has rapidly changed, with scientists warning of a potential mass extinction event, a cyclical purging of life inherent to planetary existence in the cosmos.\n\nA research plan has been developed to study planets rendered uninhabitable by major astronomical incidents. By reconstructing these events, we hope to avoid a similar fate for §Y[Root.Capital.GetName]§! and the §Y[Root.GetSpeciesName]§! race.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:761`

## country.1100.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acountry.1100.a&lang=en)

### 中文

为了[Root.GetSpeciesAdj]的未来。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:738`

### English

For a [Root.GetSpeciesAdj] future.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:762`

### English

## country.1105.name

大灭绝：崩坏的星球

### English

Mass Extinction: Broken Planet

## country.1105.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acountry.1105.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的天文学家们十分确信，这个星系的小行星带曾经是一颗宜居的行星，但是却被某种未知的力量击碎了。\n\n已发起§Y特殊项目§!来进一步研究小行星带中的主要天体。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:741`

### English

Our astronomers are confident that the asteroid belt in this system was once a habitable planet, torn asunder by unknown forces.\n\nA §YSpecial Project§! has been issued to study one of the major asteroids in the belt.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:765`

## country.1110.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acountry.1110.name&lang=en)

### 中文

大灭绝：烧焦的星球

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:742`

### English

Mass Extinction: Burned Planet

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:766`

## country.1110.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acountry.1110.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在围绕这颗恒星运转的行星中，其中一颗还清晰地留有灾难的痕迹。这颗行星的地表被半融未融的矿物海洋所覆盖着，大气炽热无比。然而[Root.GetSpeciesAdj]天文学家们却声称这里可能曾经出现过生命。\n\n已发起§Y特殊项目§!来进一步研究这颗炽热的行星。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:743`

### English

One of the planets orbiting this star bears the still-open wounds of some past calamity. Its surface is a roiling sea of half-melted minerals boiling into a superheated atmosphere, but [Root.GetSpeciesAdj] astronomers claim that it may once have supported life.\n\nA §YSpecial Project§! has been issued to study the molten planet.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:767`

## country.1115.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acountry.1115.name&lang=en)

### 中文

大灭绝：荒芜的星球

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:744`

### English

Mass Extinction: Desolate Planet

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:768`

## country.1115.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acountry.1115.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在这个星系里，有一颗行星以往生态圈所留下的化石痕迹依然清晰可见。是什么把欣欣向荣的生物圈变成了一片不毛之地？\n\n已发起§Y特殊项目§!来研究这一灾难性转变的成因。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:745`

### English

A planet in this system still wears the fossilized traces of a past ecology on its sleeve. What could turn a thriving biosphere into a blasted wasteland?\n\nA §YSpecial Project§! has been issued to trace the source of this disastrous transformation.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:769`

## country.1120.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acountry.1120.name&lang=en)

### 中文

大灭绝：剧毒的星球

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:746`

### English

Mass Extinction: Noxious Planet

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:770`

## country.1120.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acountry.1120.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

据称，这颗行星上的动植物会释放有毒气体使其他物种窒息，或许这是地壳之下在不断涌出的活性气体造成的。\n\n已发起§Y特殊项目§!来研究这一现象。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:747`

### English

Allegedly this planet's flora and fauna choked on their own poisonous emissions, likely helped along by the release of reactive gases from beneath the planet's crust.\n\nA §YSpecial Project§! has been issued to investigate the matter.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:771`

## country.1125.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acountry.1125.name&lang=en)

### 中文

大灭绝：寒冷的星球

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:748`

### English

Mass Extinction: Chilly Planet

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:772`

## country.1125.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acountry.1125.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这个星系中有一颗奇怪的行星，星球表面被一层又一层成分各异的寒冰所覆盖。可推测，这颗星球曾有过种类繁杂的生物群。\n\n已发起§Y特殊项目§!来调查这一可能性。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:749`

### English

The planet of interest in this system is covered in layer upon layer of dense ices of varying composition. Apparently it was once home to more varied and pleasant biomes.\n\nA §YSpecial Project§! has been issued to investigate this possibility.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:773`

## country.1130.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acountry.1130.name&lang=en)

### 中文

大灭绝：小行星

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:750`

### English

Mass Extinction: Asteroid

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:774`

## country.1130.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acountry.1130.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这颗小星球其实曾是一颗宜居——或许甚至有人居住过——的行星的一部分。其破裂形态富有规律但又相互重叠，可以看出这颗星球的毁灭是预先设计好的，尽管实施的时可能有些匆忙。\n\n所有的迹象都表明，这颗星球曾是某个物种的母星，但这个物种最后又摧毁了它。我们可能永远都不会知道是什么驱使它们做出毁灭星球这种行为，但是我们也许该为此感到庆幸。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:751`

### English

The asteroid is in actuality a chunk of what was once a habitable, and probably inhabited, planet. Regular but overlapping fracture patterns indicate that the planetary demolition was intentional and controlled, if somewhat rushed.\n\nAll signs point to this planet being the homeworld of whatever species destroyed it. We may never know what drove them to consider planetary annihilation to be the lesser evil, and perhaps we should be grateful for that.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:775`

## country.1135.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acountry.1135.name&lang=en)

### 中文

大灭绝：熔融

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:752`

### English

Mass Extinction: Molten

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:776`

## country.1135.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acountry.1135.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这颗星球的悲惨命运可以归结于宇宙的喜怒无常。\n\n我们科学家认为，一个由短暂时空异常导致的引力扰动，迫使这颗星球的轨道变得过于接近恒星。尽管它在缓慢地向自己的原有轨道作螺旋式运动，但仍是不断受到恒星的余热炙烤。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:753`

### English

The unfortunate fate of the planet can be laid squarely at the feet of cosmic whims.\n\nA gravitational disturbance, which our scientists suspect was a transient space-time anomaly, forced the planet from its natural orbit to one much too close to the sun. Although the planet is slowly spiraling back towards its original orbit, it has been cooking in the residual heat from the star ever since.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:777`

## country.1140.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acountry.1140.name&lang=en)

### 中文

大灭绝：荒芜

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:754`

### English

Mass Extinction: Barren

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:778`

## country.1140.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acountry.1140.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

对这颗荒芜的星球上曾存在过的生命，它们的命运从一开始就注定了。\n\n主体物种甚至还来不及演化出智能、发明农业，地理机制就已经让土壤严重酸化了。\n\n从土壤下层和化石遗骸中提取的样品表明，对土壤再生极为关键的矿物质被当地生物的内骨骼消耗殆尽。\n\n它们的骨骼会完全石化而非腐坏，它们光是存在就已经耗竭了脆弱的生态系统，使其发生了永久性的转变。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:755`

### English

Life on the now-barren planet was doomed from the start.\n\nThe dominant species barely had time to awaken to sapience and invent agriculture before geological mechanisms caused the earth to sour.\n\nSamples from lower soil strata and fossilized remains show that vital minerals needed to rejuvenate the soil ended up in the endoskeletons of the native beings.\n\nTheir bones fossilized instead of decaying, and their existence exhausted the fragile ecosystem, leaving it permanently transformed.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:779`

## country.1145.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acountry.1145.name&lang=en)

### 中文

大灭绝：剧毒

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:756`

### English

Mass Extinction: Toxic

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:780`

## country.1145.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acountry.1145.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们一度以为行星地壳上的千沟万壑是由地质活动塑造的，然而它们实际上却是破土而入的巨大根茎所遗留下来的永恒伤疤。\n\n在过去的某一个时间点，一种可能由智慧物种引发的变异导致当地的植物开始失控疯长。在下层大气环流的帮助下，许多这样的植物借助花粉进行繁殖。\n\n这种影响起先看似轻微，却愈演愈烈，大气层中愈发浓密的颗粒物最终在几个月内将星球上所有的动物呛死了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:757`

### English

The cracks in the planet's crust, initially thought to be caused by tectonic activity, are actually lasting marks of enormous roots burrowing through the earth.\n\nAt some point in the past, a mutation, possibly induced by sapient beings, caused the native plant life to grow uncontrollably. Many of these plants reproduce through pollination, with countless gametes carried by currents in the lower atmosphere.\n\nWhat was once a mild irritation escalated, as the increase in atmosphere-borne particles choked the life out of all animals on the planet within months.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:781`

## country.1150.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acountry.1150.name&lang=en)

### 中文

大灭绝：冰封

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:758`

### English

Mass Extinction: Frozen

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:782`

## country.1150.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acountry.1150.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

大气成分的一次异常变化让这颗星球慢慢地进入了冰川期，这一过程早于[Root.Owner.GetName]的形成而且可能在我们都不在了以后仍将继续下去。\n\n造成大气变化的确切原因尚不清楚，但是存在几种假说，例如环境改造失败或行星内恐怖主义活动等等。[Root.Owner.Capital.GetName]科学共同体内的一些激进分子甚至认为，这种急剧的气候变化可能是大肆排放工业废气导致的。\n\n只有少数科学家赞同这一观点。毕竟一个物种如果有能力完全实现工业化，又怎么会傻到一不小心就把自己给毁灭了呢？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:759`

### English

An unnatural shift in the atmosphere's chemical make-up resulted in the slow onset of an ice age, beginning before the formation of [Root.Owner.GetName] and likely persisting long after our passing.\n\nThe exact cause is unknown, but possible explanations include failed terraforming attempts or intra-planetary terrorism. Some radical elements within the scientific community on [Root.Owner.Capital.GetName] suggest that the climate shift may have been caused by the unchecked emission of industrial by-products.\n\nThis view is confined to the fringe, as it is unlikely any race intelligent enough for industrialization would be foolish enough to wipe themselves out.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:783`

## country.1150.desc.industrialist

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acountry.1150.desc.industrialist&lang=en)

### 中文

大气层化学成分非自然剧变导致这个星球缓慢却不可避免地进入了冰川期——这一时期在[Root.Owner.GetName]建立之前就已经开始，而且很有可能在我们灭亡之后还未结束。\n\n因为我们自己的行星上发生过的工业化经历，我们对其原因感到相当熟悉：向大气层无休止地排放工业废气导致了剧烈的气候变化，而且他们的科技还没发展到能够解决这一问题的程度。\n\n这一结论引起了我们科学界的一定关注，尽管多数人指出我们的科技“大概”已经发达到足以避免这种灭绝事件的程度了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:760`

### English

An unnatural shift in the atmosphere's chemical make-up resulted in the slow but irreversible onset of an ice age - one that began before the formation of the [Root.Owner.GetName] and is likely to persist long after our passing.\n\nThe cause appears familiar from our experience of industrialization on our own planets: the unchecked emission of gaseous industrial by-product into the atmosphere caused dramatic climatic shifts which they lacked the technology to counteract.\n\nThis conclusion has caused some concern among our scientific community, although most point out that at our technological level such an extinction event could "probably" be avoided.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:784`

## country.1155.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acountry.1155.name&lang=en)

### 中文

大灭绝：研究已完成

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:761`

### English

Mass Extinction: Study Complete

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:785`

## country.1155.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acountry.1155.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这次科研探险我们得到了许多意料之外的收获。[Root.GetSpeciesAdj]科学家现在掌握了空前庞大的数据，能够为[Root.Capital.GetName]的未来建立精确的模型。\n\n最初的报告显示，刨除诡异的宇宙活动和[Root.GetName]的突然崩溃，[Root.Capital.GetName]至少在百年内都不会真正遇到危险。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:762`

### English

While the research expedition often yielded unexpected results, [Root.GetSpeciesAdj] scientists now have access to an unprecedented wealth of data with which to accurately model the future of [Root.Capital.GetName].\n\nInitial reports suggest that [Root.Capital.GetName] will be in no real danger for at least a dozen generations, save from freak cosmic events or the violent collapse of the [Root.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:786`

## country.1155.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acountry.1155.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们还能从这些行星上学到什么呢？

### English

What else can we learn from these planets?

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:787`

### English

The [Root.GetSpeciesName] people should be put at ease.

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acountry.1155.c&lang=en)

把这些行星数据公之于众。

Make the planetary data public.

## country.1156.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acountry.1156.name&lang=en)

### 中文

大灭绝：调查已完成

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:766`

### English

Mass Extinction: Surveyed

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:790`

## country.1156.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acountry.1156.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

尽管这份数据对所有的星系都一概而论，未免有失偏颇，但通过对个别行星展开研究，[Root.GetName]能够推断出其所在星系的调查数据。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:767`

### English

While the data regarding the star systems as a whole was somewhat spotty, the detailed studies of individual planets has allowed the [Root.GetName] to extrapolate complete Survey data for the systems in question.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:791`

## country.1156.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acountry.1156.a&lang=en)

### 中文

赞。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:768`

### English

Fantastic.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:792`

## country.1157.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acountry.1157.name&lang=en)

### 中文

大灭绝：相信未来

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:769`

### English

Mass Extinction: Future Faith

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:793`

## country.1157.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acountry.1157.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

随着越来越多有关研究的消息通过媒体传入民众的耳朵，人们越发变得焦躁不安。现在我们可以一次性打消他们所有的疑虑了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:770`

### English

The populace has been growing increasingly restless as news of the study slowly filtered down through the media. Now, we have the chance to dispel all their doubts and fears in one fell swoop.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:794`

## country.1157.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acountry.1157.a&lang=en)

### 中文

母星安全了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:771`

### English

The homeworld is safe.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:795`

## country.1157.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acountry.1157.b&lang=en)
