这是[Root.GetRulerTitle]领导下又一次成功的行动。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:772`

### English

Another successful operation by the [Root.GetRulerTitle].

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:796`

## country.1158.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acountry.1158.name&lang=en)

### 中文

大灭绝：传播信息

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:773`

### English

Mass Extinction: Information Dissemination

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:797`

## country.1158.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acountry.1158.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

科研探险中收集到的数据对[Root.GetName]来说弥足珍贵。因此，数据的使用也受到严格管控，以防某些欠缺考虑的事情发生。\n\n研究现已结束。为了科学的发展，很多人呼吁公开相关数据。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:774`

### English

The information gleaned by the research expedition is the property of the [Root.GetName] and access to it is restricted; a precaution put in place early on to prevent premature and unfavorable conclusions.\n\nWith the study now complete, there is a strong lobby for releasing all associated data to the wider scientific community.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:798`

## country.1158.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acountry.1158.a&lang=en)

### 中文

很好。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:775`

### English

Very well.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:799`

## story.1.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.1.name&lang=en)

### 中文

接触报告：简单生命

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:779`

### English

Contact Report: Simple Forms of Life

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:805`

## story.1.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.1.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetName]新闻满天飞，内容都是关于不久前发现的外星生物。\n\n尽管它离智慧生物还有很大差距，但至少说明外星是有生命存在的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:780`

### English

The [Root.GetName] is abuzz with news of the alien organisms discovered some time ago.\n\nWhile far from intelligent, there is life out there.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:806`

## story.1.desc.phobe

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.1.desc.phobe&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetName]新闻满天飞，内容都是关于不久前发现的外星生物。\n\n尽管它离智慧生物还有很大差距，但至少说明外星是有生命存在的。这是一个无法回避的现实，人们开始担心未来。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:781`

### English

The [Root.GetName] is abuzz with news of the alien organisms discovered some time ago.\n\nWhile far from intelligent, there is life out there. An inescapable reality, and a deeply unsettling prospect.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:807`

## story.1.desc.phile

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.1.desc.phile&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetName]新闻满天飞，内容都是关于不久前发现的外星生物。\n\n这一生命的奇迹再次使[Root.GetSpeciesAdj]的人们对第一次接触其他智慧生物满怀期待。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:782`

### English

The [Root.GetName] is abuzz with news of the alien organisms discovered some time ago.\n\nThese little evolutionary marvels kindle in the [Root.GetSpeciesAdj] people a renewed hope of first contact with intelligent beings.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:808`

## story.1.desc.spiri

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.1.desc.spiri&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetName]新闻满天飞，内容都是关于不久前发现的外星生物。\n\n自从在[Root.Capital.GetName]之外发现了造物的奇迹，[Root.GetSpeciesAdj]神秘学家们不断举办神秘仪式，试图得到更多的答案。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:783`

### English

The [Root.GetName] is abuzz with news of the alien organisms discovered some time ago.\n\n[Root.GetSpeciesAdj] theologians have been partaking in arcane rites since this first sign that divine providence extends outside of [Root.Capital.GetName], attempting to find new answers.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:809`

## story.1.desc.gesta

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.1.desc.gesta&lang=en)

### 中文

我们确认了[Root.Capital.GetName]的生物圈之外存在低等有机生命。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:784`

### English

We have confirmed the existence of lower forms of organic life outside of [Root.Capital.GetName]'s biosphere.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:810`

## story.1.desc.term

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.1.desc.term&lang=en)

### 中文

检测到有机异形微生物。已按情况调整存在有机智慧异形概率的算法模型。如果他们真的存在，他们将会被发现。他们将会被消灭。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:785`

### English

Alien microbes of an organic nature have been detected. Models for the probability of organic alien sapients have been adjusted accordingly. If they exist, they will be found. They will be exterminated.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:811`

## story.1.desc.evopred

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.1.desc.evopred&lang=en)

### 中文

我们并不孤独。[Root.GetName]第一次确定了宇宙中有其他生命的存在；微生物、外星生命，但[Root.GetHomeWorldName]并不是唯一一个演化的舞台。\n\n一种莫名的饥渴从长久的沉眠中醒来，在我们的体内躁动不安地搅动。而我们的猎弓再度被拉开了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:786`

### English

We are not alone. For the first time, the [Root.GetName] has definitive proof that there is other life in the universe; microscopic, alien life, but [Root.GetHomeWorldName] was not the only planet where evolution occurred.\n\nAn instinctive hunger, long dormant, begins to stir within us. Once again, we hunt.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:812`

## story.2.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.2.name&lang=en)

### 中文

接触报告：深空住民

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:787`

### English

Contact Report: Deep Space Dwellers

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:814`

## story.2.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.2.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetName]新闻满天飞，内容都是关于不久前发现的外星生物。\n\n这些生物似乎居住在深空之中。相比之下，[Root.Capital.GetName]上的极端微生物简直不值一提。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:788`

### English

The [Root.GetName] is abuzz with news of the alien organisms encountered some time ago.\n\nSeemingly native to deep space, they make the extremophile creatures of [Root.Capital.GetName] appear frail by comparison.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:815`

## story.2.desc.phile

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.2.desc.phile&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetName]新闻满天飞，内容都是关于不久前发现的外星生物。\n\n这些生物似乎居住在深空之中。相比之下，[Root.Capital.GetName]上的极端微生物简直不值一提。尽管有些人持怀疑态度，但是遇见外星生命基本是意料之中的事情。虽然如此，但[Root.GetSpeciesAdj]外星生物学家仍然抑制不住激动，对这种生命体所承受的种种极端条件感到兴奋不已。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:789`

### English

The [Root.GetName] is abuzz with news of the alien organisms encountered some time ago.\n\nSeemingly native to deep space, they make the extremophile creatures of [Root.Capital.GetName] appear frail by comparison. While some doubted, encountering alien life was ultimately expected and anticipated - but nothing could have prepared expectant [Root.GetSpeciesAdj] xeno-biologists for the fascinatingly extreme conditions in which this life thrives.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:816`

## story.2.desc.followup

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.2.desc.followup&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetName]上的人们平静地接收了对异常外星生物的报告。\n\n真空中真的存在生命这一事实在[Root.Capital.GetName]的科学界引起了短暂的争议。不过在此之前已发现过低等生命，因此所有的[Root.GetSpeciesNamePlural]都并未感到震惊。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:790`

### English

The subjects of the [Root.GetName] receive the report of the encounter with new and unusual alien beings with relative calm.\n\nThe supposed vacuum habitat of the aliens sparked some short-lived controversy in certain circles of the scientific community on [Root.Capital.GetName], but the previous discovery of lower forms of life had largely prepared all [Root.GetSpeciesNamePlural] for the shock.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:817`

## story.2.desc.gesta

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.2.desc.gesta&lang=en)

### 中文

我们观测到了有机极端微生物在深空中遨游。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:791`

### English

We have observed extremophile organics navigating deep space.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:818`

## story.2.desc.evopred

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.2.desc.evopred&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetName]遭遇了太空有机体！我们的[GetScientistPlural]还不确定为什么体型如此庞大的生物能够在严苛的宇宙真空中央演化，也不知道这些极端环境中的生物可能拥有何种基因适应性。我们已经迫不及待想知道了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:792`

### English

The [Root.GetName] has encountered a space organism! Our [GetScientistPlural] do not yet know what caused such massive creatures to evolve in the harsh vacuum of space, nor what kind of genetic adaptations these extremophiles might possess. But we can't wait to find out.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:819`

## story.3.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.3.name&lang=en)

### 中文

接触报告：行星动物

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:793`

### English

Contact Report: Planetary Animalia

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:821`

## story.3.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.3.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetName]上新闻满天飞，内容都是关于发现的外星生物。\n\n尽管按照[Root.GetSpeciesAdj]标准来说，它们算不上是智慧生物，但它们的存在却否定了我们对生命的简单分类。它们提醒我们，宇宙是如此的复杂，蕴含了无尽的可能。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:794`

### English

The [Root.GetName] is abuzz with news of the alien life that was found.\n\nWhile hardly intelligent by [Root.GetSpeciesAdj] standards, the fascinating beings defy easy classification and hint at the immense complexities and possibilities of the universe.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:822`

## story.3.desc.spiri

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.3.desc.spiri&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.Capital.GetName]上的教士们普遍对找到外星有机体——虽然较为低等——一事表示了认可。\n\n不过这一发现也引发了对教义的争论——这是可作为[Root.GetSpeciesNamePlural]是万物之主的证明，又或者我们只是还没有在深空之外遇见我们的同胞？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:795`

### English

The priesthoods on [Root.Capital.GetName] had a generally favorable reaction to the reports of complex - yet dumb - organisms found on alien planets.\n\nThe findings did spark some inter-doctrinal debate - is this proof that the [Root.GetSpeciesNamePlural] are the lords of all within creation, or have we simply not yet encountered our brethren from beyond the void?

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:823`

## story.3.desc.followup

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.3.desc.followup&lang=en)

### 中文

在外星生物报告发布之后，[Root.GetSpeciesAdj]外星生物学家欣喜地发现这一复杂的生命体与[Root.Capital.GetName]上的并没有太大的不同。\n\n这一生命物的出现否定了我们对生命的简单分类，但是它除了纯粹的动物本能之外也没有什么特别之处。尽管如此，它却告诉我们，生命的某些形式是普遍存在的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:796`

### English

Following previous reports on alien life, [Root.GetSpeciesAdj] xeno-biologists are pleased to learn to complex life forms that appear not too different from what we would find on [Root.Capital.GetName].\n\nThe beings still defy easy classification and hardly possess anything more than purely animal instincts, but their existence does indicate that some patterns of life are indeed universal.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:824`

## story.3.desc.gesta

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.3.desc.gesta&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在外星星球上发现了动物。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:797`

### English

We have confirmed animal life on an alien world.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:825`

## story.3.desc.evopred

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.3.desc.evopred&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetName]发现了新的复杂生命形式！这些生物可能不具备完全的只能，但我们的文明对各种各样的染色体样品总是来者不拒。\n\n我们的[GetScientistPlural]已经着手计算需要多少数量的基因物质才足够让我们所有的人口都分一杯羹了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:798`

### English

The [Root.GetName] has discovered a new form of complex life! These creatures may not possess true sapience, but our civilization has always enjoyed sampling from a wide range of genomes.\n\nAlready, our [GetScientistPlural] are calculating the amount of genetic material needed to provide everyone with a taste.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:826`

## story.4.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.4.name&lang=en)

### 中文

接触报告：未开智生命

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:799`

### English

Contact Report: Pre-Sapient Beings

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:828`

## story.4.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.4.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetName]上新闻满天飞，内容都是不久前遭遇的外星生物。\n\n这一生命体已经接近智慧生物了，真是令人兴奋不已。但我们也要清楚地认识到，尽管[Root.GetName]才刚刚登上星系的舞台，但这一切已经是相当大的进展。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:800`

### English

The [Root.GetName] is abuzz with news of the alien beings encountered some time ago.\n\nTheir near-intelligence is fascinating, and it is sobering to realize that while the [Root.GetName] may only just have made its entrance on the galactic stage, we have already come a long way.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:829`

## story.4.desc.phobe

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.4.desc.phobe&lang=en)

### 中文

观测到外星动物一事在[Root.Capital.GetName]上造成了恐慌。视频中有一些东西——它们沉重的脚步和空洞的眼神——引起了[Root.GetSpeciesAdj]民众深深的不安。\n\n它们是不是外星的并不重要，重要的是外星生物学家们坚定地认为它们和我们非常相似。这简直就是一个扭曲的镜像，向[Root.GetName]展示着我们试图遗忘的，步履蹒跚的进化之路。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:801`

### English

The alien animals that were observed are cause for some consternation on [Root.Capital.GetName]. There is something in the video feeds - of their plodding gait and blank stares - that deeply disturbs the [Root.GetSpeciesAdj] populace.\n\nPerhaps it is not their alien nature that concerns, but the xeno-biologists' insistence that they are very much like us. A warped mirror image of a shambling evolutionary past that the [Root.GetName] would rather forget.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:830`

## story.4.desc.phile

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.4.desc.phile&lang=en)

### 中文

在遭遇外星生命后，[Root.GetSpeciesAdj]新闻网铺天盖地的都是人们对此事的争论。\n\n该生物的智慧令人叹为观止。我们还要清楚地认识到，尽管[Root.GetName]才刚刚登上星系的舞台，但这一切已经是相当大的进展。大多数[Root.GetSpeciesNamePlural]都同意我们有责任和义务帮助其他的物种踏上同样的道路，不过前提是他们已经做好了准备。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:802`

### English

The [Root.GetSpeciesAdj] newsnets are ablaze with discussion and debate over the alien beings encountered some time ago.\n\nThe creatures' intelligence is fascinating, and it is sobering to realize that while the [Root.GetName] may only just have made its entrance on the galactic stage, we have already come a long way. Most [Root.GetSpeciesNamePlural] agree that it is an obligation and our moral duty to help other races make the same journey, when they are ready.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:831`

## story.4.desc.spiri

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.4.desc.spiri&lang=en)

### 中文

遭遇外星生命后，[Root.GetSpeciesAdj]宗教事务团体纷纷奔走相告。\n\n该生物的智慧令人叹为观止。我们还要清楚地认识到，也许[Root.GetName]才刚刚登上星系的舞台，但这已经是相当大的进展。[Root.Capital.GetName]上出现了激烈的争论，如果他们已经有了足够智慧并准备探索星空，我们到底应不应该为其引路。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:803`

### English

[Root.GetSpeciesAdj] congregations are buzzing with activity following news of the alien beings encountered some time ago.\n\nThe creatures' intelligence is fascinating, and it is sobering to realize that while the [Root.GetName] may only just have made its entrance on the galactic stage, we have already come a long way. There is heated debate on [Root.Capital.GetName] whether it would be our place to shepherd these creatures, should they ever achieve sapience and reach for the stars.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:832`

## story.4.desc.followup

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.4.desc.followup&lang=en)

### 中文

多次发现低等生物后，[Root.GetSpeciesAdj]首都收到的消息称他们观察到一种非常神奇同时具有莫大进化潜力的生物。\n\n此报告受到了极大的重视。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:804`

### English

Following previous discoveries of lower forms of life, the [Root.GetSpeciesAdj] capital receives news of the observation of beings with impressive, if latent, cognitive capabilities and great evolutionary potential.\n\nThe report is received with marked enthusiasm.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:833`

## story.4.desc.gesta

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.4.desc.gesta&lang=en)

### 中文

我们确认了即将进化出自我意识的外星生物的存在。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:805`

### English

We have confirmed the existence of alien lifeforms on the evolutionary brink of consciousness.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:834`

## story.4.desc.evopred

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.4.desc.evopred&lang=en)

### 中文

我们发现了也只是全新的外星生命形式。这些生物比[GetHomeWorldName]上生活着的打大多数生命都要复杂，具有接近智慧水平的智力。我们已经开始建立必要的后勤和克隆程序来处理这些诱人的新基因组。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:806`

### English

We have uncovered a new form of alien life. These creatures are more complex than most of those that live on [GetHomeWorldName], with an intelligence that borders on sapience. We have already begun to establish the logistical and cloning routines necessary to process these tantalizing new genomes.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:835`

## story.5.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.5.name&lang=en)

### 中文

接触报告：遗迹

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:807`

### English

Contact Report: Remnants

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:837`

## story.5.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.5.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetName]的人对新近发现外星遗迹的消息议论纷纷。\n\n这些痕迹被视为外星人曾经存在并活跃过的铁证——我们现在虽然是孤独的，但至少我们不是第一个踏上星空的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:808`

### English

The [Root.GetName] is abuzz with news of the alien remnants that were recently studied.\n\nThese remains are considered definitive proof of intelligent, purposeful alien activity at some point in the past - we may still be alone now, but we are at least not the first to be so.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:838`

## story.5.desc.phobe

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.5.desc.phobe&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.Capital.GetName]上的有关部门对近日收到的关于外星遗迹的报告表示了一定忧虑。\n\n这些痕迹被广泛视为某种生命曾经活跃于宇宙的舞台上的铁证，不过一些著名的[Root.GetSpeciesAdj]专家认为这些痕迹其实只是地质构造现象或者大自然的自然变化。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:809`

### English

The administration on [Root.Capital.GetName] received the report on the alien remnants with some apprehension.\n\nThe remains in question are now widely considered to be definitive proof of unknown forces once having been active in the galaxy, though some prominent [Root.GetSpeciesAdj] thinkers reject this in favor of identifying the traces as "freak geological formations" or "results of curious natural phenomena."

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:839`

## story.5.desc.phile

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.5.desc.phile&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetSpeciesAdj]外星研究学家们很激动地发表了他们对近期外星生命发现的看法。\n\n这阵科学界的风暴对其他领域造成了暂时的负面影响。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:810`

### English

[Root.GetSpeciesAdj] xenologists are practically falling over themselves to publish their takes on the recent findings of alien life.\n\nThis fevered storm in the scientific community has had some negative, yet temporary, impact on pursuits in other fields.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:840`

## story.5.desc.followup

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.5.desc.followup&lang=en)

### 中文

“智能生物找不到伴”一则热门的新闻文章出现在[Root.Capital.GetName]上。[Root.GetName]的人们认为大家的注意力都集中在一些低等外星生物上，而真正有智能的外星种群却依然“戴着它们神秘的面纱”。\n\n最近发现关于外星生物痕迹的报告似乎只是为整件事情增添了一些讽刺意味。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:811`

### English

"Intelligent life taunts with pointed absence" reads a popular newsnet post on [Root.Capital.GetName]. The people of the [Root.GetName] are apparently finding some humor in the fact that lower forms of alien life are now a matter of public record, but potential equals from other stars continue to elude us.\n\nThe report on the traces of alien life that were recently found seemingly only add an ironic twist to the situation.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:841`

## story.5.desc.gesta

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.5.desc.gesta&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在异星上发现了智慧外星生物存在的证据。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:812`

### English

We have confirmed the first material traces of intelligent alien life on a foreign world.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:842`

## story.6.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.6.name&lang=en)

### 中文

接触报告：[GetPreFTLUpperPlural]物种

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:813`

### English

Contact Report: [GetPreFTLUpperPlural]

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:844`

## story.6.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.6.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的舰队发现[GetPreFTLLower]外星文明的消息传遍了[Root.GetName]。\n\n虽然仍未掌握太空旅行的能力，但[primitive_planet.GetName]上的外星人已具有足够的智慧，而且他们的社会也具备了先进文明的所有特征。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:814`

### English

The [Root.GetName] is abuzz with news of the [GetPreFTLLower] alien civilization found by our fleets.\n\nThey may not be capable of space-flight, but the aliens of the [primitive_planet.GetName] are quantifiably intelligent and their society shows all the hallmarks of a moderately advanced culture.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:845`

## story.6.desc.phobe

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.6.desc.phobe&lang=en)

### 中文

[GetPreFTLLower]生物，也就是所谓的[primitive_planet.GetName]文明，它们展现出基础智慧的新闻在[Root.Capital.GetName]上并不受人待见。\n\n一种深刻的不安情绪弥漫在公共辩论中，少数人大声疾呼，主张要不择手段建立[Root.GetSpeciesAdj]对外星星球的霸权，而大多数[Root.GetSpeciesNamePlural]似乎不愿意提起外星生物或完全忘记它们。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:815`

### English

News of the [GetPreFTLLower] creatures exhibiting rudimentary intelligence - the so-called civilization of [primitive_planet.GetName] - is not received well on [Root.Capital.GetName].\n\nA profound sense of unease permeates the public debate, as a vocal minority advocates establishing [Root.GetSpeciesAdj] hegemony over the alien planet by any means necessary, while most [Root.GetSpeciesNamePlural] would seemingly prefer to avoid the alien creatures or forget about them entirely.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:846`

## story.6.desc.phile

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.6.desc.phile&lang=en)

### 中文

我们舰队观测到[GetPreFTLLower]文明的报告得到了[Root.GetName]上的学术精英们在维持自己体面的范围内最大限度的狂欢庆贺。\n\n于此，我们终于可以证明[Root.GetSpeciesAdj]走向漆黑星空的试探性一步并不会一无所获。他们也许暂时比不上我们，甚至我们这一辈的人都不会跟他们直接交流，但光是他们存在本身都是一盏能够照亮这个空荡荡静悄悄的宇宙的希望明灯。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:816`

### English

The report on the [GetPreFTLLower] civilization observed by our fleets is received with something as close to exultant jubilation as the academic elite of the [Root.GetName] will allow themselves to express.\n\nHere, finally, is proof that the first tentative [Root.GetSpeciesAdj] steps into the darkness of space were not for naught. They may not be our equals, not yet, and it may be that we will not interact directly with them in our lifetimes, but their mere existence is a beacon of hope in a galaxy that otherwise appears deadened and unmoving.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:847`

## story.6.desc.followup5

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.6.desc.followup5&lang=en)

### 中文

发现智慧外星生命令[Root.Capital.GetName]的科学界欢呼雀跃。\n\n尽管仍未掌握太空旅行的能力，但[primitive_planet.GetName]上的外星人已具有足够的智慧，而且他们的社会也具备了先进文明的所有特征。著名外星研究学者表示，之前只能研究古代的遗迹，而现在确是一次研究外星社会的绝好机会。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:817`

### English

The scientific community on [Root.Capital.GetName] is excited to receive the report on intelligent alien life.\n\nThey may not be capable of space-flight, but the aliens of [primitive_planet.GetName] are quantifiably intelligent and their society shows all the hallmarks of a moderately advanced culture. Prominent xenologists note that this is an excellent opportunity to study a society perhaps not unlike the ones we have previously only found ancient remains of.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:848`

## story.6.desc.followup4

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.6.desc.followup4&lang=en)

### 中文

发现[GetPreFTLLower]文化的报告受到了[Root.GetSpeciesAdj]研究者们的欢迎，而这一发现紧跟在发现未开化物种之后。\n\n[Root.GetName]目前是我们知道的首个踏上星空的文明，但是发现有其他文明仅仅落后我们数个纪元甚至只是数个世纪真的让人非常激动。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:818`

### English

The report on a [GetPreFTLLower] culture is welcomed by [Root.GetSpeciesAdj] researchers, following previous discoveries relating to pre-sapient species.\n\nAs far as we are aware the [Root.GetName] is the first civilization to take to the stars, but it is fascinating to learn that some other cultures may only be a few millennia, or possibly even centuries, behind.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:849`

## story.6.desc.followup123

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.6.desc.followup123&lang=en)

### 中文

相比往常发现的非智慧生命，这次我们的舰队发现了[GetPreFTLLower]外星种群[primitive_planet.GetName]的报告无疑重新燃起了[Root.Capital.GetName]对外星科学的兴趣，在此之前这个领域向来被视为不过是纸上谈兵。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:819`

### English

Effectively capping off previous discoveries of lower forms of life, the report on the [GetPreFTLLower] aliens of [primitive_planet.GetName], encountered by our fleets has reignited interest in the xenological sciences on [Root.Capital.GetName], previously considered a largely theoretical field.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:850`

## story.6.desc.gesta

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.6.desc.gesta&lang=en)

### 中文

我们确认了[GetPreFTLLower]外星文明的存在。他们仍不具备超光速旅行能力，被自身分裂的个性所阻碍。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:820`

### English

We have confirmed the existence of a [GetPreFTLLower] alien culture. They have yet to develop FTL capability, handicapped as they are by their fragmented individuality.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:851`

## story.6.desc.evopred

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.6.desc.evopred&lang=en)

### 中文

我们发现了智慧生物！我们的一艘飞船遇见了一个[GetPreFTLLower]文明，这说明我们在虚空之中并不孤独。此外我们还缺人了他们的染色体很适合吸收。\n\n我们已经考虑了无数选项，包括精心计算的观测或者直接的入侵。我们的关系最终会变成什么样还有待观察，但种种可能性毫无疑问相当可口。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:821`

### English

We have discovered intelligent life! One of our vessels encountered a [GetPreFTLLower] civilization, which proves that we are not alone in the void. We have also confirmed that their genome is ripe for absorption.\n\nAlready, numerous options are being considered, from calculated observation to outright invasion. What form our relationship eventually takes remains to be seen, but the possibilities are certainly appetizing.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:852`

## story.7.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.7.name&lang=en)

### 中文

接触报告：神秘的太空旅人

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:822`

### English

Contact Report: Enigmatic Spacefarers

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:854`

## story.7.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.7.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetName]没过多久便遭遇了外星智慧生命。\n\n舰队发给[Root.GetSpeciesAdj]首都的报告很简单随意地描述了这些生物，还顺口提到了错失跟它们交流的机会。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:823`

### English

It did not take long for the [Root.GetName] to come across intelligent alien life.\n\nThe fleet's report to the [Root.GetSpeciesAdj] capital somewhat understates just how alien these forces appear, and only in passing mentions the failure to open comms.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:855`

## story.7.desc.phobe

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.7.desc.phobe&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.Capital.GetName]在收到发现未知飞船的消息后彻底沸腾了。\n\n目前尚不知道对方的目的，所以还是做好应对最坏情况的准备。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:824`

### English

[Root.Capital.GetName] is in an uproar following the news of unidentified ships sighted in the void.\n\nTheir intentions unknown, it would be prudent to assume the worst.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:856`

## story.7.desc.phile

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.7.desc.phile&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetName]终于遭遇了星空中的其他流浪者。\n\n虽然不知道这些外星人的意图，但是[Root.Capital.GetName]在收到舰队的报告后简直可用四海欢腾来形容。终于有人可以来见证一切了！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:825`

### English

The [Root.GetName] has finally encountered fellow wanderers among the stars.\n\nWhatever the intentions of these aliens, the atmosphere on [Root.Capital.GetName] can best be described as rapturous. At last, someone to bear witness!

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:857`

## story.7.desc.followup6

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.7.desc.followup6&lang=en)

### 中文

我们遭遇了可以进行太空航行的智慧生命。\n\n跟之前[Root.GetName]观察到的[GetPreFTLLowerPlural]物种不同，我们甚至还未能看清楚这些驾驶着飞船穿越深空的生物，也不清楚他们的目的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:826`

### English

We have now encountered intelligent life capable of space-flight.\n\nIn sharp contrast to the [GetPreFTLLowerPlural] previously observed by the [Root.GetName], we have yet to get a good look at the beings piloting the crafts that move through the dark with purpose unknown.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:858`

## story.7.desc.followup12345

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.7.desc.followup12345&lang=en)

### 中文

外星人飞船的消息传到了[Root.Capital.GetName]之后，可说是为[Root.GetName]踏往群星帝国的初章画上了一个圆满的句号。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:827`

### English

News of alien ships humming through the ether has reached [Root.Capital.GetName], in many ways ending the first chapter of the [Root.GetName]'s bid for a stellar empire.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:859`

## story.7.desc.mach.followup12345

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.7.desc.mach.followup12345&lang=en)

### 中文

我们曾观察到拥有先进航天能力的外星飞船经过。尽管仍未与其取得联系，但可以确定我们[Root.GetSpeciesNamePlural]并不是星系中唯一有能力探索太空的帝国。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:828`

### English

We have observed the passage of alien vessels capable of advanced spaceflight. While communications have not yet been established, we can confirm that we [Root.GetSpeciesNamePlural] are not the only empire in the galaxy capable of stellar exploration.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:860`

## story.7.desc.gesta

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.7.desc.gesta&lang=en)

### 中文

我们遭遇了高等外星生命。接触似乎不可避免。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:829`

### English

We have encountered advanced alien life. Contact seems inevitable.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:861`

## story.7.desc.evopred

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.7.desc.evopred&lang=en)

### 中文

我们发现了具有太空航行能力的生命。\n\n这一里程碑式的发现点燃了许多哲学和伦理争论：如果这些生物包含着能吸收的基因，我们应该强取豪夺，还是礼貌索要呢？只需要对自愿给予的样本进行克隆就足以满足我们整个人口所需的物质。\n\n与这些有智慧的探索者们第一次接触的结果应该会帮助我们解答这些问题。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:830`

### English

We have encountered life capable of space-flight.\n\nThis momentous discovery has accelerated many philosophical and ethical debates: if these creatures possess genes we can absorb, should we take them by force, or do we ask politely? Cloning freely-given samples could provide us with enough material for our entire population.\n\nThe result of first contact with these intelligent explorers should help determine the answer.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:862`

## story.7.a.swarm

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.7.a.swarm&lang=en)

### 中文

猎物？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:831`

### English

Prey?

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:863`

## story.contact_report.materialist

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.contact_report.materialist&lang=en)

### 中文

新的生命能带来有价值的科学洞见。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:832`

### English

New life offers valuable scientific insights.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:865`

## story.contact_report.spiritualist

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.contact_report.spiritualist&lang=en)

### 中文

我们与宇宙天人一体。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:833`

### English

We are one with the cosmos.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:866`

## story.contact_report.militarist

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.contact_report.militarist&lang=en)

### 中文

做好接触潜在威胁的准备。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:834`

### English

Prepare to confront potential threats.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:867`

## story.contact_report.pacifist

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.contact_report.pacifist&lang=en)

### 中文

生命的存在拓宽了我们的理解。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:835`

### English

The existence of life expands our understanding.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:868`

## story.contact_report.egalitarian

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.contact_report.egalitarian&lang=en)

### 中文

多样性丰富了我们的集体智慧。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:836`

### English

Diversity enriches our collective wisdom.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:869`

## story.contact_report.xenophile

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.contact_report.xenophile&lang=en)

### 中文

拥抱新生命的纽带。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:837`

### English

Embrace the bonds of new life.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:870`

## story.contact_report.xenophobe

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.contact_report.xenophobe&lang=en)

### 中文

强化我们的防御以备不测。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:838`

### English

Fortify our defenses against the unknown.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:871`

## story.contact_report.authoritarian

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.contact_report.authoritarian&lang=en)

### 中文

我们必须称霸寰宇。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:839`

### English

Our dominance must be asserted.

## story.8.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.8.name&lang=en)

### 中文

接触报告：星神兽

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:853`

### English

Contact Report: Leviathan

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:887`

## story.8.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.8.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在虚空中发现巨大生物一事使得[Root.GetName]在浩瀚之中显得更加微不足道了。我们伟大的帝国遇到的这第一只庞然大物很可能就是我们遭遇最后一只。这只巨兽的影像已经传遍了整个帝国，造成了极大恐慌，不少人要求政府采取行动。\n\n我方舰队的报告表明，这只巨兽的力量足以轻松摧毁宇宙中最为强大的舰队，建议我们小心行事。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:854`

### English

The discovery of a giant monstrosity living in the void has made the [Root.GetName] appear all the more insignificant in the grand scheme of things. The first creature our proud empire encounters might just be its last. The leviathan's image has spread across the empire, causing mass hysteria and pleas for action.\n\nOur fleet's report stated that the beast appears powerful enough to crush even the most capable armada, and recommends caution.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:888`

## story.8.desc.spirit

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.8.desc.spirit&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetSpeciesAdj]的民众都在祈祷。黑暗中的巨怪使得帝国中的很多人开始质疑自己的神志是否清晰，甚至还有些人对神灵的宗教事务都开始动摇了。这些巨怪到底是生命的创造者和守护者，还是宇宙万物的敌人？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:855`

### English

The [Root.GetSpeciesAdj] populace is praying. The giant being encountered in the dark has caused many a denizen of the empire to fear for their sanity, or doubt their faith in the divine. Are these giants the guardians of all life and that which created it, or its enemy?

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:889`

## story.8.desc.phile

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.8.desc.phile&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetName]遇到了十分庄严崇高的存在。这种巨型生物的存在本身，就是宇宙孕育的无穷奇迹的最佳证明。在这巨型生物的身旁，任何生物都会显得无比渺小，一如腐草荧光安能与日月同辉。\n\n虽说这种巨型生物的行为方式尚不明确，[Root.Capital.GetName]的民众却在敬畏的同时抱有希望，对于这巨兽将带来的一切十分期待。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:856`

### English

The [Root.GetName] has encountered something that is beyond sublime. Giant beings, their sheer existence a testament to the unfathomable wonders of the universe, that make most lifeforms appear like flickering lights next to burning stars.\n\nDespite the true nature of the leviathan being unknown, the people on [Root.Capital.GetName] are hopefully in awe of what has been found, and optimistic about what it may bring.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:890`

## story.8.desc.mach

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.8.desc.mach&lang=en)

### 中文

在深空中发现特性不明的巨大个体迫使[Root.GetName]重新定义已知敌对单独个体的最大表面面积相关的参数。[Root.GetName]与新发现的该个体发生冲突时的生存率预测并不容乐观。\n\n建议科研与战斗单位继续观察，并对我们已有舰队的规格进行升级。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:857`

### English

The discovery of a massive entity with unknown properties in the void has forced the [Root.GetName] to redefine all known parameters for the maximum surface area of any single construct with hostile capabilities. Survival rate projections should the [Root.GetName] come into conflict with the newly discovered entity are not promising. \n\nScientific and military units recommend continued observation, and specification upgrades to our existing armada.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:891`

## story.8.desc.followup7

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.8.desc.followup7&lang=en)

### 中文

我们找到了一些发展水平超越之前遭遇过的一切物种的生物。这种生物可以生长到前所未见的规模，体积和质量甚至可以与星球的卫星相比。\n\n真可谓是防不胜防。这一天将永远标示着[Root.GetName]历史的转折点。我们只希望我们种群还能存活到回顾此事的那一天。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:858`

### English

We have now found beings far surpassing anything encountered in our empire's spacefaring history. Life capable of growing to an unprecedented scale, rivaling moons in size and mass.\n\nNothing could have prepared us for this. This day will forever mark a turning point in the story of the [Root.GetName]. Let us hope we survive long enough to tell it.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:892`

## story.8.desc.followup123456

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.8.desc.followup123456&lang=en)

### 中文

近期，有关一只巨兽出没在黑暗虚空之中的消息席卷了整个帝国。有人表示关注，有人表示乐观，但最多的人还是表示恐惧。历史文档中将会着重记录这重要的一天。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:859`

### English

News of a giant being seen in the dark void of space is spreading across the empire like a plague. There is concern and optimistic wonder, but mostly brings an existential dread to the populace. Historical documents will record this day as one of tremendous importance.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:893`

## story.8.desc.mechanical

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.8.desc.mechanical&lang=en)

### 中文

有消息称在太空深处遭遇到了一个无比浩大的构筑，它正在延展贯穿整个[Root.GetName]。虽然它看起来并非是某种生物，不过其存在还是彰显了包含技术和巨型工程的可能性。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:860`

### English

News of the encounter with a deep-space construct of gargantuan proportion is spreading throughout the [Root.GetName]. While the entity does not appear to be of biological nature, its mere existence has inescapable ramifications for the possibilities of technology and mega-engineering.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:894`

## story.101.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.101.name&lang=en)

### 中文

太空变形虫

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:870`

### English

Space Amoebas

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:906`

## story.101.desc.phile

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.101.desc.phile&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的舰队不久前遇见的存在是一种全新的、美丽的、神奇的太空生命。\n\n因为分析员对传感器数据的误读，这种生物很快就获得了“太空变形虫”的昵称，它们的体积比一般的[Root.Owner.GetAdj]护卫舰更巨大。\n\n我们应该避免与它们接触，从[Root.Owner.Capital.GetName]远距离观察这些生物。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:871`

### English

The entities encountered by our fleets some time ago are new, wondrous, forms of spaceborne life.\n\nQuickly nicknamed "Space Amoeba" following an analyst's gross misreading of initial sensor output, the creature is in fact larger than the average [Root.Owner.GetAdj] corvette.\n\nWe should leave it be and remotely monitor the creature and its kin from [Root.Owner.Capital.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:907`

## story.101.desc.phobe

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.101.desc.phobe&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的舰队不久前遇见的存在是一种全新的、骇人的太空生命。\n\n因为分析员对传感器数据的误读，这种生物很快就获得了“太空变形虫”的昵称，它们的体积比一般的[Root.Owner.GetAdj]护卫舰更巨大。\n\n终结这些憎恶异形苟活于世的苦难应该没有什么害处。\n\n既是为了科学，也可以是为了其他什么。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:872`

### English

The entities encountered by our fleets some time ago are new, horrifying lifeforms.\n\nQuickly nicknamed "Space Amoeba" following an analyst's gross misreading of initial sensor output, the creature is in fact larger than the average [Root.Owner.GetAdj] corvette.\n\nThere would be little harm in putting the monstrosities out of their misery.\n\nFor science, or some equivalent.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:908`

## story.101.desc.milit

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.101.desc.milit&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的舰队不久前遇见的存在是一种太空生命。\n\n因为分析员对传感器数据的误读，这种生物很快就获得了“太空变形虫”的昵称，它们的体积比一般的[Root.Owner.GetAdj]护卫舰更巨大。\n\n这些生物对[Root.Owner.GetAdj]舰船表现出了敌意的行为，但我们授权进一步的研究。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:873`

### English

The entities encountered by our fleets some time ago are spaceborne forms of life.\n\nQuickly nicknamed "Space Amoeba" following a tactician's gross misreading of initial sensor output, the creature is in fact larger than the average [Root.Owner.GetAdj] corvette.\n\nThe creature displays aggressive behavior towards [Root.Owner.GetAdj] ships but further study might be warranted.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:909`

## story.101.desc.pacif

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.101.desc.pacif&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的舰队不久前遭遇的是一种从未见过的太空生命。\n\n因为分析员对传感器数据的误读，这种生物很快就获得了“太空变形虫”的昵称，它们的体积比一般的[Root.Owner.GetAdj]护卫舰更巨大。\n\n虽然它脾气很坏，但是想进一步研究也不是不行的——我们可以从[Root.Owner.Capital.GetName]远距离观察它们。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:874`

### English

The entities encountered by our fleets some time ago are new, spaceborne lifeforms.\n\nQuickly nicknamed "Space Amoeba" following an analyst's gross misreading of initial sensor output, the creature is in fact larger than the average [Root.Owner.GetAdj] corvette.\n\nDespite its apparent orneriness, further study is warranted - we can remotely monitor the creature and its kin from [Root.Owner.Capital.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:910`

## story.101.desc.mater

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.101.desc.mater&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的舰队不久前遇见的存在是一种太空生命。\n\n因为分析员对传感器数据的误读，这种生物很快就获得了“太空变形虫”的昵称，它们的体积比一般的[Root.Owner.GetAdj]护卫舰更巨大。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:875`

### English

The entities encountered by our fleets some time ago are spaceborne forms of life.\n\nQuickly nicknamed "Space Amoeba" following an analyst's gross misreading of initial sensor output, the creature is in fact larger than the average [Root.Owner.GetAdj] corvette.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:911`

## story.101.desc.gesta

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.101.desc.gesta&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的舰队不久前遇见的存在是一种全新的太空生命。\n\n尽管个体体型远远大于[Root.Owner.GetAdj]的护卫舰，这些“太空变形虫”兽群相对于我们的舰队依然弱小不堪。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:876`

### English

The entities encountered by our fleets some time ago are new, spaceborne lifeforms.\n\nWhile significantly larger than a [Root.Owner.GetAdj] Corvette, these "Space Amoeba" herds are minuscule next to our full corpus.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:912`

## story.101.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.101.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的舰队不久前遇见的存在是一种全新的太空生命。\n\n因为分析员对传感器数据的误读，这种生物很快就获得了“太空变形虫”的昵称，它们的体积比一般的[Root.GetAdj]护卫舰更巨大。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:877`

### English

The entities encountered by our fleets some time ago are new, spaceborne lifeforms.\n\nQuickly nicknamed "Space Amoeba" following an analyst's gross misreading of initial sensor output, the creature is in fact larger than the average [Root.GetAdj] corvette.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:913`

## story.105.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.105.name&lang=en)

### 中文

永远的太空变形虫

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:880`

### English

Space Amoebas Forever

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:917`

## story.105.desc.phile

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.105.desc.phile&lang=en)

### 中文

太空变形虫——这个昵称现在已经传遍了[Root.Capital.GetName]的大街小巷——是一种独居的生物。成年的太空变形虫身边总会伴随着一种被称为鞭毛的半独立生物。鞭毛由变形虫自行生成，并通过分泌液态RNA来进行控制。\n\n我们一度误以为这就是太空变形虫的幼体，但其实这些鞭毛并不含有繁殖器官，每个鞭毛更像是变形虫向外延伸的肢体。这些鞭毛可以在一定范围内离开主体并攻击敌人，但每隔一段时间就需要回返主体内进行重构。\n\n太空变形虫厚皮的再生特性，或鞭毛的精巧的运动模式都有着潜在的军事应用价值。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:882`

### English

The Space Amoebas - the nickname now lovingly popularized on [Root.Capital.GetName] - are solitary animals, when left to their own devices. All but the youngest specimens are accompanied by quasi-independent organisms, dubbed flagella, seemingly spawned by the Amoeba and programmed through liquid, RNA-laced secretions to do the bidding of the host.\n\nInitially assumed to be young Space Amoebas, the flagella do not seem to factor into any reproductive mechanics, each individual flagellum instead operating solely as a disconnected limb for its host Amoeba. These flagella are capable of manipulating and even attacking objects in an impressive yet restricted range around the host, periodically returning to lodge inside the host Amoeba's body for re-programming.\n\nThere are potential military applications to be found in the regenerative properties of the amoeba's thick hide, or in the flagella's graceful patterns of movement.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:919`

## story.105.desc.pacif

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.105.desc.pacif&lang=en)

### 中文

太空变形虫——这个昵称在这些生物被发现之后，在[Root.Capital.GetName]彻底火起来了。它们是一种独自生活的生物。成年的太空变形虫的身边会伴随着一种被称为鞭毛的半自主生物。鞭毛是由太空变形虫所生成，并通过液态RNA分泌物来控制。\n\n鞭毛一度被误会为太空变形虫的幼体，但其实它们并不含有繁殖器官，每个鞭毛更像是太空变形虫主体身上断开的肢体一般。这些鞭毛可以在离开主体的一定范围内攻击敌人，但每隔一段时间就需要回返太空变形虫体内被重新编译。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:883`

### English

The Space Amoebas - the nickname became popular after the story of how it came to be found its way back to [Root.Capital.GetName] - are solitary animals, when left to their own devices. All but the youngest specimens are accompanied by quasi-independent organisms, dubbed flagella, seemingly spawned by the Amoeba and programmed through liquid, RNA-laced secretions to do the bidding of the host.\n\nInitially assumed to be young Space Amoebas, the flagella do not seem to factor into any reproductive mechanics, each individual flagellum instead operating solely as a disconnected limb for its host Amoeba. These flagella are capable of manipulating and even attacking objects in an impressive yet restricted range around the host, periodically returning to lodge inside the host Amoeba's body for re-programming.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:920`

## story.105.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.105.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

太空变形虫——当为这种外星生物运用合适的分类方法来确定学名太过麻烦时，这个名称就被[Root.GetSpeciesName]的普罗大众沿用下来——是一种在太空中独居的生物。除了最幼小的那些个体以外，所有我们收集到的样本都拥有半独立的鞭毛和其他有机质，它们由变形虫本身产生并在液态的RNA分泌物中被设计，以方便宿主操纵。这些鞭毛可以被宿主操纵，甚至可以攻击宿主周围巨大但有限范围内的物体，并会定期的缩回变形虫体内，并被重新设计。\n\n太空变形虫厚皮的再生特性，或鞭毛的精巧的运动模式都有着潜在的军事应用价值。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:884`

### English

The Space Amoebas - the name has stuck as the pronunciation of the proper xeno-taxonomic denominator is too unwieldy for the average [Root.GetSpeciesName] - are solitary animals, when left to their own devices. All but the youngest specimens are accompanied by quasi-independent flagella, organisms spawned by the Amoeba and programmed through liquid, RNA-laced secretions to do the bidding of the host. These flagella are capable of manipulating and even attacking objects in an impressive yet restricted range around the host, periodically returning to lodge inside the host Amoeba's body for re-programming.\n\nThere are potential military applications to be found in the regenerative properties of the amoeba's thick hide, or in the flagella's graceful patterns of movement.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:921`

## story.105.desc.gesta

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.105.desc.gesta&lang=en)

### 中文

一只太空变形虫通常伴随着一些半自主的鞭节，用于防御以及移动物体。变形虫与其鞭节并不共享意志，而是前者使用一种带有核糖核酸的分泌物来为后者编程，指引其完成较为复杂的任务。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:885`

### English

A Space Amoeba is usually accompanied by a handful of semi-independent flagella used for defense and object manipulation. The Amoeba and its flagella do not share a mind, the former instead using RNA-laced secretions to program the latter for the execution of tasks of middling complexity.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:922`

## story.105.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.105.a&lang=en)

### 中文

确实神奇。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:886`

### English

Fascinating indeed.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:923`

## story.110.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.110.name&lang=en)

### 中文

太空变形虫已解剖

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:887`

### English

Space Amoeba Dissected

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:925`

## story.110.desc.phobe

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.110.desc.phobe&lang=en)

### 中文

太空变形虫——现在已经是负责捕猎它们的[Root.GetSpeciesAdj]部队口中的贬义词——它们比预想中更加复杂。它们的坚硬外壳保护着体内的无数小型太空变形虫，这些小型个体可比喻作保护着它们的太空变形虫母飞船的攻击机。这些攻击型太空变形虫虽然跟正常的太空变形虫很相似，但其实它们是相对简单的生物。太空变形虫体内拥有成千上百个器官，无疑都是存活于恶劣的太空环境的必要零件。\n\n在分尸太空变形虫身体时，这些器官大部分都要么破裂了要么被烧坏了，只留下了残缺的尸体。\n\n不过，参与太空变形虫项目的外星研究学家认为获取样本的过程是很重要的，所以他们发布了特殊的开火命令给整支舰队。只要针对五只或更多太空变形虫的特殊部位进行手术式打击，我们就能重组一个完整的尸体。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:889`

### English

The Space Amoebas - now a quasi-derogatory term gleefully popularized by the [Root.GetSpeciesAdj] forces tasked with bringing them down - are much more complex than anticipated. Their hardy exteriors protect a great number of pockets housing smaller "amoebas," creatures that our fleets have observed effectively acting as strike craft when engaging the "mothership amoeba." While superficially similar beyond the obvious differences in size, the "strike amoeba" are much simpler creatures than the Space Amoeba proper. Inside the Space Amoeba, beyond these pockets, lie hundreds of delicate organs doubtlessly all integral to surviving the harsh vacuum of space.\n\nMany of these organs ruptured or were simply incinerated in the process of dispatching the Amoeba, leaving us with a largely incomplete carcass.\n\nHowever, xeno-biologists assigned to the Space Amoeba project believe that the process of acquiring specimens is sound, and have distributed specific targeting instructions among the military fleets involved. With five more Amoebas, killed by surgical strikes to specific parts of their anatomy, a complete carcass can effectively be reconstructed.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:927`

## story.110.desc.milit

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.110.desc.milit&lang=en)

### 中文

太空变形虫——这是[Root.GetSpeciesAdj]部队钦定的正式名字——它们比预想中更加复杂。它们的坚硬外壳保护着体内的无数小型太空变形虫，这些小型个体可比喻作保护着它们的太空变形虫母飞船的攻击机。这些攻击型太空变形虫虽然跟正常的太空变形虫很相似，但其实它们的生理构造相对简单。太空变形虫体内拥有成千上百个器官，无疑都是存活于恶劣的太空环境的必要零件。在分尸太空变形虫身体时，这些器官大部分都要么破裂了要么被烧坏了，只留下了残缺的尸体。\n\n不过，参与太空变形虫项目的外星研究学家认为获取样本的过程是很重要的，所以他们发布了特殊的开火命令给整支舰队。只要针对五只或更多太空变形虫的特殊部位进行外科手术式打击，我们就能重组一个完整的尸体。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:890`

### English

The Space Amoebas - the nickname eventually adopted as the official target designator by [Root.GetSpeciesAdj] forces - are much more complex than anticipated. Their hardy exteriors protect a great number of pockets housing smaller "amoebas," creatures that our fleets have observed effectively acting as strike craft when engaging the "mothership amoeba." While superficially similar beyond the obvious differences in size, the "strike amoeba" are much less physiologically complex than the Space Amoeba proper. Inside the Space Amoeba, beyond these pockets, lie hundreds of delicate organs doubtlessly all integral to surviving the harsh vacuum of space. Many of these organs ruptured or were simply incinerated in the process of dispatching the Amoeba, leaving us with a largely incomplete carcass.\n\nHowever, xeno-biologists assigned to the Space Amoeba project believe that the process of acquiring specimens is sound, and have distributed specific targeting instructions among the military fleets involved. With five more Amoebas, killed by surgical strikes to specific parts of their anatomy, a complete carcass can effectively be reconstructed.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:928`

## story.110.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.110.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

太空变形虫——一个虽然违背了[Root.Owner.Capital.GetSpeciesAdj]传统的分类命名法但已经深入人心的名字——它们比预想中更加复杂。它们的坚硬外壳保护着体内的无数小型太空“变形虫”，这些小型个体可比喻作保护着它们的太空变形虫“母飞船的攻击机”。这些“攻击型太空变形虫”虽然跟正常的太空变形虫很相似，但其实它们的生理构造相对简单。太空变形虫体内拥有成千上百个器官，无疑都是存活于恶劣的太空环境的必要零件。在解决太空变形虫时，这些器官大部分都要么破裂了要么被烧坏了，只留下了残缺的尸体。但是，太空变形虫项目组的外星生物学家认为这已经足够了，他们成功辨识出了太空变形虫的生理特征和独特弱点。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:891`

### English

The Space Amoebas - as they defy easy classification according to traditional [Root.Owner.Capital.GetSpeciesAdj] taxonomy, the name has stuck - are much more complex than anticipated. Their hardy exteriors protect a great number of pockets housing smaller "amoebas," creatures that our fleets have observed effectively acting as strike craft when engaging the "mothership amoeba." While superficially similar beyond the obvious differences in size, the "strike amoeba" are much less physiologically complex than the Space Amoeba proper. Inside the Space Amoeba, beyond these pockets, lie hundreds of delicate organs doubtlessly all integral to surviving the harsh vacuum of space. Many of these organs ruptured or were simply incinerated in the process of dispatching the Amoeba, leaving us with a largely incomplete carcass. Xenobiologists assigned to the Space Amoeba project believe this to be enough however, as they have already successfully identified several physiological features and weaknesses unique to the Space Amoebas.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:929`

## story.110.desc.gesta

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.110.desc.gesta&lang=en)

### 中文

太空变形虫比当初预料的更为复杂。它们坚硬的外壳保护着大量装有小型“变形虫”的口袋，我们的舰队在与“变形虫母船”的交战中观察到它们实际上充当了攻击机。尽管除了尺寸以外它们在外表上大致相同，“攻击变形虫”在结构上比真正太空变形虫要简单得多。在太空变形虫体内的这些口袋后面，有着数以百计的脆弱器官，无疑都是为了在严酷的太空环境中生存。在与变形虫交战的过程中很多器官都破损或是被烧毁了，因此我们只获得了残缺不全的尸骸。不过负责太空变形虫计划的外星生物学家认为这就已经足够了，因为他们已经成功地鉴别出了太空变形虫数个独特生物特征以及弱点。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:892`

### English

The Space Amoebas are much more complex than anticipated. Their hardy exteriors protect a great number of pockets housing smaller "amoebas," creatures that our fleets have observed effectively acting as strike craft when engaging the "mothership amoeba." While superficially similar beyond the obvious differences in size, the "strike amoeba" are much less physiologically complex than the Space Amoeba proper. Inside the Space Amoeba, beyond these pockets, lie hundreds of delicate organs doubtlessly all integral to surviving the harsh vacuum of space. Many of these organs ruptured or were simply incinerated in the process of dispatching the Amoeba, leaving us with a largely incomplete carcass. Xenobiologists assigned to the Space Amoeba project believe this to be enough however, as they have already successfully identified several physiological features and weaknesses unique to the Space Amoebas.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:930`

## story.115.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.115.name&lang=en)

### 中文

太空变形虫已被消灭

### English

Space Amoebas Slaughtered

## story.115.desc.phobe

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.115.desc.phobe&lang=en)

### 中文

杀了数个太空变形虫后，我们终于能够研究太空变形虫跟它体内的小型生物的诡异关系。[Root.GetSpeciesAdj]的外星生物学家发现，这些以它们的行动方式来命名的条状体不是什么太空变形虫幼体也不是寄生物。它们仅仅是在主体成年后生成的工具。它们被委派的任务一旦完成，就会返回到太空变形虫主体的一个口袋结构内。它们既是操纵者同时也是主体伸向无边黑暗的附属物。它们唯一的弱点在于接受命令时的反应延迟，不过这一点被人海战术、个体的反射性反应、以及自动追踪太空变形虫主体的特点所弥补了。\n\n无论如何，我们还是尽快杀了它们吧。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:897`

### English

Several dead Amoebas later, we are able to investigate the Space Amoebas' grotesque relationship with the smaller creatures they house inside their bodies. As far as [Root.GetSpeciesAdj] xeno-biologists can tell, these flagella - named for their whip-like movements - are not proper offspring or even parasites. They are simply spawned from the host once the host reaches maturity, created as tools rather than the result of procreation. Once their task is complete they return to a pocket in the host Amoeba, seemingly to receive new orders. They work as manipulators, blind appendages reaching in the dark. The laughably obvious drawbacks resulting from their inherently delayed reaction time are somewhat offset by the Amoeba's sheer number of circulating flagella, and the individual flagellum's strong set of reflexive responses and ability to dumbly track the host Amoeba.\n\nAt any rate, we are better at killing them now.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:936`

## story.115.desc.milit

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.115.desc.milit&lang=en)

### 中文

采集了数个残骸后，我们能够研究太空变形虫跟它体内的小型生物的奇特关系。[Root.GetSpeciesAdj]的外星生物学家发现，这些以它们的行动方式来命名的条状体不是什么太空变形虫幼体也不是寄生物。这些条状体没有脑袋，而是拥有一个复杂的颗粒状处理器中心。这个中心负责解码皮层下的接收器所接收到的来自太空变形虫的RNA的命令。它们被委派的任务一旦完成，就会返回到太空变形虫主体体内并开始接受新的任务。它们是士兵、炮灰、工具、侦查员。它们唯一的弱点在于接受命令时的反应延迟，不过这一点被人海战术、个体的反射性反应、以及自动追踪太空变形虫主体的特点所弥补了。太空变形虫的深空战斗效率主要是依靠它们不断释放的条状体，这一点可供我们的部队学习。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:898`

### English

With several carcasses collected, we are able to investigate the Space Amoebas' curious relationship with the smaller creatures they house inside their bodies. As far as [Root.GetSpeciesAdj] xeno-biologists can tell, these flagella - named for their whip-like movements - are not proper offspring or even parasites. They are simply spawned from the host once the host reaches maturity, created as tools rather than the result of procreation. Where one would expect to find a brain - considering their intricate movement and inter-flagellar interactions - the flagellum instead possess a complex glandular processing center. This center decodes input - orders - in the form of RNA, secreted by the Amoeba and received by the flagellum through subcutaneous receptors. Once their task is complete they return to a pocket in the host Amoeba to receive new orders. This allows them to work as manipulators, hands severed from the body. The obvious drawbacks in this inherently delayed reaction time are somewhat offset by the Amoeba's sheer number of circulating flagella, and the individual flagellum's strong set of reflexive responses and ability to dumbly track the host Amoeba. The Space Amoebas' relative proficiency in deep-space combat is largely due to the consistent deployment of their flagella, something our forces could likely learn a lot from.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:937`

## story.115.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.115.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

击杀第四只生物后，一个完整的太空变形虫尸体终于在[Root.GetName]capital[Root.Capital.GetName]重组完毕了。我们现在开始将揭示这种生物的秘密，然后将收获的信息分发到[Root.GetSpeciesAdj]帝国的相关部门中。\n\n当前最有价值的发现莫过于太空变形虫的皮肤膜。它们能够吸收——然后通过复杂的精确流程——消化无数种宇宙辐射，甚至把这些射线转化为营养。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:899`

### English

Following the slaying of the fourth creature, a complete Space Amoeba carcass was reconstructed on the [Root.GetName]'s capital of [Root.Capital.GetName]. We are now beginning to divine some of the creatures' hidden truths, and they are being conveyed to the appropriate departments within [Root.GetSpeciesAdj] administration.\n\nThe perhaps most useful discovery at present relates to the Space Amoebas' cutaneous membranes. They absorb and - through complex glandular processes - metabolize a wide range of cosmic radiation, seemingly drawing some sustenance from the rays.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:938`
