## story.201.desc.base

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.201.desc.base&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的[This.Owner.GetResearchers]已经完成了对这种在[This.System.GetName]恒星系发现的非凡的、自由漂浮的晶态物体的调查报告。他们的发现令人震惊。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:904`

### English

Our [This.Owner.GetResearchers] have finished investigating the reports of extraordinary, free-floating crystal-like objects observed in the [This.System.GetName] system. Their findings are startling.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:944`

## story.201.desc.phile

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.201.desc.phile&lang=en)

### 中文

$story.201.desc.base$\n\n这些飞船般大小的物体以及它们的较小型的晶体卫星初看之下是无生命迹象的，然而一旦我们飞船靠近，它们就会突然改变方向并且从晶体内散发出能源波动，这说明它们也许是以生命形式存在的——这样的现象毫无疑问令人好奇，但我们得要小心处理。\n\n我们最杰出且聪敏的科学家们都准备好研究这些实体了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:905`

### English

$story.201.desc.base$\n\nThe ship-size objects and their slightly smaller but equally crystal-like satellites at first appeared inert, but sudden shifts in their orientation relative to our ships and new energy signatures emerging from within the prisms indicate that they might be alive - a fascinating prospect to be sure, but they should be approached with caution.\n\nOur best and brightest are standing by to study these entities.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:945`

## story.201.desc.phobe

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.201.desc.phobe&lang=en)

### 中文

$story.201.desc.base$\n\n这些飞船般大小的物体以及它们的较小型的晶体卫星初看之下是无生命迹象的，然而一旦我们飞船靠近，它们就会突然改变方向并且从晶体内散发出能源波动，毫无疑问它们具有可能的危险性，我们应该小心应对。\n\n我们最好派遣一支舰队试探这些晶体的侵略性，如果发现有威胁就把它们摧毁掉。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:906`

### English

$story.201.desc.base$\n\nThe ship-size objects and their slightly smaller but equally crystal-like satellites at first appeared inert, but sudden shifts in their orientation relative to our ships and new energy signatures emerging from within the prisms indicate that they should be regarded as hazardous and approached with extreme caution.\n\nWe should deploy a fleet to gauge the crystals' offensive strength, should they possess any, and destroy them if they are a threat.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:946`

## story.201.desc.pacif

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.201.desc.pacif&lang=en)

### 中文

$story.201.desc.base$\n\n这些飞船般大小的物体以及它们的较小型的晶体卫星初看之下是无生命迹象的，然而一旦我们飞船靠近，它们就会突然改变方向并且从晶体内散发出能源波动，它们毫无疑问具有可能的危险性，我们应该小心应对。\n\n既然我们不想冒险得罪这些晶体，那就从首都远距离监视它们吧。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:907`

### English

$story.201.desc.base$\n\nThe ship-size objects and their slightly smaller but equally crystal-like satellites at first appeared inert, but sudden shifts in their orientation relative to our ships and new energy signatures emerging from within the prisms indicate that they should be regarded as hazardous and approached with caution.\n\nAs we do not wish to risk coming into conflict with these prismatic enigmas, we ought to establish remote surveillance and observe them from the capital.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:947`

## story.201.desc.spiri

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.201.desc.spiri&lang=en)

### 中文

$story.201.desc.base$\n\n这些飞船般大小的物体以及它们的较小型的晶体卫星初看之下是无生命迹象的，然而一旦我们飞船靠近，它们就会突然改变方向并且从晶体内散发出能源波动，毫无疑问它们对[Root.Owner.GetSpeciesAdj]的飞船产生了反应。\n\n这些晶体的生性和来历不明，但它们的形态与不加矫饰的棱角都与[Root.Owner.GetSpeciesAdj]的灵魂产生了共鸣。[Root.Owner.GetName]应当退一步，从远距离和平地观察它们。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:908`

### English

$story.201.desc.base$\n\nThe ship-size objects and their slightly smaller but equally crystal-like satellites at first appeared inert, but sudden shifts in their orientation relative to our ships and new energy signatures emerging from within the prisms indicate that they are reacting to the presence of [Root.Owner.GetSpeciesAdj] ships.\n\nThe nature and origin of these crystals elude us at present, but there is something in their shape, their pristine angles, that resonates with the [Root.Owner.GetSpeciesAdj] soul. The [Root.Owner.GetName] ought to take a step back, and observe from a respectful distance.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:948`

## story.201.desc.milit

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.201.desc.milit&lang=en)

### 中文

$story.201.desc.base$\n\n这些飞船般大小的物体以及它们的较小型的晶体卫星初看之下是无生命迹象的，然而一旦我们飞船靠近，它们就会突然改变方向并且从晶体内散发出能源波动，毫无疑问它们具有可能的危险性，我们应该小心应对。\n\n哪怕找不到什么战斗用途，我们研究一个被击碎的晶体肯定也会有一些有趣的发现。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:909`

### English

$story.201.desc.base$\n\nThe ship-size objects and their slightly smaller but equally crystal-like satellites at first appeared inert, but sudden shifts in their orientation relative to our ships and new energy signatures emerging from within the prisms indicate that they should be regarded as hazardous and approached with caution.\n\nEven should we not find a martial use for them, studying a shattered crystal would no doubt yield interesting results.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:949`

## story.201.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.201.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$story.201.desc.base$\n\n这些飞船般大小的物体以及它们的较小型的晶体卫星初看之下是无生命迹象的，然而一旦我们飞船靠近，它们就会突然改变方向并且从晶体内散发出能源波动，毫无疑问它们具有可能的危险性，应该小心应对。\n\n我们既可以选择直接与这些危险的生命体交火，也可以从远距离安全地观察它们。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:910`

### English

$story.201.desc.base$\n\nThe ship-size objects and their slightly smaller but equally crystal-like satellites at first appeared inert, but sudden shifts in their orientation relative to our ships and new energy signatures emerging from within the prisms indicate that they should be regarded as hazardous and approached with caution.\n\nWe can either attempt to engage with the potentially dangerous objects directly, or establish remote surveillance and observe them from a safe distance.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:950`

## story.201.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.201.a&lang=en)

### 中文

发布远距离研究这些晶体的特殊项目。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:911`

### English

Issue a special project for remote study of the crystals.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:951`

## story.201.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.201.b&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的舰队应该能够击碎它们。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:912`

### English

We expect our fleets to break them apart.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:952`

## story.205.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.205.name&lang=en)

### 中文

不灭晶体

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:913`

### English

Crystalline Entities Are Unbreakable

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:954`

## story.205.desc.phile

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.205.desc.phile&lang=en)

### 中文

晶态实体——这是[Root.Capital.GetName]的外星生物学家跟外星地质学家互相妥协后所决定的名字——它们确实是生命体。\n\n一部分过于保守的[Root.GetSpeciesAdj]学术精英认为它们是“硅酸盐活性物质”，跟生命体没什么共同之处。\n\n[Root.GetName]的大部分民众不认同这种观点，而是开始赞叹这种晶态实体所代表的新概念。它们是一种单独生活的生物，每一个母体结晶通常只会带着一个哨兵以及一小队晶体军团。除了明显服从领袖命令的哨兵和军团之外，我们对于晶态实体的社会分层是一无所知——这个课题在[Root.Capital.GetName]引发了一场热烈探讨。\n\n晶体似乎不会繁殖，而且我们还没有发现它们的个体之间有明显的年龄差别。跟早前的推论不同，晶体的不同颜色与它们的年龄无关，而是受到内部的潜在变化而改变——而这种变化可以被释放出来——这种偶尔的波动会改变晶体的反射属性。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:914`

### English

The Crystalline Entities - the name itself a compromise between rival factions of xeno-biologists and xeno-geologists on [Root.Capital.GetName] - are most definitely alive.\n\nSome overly-conservative members of the [Root.GetSpeciesAdj] academic elite argue that they are "silicate-animate matter," and that they have little in common with biological life.\n\nMost of the [Root.GetName] populace rejects that regressive perspective, instead turning their view toward the fascinating new horizons that the Crystalline Entities represent. They are, somewhat regrettably, solitary beings, each individual Crystal Sovereign rarely seen with more than one Sentinel and a smaller Cohort entity. Aside from the Cohort and Sentinel clearly being subservient to the Sovereign of the group, the subtle nuances of the Crystalline Entities' socio-hierarchical relationships are lost on us - which does not stop these dynamics from being the subject of fevered study on [Root.Capital.GetName].\n\nThe Entities do not seem to mate, and we have yet to observe any Crystals that are recognizably older or younger than others. Contrary to an early hypothesis, the shifts in hue between individual Crystalline Entities seem to be related not to their age but to their latent internal charge - which can be violently unleashed - and it appear as if though sporadic fluctuations in this charge alter the refractive properties of the Crystal.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:955`

## story.205.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.205.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

晶态实体——这是[Root.Capital.GetName]的外星生物学家跟外星地质学家互相妥协后所确定的名字——它们似乎真的是生命体。\n\n早期分析给它们起的名字是活体硅酸物质，因为它们跟生命体没什么共同之处。它们是一种单独生活的生物，每一个母体结晶通常只会带着一个哨兵以及一小队晶体军团。除了明显服从领袖命令的哨兵和军团之外，我们对于晶态实体的社会分层是一无所知。\n\n晶体似乎不会繁殖，而且我们还没有发现它们的个体之间有明显的年龄差别。跟早前的推论不同，晶体的不同颜色与它们的年龄无关，而是受到内部的潜在变化而改变——而这种变化可以被释放出来——这种偶尔的波动会改变晶体的反射属性。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:915`

### English

The Crystalline Entities - the name itself a compromise between rival factions of xeno-biologists and xeno-geologists on [Root.Capital.GetName] - are probably alive.\n\nAn earlier and perhaps more descriptive name for them was "silicate-animate matter," as they have little in common with biological life. They are solitary beings, each individual Crystal Sovereign rarely seen with more than a Sentinel and a smaller Cohort entity. Aside from the Cohort and Sentinel clearly being subservient to the Sovereign of the group, the subtle nuances of the Crystalline Entities' socio-hierarchical relationships are lost on us.\n\nThe Entities do not seem to mate, and we have yet to observe any Crystals that are recognizably older or younger than others. Contrary to an early hypothesis, the shifts in hue between individual Crystalline Entities seem to be related not to their age but to their latent internal charge - which can be violently unleashed - and it appear as if though sporadic fluctuations in this charge alter the refractive properties of the Crystal.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:956`

## story.205.desc.pacif

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.205.desc.pacif&lang=en)

### 中文

晶态实体——这是[Root.Capital.GetName]的外星生物学家跟外星地质学家互相妥协后所确定的名字——它们似乎真的是生命体。\n\n早期分析给它们起的名字是活体硅酸物质，因为它们跟生命体没什么共同之处。它们是一种单独生活的生物，每一个母体结晶通常只会带着一个哨兵以及一小队晶体军团。除了明显服从领袖命令的哨兵和军团之外，我们对于晶态实体的社会分层可以说是一无所知。\n\n晶体似乎不会繁殖，而且我们还没有发现它们的个体之间有明显的年龄差别。跟早前的推论不同，晶体的不同颜色与它们的年龄无关，而是受到内部的潜在变化而改变——而这种变化可以被释放出来——这种偶尔的波动会改变晶体的反射属性。晶态实体可以对指定的目标释放这种波动，而且很不幸的，它们似乎热衷于对任何靠近的陌生种群如此做。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:916`

### English

The Crystalline Entities - the name itself a compromise between rival factions of xeno-biologists and xeno-geologists on [Root.Capital.GetName] - are probably alive.\n\nAn earlier and perhaps more descriptive name for them was "silicate-animate matter," as they have little in common with biological life. They are solitary beings, each individual Crystal Sovereign rarely seen with more than a Sentinel and a smaller Cohort entity. Aside from the Cohort and Sentinel clearly being subservient to the Sovereign of the group, the subtle nuances of the Crystalline Entities' socio-hierarchical relationships are lost on us.\n\nThe Entities do not seem to mate, and we have yet to observe any Crystals that are recognizably older or younger than others. Contrary to an early hypothesis, the shifts in hue between individual Crystalline Entities seem to be related not to their age but to their latent internal charge, and it appears as if though sporadic fluctuations in this charge alter the refractive properties of the Crystal. The Crystalline Entities are capable of violently discharging at specific target, and unfortunately seem to prefer to do so rather than let foreign entities pass by unscathed.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:957`

## story.205.desc.spiri

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.205.desc.spiri&lang=en)

### 中文

晶态实体——这个名字本身就是[Root.Capital.GetName]上两大对立派系，外星生物学家和外星地理学家妥协的结果——并不能用简单的活着或是死亡来描述。[Root.Capital.GetName]的一些缺乏想象力的研究者称它们为“活体硅酸物质”，他们认为这种生物虽然很不寻常，但依旧只是一种低等生物。\n\n无论如何，晶态实体都是是一种非常独特而神奇的存在，每只母体结晶附近有两只以上的随从，一只哨兵和一只士兵。晶态实体是不会找伴侣的，也不会衰老——它们诞生于很久以前。每个晶态实体的颜色不同，似乎与它们内在的能源大小有关。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:917`

### English

The Crystalline Entities - the name itself a compromise between rival factions of xeno-biologists and xeno-geologists on [Root.Capital.GetName] - exist beyond such base concepts as life, and death. Some of the less imaginative purveyors of wisdom on [Root.Capital.GetName] call them "silicate-animate matter," claiming that the specimens, while unusual, are still subject to the same supposed unfeeling natural laws that some claim govern us all.\n\nAt any rate, the Crystalline Entities are solitary, majestic beings. Each individual Crystal Sovereign is rarely accompanied by more than two subservient entities, a Sentinel and a smaller Crystal Cohort. The Entities do not mate, nor do any of them appear older or younger than others - they are all sprung from some immaculate, past eon. The shifts in hue between individual Sovereigns that have been observed seem to be related to their latent internal charge - which can be violently unleashed - and it appears as if though sporadic fluctuations in this charge alter the refractive properties of the Crystal.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:958`

## story.205.desc.gesta

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.205.desc.gesta&lang=en)

### 中文

晶态实体很可能是活的。\n\n或许称它们为“硅酸活体物质”更为恰当，因为它们与常见的生物或是传统人造生命大相径庭。\n\n这些实体似乎并不繁殖，并且我们所观察到的晶体在年龄上似乎都没有什么差别。与一个早期假设相反的是，晶态实体个体所发生的色调转变似乎与其年龄无关，而是受其潜在的内部电荷影响——而且可以被猛烈地释放——而偶尔产生的能源波动会改变晶体的折射特性。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:918`

### English

The Crystalline Entities are probably alive.\n\nA perhaps more descriptive name for them is "silicate-animate matter," as they have little in common with biological or traditionally artificial life.\n\nThe Entities do not seem to mate, and we have yet to observe any Crystals that are recognizably older or younger than others. Contrary to an early hypothesis, the shifts in hue between individual Crystalline Entities seem to be related not to their age but to their latent internal charge - which can be violently unleashed - and it appear as if though sporadic fluctuations in this charge alter the refractive properties of the Crystal.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:959`

## story.210.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.210.name&lang=en)

### 中文

晶态实体被粉碎

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:925`

### English

Crystalline Entity Shattered

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:967`

## story.210.desc.milit

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.210.desc.milit&lang=en)

### 中文

晶态实体——一种活体硅酸物质形态——是非常可怕的敌人。\n\n我们发现了可能是所有晶态实体共有的一个结构上的弱点，如果幸运的话，我们可能只需要再通过几只晶态实体就能判断出这个所谓的弱点是否有效。目前我们已经知道的是，它们所释放的能源强弱与水晶闪烁的光芒有关。\n\n简而言之，就是[Root.GetSpeciesAdj]以后一定能够遇到多个色彩的晶态实体，而且这些晶状体的行为方式和战斗能力都将存在差异。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:926`

### English

The Crystalline Entities - a new designation better reflecting their apparent status as silicate yet animate matter - are tough and aggressive adversaries.\n\nOur engagements have allowed us to pinpoint structural weaknesses that should, theoretically, be shared among all individual Crystalline Entities. This is fortunate, as our fleets will need to engage with several more specimens if we are to learn anything about these enigmatic prisms. What we do know is that changes, in levels of apparent aggression and the internal generation of power that drives their weapon-like discharge, are accompanied by shifts in how the crystals break light.\n\nIn essence, it is likely that [Root.GetSpeciesAdj] ships will eventually encounter Crystalline Entities in different colors, and should be prepared for differences in behavior and combat capabilities.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:968`

## story.210.desc.mater

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.210.desc.mater&lang=en)

### 中文

晶态实体——目前我们认为它比起稀有的矿物来说，更接近生物一些——对[Root.GetSpeciesAdj]飞船表现出强烈的攻击意愿，或许它们认为我们的舰队是金属实体，必须驱赶。\n\n多次交火使得我们发现了在理论上晶态实体的结构中可能存在一个共通的弱点，此外，它们所能释放能源的强弱取决于水晶闪光的频率。\n\n简而言之，就是[Root.GetSpeciesAdj]会遭遇多种颜色的晶态实体，而且这些晶状体的行为方式和战斗能力都将存在差异。\n\n现在为了对它们有更进一步的了解，我们只能继续与它们进行正面交火，虽然我们无法像开采天然资源一样开采它们，但是[Root.GetName]是绝对不会空手而归的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:927`

### English

The Crystalline Entities - re-designated as such once it became clear they have more in common with animals than precious minerals - are aggressive and outright hostile to [Root.GetSpeciesAdj] ships. Perhaps they perceive our fleets as Metallic Entities, to be ousted from Crystal territory by any means necessary.\n\nOur engagements have allowed us to pinpoint structural weaknesses that should, theoretically, be shared among all individual Crystalline Entities. Additionally, changes in levels of apparent aggression and the internal generation of power - that drives their weapon-like discharge - are accompanied by shifts in how the crystals break light.\n\nIn essence, it is likely that [Root.GetSpeciesAdj] ships will eventually encounter Crystalline Entities in different colors, and should be prepared for differences in behavior and combat capabilities.\n\nWe are left with little choice but to continue to engage the Crystalline Entities in direct combat if we wish to learn more about them. Despite our inability to easily exploit them as a natural resource, the [Root.GetName] is, of course, unwilling to just walk away empty-handed.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:969`

## story.210.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.210.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

晶态实体——鉴于其水晶化的外表的来源和普通矿物质不同——对[Root.GetSpeciesAdj]飞船表现出强烈的攻击意愿，或许它们认为我们的舰队是金属实体，必须驱赶。\n\n多次交火使得我们发现了在理论上晶态实体的结构中可能存在一个共通的弱点，此外，它们所能释放能源的强弱取决于水晶闪光的频率。\n\n简而言之，就是[Root.GetSpeciesAdj]会遭遇多种颜色的晶态实体，而且这些晶状体的行为方式和战斗能力都将存在差异。\n\n目前我们认为与它们进行和平的交流的可能性还是比较低的，所以为了对它们有更进一步的了解，我们只能继续与它们进行正面交火。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:928`

### English

The Crystalline Entities - informally re-named after it became obvious that the crystals' behavior is actual behavior and not some obscure mineralogical process - are aggressive and outright hostile to [Root.GetSpeciesAdj] ships. Perhaps they perceive our fleets as Metallic Entities, to be ousted from Crystal territory by any means necessary.\n\nOur engagements have allowed us to pinpoint structural weaknesses that should, theoretically, be shared among all individual Crystalline Entities. Additionally, changes in levels of apparent aggression and the internal generation of power - that drives their weapon-like discharge - are accompanied by shifts in how the crystals break light.\n\nIn essence, it is likely that [Root.GetSpeciesAdj] ships will eventually encounter Crystalline Entities in different colors, and should be prepared for differences in behavior and combat capabilities.\n\nAs peaceful interaction seems impossible at present, we are left with little choice but to continue to engage the Crystalline Entities in direct combat if we wish to learn more about them.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:970`

## story.210.desc.gesta

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.210.desc.gesta&lang=en)

### 中文

晶态实体具有侵略性，并且对我们的飞船展现出了敌意。或许它们将我们的飞船视为金属实体，必须全力将其驱逐出晶体实体的领地。\n\n我们在交战中发现了所有晶态实体在理论上讲都具有的结构弱点。另外，随着侵略性程度的变化以及内部电荷的产生——用于驱动其武器式放电——晶体折射光线的方式也会产生变化。\n\n本质上来讲，[Root.GetSpeciesAdj]飞船很有可能会遭遇不同颜色的晶态实体，并且我们该为它们在行为模式与战斗能力上的区别做好准备。\n\n鉴于目前似乎无法与其进行和平交流，如果我们要继续研究晶态实体，就必须继续与其直接交战。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:929`

### English

The Crystalline Entities are aggressive and outright hostile to us and our ships. Perhaps they perceive our fleets as Metallic Entities, to be ousted from Crystal territory by any means necessary.\n\nOur engagements have allowed us to pinpoint structural weaknesses that should, theoretically, be shared among all individual Crystalline Entities. Additionally, changes in levels of apparent aggression and the internal generation of power - which drives their weapon-like discharge - are accompanied by shifts in how the crystals break light.\n\nIn essence, it is likely that [Root.GetSpeciesAdj] ships will eventually encounter Crystalline Entities of different colors, and we should be prepared for differences in behavior and combat capabilities.\n\nAs peaceful interaction seems impossible at present, we are left with little choice but to continue to engage the Crystalline Entities in direct combat if we wish to learn more.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:971`

### English

## story.215.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.215.name&lang=en)

### 中文

已摧毁晶态实体

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:931`

### English

Crystalline Entities Crushed

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:974`

## story.215.desc.milit

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.215.desc.milit&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetName]离开后留下了一片破碎的水晶。\n\n晶态实体的秘密可能将会成为一个永远的谜，但那又如何呢？\n\n[Root.GetSpeciesAdj]的部队现在已经非常擅长摧毁晶体了，同时我们也发现了提升我们武器威力的方式。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:932`

### English

The fleets of the [Root.GetName] leave crushed crystal in their wake wherever they go.\n\nThe Crystalline Entities' true nature is likely to forever remain a mystery to us, but what does it matter?\n\n[Root.GetSpeciesAdj] forces are now exceptionally skilled at annihilating the prisms, and we have, over the course of the campaign, found ways to tune up our weaponry.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:975`

## story.215.desc.mater

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.215.desc.mater&lang=en)

### 中文

在摧毁了数个水晶帝国后，凭借数千样本，[Root.GetSpeciesAdj]的工程师终于研究出了将晶态实体的残骸转化成可用材料的方法。\n\n虽说与晶态实体的战斗可能还是会造成很大伤亡，但是无论如何，这也算得上是工业上的胜利了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:933`

### English

After the destruction of several crystal specimens and collection of thousands of samples, [Root.GetSpeciesAdj] engineers have finally devised a cost-effective process for refining the remains of Crystalline Entities into usable materials.\n\nWhether this will actually offset the cost of engaging with the crystals and their weaponized emissions remains to be seen, but it is a victory for industry nonetheless.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:976`

## story.215.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.215.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在多次冲突后，晶态实体对我们来说仍然是一个谜，它们似乎没有所谓的配偶，而且以它们的形体，我们也能很难想象出它们繁殖的方式。\n\n我们发现了一些被摧毁的晶态实体的碎片的用途，但是我们对它们的了解仍然非常少。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:934`

### English

Despite frequent clashes, the Crystalline Entities remain as enigmatic as ever. They do not seem to mate, and it is difficult to imagine some kind of vast, spacer geode naturally producing the things.\n\nWe have found some uses for the shards of destroyed Crystalline Entities, but fear that some things, such as the truth of their actual nature, simply are fundamentally unknowable.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:977`

## story.215.desc.gesta

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.215.desc.gesta&lang=en)

### 中文

尽管频繁发生冲突，晶态实体对我们来说仍是个谜。它们似乎并不繁殖，并且也很难想象某种巨大的太空晶洞中会天然产生这种东西。\n\n我们发现被摧毁的晶态实体碎片有一些实际用途，但恐怕某些事情，比如它们本质的真相，将永远无法得知。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:935`

### English

Despite frequent clashes, the Crystalline Entities remain as enigmatic as ever. They do not seem to mate, and it is difficult to imagine some kind of vast, spacer geode naturally producing the things.\n\nWe have found some uses for the shards of destroyed Crystalline Entities but fear that some things, such as the truth of their actual nature, simply are fundamentally unknowable.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:978`

### English

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.219.name&lang=en)

### 中文

水晶声纳

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:941`

### English

Crystal Sonar

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:984`

## story.219.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.219.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.GetFleetName]在[From.From.GetName]星系遭遇了晶态实体。\n\n使用存储的能源，我们的飞船可以向晶态实体释放出一道强力但是无害的高频光束，由于晶态实体的结构使得他们在被光束照射之后会产生类似于棱镜和共振器的效果，这道光束将会使得它们从内部瓦解，再由我们飞船的传感器将它们的碎片回收。\n\n这整套系统的测试只需要一瞬间。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:942`

### English

The [From.GetFleetName] has encountered Crystalline Entities in the [From.From.GetName] system.\n\nUsing some stored Energy, our ships can generate an intense but harmless high-frequency blast to fire at the Crystalline Entities. The Entities act as prisms and resonators, breaking up the pulse and scattering it across the star system, and the waves reflected from planetary bodies are picked up by our ship sensors.\n\nThe whole system surveyed, in an instant.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:985`

## story.220.desc.hostile

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.220.desc.hostile&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetName]报告，在[From.GetName]星系中发现了大量晶态实体。至于其是否是生物的古代家园，目前尚无明确指征，但在同一星系中发现如此数量的晶态实体可谓是前所未见。\n\n目前的读数表明，如果能够将这些不寻常的晶体结构……拆开的话，应该会拥有一定的研究价值，

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:948`

### English

The [Root.GetName] reports a significant crystalline presence in the [From.GetName] system. There is little to indicate whether this is an ancestral home of the creatures, but never before have this many crystalline entities been observed in one system at once.\n\nInitial readings point to the unusual crystal construct in the system being of some interest, should we be able to... disassemble it.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:992`

## story.220.desc.nonhostile

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.220.desc.nonhostile&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetName]报告称在[From.GetName]星系中发现大量晶状生命体的存在。这很明显是某种巢穴——或者至少是晶状生命体的聚集地，就如在[From.GetName]有一个我们从未见过的晶状结构。\n\n这个结构展现出了很多类似晶状生命体的性质，但它的体积大得吓人。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:949`

### English

The [Root.GetName] reports a significant crystalline presence in the [From.GetName] system. Evidently some kind of den - or at least a hub of crystalline activity, such as it is - [From.GetName] is home to a crystal construct unlike any previously encountered.\n\nThis construct exhibits several properties associated with the crystalline entities, but greatly exaggerated.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:993`

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:950`

### English

We see significant crystalline presence in the [From.GetName] system. Evidently some kind of den - or at least a hub of crystalline activity, such as it is - [From.GetName] is home to a crystal construct unlike any previously encountered.\n\nThis construct exhibits several properties associated with the crystalline entities, but greatly exaggerated.

## story.221.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.221.name&lang=en)

### 中文

水晶调研完成

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:952`

### English

Scried

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:997`

## story.221.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.221.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.Capital.GetName]的外星科技研究部门报告，他们对晶态结构的研究已经结束了。\n\n一如以往，对象可观察活动的缺乏导致十分难以得出具体结论。然而他们的身体结构依旧十分神奇。在我们材料工程师的帮助下，对该结构的研究使得我们的外星生物学家得以开始对这种晶态结构的高级重建性能进行模拟。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:953`

### English

A xenoscience department on [Root.Capital.GetName] reports that they have concluded their remote study of the crystalline construct.\n\nAgain, the sheer lack of observable behavior exhibited by these entities makes it difficult to draw any solid conclusions. However, their physiological properties continue to intrigue. The study of the construct has allowed our xenobiologists, in close cooperation with our materials engineers, to begin to emulate the advanced reconstructive properties observed in the crystalline construct.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:998`

## story.221.desc.gesta

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.221.desc.gesta&lang=en)

### 中文

我们对晶体构造的研究有了结论。\n\n这些构造从不展现出任何行为，使我们很难下结论。不过，它们的生物特性仍然很有意思，而且我们现在可以复制在其身上观察到的高级重构特性了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:954`

### English

We have concluded our remote study of the crystalline construct.\n\nThe sheer lack of observable behavior exhibited by these entities makes it difficult to draw any solid conclusions. However, their physiological properties continue to intrigue, and we can now begin to emulate the advanced reconstructive properties observed in the crystalline construct.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:999`

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:955`

### English

Commendable.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1000`

## story.223.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.223.desc&lang=en)

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:957`

### English

A xenoscience department on [Root.Capital.GetName] reports that the crystalline construct observed in a distant system has been destroyed, and remote study is no longer possible.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1003`

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:958`

### English

The crystalline construct observed in a distant system has been destroyed, and remote study is no longer possible.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1004`

## story.301.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.301.name&lang=en)

### 中文

古代采矿无人机

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:961`

### English

Ancient Mining Drones

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1008`

## story.301.desc.pacif

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.301.desc.pacif&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在某个恒星系里发现了深空无人机。这些无人机似乎是千年以前进行自动轨道采矿作业的矿机和监视用机器，但在不久之后采矿行动就被人放弃了，只剩无人机在继续运作。\n\n这些无人机似乎没有任何通讯协议——至少没有[Root.Owner.GetName]能破译的协议——并对进入了恒星系的[Root.Owner.GetSpeciesAdj]飞船采取了攻击行为。\n\n在[Root.System.GetName]发现这种无人机实在是喜人的收获，我们应该进一步研究一下。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:962`

### English

Reports of deep-space drones in certain systems have been investigated. The drones appear to be the workers and custodians of an autonomous orbital mining operation, established millennia ago and then soon abandoned by all but the drones themselves, judging by the state of the processing equipment.\n\nThe drones seem to lack any diplomatic comms protocols - at least any decipherable by the [Root.Owner.GetName] - and react with hostility to [Root.Owner.GetSpeciesAdj] ships entering the system.\n\nTheir presence in the [Root.System.GetName] system is intriguing, and we should attempt to learn more about them.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1009`

## story.301.desc.mater

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.301.desc.mater&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在某个恒星系里发现了深空无人机，这些无人机似乎是千年以前进行自动轨道采矿作业的矿机和监视用机器，但在不久之后采矿行动就被人放弃了，只剩无人机在继续运作。它们的功能很明确——采矿——但是是谁下的命令，目前我们还不得而知，很显然没有任何矿物在百年乃至千年内曾从这个恒星系出口过。\n\n尽管这些可能只是老旧的无人机，但是[Root.Owner.GetName]还是应该注意一下它们。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:963`

### English

Reports of deep-space drones in certain systems have been investigated. The drones appear to be the workers and custodians of an autonomous orbital mining operation, established millennia ago and then soon abandoned by all but the drones themselves, judging by the state of the processing equipment. Their purpose is obvious enough - mining - but who it is that tasked them is not immediately apparent. What is obvious is that no shipment of minerals has been retrieved from this system in hundreds, if not thousands of years.\n\nThey may only be old drones, but they should be kept under close watch by the [Root.Owner.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1010`

## story.301.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.301.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在某个恒星系里发现了深空无人机，这些无人机似乎是千年以前进行自动轨道采矿作业的矿机和监视用机器，但在不久之后采矿行动就被人放弃了，只剩无人机在继续运作。无人机利用强力的采矿激光工作，尽管目前没有表现出敌意，这些无人机依然对靠近的[Root.Owner.GetSpeciesAdj]飞船造成了威胁。\n\n这些可能只是老旧的无人机，但是[Root.Owner.GetName]还是应该注意一下它们。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:964`

### English

Reports of deep-space drones in certain systems have been investigated. The drones appear to be the workers and custodians of an autonomous orbital mining operation, established millennia ago and then soon abandoned by all but the drones themselves, judging by the state of the processing equipment. The drones possess powerful mining lasers and make obviously threatening, if not outright hostile, overtures toward [Root.Owner.GetSpeciesAdj] vessels when approached.\n\nThey may only be old drones, but they should be kept under close watch by the [Root.Owner.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1011`

## story.301.desc.gesta

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.301.desc.gesta&lang=en)

### 中文

我们遭遇的深空无人机似乎是自动轨道采矿作业的工人和监工。根据加工设备的状况来判断，这些采矿作业似乎开始于数千年前，然后很快被抛弃，只留下了无人机继续运作。这些无人机拥有强力的采矿激光，并且在我们试图靠近时表现出了明显具有威胁性，甚至是侵略性的举动。\n\n或许它们只是些老旧的无人机，但仍应该密切观察它们。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:965`

### English

The deep-space drones we have encountered appear to be the workers and custodians of autonomous orbital mining operations. Judging by the state of the processing equipment, these sites were established millennia ago and then soon abandoned by all but the drones themselves. The drones possess powerful mining lasers and make obviously threatening, if not outright hostile, overtures toward us when approached.\n\nThey may only be old drones, but they should be kept under close watch.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1012`

## story.301.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.301.a&lang=en)

### 中文

在[Root.Capital.GetName]建立一个监听哨站。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:966`

### English

Establish a listening post on [Root.Capital.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1013`

不知道它们的内部结构是怎么样的——把它们拆开来看看吧。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:967`

### English

Let us see what they are made of - take them apart.

## story.305.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.305.name&lang=en)

### 中文

无人挖掘机工作准则

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:968`

### English

Mining Drone Work Ethic

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1016`

## story.305.desc.pacif

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.305.desc.pacif&lang=en)

### 中文

除了对[Root.GetSpeciesAdj]飞船进行威胁的时候，这些无人机只会进行机械化的采矿工作。\n\n但有趣的是，这些无人机并不是完全静默，它们会发射出声脉冲信号，不知道这些信号到底是发给谁的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:970`

### English

When not posturing with their mining lasers aimed at [Root.GetSpeciesAdj] ships, the drones appear steadfast and dumbly hard-working, hacking away at mineral-poor asteroids for want of something better to do.\n\nWhat is more interesting is that the drones are not completely silent, contrary to earlier assumptions. They emit signal pings, though extremely infrequently and at a wavelength hard to isolate from background noise. If there is anyone left to receive these pings may be a mystery for another time.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1018`

## story.305.desc.mater

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.305.desc.mater&lang=en)

### 中文

这些无人机的挖掘效率比十分优秀，要是[Root.GetSpeciesAdj]的矿工的话，这些地方可能早就被认为是挖无可挖了。我们的高级工程师正在将这些无人机的运作方式应用在[Root.GetName]的复合矿场和太空港中以增加我们的采矿效率，减少能源浪费。\n\n但有趣的是，这些无人机并不是完全静默，它们会发射出声脉冲信号，虽然不是非常频繁，但用的却是一种很难从杂音中识别出的信号。不知道这些信号到底是发给谁的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:971`

### English

The drones are remarkably efficient at squeezing ever-diminishing returns from rocks [Root.GetSpeciesAdj] miners would have soon discarded, deemed exhausted of all worthwhile mineral content. Our top engineers are working on adapting the drones' methodology for use in the [Root.GetName]'s Mining Networks and Stations which should improve efficiency, effectively lessening energy expenditure.\n\nOf lesser import is the fact that the drones are not completely silent, contrary to previous assumptions. They emit signal pings, though extremely infrequently and at a wavelength hard to isolate from background noise. If there is anyone left to receive these pings may be a mystery for another time.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1019`

## story.305.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.305.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

无人机在他们所在的小行星上永无休止的对不断减少的资源进行开采，这一单调的过程很值得研究。[Root.Capital.GetName]上的复合矿场可以从中学到如何在保存能源的同时最大化的提取矿物。\n\n此外，这些无人机并不是完全静默，它们会偶尔发射声脉冲信号，而且这些声脉冲信号的波长很难和背景杂音区分开，是否有人收到这些声脉冲就不得而知了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:972`

### English

The drones and their tireless, endless work of extracting ever-diminishing resources from their local planetoids make for a somewhat dull, but nevertheless informative subject of study. In fact, Mining Networks on [Root.Capital.GetName] could stand to learn a thing or two about maximizing mineral extraction rates while conserving energy.\n\nAdditionally, we have found that the drones are not completely silent. They emit signal pings, though extremely infrequently and at a wavelength hard to isolate from background noise. If there is anyone left to receive these pings may be a mystery for another time.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1020`

## story.305.desc.gesta

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.305.desc.gesta&lang=en)

### 中文

无人机在其小行星上永无休止地对不断减少的资源进行开采。对其进行研究的过程较为枯燥，但仍具有研究价值。事实上，我们可以学到如何在最大化开采效率的同时减少能源消耗。\n\n另外，我们发现无人机并非完全处于静默。它们会发出脉冲信号，不过频率极低并且波长很难与背景杂讯区分开来。是否有人在接收这些脉冲信号就不得而知了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:973`

### English

The drones and their tireless, endless work of extracting ever-diminishing resources from their local planetoids make for a somewhat dull, but nevertheless informative subject of study. In fact, we could stand to learn a thing or two about maximizing mineral extraction rates while conserving energy.\n\nAdditionally, we have found that the drones are not completely silent. They emit signal pings, though extremely infrequently and at a wavelength hard to isolate from background noise. Whether there is anyone left to receive these pings may be a mystery better left for another time.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1021`

### English

搞清楚他们是如何分析采矿点的。

## story.310.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.310.name&lang=en)

### 中文

拆解无人挖掘机

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:978`

### English

Mining Drone Disassembly

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1027`

## story.310.desc.mater

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.310.desc.mater&lang=en)

### 中文

采矿无人机在与[Root.GetSpeciesAdj]飞船的一场激烈的战斗后被摧毁了，幸运的是，采矿无人机中的能源电池是机体中最坚固的部分，因此我们的飞船成功回收了一些没有被摧毁的能源电池。这些能源电池使用的技术远远高于我们目前的技术，但是初步测试表明我们可以将它们的能源输出转化成能源。\n\n在最后一个能源电池用完之后，负责测试的工程师在背景辐射中发现了一些异常，这些无人机在和我们初次相遇后就一直在以一种罕见的频率进行信号广播，但这些信号广播是发给谁的呢？\n\n这些无人机中蕴含大量的能源，但这些能源也是有限的。如果我们发现它们之后能及时停掉它们的话，这些能源就能为我们所用。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:979`

### English

The mining drones were destroyed in a heated battle with [Root.GetSpeciesAdj] vessels. As luck would have it, their energy cells are by far the sturdiest component used in their construction, and some were recovered by our ships. The technology behind these cells is beyond us at present, but initial tests show we are quite capable of converting their output to Energy Credits.\n\nAs the final energy cell bled out, the engineers handling the test noticed a dip in background radiation. It would appear that the drones are not silent, but have been sporadically broadcasting on unusual frequencies since we first encountered them, possibly ever since they were first deployed. It is unlikely that there is anyone left to receive these transmissions.\n\nAt any rate, the drones could prove to be a potent but limited source of energy, should we be able to find and disable them efficiently enough.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1028`

## story.310.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.310.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

采矿无人机在与[Root.GetSpeciesAdj]飞船的一场激烈的战斗后被摧毁了，幸运的是，采矿无人机中的能源电池是机体中最坚固的部分，因此我们的飞船成功回收了一些没有被摧毁的能源电池。这些能源电池使用的技术远远高于我们目前的技术，但是初步测试表明我们可以将它们的能源输出转化成能源。\n\n在最后一个能源电池用完之后，负责测试的工程师在背景辐射中发现了一些异常，这些无人机在和我们初次相遇后就一直在以一种罕见的频率进行信号广播，但这些信号广播是发给谁的呢？\n\n这些无人机中蕴含大量的能源，但这些能源也是有限的。如果我们发现它们之后能及时停掉它们的话，这些能源就能为我们所用。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:980`

### English

The mining drones were destroyed in a heated battle with [Root.GetSpeciesAdj] vessels. As luck would have it, their energy cells are by far the sturdiest component used in their construction, and some were recovered by our ships. The technology behind these cells is beyond us at present, but initial tests show we are quite capable of converting their output to Energy Credits.\n\nAs the final energy cell bled out, the engineers handling the test noticed a dip in background radiation. It would appear that the drones are not silent, but have been sporadically broadcasting on unusual frequencies since we first encountered them, possibly ever since they were first deployed. It is unlikely that there is anyone left to receive these transmissions.\n\nAt any rate, the drones could prove to be a potent but limited source of energy, should we be able to find and disable them efficiently enough.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1029`

## story.310.desc.gesta

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.310.desc.gesta&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在激烈的交战中摧毁了采矿无人机。幸运的是，能源电池是它们身上最为坚固的部件，我们因此有幸缴获了一些。这些电池的科技水平目前超出了我们的理解，但初步测试表明我们有能力将其能源转化为能源。\n\n在最后一颗能源电池耗尽之后，我们注意到了参与实验的[Root.GetUnitDronePlural]周围的背景辐射有了明显下降。看来这些无人机并非是静默的，而是在我们初次遭遇它们之后就偶尔在奇特的频率上发射信号，而且很可能从它们部署之初就开始了。这些信号原本的接收者很可能已经不在了。\n\n总而言之，这些无人机可以成为高效但有限的能源来源，只要我们能够有效率地找到它们并让它们丧失行动能力。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:981`

### English

We destroyed the mining drones in a heated battle. As luck would have it, their energy cells are by far the sturdiest component used in their construction, and some were recovered. The technology behind these cells is beyond us at present, but initial tests show that we are quite capable of converting their output to Energy Credits.\n\nAs the final energy cell bled out, we noticed a dip in background radiation through the [Root.GetUnitDronePlural] handling the test. It would appear that the drones are not silent, but have been sporadically broadcasting on unusual frequencies since we first encountered them, possibly ever since they were first deployed. It is unlikely that there is anyone left to receive these transmissions.\n\nAt any rate, the drones could prove to be a potent but limited source of energy, should we be able to find and disable them efficiently enough.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1030`

## story.320.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.320.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

通过对古代采矿无人机的评估算法进行逆向工程破解，我们找到了所有含大量矿产的存储点并对其进行了调查。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:985`

### English

All high-mineral deposits discovered by the reverse-engineered Ancient Mining Drone evaluation algorithms have been surveyed.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1034`

## story.330.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.330.name&lang=en)

### 中文

古代矿石处理中心

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:986`

### English

Ancient Mineral Processing Hub

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1036`

## story.330.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.330.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetName]在[From.GetName]星系发现了大量的古代采矿无人机。看来这里曾是一个巨大的采矿与矿石处理中心，甚至可能是整个星系中最后一个类似的存在。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:987`

### English

The [Root.GetName] has encountered a significant Ancient Mining Drone presence in the [From.GetName] system. It appears to be a major mining and mineral processing hub, perhaps the last of its kind in the galaxy.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1037`

## story.330.desc.gesta

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.330.desc.gesta&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在[From.GetName]星系发现了大量的古代采矿无人机。看来这里是一个巨大的采矿与矿石处理中心，甚至可能是整个星系中最后一个类似的存在。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:988`

### English

We have encountered a significant Ancient Mining Drone presence in the [From.GetName] system. It appears to be a major mining and mineral processing hub, perhaps the last of its kind in the galaxy.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1038`

### English

## story.331.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.331.name&lang=en)

### 中文

处理过程观察完毕

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:991`

### English

Processing Observed

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1042`

## story.331.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.331.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

事实最终证明，不对这个古代采矿无人机处理中心进行干涉是一个明智的选择。通过对于其单调节奏进行的长期观察，我们成功发现了其采用的数项原材料自动处理技术，其能力远超我们当前的技术水平。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:992`

### English

Leaving the ancient mining drone mineral processing hub undisturbed was, ultimately, a wise decision. By observing its monotonous rhythms over an extended period of time, we have been able to identify several systemic techniques in its automated handling of raw materials that surpass our own.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1043`

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:993`

### English

$story.331.desc$

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1044`

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:994`

### English

Then we make these techniques ours.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1045`

已摧毁采矿无人机基地

### English

Mining Drone Base Destroyed

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1048`

## story.333.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.333.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们收到报告称，[location_planet.GetStarName]星系发现的矿物处理中心已经被摧毁。其储藏的大量原矿石与经过处理的矿石已投弃到宇宙当中，并以陨石形态坠落到了当地小行星带的[location_planet.GetName]。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:997`

### English

We have received reports that the mineral processing hub observed in the [location_planet.GetStarName] system has been destroyed. Its significant stores of raw and processed minerals were jettisoned and have made planetfall on [location_planet.GetName] in the local asteroid belt.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1049`

## story.333.desc.gesta

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.333.desc.gesta&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在[location_planet.GetStarName]星系中观察到的矿石处理中心已经被摧毁了。其储藏的大量原矿石与经过处理的矿石已投弃到宇宙当中，并以陨石形态坠落到了当地小行星带的[location_planet.GetName]。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:998`

### English

The mineral processing hub we observed in the [location_planet.GetStarName] system has been destroyed. Its significant stores of raw and processed minerals were jettisoned and have made planetfall on [location_planet.GetName] in the local asteroid belt.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1050`

## story.401.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.401.name&lang=en)

### 中文

虚空之云

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1003`

### English

Void Clouds

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1056`

## story.401.desc.spiri

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.401.desc.spiri&lang=en)

### 中文

“如云，”一份从多个材料整合出来的报道上写着，“如云般的光辉星辰在暗潮中汹涌”。这份报告描述的极具诗意的现象是“虚空之云”。\n\n对于[Root.Owner.GetName]的祭司来说，这些星云是被某种更伟大的意志所驱动的信使，目前没有人知道在背后操控虚空之云的到底是谁或是什么，在确定虚空之云的来龙去脉之前，还是不要轻举妄动为好。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1004`

### English

"A cloud," reads the awed report, collated from multiple sources. "Like a cloud of luminous star-stuff, billowing in the dark." The report goes on to refer to the phenomenon, somewhat poetically, as a "void cloud."\n\nIt is evident to the priesthoods of the [Root.Owner.GetName] that these clouds are heralds animated by some higher will. Exactly who or what this will belongs is the subject of heated debate, and is likely to remain so for the foreseeable future. Until such time as the Void Cloud's true nature can be ascertained, it would perhaps be prudent to treat the immense power evident in its roiling motion with some caution.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1057`

## story.401.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.401.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一项对此前以§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!为代称的一堆残骸和太空尘埃的调查居然得到了惊人的发现：微粒聚集而成的云体，散发着未知的强大能源，它原本连反射性动作都不应该做出，更不要说是自主移动了。\n\n但它确实在自主地移动着。\n\n这份录像中移动的飘渺物体透着一股不祥之兆，或许这个宇宙里有一些东西是我们不该去碰的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1005`

### English

An investigation into a peculiar cluster of debris and space-dust previously dubbed §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§! has yielded surprising results; it is a particulate cloud bristling with a powerful charge of unknown origin that has no business exhibiting simple reflexive actions, let alone movements with purpose.\n\nAnd yet it does.\n\nThere is something undeniably ominous about the hazy subject of the relayed video feeds. Perhaps there are things in the universe we should not tangle with.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1058`

## story.401.desc.gesta

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.401.desc.gesta&lang=en)

### 中文

一项对此前以§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!指代的一堆残骸和太空尘埃的调查居然得到了惊人的发现：它是一团由微粒聚集而成的云体，蕴含着来源不明的强大能源。它原本连反射性动作都不应该做出，更不要说是自主移动了。\n\n可它确实在自主移动。\n\n这份录像中移动的飘渺物体透着一股不祥之兆，或许这个宇宙里有一些东西是我们不该去碰的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1006`

### English

Our investigation into the peculiar cluster of debris and space-dust with the designation §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§! has yielded surprising results: it is a particulate cloud bristling with a powerful charge of unknown origin. It has no business exhibiting simple reflexive actions, let alone movements with purpose.\n\nAnd yet it does.\n\nThere is something undeniably ominous about the hazy subject of the relayed video feeds. Perhaps there are things in the universe we should not tangle with.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1059`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.401.a.spiri&lang=en)

### English

Conduit for the divine or not, it must be studied.

## story.405.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.405.name&lang=en)

### 中文

虚空渐晰

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1012`

### English

Void Unclouded

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1066`

## story.405.desc.spiri

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.405.desc.spiri&lang=en)

### 中文

虚空之云是宇宙的观察者，在了解了虚空之云的特征之后，[Root.GetSpeciesAdj]的宇航员在宇宙各地都发现了虚空之云的踪迹。\n\n它们产生于万物初始的时候，观察着恒星闪耀、行星新生。现在它们在宇宙间漫无目的地移动着，似乎在有意识地收集过去留下的残骸，在死去的恒星和破碎的世界之间漂流。\n\n它们的存在时刻提醒着我们自己是多么的渺小。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1013`

### English

The Void Clouds are stewards of the cosmos. Once [Root.GetSpeciesAdj] astronomers knew what to look for, they found traces of the Void Clouds everywhere.\n\nFormed at the dawn of creation, they stood watch as stars were lit and young planets formed. Now they drift, quasi-conscious agglomerations of inanimate matter from the earliest hours of time itself, afloat between dying stars and breaking worlds.\n\nThey are a constant reminder that we are but all small parts of something greater.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1067`

## story.405.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.405.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

目前，[Root.GetSpeciesAdj]的研究者认为虚空之云是宇宙中最古老的存在，它们似乎是在宇宙诞生仅仅几十亿年后出现的，这是年轻宇宙用强大的力量凭空创造出来的产物，它们本可以成为恒星，但却没有。\n\n想要解释它们那种原始的动物本能更是困难，但答案的关键可能就在将它们紧密结合在一起的异常强大的电磁场中——这也是在它们出现的时候就存在的东西。可以预见，只要有足够的时间，它奇怪的星尘和引力场循环将会变得和神经回路一样。\n\n还有，尽管彼此之间相距数光年的距离，虚空之云似乎在互相发送脉冲波，这之中可能存在量子纠缠的现象。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1015`

### English

The Void Clouds are, as far as [Root.GetSpeciesAdj] researchers can tell, among the oldest entities in the universe. They seem to have originated just a scant few billion years after matter as we know it first appeared, the tremendous forces of the young cosmos making something out of nothing. They would have been stars once, but were not.\n\nExplaining their apparent animal intelligence is more difficult, but the answer might lie in the abnormally strong electromagnetic fields that keep them together - another product of their primordial beginnings. It is not inconceivable that, given enough time, the circulation of its strange stardust and gravitic fields aligned in a flexible approximation of neural pathways.\n\nMoreover, the Clouds seem to be receiving impulses from one another, if not outright communicating, lightyears apart. Quantum entanglement is suspected to play a role in this phenomenon.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1069`

## story.405.desc.gesta

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.405.desc.gesta&lang=en)

### 中文

虚空之云可能是宇宙中最古老的存在之一。在宇宙中刚开始形成物质之后仅仅数十亿年里，它们似乎就已经出现了，这是年轻宇宙用强大的力量凭空创造出来的产物，它们本可以成为恒星，但却没有。\n\n它们动物般的智慧可能更加难以解释，但答案或许就在维持其形态的强大电磁场中——另一个宇宙诞生之初的产物。只要有足够长的时间，它奇怪的星尘与引力场循环完全有可能会形成类似神经网络或是计算机电路的东西。\n\n而且，这些虚空之云似乎在互相发送脉冲信号，甚至可能是在进行沟通，尽管彼此之间的距离以光年计算。我们怀疑这一现象可能与量子纠缠有关。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1016`

### English

The Void Clouds may be among the oldest entities in the universe. They seem to have originated just a scant few billion years after matter as we know it first appeared, the tremendous forces of the young cosmos making something out of nothing. They would have been stars once, but were not.\n\nExplaining their apparent animal intelligence is more difficult, but the answer might lie in the abnormally strong electromagnetic fields that keep them together - another product of their primordial beginnings. It is not inconceivable that, given enough time, the circulation of its strange stardust and gravitic fields aligned in a flexible approximation of neural pathways or computational circuits.\n\nMoreover, the Clouds seem to be receiving impulses from one another, if not outright communicating, lightyears apart. Quantum entanglement is suspected to play a role in this phenomenon.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1070`

## story.410.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.410.name&lang=en)

乌云密布

### English

Dark Clouds

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1078`

## story.410.desc.spiri

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.410.desc.spiri&lang=en)

### 中文

虚空之云被[From.GetName]摧毁了，但是我们也付出了相当的代价。\n\n虚空之云的电磁场最终崩塌，释放出的星体物质螺旋一样运动。就在电磁场崩塌之前，我们的船员都出现了一种末日即将到来的感觉。\n\n或许我们真的做错了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1025`

### English

Brought down by the [From.GetName], the Void Cloud did not die easy.\n\nJust before the Void Cloud's electromagnetic field finally collapsed - sending its star-stuff spiraling through the system - many crewmembers engaged in the battle reported a sudden sense of dread, of coming doom.\n\nPerhaps we have sinned.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1080`

## story.410.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.410.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们艰难地战胜了虚空之云。\n\n虽然我们伤亡惨重，但是毫无疑问，[Root.GetName]战胜了星系中最可怕的奥秘之一。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1026`

### English

Victory over the Void Cloud was hard-won.\n\nWhile we suffered heavy casualties, the fact remains that the [Root.GetName] has overcome one of the galaxy's most terrifying enigmas.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1081`

## story.410.desc.gesta

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.410.desc.gesta&lang=en)

### 中文

我们花费了巨大的代价才战胜了虚空之云。\n\n尽管我们伤亡惨重，但我们仍征服了星系中最可怕的谜团之一。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1027`

### English

Victory over the Void Cloud was hard-won.\n\nWhile we suffered heavy casualties, the fact remains that we have overcome one of the galaxy's most terrifying enigmas.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1082`

## story.410.a.spiri

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.410.a.spiri&lang=en)

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1028`

### English

How could we atone, not knowing who we have wronged?

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1083`

### English

## story.501.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.501.name&lang=en)

### 中文

缇扬奇

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1030`

### English

Tiyanki

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1086`

## story.501.desc.spiri

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.501.desc.spiri&lang=en)

### 中文

圣者们将那个漂浮在黑暗中的存在称为“§Y缇扬奇§!”。他们是神圣而酷爱和平的太空生命体，从一个恒星系漫步到另一个恒星系，依靠众多气态巨行星上表面的常见气体进食，几乎不会有任何表现敌意的情况。\n\n我们应该与其保持距离，以示敬意。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1031`

### English

The Diviners name the beings, floating through the dark, "§YTiyanki§!." They are a sacred and peaceful spaceborne life form that roams from system to system, feeding on gases common to the upper layers of many gas giants, and rarely showing any signs of hostility.\n\nWe are to keep at a respectful distance.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1087`

## story.501.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.501.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetSpeciesAdj]社会学研究首席研究员将太空生命称为“§Y缇扬奇§!”，它们非常温和，且能够进入低维度亚空间，因此，在各星系间移动对它们而言轻而易举。\n\n他们大多栖息在气态巨行星的上层大气之中。虽然有些难以置信，但空气就是他们唯一的食物来源，但是否还有其他的养分摄取，这一点还有待观察。\n\n它们生性温和，即使受到挑战，也很少会主动攻击。碰上的时候，可以放心大胆地无视他们。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1032`

### English

The spaceborne lifeforms - which the [Root.GetSpeciesAdj] Head of Society Research has come to refer to as "§YTiyanki§!" - are docile creatures. Capable of accessing some lower dimension of subspace, they roam from system to system with remarkable ease.\n\nThey graze on gases common to the upper layers of many gas giants. It is highly unlikely, to say the least, that this is their only food source, but intake of other nutrients has yet to be observed.\n\nThey will rarely, if ever, attack, even when provoked. They can safely be ignored.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1088`

## story.501.desc.gesta

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.501.desc.gesta&lang=en)

### 中文

我们称之为“§Y缇扬奇§!”的太空生物十分温顺。它们可以进入低维度的亚空间，因此可以轻易地在星系之间遨游。\n\n它们以气态巨行星外层常见的气体为食。这不太可能是它们唯一的食物来源，但到目前为止尚未观察到其他的进食方式。\n\n它们不会主动进攻，因此可以安全地无视它们。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1033`

### English

The spaceborne lifeforms - which we call "§YTiyanki§!" - are docile creatures. Capable of accessing some lower dimension of subspace, they roam from system to system with remarkable ease.\n\nThey graze on gases common to the upper layers of many gas giants. It is highly unlikely, to say the least, that this is their only food source, but intake of other nutrients has yet to be observed.\n\nThey will not attack unless provoked. They can safely be ignored.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1089`

## galactic_features.361.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Agalactic_features.361.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在[From.From.From.GetStarName]星系的舰队被全灭后，那里的虚空之云从碎屑中聚集了足够的物质开始进行某种宏观上的有丝分离，也就是无性繁殖。\n\n现在追杀[From.From.From.GetStarName]的虚空之云又多了一个。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1038`

### English

Following the destruction of our fleet in the [From.From.From.GetStarName] system, the local Void Cloud appears to have gathered enough material from the debris to undergo what can best be described as a form of mitosis on the macro level - asexual reproduction.\n\nThere is now one more Void Cloud haunting [From.From.From.GetStarName].

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1095`

发现未开智物种
