### English

Pre-Sapient Species Found

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1110`

## uplift.6000.continental.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Auplift.6000.continental.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

迷人的生物在[From.GetName]的平原和海岸上来回游走。[Root.GetName]报告说，[presapient_species.GetSpeciesClass]生命体正在使用工具，虽然现在使用范围很有限，但最终可能具有实现称为智慧种的潜力。\n\n[Owner.GetScientistCapital][Root.GetLeaderName]强调，在这种状态下发现外星生命是一个罕见的机会。如果我们选择提升它们，它们可能会产生很大的用处。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1052`

### English

Fascinating creatures roam the plains and coasts of [From.GetName]. The [Root.GetName] reports that the [presapient_species.GetSpeciesClass] life-forms are using tools, however limited, and may have the potential for eventually achieving sapience.\n\n[Owner.GetScientistCapital] [Root.GetLeaderName] stresses that finding alien life in this state is a rare opportunity. Should we choose to uplift them, they may prove useful.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1112`

## uplift.6000.ocean.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Auplift.6000.ocean.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

根据[Root.GetName]的说法，一个[From.GetName]的原生[presapient_species.GetSpeciesClass]物种不仅已经初步掌握了这颗星球的广阔海洋，而且还占领了点缀其表面的一小块土地。\n\n[Owner.GetScientistCapital][Root.GetLeaderName]提议，我们应该密切监视他们的发展，并尽可能帮助他们朝着实现完全智性的道路前进。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1053`

### English

According to the [Root.GetName], one [presapient_species.GetSpeciesClass] species native to [From.GetName] has not only achieved a tentative mastery of the planet's vast oceans, but also the small plots of land that dot its surface.\n\n[Owner.GetScientistCapital] [Root.GetLeaderName] proposes that we should monitor their development closely, and possibly help them along the path to full sapience.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1114`

## uplift.6000.tropical.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Auplift.6000.tropical.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.GetName]茂密的热带树林中隐藏着[Root.GetName]的[Owner.GetScientistCapital][Root.GetLeaderName]所说的“元文明”。\n\n组成这个新生社会的[presapient_species.GetSpeciesClass]生物仍然远远落后于进化曲线，但他们已经将社会基础建立在了初步形成的智性语言和抽象思维之上。\n\n[Owner.GetScientistCapital][Root.GetLeaderName]建议，这些特殊的生物可以成为诱导快速进化相关科技的合适的实验平台。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1054`

### English

The dense tropical foliage of [From.GetName] hides what [Owner.GetScientistCapital] [Root.GetLeaderName] of the [Root.GetName] calls a 'proto-civilization.'\n\nThe [presapient_species.GetSpeciesClass] beings that make up this nascent society are still far behind the evolutionary curve, but their social order seems to be underpinned by the beginnings of intelligible language and abstract thought.\n\n[Owner.GetScientistCapital] [Root.GetLeaderName] suggests that these particular creatures may prove a suitable testbed for technologies intended to induce rapid evolution.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1116`

## uplift.6000.arid.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Auplift.6000.arid.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.GetName]上的一个[presapient_species.GetSpeciesClass]物种表现出不同寻常的智慧。在整个星球干燥、多岩的大环境下，这些生物已经进化到可以主宰他们的环境。由[Root.GetName]的[Owner.GetScientistCapital][Root.GetLeaderName]进行的测试表明，通过采用正确的技术，它们进化的速度能够大大加快。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1055`

### English

One [presapient_species.GetSpeciesClass] species on [From.GetName] exhibits unusual intelligence. Throughout the planet's dry and rocky landscape, these creatures have evolved to dominate their environment. Tests run by [Owner.GetScientistCapital] [Root.GetLeaderName] of the [Root.GetName] show that their evolution can be accelerated with the application of the right technology.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1118`

## uplift.6000.desert.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Auplift.6000.desert.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在[From.GetName]的沙漠中穿行，这种[presapient_species.GetSpeciesClass]生命的有趣形式展现出了能进化出更高级理性的相关特质。\n\n尽管物种的发展进程似乎进入平台期已近千年，我们或许能够为进化提供一次科技上的刺激。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1056`

### English

Scurrying through the sands of [From.GetName], a curious form of [presapient_species.GetSpeciesClass] life exhibits many of the traits associated with the evolution of higher reasoning.\n\nWhile the species' development appears to have plateaued some millennia ago, we might be able to provide a technological impetus for evolution.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1120`

## uplift.6000.savannah.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Auplift.6000.savannah.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在[From.GetName]的平原上，人们观察到长长的、优雅的生物在进行初级形式的交流，彼此合作寻找食物，同时躲避行星上的捕食者。[Root.GetName]的[Owner.GetScientistCapital][Root.GetLeaderName]认为这些[presapient_species.GetSpeciesClass]生物可能适用于旨在提高进化速度的相关技术。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1057`

### English

Across the plains of [From.GetName] long, graceful creatures have been observed engaging in rudimentary forms of communication, cooperating with one another to find food and avoid the planet's predators. [Owner.GetScientistCapital] [Root.GetLeaderName] of the [Root.GetName] believes these [presapient_species.GetSpeciesClass] beings may prove suitable for technologies intended to heighten the pace of evolution.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1122`

## uplift.6000.tundra.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Auplift.6000.tundra.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

众所周知，严苛的环境能够催生适应性强的进化，[From.GetName]的严寒环境也不是一个例外。\n\n[Owner.GetScientistCapital][Root.GetLeaderName]对于[presapient_species.GetSpeciesClass]生物中的其中一种产生了特别的兴趣，这种生物能够在行星的结冰表面上滑行。只要在正确的方向上轻轻一推，它们甚至就可以实现智性。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1058`

### English

Harsh environments are known to propel adaptive evolution, and the frigid biosphere of [From.GetName] is no exception.\n\n[Owner.GetScientistCapital] [Root.GetLeaderName] has taken a special interest in one of the [presapient_species.GetSpeciesClass] creatures that skitter across the planet's icy face. With a push in the right direction, they may even achieve sapience.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1124`

## uplift.6000.arctic.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Auplift.6000.arctic.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

虽然[From.GetName]的温度常年处于零度以下，但当地并非不宜居，这个行星也不贫瘠。\n\n几个[presapient_species.GetSpeciesClass]物种已经进化到可以承受这种环境的地步，而且[Owner.GetScientistCapital][Root.GetLeaderName]相信，只要引入特定的外部刺激，这些生物中的一个或多个就有能力实现智性……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1059`

### English

Although [From.GetName]'s sub-zero climate is far from hospitable, the planet is not barren.\n\nSeveral [presapient_species.GetSpeciesClass] species have evolved to withstand the environment, and [Owner.GetScientistCapital] [Root.GetLeaderName] believes that one or more of these creatures is capable of sapience, given the introduction of certain external stimuli...

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1126`

## uplift.6000.alpine.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Auplift.6000.alpine.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

事实证明，[From.GetName]上绵延不绝的山脉为这颗行星的独特生命形式提供了肥沃的环境。特别是一个[presapient_species.GetSpeciesClass]物种已经适应了极端的海拔高度，甚至表现出一种基本的（好似咕噜声）语言交流形式。在正确的刺激下，这些生物或许最终能够实现完全的智性。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1060`

### English

The continent-wide mountain ranges of [From.GetName] have proven a fertile environment for the planet's unique forms of life. One [presapient_species.GetSpeciesClass] species in particular has adapted to the extreme altitude, and has even demonstrated a basic (if guttural) form of verbal communication. With the right stimuli, these creatures may prove capable of full sapience.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1128`

## uplift.6000.nuked.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Auplift.6000.nuked.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

虽然这颗行星上充斥着活物勿近的辐射，但至少有一种先进的生命形式在[From.GetName]上蓬勃发展。[Owner.GetScientistCapital][Root.GetLeaderName]报告说，这种[presapient_species.GetSpeciesClass]生物表现出令人印象深刻的集体决策能力，外部刺激可能会促使它们达到更大的进化高度。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1061`

### English

While the planet is prohibitively irradiated, at least one advanced form of life flourishes on [From.GetName]. [Owner.GetScientistCapital] [Root.GetLeaderName] reports that the [presapient_species.GetSpeciesClass] creatures exhibit impressive collective decision-making skills, and that outside stimulus may spur them to even greater evolutionary heights.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1130`

## uplift.6000.gaia.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Auplift.6000.gaia.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.GetName]的环境对于生命的进化来说近乎完美；其丰富的自然资源为种类繁多的动植物类群缔造了一个丰饶的家园。在这个天堂里，[Owner.GetScientistCapital][Root.GetLeaderName]发现了一个由智能[presapient_species.GetSpeciesClass]生物组成的原始社会，它可以成为快速进化的绝佳候选者。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1062`

### English

[From.GetName] is a near-perfect environment for the evolution of life; its bountiful natural resources are a fertile home for a staggering variety of flora and fauna. Within this paradise, [Owner.GetScientistCapital] [Root.GetLeaderName] has discovered a proto-society of intelligent [presapient_species.GetSpeciesClass] creatures - one that could prove an excellent candidate for rapid evolution.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1132`

## uplift.6000.relic.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Auplift.6000.relic.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在[From.GetName]的废墟高塔和尘土飞扬的街道中，游荡着一种有趣的未开智的生命。[Owner.GetScientistCapital][Root.GetLeaderName]认为，这些小而聪明的[presapient_species.GetSpeciesClass]生物可能曾经被当作宠物饲养。根据这一理论：一旦它们的主人物种衰落，这些动物就会迅速进化，如果有合适的外部因素，它们现在已经具备了实现完全智慧的条件。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1063`

### English

Amid the ruined towers and dusty streets of [From.GetName] roams an intriguing form of pre-sapient life. [Owner.GetScientistCapital] [Root.GetLeaderName] suggests that the small, intelligent [presapient_species.GetSpeciesClass] creatures may once have been kept as pets. According to this theory: once their owner species declined, these animals rapidly evolved and are now primed to achieve full sapience, should the right external factors be applied.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1134`

## uplift.6000.fallback.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Auplift.6000.fallback.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

根据[Root.GetName]，一个[From.GetName]的原生[presapient_species.GetSpeciesClass]物种实现了对行星环境的初步掌控。\n\n[Owner.GetScientistCapital][Root.GetLeaderName]建议对它们的发展进行密切监视，如果可能的话，将它们提升到拥有完全智慧的条件。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1064`

### English

According to the [Root.GetName], one [presapient_species.GetSpeciesClass] species native to [From.GetName] has achieved a tentative mastery of the planet's environment.\n\n[Owner.GetScientistCapital] [Root.GetLeaderName] recommends monitoring their development closely, and - if possible - uplifting them to full sapience.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1136`

## uplift.6000.wilderness.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Auplift.6000.wilderness.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetName]发现有个体型智慧生命居住在[from.GetName]。考虑到缺乏共生智能的指导，这种[presapient_species.GetSpeciesClass]物种已经达到了对环境的显著控制程度。\n\n我们会继续密切关注这种生物的发展，也许有一天将其并入我们。这些生物的出现预示着[from.GetName]觉醒的潜质。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1065`

### English

The [Root.GetName] has discovered individualistic, presapient life dwelling on [from.GetName]. This [presapient_species.GetSpeciesClass] species has achieved a remarkable degree of mastery over its environment already, considering the lack of a Mutualistic Intelligence to guide it.\n\nWe would do well to monitor their development closely, and perhaps one day bring them into our fold. The presence of these creatures bodes well for the potential awakening of [from.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1138`

## uplift.6000.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Auplift.6000.a&lang=en)

### 中文

是的，我们有那种知识。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1066`

### English

Yes, we have that knowledge.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1140`

## uplift.6000.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Auplift.6000.b&lang=en)

### 中文

以后再说吧。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1067`

### English

Something to keep in mind for the future.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1141`

## uplift.6000.nomads

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Auplift.6000.nomads&lang=en)

### 中文

将他们带上船。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1068`

### English

Bring some of them aboard!

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1142`

## uplift.6010.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Auplift.6010.name&lang=en)

### 中文

提升成功

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1070`

### English

Uplift Successful

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1145`

## uplift.6010.continental.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Auplift.6010.continental.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetName]上的主导物种，[From.GetName]，已经被提升了。只需要轻微的基因改造，就可以释放出他们内在的潜力，使他们的认知能力和操纵的灵活性得到极大的提高，而前几代的成员将被安装上小型的义体植入物。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1071`

### English

The dominant species on [Root.GetName], the [From.GetName], have been uplifted. Only minor genetic alterations were needed to unlock their inherent potential for vastly improved cognitive ability and manual dexterity, while members of previous generations will be fitted with small, cybernetic implants.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1147`

## uplift.6010.ocean.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Auplift.6010.ocean.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们让[From.GetName]沿着有利的轨迹加速进化的工作取得了巨大的成功。教育计划已经为最年轻的样本部署妥当——一些个人，现在——而他们的长辈则可以接受心理基因调节。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1072`

### English

Our efforts to accelerate the evolution of the [From.GetName] along a favorable trajectory have been tremendously successful. Educational programs are in place for the youngest specimens - individuals, now - while their elders are proving receptive to psycho-genetic conditioning.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1149`

## uplift.6010.tropical.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Auplift.6010.tropical.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.GetName]已经被成功提升完毕，成为了合格的[Root.Owner.GetName]人，并住进了我们在热带星球[Root.GetName]上建立的殖民地。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1073`

### English

The [From.GetName] have been successfully uplifted to become productive citizens of the [Root.Owner.GetName] and inhabitants of our colony on the tropical planet [Root.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1151`

## uplift.6010.arid.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Auplift.6010.arid.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

成功了！\n\n[This.GetName]当地的[From.GetName]已被提升为[This.Owner.GetName]人口。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1074`

### English

Success!\n\nThe [From.GetName] native to [This.GetName] have been uplifted to become citizens of the [This.Owner.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1153`

## uplift.6010.desert.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Auplift.6010.desert.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.GetName]现在就是[Root.Owner.GetName]科技实力的证明。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1075`

### English

The [From.GetName] now stand as living testament to the scientific and technological might of the [Root.Owner.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1155`

## uplift.6010.savannah.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Auplift.6010.savannah.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们提升[From.GetName]的工作成效显著：只需对它们的基因密码进行相对少许的修饰，我们就成功地引导它们进化成为了有认知能力的同僚。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1076`

### English

Our efforts to uplift the [From.GetName] have borne fruit: with some relatively minor modifications to their genetic code, we have succeeded in raising their cognitive faculties.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1157`

## uplift.6010.tundra.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Auplift.6010.tundra.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.GetName]现在是智慧生命体，而且相当聪明。\n\n它们现在处于[Root.Owner.GetName]的统治之下。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1077`

### English

The [From.GetName] are now sapient, and fairly intelligent at that.\n\nThey now fall under the dominion of the [Root.Owner.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1159`

## uplift.6010.arctic.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Auplift.6010.arctic.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在[Root.GetName]冰雪平原上生活的[From.GetName]已经在[Root.Owner.GetSpeciesName]的帮助下获得了感知能力。\n\n现在他们已经加入了我们建于他们星球[Root.GetName]上的殖民地，并成为了[Root.Owner.GetName]社会的一员。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1078`

### English

The [From.GetName], risen from the icy plains of [Root.GetName], have achieved sapience with [Root.Owner.GetSpeciesName] help.\n\nThey have joined our colony on their native planet [Root.GetName] as productive members of [Root.Owner.GetName] society.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1161`

## uplift.6010.alpine.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Auplift.6010.alpine.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们对[From.GetName]进行了基因改造，成功让这个物种进化到了完全的智性。现在，当它们自崎岖的山峦中遥望星空时，它们对自我有了更加真实的感知。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1079`

### English

The genetic modifications we made to the [From.GetName] have successfully raised the species to full sapience. Now, as they gaze up at the stars from their mountain homes, they do so with a true sense of themselves.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1163`

## uplift.6010.nuked.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Auplift.6010.nuked.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

自卑微的起始，[From.GetName]已经进化到能够加入[Root.Owner.GetName]。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1080`

### English

From their humble beginnings, the [From.GetName] have risen to join the [Root.Owner.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1165`

## uplift.6010.gaia.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Auplift.6010.gaia.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

多亏了我们的努力，[From.GetName]已经被成功提升。能让它们彻底理解自己居住在怎样的天堂上，这是何等的福气。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1081`

### English

Thanks to our efforts, the [From.GetName] have been successfully uplifted. What a gift, to fully understand the paradise they inhabit.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1167`

## uplift.6010.relic.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Auplift.6010.relic.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

环顾它们的遗迹星球，[Root.GetName]的主导物种从常年的沉睡中苏醒过来。我们将它们提升至完全智性的工作成为了令人瞩目的成功。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1082`

### English

All across their ruined world, the dominant species of [Root.GetName] have awoken from their long slumber. Our efforts to uplift them to full sapience have been a resounding success.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1169`

## uplift.6010.fallback.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Auplift.6010.fallback.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.GetName]经提升实现了完全的智性，现在正作为[Root.Owner.GetName]科学与技术能力的活的证明。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1083`

### English

Uplifted to full sapience, the [From.GetName] now stand as a living testament to the scientific and technological might of the [Root.Owner.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1171`

## uplift.6010.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Auplift.6010.a&lang=en)

### 中文

[This.Owner.GetName]非常欢迎它们。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1084`

### English

The [This.Owner.GetName] welcomes them.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1173`

## uplift.6010.tt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Auplift.6010.tt&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[This.Owner.GetName]§!获得§Y50$r_influence$§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1085`

### English

The §Y[This.Owner.GetName]§! gains §Y50 $r_influence$§!.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1174`

## uplift.7000.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Auplift.7000.name&lang=en)

### 中文

起来，跟上

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1089`

### English

Lift Up, Follow Up

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1180`

## uplift.7000.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Auplift.7000.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.From.From.GetName]已经与我们相处了数日了。\n\n[From.GetName]行星上的情况目前一切安好。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1090`

### English

The [From.From.From.GetName] have been among us for several days now.\n\nAll is well on planet [From.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1181`

## uplift.7010.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Auplift.7010.name&lang=en)

### 中文

艰难的开始

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1091`

### English

A Rocky Start

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1182`

## uplift.7010.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Auplift.7010.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

经过我们提升的[From.From.From.GetName]的整合比预期的要顺利，但没有我们希望的那样顺利。\n\n尽管我们已经尽量对[From.From.From.GetName]进行了心理学软塑造，但是他们仍然不太适应我们在[From.Owner.GetName]的行事方式。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1092`

### English

The integration of the [From.From.From.GetName] who we uplifted is going more smoothly than expected, but not as smoothly as we had hoped.\n\nDespite our efforts to soft-shape of their psychology, the [From.From.From.GetName] still occasionally struggle with the way we do things in the [From.Owner.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1183`

## uplift.7200.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Auplift.7200.name&lang=en)

### 中文

关注内部

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1098`

### English

Looking Inwards

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1192`

## uplift.7200.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Auplift.7200.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

相当多的[From.From.From.GetName]公民对我们教授的宇宙学说似乎很不满。\n\n他们中的某个未知个体或者组织正在传播一些虚假信息，过分强调了[From.From.From.GetName]、以及[Root.Owner.GetName]他们进行提升一事的重要性。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1099`

### English

A statistically significant group of [From.From.From.GetName] citizens appear unsatisfied with our teachings on the nature of the cosmos.\n\nAn unknown individual or group among them is circulating some spurious codex that attaches undue importance to the [From.From.From.GetName] and their uplifting by the [Root.Owner.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1193`

## uplift.7200.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Auplift.7200.a&lang=en)

### 中文

这种事肯定会无疾而终。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1100`

### English

Nothing will come of it.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1194`

## uplift.7200.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Auplift.7200.b&lang=en)

### 中文

让他们保留自己的神秘感吧。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1101`

### English

Let them have their mysteries.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1195`

## uplift.7200.b.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Auplift.7200.b.tooltip&lang=en)

### English

The [From.From.From.GetName] gain the §YSpiritualist§! Ethic.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1196`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Auplift.7200.c&lang=en)

不惜一切代价让他们回归正道。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1103`

### English

Set them straight by any means necessary.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1197`

[From.From.From.GetName]获得了§Y唯物主义§!思潮。

## uplift.7205.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Auplift.7205.name&lang=en)

### 中文

拒绝加入军团者

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1105`

### English

Conscientious Objectors

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1199`

## uplift.7205.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Auplift.7205.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

由于[From.From.From.GetName]的人们对武器的厌恶，[Root.GetName]的人们提出了划定非战斗区域的正式请愿。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1106`

### English

Some issues have arisen on [Root.GetName] as a result of the [From.From.From.GetName] populace's aversion to arms. A representative has made a formal request for clearly delineated demilitarized zones.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1200`

## uplift.7205.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Auplift.7205.a&lang=en)

### 中文

很好。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1107`

### English

Very well.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1201`

## uplift.7205.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Auplift.7205.b&lang=en)

### 中文

他们坚持信念的勇气值得赞扬。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1108`

### English

The courage of their convictions should be celebrated.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1202`

## uplift.7205.b.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Auplift.7205.b.tooltip&lang=en)

[From.From.From.GetName]获得了§Y和平主义§!思潮。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1109`

### English

The [From.From.From.GetName] gain the §YPacifist§! Ethic.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1203`

## uplift.7205.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Auplift.7205.c&lang=en)

### 中文

他们必须明白战争的阴影时刻笼罩着我们。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1110`

### English

They must learn that the specter of war looms over us all.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1204`

## uplift.7205.c.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Auplift.7205.c.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.From.From.GetName]获得了§Y军国主义§!思潮。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1111`

### English

The [From.From.From.GetName] gain the §YMilitarist§! Ethic.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1205`

## uplift.7210.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Auplift.7210.name&lang=en)

### 中文

陌生人危机

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1112`

### English

Stranger Danger

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1206`

## uplift.7210.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Auplift.7210.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

根据报道，在[Root.GetName]上刚刚受到提升的[From.From.From.GetName]公民开始担忧，因为他们的[Root.Owner.GetSpeciesName]老师提到了和外太空的某种未知存在交流的可能性。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1113`

### English

According to reports, newly-uplifted [From.From.From.GetName] citizens on [Root.GetName] grow anxious as their [Root.Owner.GetSpeciesName] teachers bring up the likelihood of contact with as-yet-unknown entities from the far reaches of space.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1207`

## uplift.7210.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Auplift.7210.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我知道了……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1114`

### English

I see...

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1208`

## uplift.7210.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Auplift.7210.b&lang=en)

### 中文

所谓的外星生物，对我们来说不过是另一个机会罢了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1115`

### English

What is alien to us is simply opportunity in disguise.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1209`

## uplift.7210.b.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Auplift.7210.b.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.From.From.GetName]获得了§Y亲外主义§!思潮。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1116`

### English

The [From.From.From.GetName] gain the §YXenophile§! Ethic.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1210`

## uplift.7210.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Auplift.7210.c&lang=en)

### 中文

他们对未知事物的恐惧是很正常的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1117`

### English

They are right to fear the unknown.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1211`

## uplift.7210.c.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Auplift.7210.c.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.From.From.GetName]获得了§Y排外主义§!思潮。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1118`

### English

The [From.From.From.GetName] gain the §YXenophobe§! Ethic.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1212`

## uplift.7215.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Auplift.7215.name&lang=en)

### 中文

内变

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1119`

### English

Familial Fission

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1213`

## uplift.7215.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Auplift.7215.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

尽管[Root.Owner.GetName]提供了一个相当可行的结构框架，我们可以利用这个框架来调节他们的内部社会序列；只不过这可能并不适合经过提升的[From.From.From.GetName]。\n\n让他们的智能突然变得与我们相当，这剥夺了他们数千年中社会与文化发展的潜在可能。因此，[From.From.From.GetName]的社会秩序极不稳定，比如今天他们奉行家族至上，明天社区领袖可能就会宣布取消所有家族之间的关系。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1120`

### English

While the [Root.Owner.GetName] provides a solid structure upon which to model their internal social order, it may not necessarily be what suits the uplifted [From.From.From.GetName] best.\n\nTheir sudden rise to becoming our intellectual equals robbed them of millennia of potential societal and cultural development. Perhaps as a result of this, the [From.From.From.GetName] social order is in a state of extreme flux; one day the family unit is paramount, and the next day community leaders are calling for a dissolution of all clan-like bonds.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1214`

## uplift.7215.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Auplift.7215.a&lang=en)

### 中文

很迷人。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1121`

### English

Fascinating.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1215`

## uplift.7215.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Auplift.7215.b&lang=en)

### 中文

希望[From.From.From.GetName]能在同族之中发现自身的价值。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1122`

### English

May the [From.From.From.GetName] find themselves, in each other.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1216`

## uplift.7215.b.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Auplift.7215.b.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.From.From.GetName]得到§Y威权主义§!思潮。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1123`

### English

The [From.From.From.GetName] gain the §YAuthoritarian§! Ethic.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1217`

## uplift.7215.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Auplift.7215.c&lang=en)

### 中文

只有独立自主才能实现自我价值。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1124`

### English

Self-realization comes only through independence.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1218`

## uplift.7215.c.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Auplift.7215.c.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.From.From.GetName]得到§Y平等主义§!思潮。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1125`

### English

The [From.From.From.GetName] gain the §YEgalitarian§! Ethic.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1219`

## uplift.7500.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Auplift.7500.name&lang=en)

### 中文

支链

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1129`

### English

Forked Chain

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1225`

## uplift.7500.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Auplift.7500.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.GetName]的一批未开化物种不知为何进入了我们殖民地外围的安保隔离区域，尽管他们在不久之后就被赶出去了，但他们已经对我们专门负责运输货币的几条星际上行运输线造成了严重的破坏。\n\n恐怕我们要失去几百能量币。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1130`

### English

A number of individuals belonging to the pre-sapient, native population of [From.GetName] somehow got into a secure compound on the outskirts of our colony. They were chased off within hours, but not before severely damaging several interstellar uplinks dedicated to currency transfers.\n\nWe fear several hundred Credits may have been lost in the ether.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1226`

## uplift.7510.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Auplift.7510.name&lang=en)

### 中文

活体实验

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1131`

### English

In Vivo

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1227`

## uplift.7510.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Auplift.7510.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetName]的外星生物研究员发表了一封公开信，要求当地立法部门解除对接近智慧生物进行实验的限制。\n\n他们从科研角度出发阐述了自己的观点，警告称如果法律得不到修改，[Root.GetName]的整个科研团体的实验效率都会下降。\n\n其中涉及的伦理问题仍有争议。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1132`

### English

Xeno-researchers on [Root.GetName] have published an open letter demanding that the local legislation restricting experiments on near-sapient alien species be relaxed.\n\nThey make a convincing case for the scientific benefits, and warn that the output of the entire scientific community on [Root.GetName] will suffer unless the laws are revised.\n\nThe ethics of it all remain questionable.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1228`

## uplift.7510.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Auplift.7510.a&lang=en)

### 中文

无视那些自大狂。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1133`

### English

Ignore the pompous poindexters.

## uplift.7520.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Auplift.7520.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetName]的近智慧生物正在我们的殖民地外围闹事。[Root.Owner.GetName]的存在似乎激怒了他们，他们一直不停的在殖民地的边界和东边的某处之间来回往返。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1140`

### English

The near-sapient natives of [Root.GetName] are causing a ruckus on the outskirts of our colony. Something about the [Root.Owner.GetName]'s continued presence here visibly agitates them, as packs of them scurry to and fro between the settlement's borders and some unknown point to the east.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1236`

### English

### English

## uplift.7525.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Auplift.7525.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一群异种学家和一队军事人员一起离开了殖民地，去追寻当地生命的行踪。\n\n他们发现的是某种古老的播种船的残骸，这显然是某个小型[Owner.GetPreFTLLower]文明的核心，它们早就腐朽成虚无了。\n\n[Root.GetName]的优势本地物种似乎是某种古老的播种或基因编辑程序的产物；从播种船周围的结构来看，他们显然曾经存在部落，并拥有更高的智力，甚至还有一种基本的书面语言。\n\n我们无从猜测是什么导致了它们发生了物种层面上的退化，但它们的行为本身更有意义了。他们近乎本能地认为，我们[Root.Owner.GetSpeciesNamePlural]与他们最初来自星间的提升者相似。也许我们井然有序的社会唤醒了某种被它们更卑鄙的本性所淹没的形成秩序的冲动。\n\n也许我们最终可以通过将他们提升到我们的水平来缓解他们的进化焦虑，但就目前而言，我们已经对古代太空文化对有机体的操纵有了宝贵的见解。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1147`

### English

A group of xenologists departed the colony together with a contingent of military personnel, tracking the movements of the native life-forms.\n\nWhat they found were the remains of some ancient seed-ship, evidently the nucleus of some small, [Owner.GetPreFTLLower] civilization, long since decayed into nothing.\n\nThe dominant native species of [Root.GetName] seem to be the product of some age-old seeding or genetic manipulation program; going by the structures surrounding the seed ship, they were once distinctly tribal, with greater intelligence and even a rudimentary written language.\n\nWe cannot begin to guess what caused their species-wide devolution, but their behavior makes more sense now. On some instinctual level they recognize us [Root.Owner.GetSpeciesNamePlural] as similar to their original uplifters from the stars. Perhaps our ordered society calls out to some instilled impulse to order, drowned out by their baser nature.\n\nMaybe we can eventually ease their evolutionary anxiety by uplifting them to our level, but for now, we have gained valuable insights into the manipulation of organics by ancient spacefaring cultures.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1243`

## cloud_chain_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acloud_chain_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

所有的虚空之云在量子层面上都是相连的；这些散发能源的个体样本很可能就是一个单独的亚空间实体的多个表示形式。\n\n对一个虚空之云进行了仔细研究之后，我们找到了其他可能富含这些云的星系。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1176`

### English

All Void Clouds are entangled with one another on the quantum level; it is possible that the energies animating the individual specimens are nothing but multiple instances of a singular sub-space entity.\n\nThrough careful study of one Void Cloud, we have been able to locate a number of systems that are likely to house other Clouds.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1282`

## old_gods_chain_title

## old_gods_chain_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aold_gods_chain_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在发现神秘的未叙尊者的小行星古龛之后，首席科学官认为可能还有其他关于我们——或者自以为是我们——供奉的古神宫殿存在于附近的恒星系中。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1196`

### English

After the discovery of the mysterious asteroid-shrine to the Untold, top scientists have proposed that there may be additional shrines dedicated to our - or what we believed was only our - pantheon, in neighboring systems.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1308`

## ransomeer_chain_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aransomeer_chain_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一群自称为“星际绑架者”的海盗关押了[Root.GetSpeciesAdj]私人运输飞船的船员。\n\n我们想在海盗们提出条件之前就救出他们。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1199`

### English

A band of pirates calling themselves the "Ransomeers" have kidnapped the crew of a [Root.GetSpeciesAdj] private transport vessel.\n\nWe intend to free them before the pirates can make any demands.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1312`

## wrecked_fleet_chain_title

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Awrecked_fleet_chain_title&lang=en)

### 中文

漂流的战场

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1203`

### English

Drifting Battlefield

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1318`

## wrecked_fleet_chain_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Awrecked_fleet_chain_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

千年之前，某舰队里的成员背弃了盟约，将枪口对准了彼此。在知己知彼的情况下，盟友的飞船顷刻之间就被消灭了，只留下了失去压力的飞船外壳。\n\n我们收集了足够的信息，可以对这些舰队残骸在宇宙中缓慢漂移的过程进行建模。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1204`

### English

Millennia ago, treachery saw a fleet of allied ships turn on each other. With swift strikes to critical systems and known weak points in the hull designs, the formerly allied crews were wiped out with ruthless efficiency, supposedly leaving the depressurized hulks of the ships largely intact.\n\nWe have gleaned enough information to model this ship graveyard's slow drift through the cosmos.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1319`

## wrecked_fleet_poi_title

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Awrecked_fleet_poi_title&lang=en)

预计位置

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1205`

### English

Projected Location

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1320`

## wrecked_fleet_poi_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Awrecked_fleet_poi_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们已经计算出漂移战场在当前星系的大致位置。有机会的话，最好能去调查一下现场。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1206`

### English

We have calculated the drifting battlefield's current position to be somewhere in the indicated system. It would be prudent to investigate the site, given the opportunity.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1321`

## rogue_scientist_1_chain_desc

### 中文

[Root.GetName]理论科学委员会的主席和一架非常规科研船原型机一起消失了。\n\n我们已经将他们的下落锁定到三个可能的地点，可以依次排查。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1210`

### English

The Chair of the [Root.GetName]'s Abstract Sciences Board has gone missing along with an unorthodox Science Ship prototype.\n\nTheir current whereabouts have been narrowed down to three likely locations, to be investigated in turn.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1327`

## rogue_scientist_3_chain_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Arogue_scientist_3_chain_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

无论他们的动机是什么，必须把首席带回[Root.Capital.GetName]。\n\n他们是要写下革命性的论文还是只受到纪律处分呢？我们拭目以待吧。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1216`

### English

Whatever their motives may be, the Chair must be brought back to [Root.Capital.GetName].\n\nWhether they will write a revolutionary thesis or simply face disciplinary action remains to be seen.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1337`

## scientist_poi_2_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ascientist_poi_2_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

根据他们传给[Root.Capital.GetName]的最后信息来看，这就是首席的目的地。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1221`

### English

The Chair's destination, as per their last message to [Root.Capital.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1344`

## scientist_poi_3

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ascientist_poi_3&lang=en)

### 中文

预计超光速终点

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1222`

### English

Projected FTL Endpoint

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1345`

## scientist_poi_3_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ascientist_poi_3_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

碰巧的是，[Root.Capital.GetName]的一个远距离传感列阵检测到了疑似超光速旅行留下的微弱能源信号。\n\n此路径与已知的任何航线均不匹配，很可能是首席科学官留下的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1223`

### English

As luck would have it, a long-distance sensor array on [Root.Capital.GetName] picked up faint energy signatures that may or may not have resulted from FTL travel.\n\nThe trajectory does not match any known flight paths, making the Chair a likely suspect.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1346`

## scientist_poi_4

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ascientist_poi_4&lang=en)

### 中文

继续追踪

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1224`

### English

The Hunt Continues

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1347`

## scientist_poi_4_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ascientist_poi_4_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在破解了科研船原型机的特殊能源特征后，我们又进行了一些复杂的数学运算，终于知道首席逃到了哪个星系。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1225`

### English

Having deciphered the prototype Science Ship's peculiar energy signatures and applied some fairly complex mathematics, we now know what system the Chair fled to.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1348`

## scientist_poi_5

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ascientist_poi_5&lang=en)

一丝机会

### English

The Window

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1349`

## scientist_poi_5_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ascientist_poi_5_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

看来首席快要到达他们的最终目的地了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1227`

### English

It seems the Chair is about to reach their final destination.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1350`

## mass_extinction_chain_title

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Amass_extinction_chain_title&lang=en)

### 中文

古往今来的大灭绝

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1229`

### English

Mass Extinction Through the Ages

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1354`

## mass_extinction_chain_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Amass_extinction_chain_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

为了避免[Root.Capital.GetName]上可能发生的大灾难造成严重后果，我们必须研究其他星球上的灾难都造成了什么后果。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1230`

### English

To avoid a potential apocalypse on [Root.Capital.GetName], we must study the effects that major calamities have had on other planets.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1355`

## massex_analyzed

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Amassex_analyzed&lang=en)

### 中文

重建大灭绝场景

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1231`

### English

Mass Extinctions Reconstructed

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1357`

崩坏的行星

Broken Planet

## massex_poi_1_desc

### 中文

环绕行星的小行星带成了曾经巨大灾难的唯一见证。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1233`

### English

An asteroid belt orbits a star as the only witness to some great cataclysm.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1360`

燃烧的行星

Burned Planet

## massex_poi_2_desc

### 中文

这个行星被自身地狱般的熔岩吞噬了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1235`

### English

This planet wallows in its own infernal crucible.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1362`

## massex_poi_3

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Amassex_poi_3&lang=en)

### 中文

荒芜的行星

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1236`

### English

Desolate Planet

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1363`

## massex_poi_3_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Amassex_poi_3_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这个行星虽然非常适合生命的繁衍，但十分荒芜。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1237`

### English

This planet is impressively barren, despite advantageous conditions for sustaining life.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1364`

## massex_poi_4

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Amassex_poi_4&lang=en)

### 中文

充满毒气的行星

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1238`

### English

Noxious Planet

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1365`

## massex_poi_4_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Amassex_poi_4_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

充斥整个行星的大气层的毒气似乎是最近突然出现的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1239`

### English

The toxic fumes that now dominate this planet's atmosphere appear to have originated fairly recently and suddenly.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1366`

## massex_poi_5

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Amassex_poi_5&lang=en)

### 中文

寒冷的行星

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1240`

### English

Chilly Planet

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1367`

## massex_poi_5_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Amassex_poi_5_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

天文观测数据显示这颗星球过去没这么寒冷荒芜。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1241`

### English

Astronomical records indicate that this planet was not always a frozen wasteland.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1368`

## UPLIFT_SNAKEOID_CAT

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AUPLIFT_SNAKEOID_CAT&lang=en)

滑动

### English

Slithering

## UPLIFT_SNAKEOID_CAT_DESC
