### 中文

有什么东西正在[Root.GetName]的沙中移动……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1246`

### English

Something moves with near-purpose through the sands of [Root.GetName]...

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1375`

## UPLIFT_GENERIC_CAT

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AUPLIFT_GENERIC_CAT&lang=en)

### 中文

似乎是某种近亲

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1247`

### English

Kinship, of a Sort

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1376`

## UPLIFT_GENERIC_CAT_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AUPLIFT_GENERIC_CAT_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

有什么东西正在[Root.GetName]下面移动……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1248`

### English

Something moves with near-purpose down on [Root.GetName]...

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1377`

## UPLIFT_OCEAN_CAT

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AUPLIFT_OCEAN_CAT&lang=en)

### 中文

水生生物

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1249`

### English

The Life Aquatic

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1378`

## UPLIFT_OCEAN_CAT_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AUPLIFT_OCEAN_CAT_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetName]的海域及其周边都有非常明显的生命迹象。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1250`

### English

There are distinct signs of life in and near the seas of [Root.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1379`

## UPLIFT_ARID_CAT

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AUPLIFT_ARID_CAT&lang=en)

### 中文

监视荒漠

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1251`

### English

Stalking the Wastes

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1380`

## UPLIFT_ARID_CAT_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AUPLIFT_ARID_CAT_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

即使是性能较弱的光学阵列也能轻易识别出[Root.GetName]地表的生物活动迹象。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1252`

### English

Even weak optical arrays can easily pick out traces of biological activity on the surface of [Root.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1381`

## UPLIFT_CONTINENTAL_CAT

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AUPLIFT_CONTINENTAL_CAT&lang=en)

### 中文

陆与海之间

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1253`

### English

Between Land and Sea

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1382`

## UPLIFT_CONTINENTAL_CAT_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AUPLIFT_CONTINENTAL_CAT_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

即使在一个一半是岩石类土壤的陆地、一半是海水的行星上生命也能顽强地生存下来。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1254`

### English

It seems life has found a way, even on a planet torn between the extremes of rocky soils and liquid seas.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1383`

## UPLIFT_TROPICAL_CAT

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AUPLIFT_TROPICAL_CAT&lang=en)

### 中文

相当潮湿

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1255`

### English

Fairly Humid

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1384`

## UPLIFT_TROPICAL_CAT_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AUPLIFT_TROPICAL_CAT_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetName]的环境很适合生命繁衍，至少很适合微生物——再大些的生物应该也行。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1256`

### English

The conditions on [Root.GetName] are ideal for life, at least on the microbial level - and maybe beyond.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1385`

## UPLIFT_TUNDRA_CAT

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AUPLIFT_TUNDRA_CAT&lang=en)

### 中文

低温生物

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1257`

### English

Cryostatic Biology

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1386`

## UPLIFT_TUNDRA_CAT_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AUPLIFT_TUNDRA_CAT_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetName]天寒地冻，光学扫描效果不好，但热度特征显示地表下存在一些生命活动。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1258`

### English

[Root.GetName] is frozen and anonymous to optical scans, but heat signatures indicate some activity just below the surface.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1387`

## UPLIFT_ARCTIC_CAT

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AUPLIFT_ARCTIC_CAT&lang=en)

### 中文

在厚厚的冰上

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1259`

### English

On Thick Ice

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1388`

## UPLIFT_ARCTIC_CAT_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AUPLIFT_ARCTIC_CAT_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetName]的永冻层与暴风雪之间有生物在移动。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1260`

### English

Somewhere below the ice-storms and above the permafrost of [Root.GetName], there is movement.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1389`

## UPLIFT_DESERT_CAT

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AUPLIFT_DESERT_CAT&lang=en)

### 中文

漫天黄沙

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1261`

### English

Billowing Sands

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1390`

## UPLIFT_DESERT_CAT_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AUPLIFT_DESERT_CAT_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

很难想象[Root.GetName]上会有生命，地表是无尽的硅酸盐分子海洋。但就算这样……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1262`

### English

It is hard to imagine life on [Root.GetName], its surface an endless sea of silicate particles. And yet...

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1391`

## UPLIFT_ROACHOID_CAT

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AUPLIFT_ROACHOID_CAT&lang=en)

### 中文

匍匐于行星表面

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1263`

### English

Crawling, on the Planet's Face

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1392`

## UPLIFT_ROACHOID_CAT_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AUPLIFT_ROACHOID_CAT_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

节肢动物们在[Root.GetName]上轻松地移动。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1264`

### English

Arthropodal herds navigate [Root.GetName]'s blasted surface with ease.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1393`

## anomaly_failure.2500.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly_failure.2500.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

近距离观察的话，小行星[From.From.GetName]非常宁静。[Root.GetLeaderName]的一个助手终于承认他们可能误读了仪器数据。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1270`

### English

On closer inspection, the asteroid [From.From.GetName] is found to be silent. One of [Root.GetLeaderName]'s assistants eventually confesses that they may have misread the instruments.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1401`

## anomaly_failure.2501.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly_failure.2501.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetName]艰难的在[From.From.GetName]周围密集的遗骸区域中航行。等到[Root.GetLeaderName]和船员抵达的时候，之前传感器上捕捉到的数据已经消失了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1271`

### English

The [Root.GetName] struggles to safely navigate the dense debris field surrounding [From.From.GetName]. Whatever their sensors picked up on is gone by the time [Root.GetLeaderName] and crew arrive.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1402`

## anomaly_failure.2502.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly_failure.2502.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetName]的探针对[From.From.GetName]地表的数次扫描无功而返。我们把时间和资源对此行星进行详尽的调查，却最终没有什么成果，科学专员[Root.GetLeaderName]为此向大家诚挚道歉。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1272`

### English

The [Root.GetName]'s probes find over the course of their many sweeps over [From.From.GetName]'s surface. Science Officer [Root.GetLeaderName] has issued a formal apology for wasting time and resources on a detailed, fruitless, survey of the planet.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1403`

## anomaly_failure.2503.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly_failure.2503.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在太空中看到的壮丽景象离近了往往就没那么好看。[Root.GetName]已经绕[From.GetName]运行了一段时间来研究它的磨损情况，科学专员[Root.GetLeaderName]最终确认行星的结构并非智能生命之功，而是由普通的气象学因素造成的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1273`

### English

What appeared from space to be impressive structures are, up close, somewhat disappointing. The [Root.GetName] remained in orbit around [From.GetName] for a time to study the planet's weather patterns, after which Science Officer [Root.GetLeaderName] was able to confirm that the planetary structures are the result of mundane meteorological factors and not the efforts of intelligent life.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1404`

## anomaly_failure.2504.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly_failure.2504.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在[Root.GetName]的船员能够准确将来自[From.From.GetName]极热地表的求救信号进行三角测量之前，信号就消失了。鉴于没有准确的地理位置信息，科学专员[Root.GetLeaderName]觉得贸然发起营救行动太过危险。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1274`

### English

Before the crew of the [Root.GetName] could decisively triangulate the distress signal from the super-heated surface of [From.From.GetName], it disappeared. Without a specific geographic point to focus on, Science Officer [Root.GetLeaderName] deems a rescue attempt too dangerous to pursue.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1405`

## anomaly_failure.2535.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly_failure.2535.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetName]的科学专员[Root.GetLeaderName]报告称[From.From.GetName]的地表结构根本不是我们的关注点，继续研究下去就是浪费时间。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1275`

### English

Science Officer [Root.GetLeaderName] of the [Root.GetName] reports that the structures on the surface of [From.From.GetName] are utterly beneath our notice and their time is better spent elsewhere.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1406`

## anomaly_failure.2540.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly_failure.2540.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetName]的成员报告称被雕刻成寺庙状的小行星确实很漂亮。\n\n以上为报告的全部内容。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1276`

### English

The crew of the [Root.GetName] report that the asteroid, carved into the likeness of some sort of temple, is indeed pretty.\n\nThat is all.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1407`

## anomaly_failure.2545.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly_failure.2545.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

登上[Root.GetName]的[Root.GetLeaderName]小队发现[From.From.GetName]的独特声音其实是星球上的野生生物在一起唱歌的声音。\n\n他们觉得继续调查下去并不合适。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1277`

### English

[Root.GetLeaderName]'s crew aboard the [Root.GetName] have determined that the unique aural features of [From.From.GetName] are due to the wildlife on the planet coming together in a capella renditions of unknown, alien songs.\n\nThey did not see fit to investigate the matter further.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1408`

## anomaly_failure.2550.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly_failure.2550.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetName]未能从冰冻气体星球[From.From.GetName]取得任何有价值的样本。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1278`

### English

The [Root.GetName] was unable to recover any samples of interest from the ice giant [From.From.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1409`

## anomaly_failure.2555.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly_failure.2555.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetName]的探测器发现了一个非常原始的外星科技构造。科学专员[Root.GetLeaderName]急切地要启动它，结果反倒破坏了它。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1279`

### English

The [Root.GetName]'s exploratory probe uncover a fairly slapdash configuration of alien technology. Science Officer [Root.GetLeaderName]'s ill-advised attempts to activate the thing unfortunately lead to its destruction.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1410`

## anomaly_failure.2560.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly_failure.2560.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

科学专员[Root.GetLeaderName]起草了一份报告，称他们对不得不研究[From.From.GetName]感到很不满意，因为从中没有发现任何有价值的东西。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1280`

### English

Science Officer [Root.GetLeaderName] has issued a report indicating that they are none too pleased having been ordered to study [From.From.GetName], where they have found nothing of interest.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1411`

## anomaly_failure.2600.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly_failure.2600.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetName]已经绕[From.GetName]运动了一段时间了，全天候检测它的放射物。此时科学专员[Root.GetName]决定穿上宇航服登上这颗小行星采集一些矿物样本。\n\n他们登上行星表面之后，[From.From.GetName]的类无线电波传输功率突然呈几何倍数增加。微波在几分钟之内就把[Root.GetLeaderName]活活烤死了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1281`

### English

The [Root.GetName] had been orbiting [From.GetName] for some time, monitoring its emanations without incident, when Science Officer [Root.GetName] decided to suit up and spacewalk on the asteroid and procure some mineral samples.\n\nAs they touched down on its surface, [From.From.GetName]'s radio-like transmissions suddenly and drastically started increasing in intensity. The microwaves boiled [Root.GetLeaderName] alive within minutes.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1412`

## anomaly_failure.2601.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly_failure.2601.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在[Root.GetName]进入[From.From.GetName]周围密集的碎片区后不久就与我们失去了联系。\n\n几天之后，我们在场的边缘发现了一个救生舱；科学专员[Root.GetName]活了下来，但不记得[Root.GetName]和她的船员遭遇了什么事。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1282`

### English

We lost track of the [Root.GetName] shortly after it entered the dense field of debris surrounding [From.From.GetName].\n\nDays later, we recovered an escape pod from the edge of the field; Science Officer [Root.GetName] survived, but has no memory of what became of the [Root.GetName] or her crew.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1413`

## anomaly_failure.2602.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly_failure.2602.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

科学家[Root.GetLeaderName]对[From.GetName]粗鲁的探测惹恼了行星轨道上的两只巨大的§Y缇扬奇§!。这些生物对[Root.GetName]构成了巨大威胁。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1283`

### English

Science Officer [Root.GetLeaderName]'s careless probing of [From.GetName] has angered the two massive §YTiyanki§! that orbit the planet. These creatures pose a substantial threat to the [Root.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1414`

## anomaly_failure.2603.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly_failure.2603.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.From.GetName]上的奇特结构是个古老的陷阱。在[Root.GetName]着陆之前，飞船就被几个古代战斗无人机偷袭了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1284`

### English

The curious formations on [From.From.GetName] are an ancient trap. Before the [Root.GetName] was able to land, the ship was set upon by a small number of old combat drones.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1415`

## anomaly_failure.2604.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly_failure.2604.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetName]在低飞搜寻求救信号的过程中被[From.From.GetName]表面的岩浆喷发击中了。\n\n没有生还者。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1285`

### English

The [Root.GetName] was struck by a sudden eruption of magma from the surface of [From.From.GetName] as it was flying low, trying to get a lock on the distress signal.\n\nThere were no survivors.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1416`

## anomaly_failure.2635.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly_failure.2635.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

根据[Root.GetName]的报告，[From.From.GetName]关注点是这里之前有专属的娱乐场地。这个建筑距今多长时间，当初为何会建在[From.From.GetName]上，尚不确定。\n\n[Root.GetName]的船员还报告了科学专员的逝世，他被“摩天轮”般的建筑砸死了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1286`

### English

The point of interest on [From.From.GetName] was once, according to reports from the [Root.GetName], some sort of area dedicated to recreation and amusement. Its age is uncertain, as is the reason why it was established on [From.From.GetName] in the first place.\n\nThe crew of the [Root.GetName] also report the regrettable passing of their Science Officer, who was crushed by a toppling structure identified only as a "Ferris wheel."

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1417`

## anomaly_failure.2640.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly_failure.2640.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

科学专员[Root.GetLeaderName]依然坚称[Root.GetName]的船员在雕刻华丽的中空的[From.From.GetName]里没有任何发现。\n\n但科学家身上出现了一些变化。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1287`

### English

Science Officer [Root.GetLeaderName] remains adamant that the crew of the [Root.GetName] found nothing within the ornately sculpted, hollowed-out [From.From.GetName].\n\nBut something about the scientist has changed.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1418`

## anomaly_failure.2645.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly_failure.2645.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在他们的最后一条信息里，[Root.GetName]的成员在靠近[From.From.GetName]地表时观测到一些声响。之后通讯就中断了，飞船和全体船员失踪，可能已经死亡。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1288`

### English

In their last message, the crew of the [Root.GetName] reported aural activity spiking on [From.From.GetName] as they were approaching the surface. Communications were cut off shortly after, and the ship and all aboard are missing, presumed dead.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1419`

## anomaly_failure.2650.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly_failure.2650.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetName]的船员为了收集尽可能多的冰层样本，使得飞船的飞行高度低于最低安全高度。大气中的超临界流体进入了飞船的后部推进器，造成了灾难性的后果。\n\n没有人活了下来。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1289`

### English

The crew let the [Root.GetName] slip below minimum safe altitude in their attempt to gather as many ice samples as possible. By some happenstance atmospheric supercritical fluids got into the ship's rear thrusters, with disastrous results.\n\nThere were no survivors.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1420`

## anomaly_failure.2655.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly_failure.2655.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

当[Root.GetName]靠近[From.From.GetName]时，它被一些充满敌意的走私船拦截了。这些家伙攻击了小行星，摧毁了他们之前在上面活动的一切证据。下一个目标应该就是[Root.GetName]了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1290`

### English

As the [Root.GetName] approaches [From.From.GetName], it is intercepted by a couple of less-than-friendly smuggling ships. The hostiles turn their weapons on the asteroid, destroying all evidence of whatever operation they were running there. The [Root.GetName] is next, it seems.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1421`

## anomaly_failure.2660.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly_failure.2660.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetName]的成员报告称科学专员对[From.From.GetName]的雨特别感兴趣，用他们的话说，“那里的雨水居然是紫色的”。\n\n不幸的是，这些矿藏样本非常的不稳定，在[Root.GetName]的实验室引起了一场爆炸，科学专员当场死亡。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1291`

### English

The crew of the [Root.GetName] report that the ship's Science Officer took a particular interest in the rain on [From.From.GetName], as it is, in their words, "kind of purple."\n\nUnfortunately, the precipitation samples retrieved turned out to be highly volatile, and once unsealed in the lab aboard the [Root.GetName] the liquid violently combusted, killing the Science Officer instantly.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1422`

## anomaly_failure.2665.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly_failure.2665.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在他们发出的最后一条信息里，[Root.GetName]的成员在靠近[From.From.GetName]地表时观测到一些声响。之后通讯就中断了，飞船和全体船员失踪，可能已经死亡。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1292`

### English

In their last message, the crew of the [Root.GetName] reported activity spiking on [From.From.GetName] as they approached the surface. Communications were cut off shortly thereafter, and the ship and all aboard are missing, presumed dead.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1423`

## anomaly_failure.2670.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly_failure.2670.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

实际上[Root.GetName]对旧式结构体系统的狂热探查已经适得其反了。\n\n在古老电路彻底熔断之前，[Root.GetName]设法远程启动了轨道站的变位推进器——及时的将其踢出了原有轨道，使其进入了该行星的重力井中。这次任务的不幸结果对从这一经历中成长起来的指挥官[Root.GetLeaderName]产生了深远的影响。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1293`

### English

It appears as if the [Root.GetName]'s enthusiastic probing of the old construct's systems has, quite literally, backfired.\n\nBefore the ancient circuits finally fried, the [Root.GetName] managed to remotely activate the orbital station's positional thrusters - promptly knocking it out of its gentle orbit and into the star's gravity well. The mission's unfortunate outcome has had a profound impact on the ship's commander, [Root.GetLeaderName], who has grown from the experience.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1424`

## anomaly_failure.2670.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly_failure.2670.a&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetLeaderName]……把它发射到恒星里去？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1294`

### English

[Root.GetLeaderName]... launched it into the sun?

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1425`

## anomaly_failure.2670.a.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly_failure.2670.a.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y古代工厂§!已被摧毁。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1295`

### English

The §YAncient Manufactory§! is destroyed.\n

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1426`

## colony.1500.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.1500.name&lang=en)

### 中文

序幕

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1300`

### English

A Prelude

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1434`

## colony.1500.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.1500.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetName]的卫星上有异动。\n\n初步扫描显示一些古代科技已经被启动，或许是对我们的殖民行为作出的反应。\n\n我们的几个顶级科学家非常想去调查一下这个卫星，并为此准备好了一项特殊研究项目。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1301`

### English

Something stirs on the moon of [Root.GetName].\n\nPreliminary scans indicate that some ancient technology has become active, possibly as a response to our colonization efforts.\n\nSeveral of our top scientists are eager to take a closer look at the moon, and have prepared a special research project.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1435`

## colony.1500.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.1500.a&lang=en)

### 中文

他们当然感兴趣了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1302`

### English

Of course they have.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1436`

## colony.1501.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.1501.name&lang=en)

### 中文

古代无人机：发现

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1303`

### English

Ancient Drones: Discovery

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1437`

## colony.1501.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.1501.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这当然是一群难以控制的无人机。\n\n无人机对我们的错误敌我识别令早先的失望一扫而空——它们把我们当成已经逝去的主人的种群！见到我们似乎让他们很激动——如果这么老的人工智能有“激动”这种功能的话。但不幸的是，这些无人机很快就再次进入了休眠状态，我们也无法重新唤醒它们。\n\n这样的话，不如拆开来看看吧。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1304`

### English

It is a gaggle of wayward drones, of course.\n\nInitial disappointment was swept away by the drones' erroneous IFF classification of us - they think we are a race affiliated with their long-dead masters! They seemed very excited, if old AI constructs can be excited, to see us. Unfortunately, the drones again lapsed into inactivity shortly after and we have been unable to resuscitate them.\n\nOr take them apart, for that matter.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1438`

## colony.1501.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.1501.a&lang=en)

### 中文

时刻注意情况。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1305`

### English

Keep an eye on the situation.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1439`

## colony.1502.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.1502.name&lang=en)

### 中文

古老无人机：觉醒

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1306`

### English

Ancient Drones: Awakening

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1440`

## colony.1502.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.1502.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这些无人机从休眠中苏醒，并来到了[Root.GetName]的地表。它们礼貌的拒绝了我们的友好沟通，并开始将行星上的一小片地区环境改造。我们不清楚他们这种行为有何目的，但对殖民地似乎是无害的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1307`

### English

The drones woke from their slumber and descended to the surface of [Root.GetName]. Attempts to communicate were met with polite dismissal as they set about terraforming a small area of the planet. We do not know the nature of whatever ancient directive they are executing, but it does not seem detrimental to the colony.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1441`

## colony.1502.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.1502.a&lang=en)

### 中文

呃，只要他们的行为能对我们有益就好……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1308`

### English

Well, as long as their activities benefit us...

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1442`

## colony.1502.a.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.1502.a.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

无人机从地表移走了一个随机的£blocker£§Y地块障碍§!，没有碰其他的地方。\n

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1309`

### English

The drones remove a random £blocker£ §YTile Blocker§! from the surface, leaving the planet otherwise untouched.\n

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1443`

## colony.1503.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.1503.name&lang=en)

### 中文

古老无人机：到达过去的天空

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1310`

### English

Ancient Drones: Reaching For Past Skies

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1444`

## colony.1503.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.1503.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetName]卫星上陆陆续续有休眠的无人机再苏醒过来，它们做的事情也让人喜忧参半。\n\n在天上无数监视器的监视下，它们在夜色中于我们在[Root.GetName]上的主要殖民地外不远处建立起了一个巨大建筑的初步架构。工程师认为这是叫做“太空电梯”的过时产物，或许在我们[Root.Owner.GetSpeciesName]进入星际旅行之前的那个时代很常见。最终我们还是要摧毁它，因为它阻碍了殖民地的发展，不过安全的摧毁它还是需要花些时间。我们的工程师正好想近距离研究一下这个东西。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1311`

### English

The old drones fitfully dormant on [Root.GetName]'s moon have become active again, and their work is a mixed blessing.\n\nUnder the cover of night and spotty airspace surveillance, they have erected the framework of some gigantic structure a short distance away from our primary settlement on [Root.GetName]. Our engineers are convinced it is the realization of some outmoded concept known as a "space elevator," perhaps a common sight in the times before we [Root.Owner.GetSpeciesName] first took to the stars. It will eventually have to be razed as it stands in the way of our expansion plans for the colony, and bringing it down safely will take some time. At least our engineers are giddy with excitement at the prospect of studying the thing up close.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1445`

## colony.1503.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.1503.a&lang=en)

### 中文

随他们去吧。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1312`

### English

Good for them.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1446`

## colony.1504.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.1504.name&lang=en)

### 中文

古老无人机：本性慷慨

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1313`

### English

Ancient Drones: Spontaneous Generosity

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1447`

## colony.1504.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.1504.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

再次苏醒之后，这些无人机暂时在传感器上消失了。回来的时候，它们拖着一大箱不知从什么地方运来的矿物。在发出一个损坏严重的猝发传输信号表明他们已经完成了指定任务后，它们就把这些矿物丢在了[Root.GetName]的地表。\n\n之后它们就回到了月球上。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1314`

### English

After waking yet again, the drones briefly disappeared from sensors. When they returned, it was with a large cache of minerals of unknown origin in tow. They dumped it on the surface of [Root.GetName] after sending a badly corrupted burst transmission indicating that they had fulfilled a requisition request.\n\nThen they went back to the moon.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1448`

## colony.1504.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.1504.a&lang=en)

### 中文

免费的矿物，这种好事不常有。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1315`

### English

Free minerals. No complaints.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1449`

## colony.1505.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.1505.name&lang=en)

### 中文

古老无人机：远亲

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1316`

### English

Ancient Drones: An Estranged Relative

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1450`

## colony.1505.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.1505.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

卫星上的信号激增提醒我们[Root.GetPlanetMoon]上的无人机已再次苏醒。这次它给我们带来了一个好东西：深层出土的古老卫星。并且它们已经对其进行了一定程度的整修。我们不清楚卫星是怎么跑到这里来的，也不知道无人机为什么到现在才把它挖出来。尽管如此，即使它的数据库里只有一小部分保存了下来，也足够我们了解这些无人机的主人曾经所在的帝国的日常生活情况了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1317`

### English

Frantic signal bursts from the moon alerted us to the fact that the old drones on [Root.GetPlanetMoon] have woken yet again. This time they bring us a significant prize, as they have unearthed an ancient satellite from the depths of their moon and partially repaired it. We do not know how the satellite ended up there, nor why the drones have ignored it until now. Regardless, if even a fragment of its databanks have survived, there is a lot we can learn about day-to-day life in the empires that the drones first served.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1451`

## colony.1506.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.1506.name&lang=en)

### 中文

古代无人机：无人机的崩溃

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1318`

### English

Ancient Drones: Drone Debacle

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1452`

## colony.1506.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.1506.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一直沉睡的无人机发生了巨大转变，不再做不规则运动。一部分苏醒了过来，像往常一样进入[Root.GetName]的大气中，却一头全速撞向了行星的地面。\n\n相同的情况一直在发生。\n\n殖民地没有受到多大损失，但这些脆弱无比的无人机却变成了一团团闪着蓝色火花的废铁，好像在诉说着它们自我毁灭的决心和力量。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1319`

### English

The erratic behavior of the narcoleptic drones have taken a violent turn. A number of them woke and entered the atmosphere of [Root.GetName], as is their wont, only to slam into the surface of the planet at full thrust.\n\nRepeatedly, in many cases.\n\nDamage to the colony was negligible but the nigh-invulnerable drones were destroyed in bright showers of blue sparks, indicative of the tremendous force of, and dedication to, their self-destruction.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1453`

## colony.1506.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.1506.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们能搞清楚它们到底是怎么了吗？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1320`

### English

Can we find out what was wrong with them?

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1454`

## colony.1507.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.1507.name&lang=en)

### 中文

古代无人机：寻找无人机

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1321`

### English

Ancient Drones: Looking For Drones

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1455`

## colony.1507.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.1507.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

自毁事件之后，剩余的无人机就消失了。[Root.GetName]的历史学家认为它们仍徘徊在附近，在某些失落科技的保护下隐蔽不见，但它们并没与在本应苏醒的时间苏醒过来。\n\n对自毁无人机遗迹的深度分析进展缓慢，但应该马上就会有突破了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1322`

### English

The surviving drones have been missing since the suicide incident. History buffs on [Root.GetName] are convinced the drones are still in close proximity, shielded from view by some lost technology, but the expected time window for their next activation has come and gone.\n\nProgress on deep analysis of the destroyed drones' remains has been slow, but a breakthrough is expected soon.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1456`

## colony.1507.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.1507.a&lang=en)

### 中文

有新消息随时通知我。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1323`

### English

Keep me posted.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1457`

## colony.1508.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.1508.name&lang=en)

### 中文

古代无人机：淘金

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1324`

### English

Ancient Drones: Panning For Gold

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1458`

## colony.1508.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.1508.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

从破碎的无人机内部找寻故障所花费的时间比预计的长得多，因为每个无人机的内部结构和组装部件都不尽相同。知晓了这一点，那当务之急就是要找出相同的部件。单独来看这些部件的话，如果是外星人制作的，那肯定是现代的东西。如此就很可能是有人做了我们做不到的事情，篡改了这些无人机。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1325`

### English

Sorting out the errant innards of the shattered drones took much longer than anticipated, as each drone seems to have been built from a slightly different design, assembled from slightly different parts. Once this fact was established, one thing stood out - the parts that were identical. Separated from the rest, it is clear these parts are of somewhat modern, if alien, make. It is likely that someone has done what we could not, and tampered with the drones.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1459`

## colony.1508.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.1508.a&lang=en)

### 中文

这需要我们进行深入调查。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1326`

### English

This warrants further investigation.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1460`

## colony.1509.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.1509.name&lang=en)

### 中文

古代无人机：外星人没见过的

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1327`

### English

Ancient Drones: Alien to the Aliens

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1461`

## colony.1509.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.1509.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

将它们组装起来之后，我们的专家认为这些无人机残骸内部的外星部件是单向通讯设备。它们并未处在接收状态，所以我们可以推断出这些无人机不是在第三方的指令下进行活动，而是有什么个体一直在监视这些无人机的活动。建议对此事进行进一步调查。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1328`

### English

Having pieced them together, the foreign objects inside the destroyed drones have been identified by our experts as one-way transmitters. Since they are not set to receive, it is safe to assume that the drones were not acting at the behest of some third party. Rather, some entity has been keeping tabs on the drones' activities. It is advisable to look into this matter.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1462`

## colony.1509.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.1509.a&lang=en)

### 中文

继续进行监视。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1329`

### English

Continue our monitoring efforts.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1463`

## colony.1510.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.1510.name&lang=en)

### 中文

古老无人机：上天的回答

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1330`

### English

Ancient Drones: Answers Above

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1464`

## colony.1510.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.1510.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们找到了剩下的无人机，它们漂浮在[Root.GetPlanetMoon]的轨道上。\n\n我们已经了解到它们只是某些派系周密计划里的小喽啰。根据我们了解的类似行动的掌握，这个派系很可能是某个特别古老而智慧的海盗集团。他们比我们先发现了这些无人机，做了我们做不到的事情，改装了这些无人机。虽然听起来没那么厉害—他们做的只是将单独的外星科技放进无人机里，但是这些外星海盗的科技水平比我们高出许多，至少在某些领域是这样。安装完毕之后，这些海盗将它们放回太空，他们知道这些无人机的目的是为了帮助原主人建设殖民地，而随着时间的流逝，这些无人机鉴别主人的能力正在减弱。无人机一直在帮我们建设殖民地，而海盗一直在监视，一直在等待发动攻击的好时机。\n\n我们的战斗分析员预测这些海盗会在两年之内发起攻击。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1331`

### English

We have found the remaining drones, drifting into orbit around [Root.GetPlanetMoon]. We have learned that they are unwitting pawns in an elaborate scheme set up by an unknown faction.\n\nGoing by the modus operandi of this operation, this faction is most likely a particularly old and particularly clever pirate syndicate. They must have found the drones drifting through space and done what we could not; modified them. While not as impressive as it sounds - all they did was introduce self-contained alien technology into the drones - fact remains that these alien pirates possess a technological know-how far in advance of our own, at least in certain areas. Having installed their comms devices in the drones, the pirates let them drift through space again, knowing that they are set to assist their original masters' colonists and that the drones' ability to accurately identify such colonists has degraded with age. The drones have essentially been fattening up our colony as the pirates watch, waiting for the ideal time to strike.\n\nOur military analysts predict that the pirates will strike within the next two years.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1465`

## colony.1510.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.1510.a&lang=en)

### 中文

尽管来吧。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1332`

### English

Let them come.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1466`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.1510.b&lang=en)

### 中文

夺取无人机，用它们毁灭它们将来的主人。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1333`

### English

Capture the drones, send them to destroy their would-be masters.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1467`

## colony.1510.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.1510.c&lang=en)

### 中文

我们不能再放任这种事情发生。拆除所有无人机。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1334`

### English

We must not let this happen again. Dismantle the drones.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1468`

## colony.1511.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.1511.name&lang=en)

### 中文

古代无人机：永诀

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1335`

### English

Ancient Drones: A Final Goodbye

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1469`

## colony.1511.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.1511.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们夺取了无人机，在其中装满了高爆炸药。我们知道了如何拆解，对它们进行重新设置就没那么难了，我们在海盗舰队可能会经过的位置给他们准备了一份大餐。顶级[Root.Owner.GetSpeciesName]科学家们植入了我们的敌我识别技术来把误伤平民的几率降到最低。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1336`

### English

We have captured the drones and fitted them with explosive charges. Reprogramming them proved to be a relatively easy task now that we know how to break them open, and we have plotted a course for the estimated location of the pirate fleet. Top [Root.Owner.GetSpeciesName] scientists have implemented our own IFF technology to minimize the risk of the drones attacking innocents.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1470`

## colony.1511.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.1511.a&lang=en)

### 中文

让它们去吧。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1337`

### English

Let them fly.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1471`

## colony.1512.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.1512.name&lang=en)

### 中文

古代无人机：伤痕累累，惶恐不安

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1338`

### English

Ancient Drones: Wounded and Volatile

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1472`

## colony.1512.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.1512.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

海盗舰队来袭，比预期早一些。我们的先发制人成功地打击并激怒了他们——舰队规模比预期少了30%。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1339`

### English

The pirate fleet is inbound, earlier than expected. It is likely our preemptive strike was successful both in provoking and damaging them - their fleet is roughly 30% smaller than projected.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1473`

## colony.1513.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.1513.name&lang=en)

### 中文

古代无人机：他们无所不在

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1340`

### English

Ancient Drones: They're Everywhere

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1474`

## colony.1513.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.1513.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

海盗舰队来袭，比预期晚一些。从舰队的组成和阵型来看，他们为了尽快消灭[Root.GetName]而找来了盟友。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1341`

### English

The pirate fleet has arrived, somewhat later than expected. Judging by the makeup of the fleet and formations used, they have rallied their allies in an effort to pillage and burn [Root.GetName] as quickly and decisively as possible.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1475`

## colony.1513.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.1513.a&lang=en)

### 中文

绝不允许他们这么做。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1342`

### English

They will do no such thing.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1476`

## colony.1514.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.1514.name&lang=en)

### 中文

古代无人机：决战

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1343`

### English

Ancient Drones: Showdown

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1477`

## colony.1514.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.1514.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

时间到了，海盗舰队准时到达。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1344`

### English

The time has come, and the pirate armada arrives on cue.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1478`

## colony.1514.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.1514.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们早已迫不及待地想战斗了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1345`

### English

This fight has been a long time coming.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1479`

## colony.1520.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.1520.name&lang=en)

### 中文

玛琳·卡琳：花之魅力

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1347`

### English

Mharin Kharin: Floral Fascination

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1483`

## colony.1520.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.1520.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在[Root.GetName]建立了殖民地，当地的一些植物在释放大量含有复杂化学成分的花粉。\n\n为此准备了一项特别研究计划，来研究这种植物。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1348`

### English

Several plants native to [Root.GetName], where we recently established a colony, exude copious amounts of a pollen with an astounding chemical complexity.\n\nA special research project has been prepared for further study of this flora.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1484`

## colony.1521.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.1521.name&lang=en)

### 中文

玛琳·卡琳：唤醒嗅觉

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1349`

### English

Mharin Kharin: Olfactory Arousal

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1485`

## colony.1521.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.1521.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

长期暴露在[Root.GetName]大气中的花粉会对信息素受体产生轻微催情效果，随之而来的是一种不易察觉的“兴奋”。而效果最强的植物在这颗星球各处都很常见；被[Root.GetName]的科学家储存在了一个隐蔽的地方，在[Root.Owner.GetSpeciesName]当地语言中被称为“玛琳·卡琳”。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1350`

### English

Long-term exposure to the pollen naturally occurring in [Root.GetName]'s atmosphere acts on the colonists' pheromone receptors with a mild aphrodisiac effect, accompanied by a barely-perceptible "high." The plant that produces the most potent strain is a flower common to all areas of the planet; squirreled away into some obscure taxonomic nook by scientists on [Root.GetName] but known in the local [Root.Owner.GetSpeciesName] dialect as "Mharin Kharin."

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1486`

## colony.1521.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.1521.b&lang=en)

### 中文

我们不能让这种影响心智的东西留在大气中。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1351`

### English

We cannot allow mind-altering particles in the atmosphere.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1487`

## colony.1521.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.1521.c&lang=en)

### 中文

我们能彻底消灭这种植物的手段。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1352`

### English

We do not have the resources to exterminate the plant.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1488`

## colony.1522.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.1522.name&lang=en)

### 中文

玛琳·卡琳：准备应付花朵

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1353`

### English

Mharin Kharin: Making Time for Petals

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1489`

## colony.1522.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.1522.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetName]的报告显示最近的工作效率开始下降，在比[Root.Owner.GetName]的平均值低好几个百分点的地方稳定下来。\n\n这有可能是由星球上的刺激性花粉造成的。\n\n很可能是。\n\n已发起§Y特殊项目§!来进一步，以期提高生产线效率来弥补损失。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1354`

### English

Reports from [Root.GetName] indicate that worker productivity has declined recently, becoming stable at levels several percent lower than the [Root.Owner.GetName] average.\n\nThis may or may not be due to the stimulating effects of the pollen native to the planet.\n\nIt probably is.\n\nA §YSpecial Project§! has been prepared should we wish to make our production pipelines more efficient to make up for the loss.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1490`

## colony.1522.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.1522.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们看看能做点什么。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1355`

### English

Let's see what can be done.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1491`

## colony.1523.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.1523.name&lang=en)

### 中文

玛琳·卡琳：研究花朵

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1356`

### English

Mharin Kharin: Work Over Flowers

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1492`

## colony.1523.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.1523.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

通过对[Root.GetName]上的时间管理模型进行深刻革命，并对所有生产领域的责任和各项职责进行重新分配，我们收到了良好的效果。大众的要求显而易见，而外界观察者会将其称作公开需求，那就是要与当地的植物群落保持紧密的联系。\n\n可是即使运用合理的时间分配和额外的即时检查，生产效率仍旧很低，只是至少已经脱离了不可接受的最低水平。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1357`

### English

Far-reaching changes to time management paradigms and the way responsibility is delegated within all fields of production and other duties on [Root.GetName] have produced good results. The populace has an evident desire - outside observers would call it an outright need - to be in close proximity to the native flora.\n\nWith clever scheduling and additional checks in place, productivity remains low but has risen above the lower limit of what can be deemed acceptable.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1493`

## colony.1523.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.1523.a&lang=en)

### 中文

干得好。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1358`

### English

Good work.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1494`

## colony.1524.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.1524.name&lang=en)

### 中文

玛琳·卡琳：无法移动

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1359`

### English

Mharin Kharin: Rooted in Place

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1495`

## colony.1524.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.1524.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

最近的调查报告显示，[Root.GetName]上的居民比[Root.Owner.GetName]的其他居民更不愿意迁移到其他殖民地。\n\n玛琳·卡琳和它的近亲植物会造成一种轻度的心理上的感知依赖。但是调查报告中并没有指明两件事情之间有任何关联。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1360`

### English

A recent survey indicates that residents of [Root.GetName] are much less likely than other denizens of the [Root.Owner.GetName] to migrate to other colonies.\n\nThe survey does not address whether this could be due to a psychological dependency on the low-level sensory arousal caused by Mharin Kharin and its sibling-plants.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1496`

## colony.1525.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.1525.name&lang=en)

### 中文

玛琳·卡琳：通信故障

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1361`

### English

Mharin Kharin: Transmission Trouble

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1497`

## colony.1525.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.1525.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

尽管星球上的生产力不再下降了，[Root.GetName]上殖民城市和[Root.Owner.Capital.GetName]上首府之间的通信却开始崩溃。[Root.GetName]上的高级官员之间开始变得越来越冷淡，我们的办事员不断抱怨说即使只是发送一个简单的指令现在都变成了一件困难重重的事。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1362`

### English

While productivity is no longer declining on the planet, communications between the colony on [Root.GetName] and the capital on [Root.Owner.Capital.GetName] are breaking down. Senior officials on [Root.GetName] are growing increasingly distant, and our clerks are complaining that even relaying simple directives is becoming an exercise in frustration.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1498`

## colony.1525.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.1525.a&lang=en)

### 中文

哎。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1363`

### English

Sigh.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1499`

## colony.1525.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.1525.b&lang=en)

### 中文

派遣一支行政特遣队。代号淡紫。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1364`

### English

Deploy an administrative task force. Code mauve.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1500`

## colony.1526.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.1526.name&lang=en)

### 中文

玛琳·卡琳：高层统治者的背叛

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1365`

### English

Mharin Kharin: Upper Management Betrayal

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1501`

## colony.1526.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.1526.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们送去[Root.GetName]处理问题的行政小队好像背叛了。幸运的是，他们的计划起到的作用简直是微乎其微。他们的计划进行得极慢，一下子就被我们发现了，真是一点都不令人感到惊奇。风水轮流转——这只是个比喻而已——我们最后的手段就是废除当地的政府，然后直接从首府那里管理殖民地的事务。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1366`

### English

It appears that the crack team of administrators we sent to [Root.GetName] in order to set things straight have been turned. Fortunately for us, their plot to do as little work as possible was, unsurprisingly, sloppily executed and easily discovered. Heads will roll - metaphorically speaking of course - as our last recourse is to depose the local government and manage the colony's affairs from the capital.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1502`

## colony.1526.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.1526.a&lang=en)

### 中文

结果可能会有点难看。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1367`

### English

It won't be pretty.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1503`

## colony.1527.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.1527.name&lang=en)

### 中文

玛琳·卡琳：远处来的铁腕

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1368`

### English

Mharin Kharin: With an Iron Fist, From Afar

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1504`

## colony.1527.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.1527.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的从属政策已经在[Root.GetName]上根深蒂固了。它成功地控制了花粉植物玛琳·卡琳的繁殖。它还成功减少了人们对“使用”这种植物的接受度——这成就可不小，因为这种行为和闻闻花香一样没什么可指摘的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1369`

### English

Our remote-government of [Root.GetName] is now firmly established. It has met with some success in suppressing the proliferation of the pollen-bearing plant Mharin Kharin as well as in eroding the community's acceptance of "using" it - no small feat considering the act is as innocent as smelling a flower.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1505`

## colony.1528.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.1528.name&lang=en)

### 中文

玛琳·卡琳：砍掉，烧掉

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1370`

### English

Mharin Kharin: Slash and Burn

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1506`

## colony.1528.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.1528.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在[Root.GetName]上面的从属治理一直在进行着生硬而卓有成效的管理。据说掌控大权的国会——显然他们更喜欢躲在房间里呼吸玛琳·卡琳的花粉——已经控制了我们安排在星球上面的一支军团。从属治理希望用这些部队对那些花发起一次进攻，让他们安装一些大气过滤机，并把这种植物从市区完全抹除。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1371`

### English

Our remote-governance of [Root.GetName] continues to be a somewhat awkward but effective arrangement. The council in charge has allegedly secured the loyalty of a faction of our armed forces on the planet - apparently they prefer staying indoors to breathing in the Mharin Kharin pollen. The remote-government wishes to use these troops to mount an offensive on the flower, having them erect atmospheric purifiers and wiping the plant from all urban areas entirely.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1507`

## colony.1528.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.1528.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们早就该这么做了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1372`

### English

We should have done this a long time ago.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1508`

## colony.1528.a.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.1528.a.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

这种会使人头脑不清的植物现在已经被严格管控了。它被隔离在人口密集的局域之外。这会移除所有与之相关的修正。\n

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1373`

### English

The intoxicating plant is strictly controlled and contained outside of populated areas, removing all related modifiers.\n

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1509`

## colony.1529.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.1529.name&lang=en)

### 中文

玛琳·卡琳：花粉崇拜

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1374`

### English

Mharin Kharin: Pollen Worship

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1510`

## colony.1529.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.1529.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetName]上的边缘分子联合起来，组成了一个准宗教事务性质的组织。他们想要控制玛琳·卡琳的种植。他们的领袖，一个自封为“圣花宫殿”的“绿色祭司”的人，宣称他为[Root.GetName]上的整个生态圈代言，并要求获得这种植物的独家贸易权。\n\n这当然是不可能的，我们也不会接受。[Root.GetName]当地植物体系并没有什么战略或经济价值，因此依法也不能运送到其它星球。\n\n不幸的是，他们似乎很不情愿接受这个现实。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1375`

### English

Fringe elements on [Root.GetName] have banded together to form a quasi-religious organization seeking to control all aspects of the Mharin Kharin flower and its cultivation. Their leader, a self-styled "Green Hierophant" of this "Flower Temple" claims to speak for all flora and fauna of [Root.GetName] and demands exclusive trading rights for the plant.\n\nThis is of course completely unacceptable and impossible, as the native flora of [Root.GetName] is of little strategic or economic value and thus may not be brought off-world by law.\n\nUnfortunately, they are unlikely to accept this fact gracefully.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1511`

## colony.1530.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.1530.name&lang=en)

### 中文

玛琳·卡琳：花粉末日的先兆

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1376`

### English

Mharin Kharin: Beginning of Pollen's End

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1512`

## colony.1530.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.1530.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

圣花宫殿和它的追随者们的人数现在在[Root.GetName]的总人口中占了不小的比例。报告显示，他们组织的运动出现了一些可疑的改变。他们越来越不热心于走私玛琳·卡琳。圣花宫殿似乎逐渐转变成了一种相信世界末日的宗教事务。大气检测器发现玛琳·卡琳的花粉近期出现了一些变化，也许此事和圣花宫殿的改变有关联。\n\n已发起§Y特殊项目§!来评估此事可能会带来的风险。建议在可能的灾难发生前完成这个项目。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1377`

### English

The Flower Temple and its followers now make up a sizable minority of the population on [Root.GetName], and reports indicate a disturbing change in the movement. Their futile attempts to smuggle Mharin Kharin specimen off-world have been growing less enthusiastic by the day, as the Flower Temple seems to be transitioning into a doomsday cult. There is likely a correlation with the recent changes in the Mharin Kharin pollen's chemical makeup, detected by atmospheric sensors.\n\nA §YSpecial Project§! has been prepared to assess the risks associated with this, and it is recommended to complete it before something goes horribly wrong.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1513`

## colony.1530.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.1530.a&lang=en)

### 中文

很好。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1378`

### English

Very well.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1514`

## colony.1531.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.1531.name&lang=en)

### 中文

玛琳·卡琳：灾难已避免

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1379`

### English

Mharin Kharin: Disaster Averted

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1515`

## colony.1531.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.1531.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

坚决而迅速的反制措施使我们迅速发现了[Root.GetName]上原生花朵的花粉变化所带来的危险。我们迅速采取了必要措施。\n\n我们彻底疏散了星球上的人群，并且针对玛琳·卡琳及其近亲植物进行了多轮战术性除草轰炸。\n\n这些植物很快就变得对除草剂免疫了。如果这些花粉没有被清除的话，它们就会开始散发一种神经剧毒。所有拥有嗅觉器官的[Root.GetName]生物都会受到致命威胁。\n\n殖民议会的科学家认为这是[Root.GetName]上的一种自然循环。虽然花粉会对我们产生种种不良影响：使我们的当地管理瘫痪，引导圣花骑士教团建立，甚至最终产生剧毒。但是这其实只是偶然的副作用，而不是植物为了自卫而设计的阴谋诡计。\n\n如今，星球的表面上如今已经见不到这些花的踪迹了。我们的殖民地居民们也得到了安全保障，再也不用受到花粉的威胁了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1380`

### English

Quick and decisive action allowed us to identify the risks associated with the rapid changes in the pollen native to [Root.GetName] and take the necessary steps.\n\nThe necessary steps were a complete planetary evacuation and a planet-wide tactical pesticide bombing run, targeting the Mharin Kharin flower and its ilk.\n\nThe plants were rapidly mutating and their unique pollen would, had they not been destroyed, soon have begun delivering a potent neurotoxin to the olfactory organs of all sapient life on [Root.GetName].\n\nThe scientists on the colonization council theorize that this is part of a natural cycle on [Root.GetName] and that the pollen's adverse effects on us, in the form of a crumbling local administration, the rise of the Flower Temple cult, and eventual pollen toxicity, were chance side-effects rather than the product of a plant's sinister self-defense mechanism.\n\nRegardless, any and all traces of the flower have been wiped from the planet's surface, and our colonists are safe and free of the pollen's influence.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1516`

## colony.1531.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.1531.a&lang=en)

### 中文

真是大团圆的结局。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1381`

### English

All's well that ends well.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1517`

## colony.1531.a.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.1531.a.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

这颗星球上的危险花类已经被清除，所有与之相关的修正也都被移除。\n

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1382`

### English

The planet has been purged of the dangerous flower, removing all related modifiers.\n

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1518`

## colony.1532.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.1532.name&lang=en)

### 中文

玛琳·卡琳：我们的花粉末日

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1383`

### English

Mharin Kharin: The Pollen's End of Us

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1519`

## colony.1532.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.1532.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

灾难降临了。\n\n无数的玛琳·卡琳开始自发地进行变异，就像野火那样一发不可收拾地蔓延开来。它们的花粉开始变得剧毒，我们已经束手无策了。\n\n因为我们发现的太晚，我们没能撤离星球上的居民，中和花粉的尝试也以失败告终。[Root.GetName]上的居民遭受了灭顶之灾。\n\n[Root.GetName]上的植物获得了胜利，但是它们也将付出代价，尽管这件事对我们来说只是一种毫无意义的安慰。玛琳·卡琳所属的种群由于过度繁殖，导致土壤中缺少它所需要的某种营养物质——很快，这些植物就会死亡。要经过非常长得一段时间以后，这些植物才能重新在生态系统中找到自己的落脚点。谁知道它们到底会不会找到。\n\n[Root.GetName]的居民都丧生了。星球的大气由于植物排放的气体而暂时变得有毒。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1384`

### English

Something went horribly wrong.\n\nVast swaths of Mharin Kharin flowers spontaneously mutated in unison, spreading like wildfire and turning their pollen poisonous, and we were too late.\n\nBoth our evacuation efforts and our attempts to neutralize the pollen fell short of the mark and the inhabitants of [Root.GetName] paid the price.\n\nThe plant life of [Root.GetName] achieved only a pyrrhic victory, though this fact is scant consolation. The whole genus that Mharin Kharin belonged to burned in the fire of its own unnaturally rapid proliferation. The soil is starved of the specific nutrients the pollinating plants need - once the currently living specimens die, which will be soon, it will be a long time before that flora can again establish a foothold in the ecosystem, if it ever will.\n\nThe inhabitants of [Root.GetName] are dead, and the planet's atmosphere is temporarily noxious with plant emissions.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1520`

## colony.1532.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.1532.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们不会忘却这场悲剧的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1385`

### English

We will not forget this tragedy.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1521`

## colony.1533.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.1533.name&lang=en)

### 中文

玛琳·卡琳：一个新的开始

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1386`

### English

Mharin Kharin: A New Beginning

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1522`

## colony.1533.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.1533.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

行星调查队伍汇报说，两年之前导致殖民地毁灭的植物毒性瘴气如今已经挥发殆尽了。这颗星球如今已经不同了。在玛琳·卡琳的怒放之后，它现在变成了一颗寸草不生的沙漠星球，大气中到处弥漫着花粉。\n\n两年也许不足以令我们遗忘[Root.Owner.GetName]曾经居住的那些人所遭遇的悲剧，但是至少现在这颗行星对智慧生命不再抱有敌意了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1387`

### English

A planetary surveillance team reports that the toxic miasma that enveloped a planet two years ago and led to the destruction of our colony has lifted. The planet is different now, changed into a desert world after the violent blooming of Mharin Kharin and the atmosphere becoming saturated with pollen.\n\nIt will take more than these two years for the [Root.Owner.GetName] to forget the tragic fate of those who lived there, but the planet itself is no longer actively hostile to sapient life.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1523`

## colony.1533.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.1533.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我知道了……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1388`

### English

I see...

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1524`

## pheromonefrenzy.revived.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apheromonefrenzy.revived.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

你的前殖民地如今已经变成了一颗荒芜的星球，如果有正确的技术，您可以将其变成一个可居住的地方。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1389`

### English

Your former colony has changed into a desert planet, and can be colonized with the right technology.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1525`

## colony.1534.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.1534.name&lang=en)

### 中文

玛琳·卡琳：花蕊的沉思

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1390`

### English

Mharin Kharin: Pistil Pondering

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1526`

## colony.1534.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.1534.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在仔细检查之后，我们发现[Root.GetName]上的植物其实构造非常简单。其中一些花粉有淡淡的刺激性以及迅速变异的倾向；但没有其他的特别之处，没有什么战略用途，也不是什么奢侈品资源。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1391`

### English

On closer examination, the plants native to [Root.GetName] turn out to be rather dull. Some excrete pollen that have a mild stimulating effect and they are likely to have a propensity for rapid genetic change; hardly impressive enough to warrant adopting as a strategic or luxury resource.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1527`

## communications_spread.1.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acommunications_spread.1.name&lang=en)

### 中文

通讯截获

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1395`

### English

Communications Intercepted

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1532`

## communications_spread.1.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acommunications_spread.1.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们截获了一条发往[CommSpreader.GetName]的消息，我们发现他们和另一个我们尚未发现的帝国建立了定期联系。我们追踪这条消息，与这位星系中的神秘角色建立了通讯线路。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1396`

### English

By intercepting a message sent to the [CommSpreader.GetName], we have learned that they are in regular contact with another spacefaring empire that we ourselves have no contact with. By tracing this message to its source, we have been able to establish our own line of communications with this previously unknown actor on the galactic stage.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1533`

## communications_spread.1.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acommunications_spread.1.a&lang=en)

## communications_spread.2.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acommunications_spread.2.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们从一个太空深处的未知帝国那里收到了一份公报，他们自称§H[From.GetName]§!。他们还声称，他们从我们与另一个帝国的通讯中发现了我们的存在。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1400`

### English

We have received a communiqué from a previously unknown spacefaring empire that call themselves the §H[From.GetName]§!. They claim to have learned of our existence by listening in on the communications of another empire we are in contact with.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1537`

## fallen_empires.2.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afallen_empires.2.name&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.GetName]暗杀[Root.GetRulerTitle]！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1403`

### English

[From.GetName] assassinate [Root.GetRulerTitle]!

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1541`

## fallen_empires.2.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afallen_empires.2.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

似乎作为我们不仅同[From.GetName]停滞圣权开战，更是随后接受了战败这一事实的惩罚，和平谈判结束之后，[From.GetAdj]特工出现在了[Root.GetRulerTitle][rulername.GetName]身旁。\n\n他们用闻所未闻的武器处决了我们的[Root.GetRulerTitle]。行凶后他们就消失在虚空之中，就好像从未出现过一样。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1404`

### English

As if to punish us for not only warring with the Stagnant Ascendancy of the [From.GetName] but for then accepting defeat, [From.GetAdj] agents appeared by [Root.GetRulerTitle] [rulername.GetName]'s side after the peace negotiations concluded.\n\nThey dispatched our [Root.GetRulerTitle] with unseen weaponry and practiced brutality before melting away as if they were never there.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1542`

## fallen_empires.2.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afallen_empires.2.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们战败了，我们被羞辱了，但是我们还会东山再起的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1405`

### English

We are defeated and humiliated, but will rise again.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1543`

## fallen_empires.2.a.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afallen_empires.2.a.tooltip&lang=en)

### English

§Y[Root.GetRulerTitle] [rulername.GetName] §Rdies§!.§!

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1544`

## fallen_empires.4.name

来自§H[From.GetName]§!的通讯

### English

Communication from §H[From.GetName]§!

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1546`

## fallen_empires.4.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afallen_empires.4.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

傲慢的虫子啊！你们认为[Root.GetSpeciesName]玷污者我们圣地的殖民者能够不被注意到吗？立即离开我们的星球或者面对我们舰队的力量吧！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1408`

### English

Arrogant little wretch! Did you think that the [Root.GetSpeciesName] colonists sullying our holy places would go unnoticed? Leave our worlds immediately or face the might of our fleets!

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1547`

## fallen_empires.4.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afallen_empires.4.a&lang=en)

我们别无选择，只能接受……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1409`

### English

We have no choice but to accept...

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1548`

## fallen_empires.4.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afallen_empires.4.b&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetName]绝不会受到欺凌！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1410`

### English

The [Root.GetName] will not be bullied!

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1549`

## fallen_empires.5.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afallen_empires.5.name&lang=en)

### English

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1550`

## fallen_empires.5.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afallen_empires.5.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetSpeciesName]，我们发现你们正在笨拙地尝试创造人工智能，认为你们正在走上灵魂自毁的错误道路上。立即停止这个愚蠢的举动，否则我们会帮你停止。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1412`

### English

[Root.GetSpeciesName], we have observed your fumbling attempts at creating artificial intelligence and come to the conclusion that you are headed down the path of spiritual self-destruction. Abandon this foolish endeavour at once, or we will MAKE you.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1551`
