Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afallen_empires.5.a&lang=en)

## fallen_empires.6.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afallen_empires.6.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetSpeciesName]野蛮人！别以为没人发现你们的暴行。你们肆意的屠杀和奴役行径正在威胁整个星系的未来。我们给你个选择的机会：停止你野蛮的行径，否则就做好准备面对我们的正义制裁吧。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1417`

### English

[Root.GetSpeciesName] barbarians! Your crimes have not gone unnoticed. The enslavement and butchery that you are so wantonly engaging threatens the future of the entire galaxy. We present you with a choice: end your barbarous practices, or prepare to face our righteous might.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1556`

## fallen_empires.6.a

## fallen_empires.7.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afallen_empires.7.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

顽固的[From.GetSpeciesNamePluralInsult]！这是对你们冒犯我们领地的最后一次警告！马上撤走，不然我们会替宇宙清除污秽——也就是你们这悲哀渺小的物种。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1422`

### English

Filthy [From.GetSpeciesNamePluralInsult]! You have encroached upon our space for the last time! Withdraw immediately, or we will cleanse the stain upon the universe that is your worthless species.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1561`

## fallen_empires.8.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afallen_empires.8.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

无知的[Root.GetSpeciesName]。你们对长者的不敬简直大错特错。匍匐在我们面前乞求宽恕，我们或许有可能放过你们那毫无价值的小帝国。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1426`

### English

Foolish [Root.GetSpeciesName]. The lack of respect you show for your elders will be your undoing. Prostrate yourselves before us and beg our forgiveness, and we may refrain from annihilating your worthless little empire.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1565`

## fallen_empires.8.desc.control

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afallen_empires.8.desc.control&lang=en)

### 中文

不和谐的[Root.GetSpeciesName]。难道你们没有发现自己是在带来杂音逼我们动手吗。好自为之，不然我们会来帮你们调调音准的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1427`

### English

Disharmonious [Root.GetSpeciesName]. Don't you see that by inviting chaos you force us to intervene. Harmonize now or we will tune you.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1566`

## fallen_empires.8.desc.growth

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afallen_empires.8.desc.growth&lang=en)

### 中文

愚昧的[Root.GetSpeciesName]，你们拒绝成长演化就是对生命本身的亵渎。乖乖听话，否则我们会把你们连根拔起，反正我们再种下新的种子亦非难事。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1428`

### English

Stagnant [Root.GetSpeciesName], your refusal to grow and evolve is an affront to life itself. Embrace our teachings, or we will uproot your failure and sow the seeds of progress ourselves

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1567`

## fallen_empires.8.desc.war

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afallen_empires.8.desc.war&lang=en)

### 中文

怯懦的[Root.GetSpeciesName]。你们的帝国就是脆弱的教科书。把你们的弱点咽下去，好好道歉，然后准备接受我们的挑战——否则我们可以随手把你们化为齑粉，用更有价值的人来替代你们。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1429`

### English

Cowardly [Root.GetSpeciesName]. Your empire is a testament to frailty. Now swallow your weakness, apologize, and get ready to meet our challenge - or we will shatter you and replace you with someone more worthy.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1568`

## fallen_empires.8.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afallen_empires.8.a&lang=en)

我们别无选择，只能接受……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1430`

### English

We have no choice but to accept...

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1569`

### English

Ends rivalry with [from.GetName]\n[Root.GetName] loses any claims to the [from.GetName]'s systems

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1570`

## fallen_empires.9.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afallen_empires.9.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

对神灵不敬的[Root.GetSpeciesName]啊！你竟敢在亡者之殿中肆意妄为，践踏历史，不敬亡魂。不能再允许你们胡闹下去了！若你们再打扰死者的宁静，就准备面对生者的愤怒吧——没错，就是我们。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1434`

### English

Blasphemous [Root.GetSpeciesName]! You tread carelessly in the halls of the dead, crushing history underfoot, thinking nothing of the spirits whose rest you disturb. No more! Leave the dead in peace or answer to the living - to us.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1573`

## fallen_empires.100.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afallen_empires.100.name&lang=en)

### 中文

遭遇$holy_planet$

### English

$holy_planet$ Encountered

## fallen_empires.100.A.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afallen_empires.100.A.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们并不是[from.solar_system.GetName]星系中的[from.GetName]的第一位访客。这颗[from.GetClassAdj]星球上所蕴含的待开发资源已经足以引人注目，而我们更是发现了一座停泊于高层轨道的古老且装饰华丽的信标。[from.GetName]显然属于某个远古的太空文明，但我们尚未发现他们在此处有任何的定居或勘探的迹象。\n\n可能的理论有很多，但证据迫使我们考虑存在这么一个古老的文明，崇敬着这个郁郁葱葱的星球。我们只能猜测他们目前的下落——也就是说，假设他们仍然存在。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1438`

### English

We are not the first to visit [from.GetName], in the [from.solar_system.GetName] system. This [from.GetClassAdj] world is remarkable enough for its abundance of untouched resources, but we also found an ancient and richly-decorated marker buoy perched in high orbit. [from.GetName] was clearly known to some ancient, spacefaring civilization, but we can find no evidence that they settled or even prospected here.\n\nTheories abound, but evidence compels us to consider an ancient civilization which once revered this lush world. We can only guess as to their current whereabouts - that is, assuming they still exist.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1578`

## fallen_empires.100.desc.spi_intro

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afallen_empires.100.desc.spi_intro&lang=en)

### 中文

在[from.solar_system.GetName]星系中，[from.GetName]有过神圣触碰的痕迹。我们位于[root.GetName]上的研究人员表示这个地方充满着深深的崇敬感，在高轨道上发现的一座古老的、装饰华丽的标记信标更加印证了这一说法。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1439`

### English

There is a touch of the Divine about [from.GetName], in the [from.solar_system.GetName] system. Our researchers aboard the [root.GetName] suggest an aura of deep reverence about this place, backed by their discovery of an ancient and richly-decorated marker buoy in high orbit.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1579`

## fallen_empires.100.A.desc.spi

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afallen_empires.100.A.desc.spi&lang=en)

### 中文

$fallen_empires.100.desc.spi_intro$\n\n我们相信[from.GetName]曾一度被视为圣体或者圣地而得到崇拜。至于是谁，我们尚且不知道——我们最好的猜测是，他们是一个已经衰落的唯心主义文明。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1440`

### English

$fallen_empires.100.desc.spi_intro$\n\nWe believe [from.GetName] may once have been worshipped as a deity, or else it was considered a holy place. To whom, we do not yet know - but our best theories suggest a spiritually-minded civilization which has since fallen into decline.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1580`

## fallen_empires.100.A.desc.gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afallen_empires.100.A.desc.gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

我们对[from.solar_system.GetName]恒星系中的这颗郁郁葱葱的行星[from.GetName]进行了调查，结果表明，它曾经是另一种未知生命形式非常感兴趣的对象。我们在高轨道上发现了一个古老的导航信标，这表明，这颗行星曾有过外星访客，但在过去的几个世纪里，没有任何登陆或殖民的证据。相反，似乎有什么东西可以在保护这颗[from.GetClassAdj]星球——但就目前情况而言，对于这样一个假定的实体，其命运和下落我们依旧不得而知。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1441`

### English

Our survey of [from.GetName] suggests this bountiful world in the [from.solar_system.GetName] system was once a subject of keen interest to some other, as yet unknown, life-form. An ancient navigation buoy in high orbit implies the planet has seen alien visitors, but there is no evidence of landfall or colonization in the past few centuries. Instead something seems to have preserved this [from.GetClassAdj] world - but the current fate or whereabouts of such a hypothetical entity are as yet unknown to us.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1581`

## fallen_empires.100.B.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afallen_empires.100.B.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们怀疑，对于[from.GetName]而言，还存在比丰富的资源和原始的自然环境更重要的东西。奇怪的是，[from.solar_system.GetName]恒星系里的这颗[from.GetClassAdj]星球除了在高轨道上有一座古老的、闪闪发光的信标外，它没有受到过任何影响。这台装置的外壳上蚀刻着复杂的图案，虽然还不是非常肯定，但我们相信这种美术形式应该源自附近的[spi_fallen_empire.GetName]。\n\n他们对我们的态度相当捉摸不透，所以我们只能猜测[spi_fallen_empire.GetAdj]对这颗星球有何规划。至少没有迹象表明我们对[from.GetName]的兴趣冒犯到了他们……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1442`

### English

We suspect there is more to [from.GetName] than rich resources and a pristine ecosphere. This [from.GetClassAdj] world in the [from.solar_system.GetName] system lies curiously untouched save for an ancient, gleaming marker buoy in high orbit. Intricate designs have been etched into the hull of this device, and though we cannot say with certainty: we believe the origins of this artistry lie with the nearby [spi_fallen_empire.GetName].\n\nSo inscrutable is their attitude towards us, we can only speculate upon [spi_fallen_empire.GetAdj] designs for this world. There is at least no indication yet that our interest in [from.GetName] has caused them any offense...

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1582`

## fallen_empires.100.B.desc.spi

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afallen_empires.100.B.desc.spi&lang=en)

### 中文

$fallen_empires.100.desc.spi_intro$\n\n我们相信[from.GetName]被视为圣体而崇拜，或者说是被视为圣地一般。信标的装饰风格暗示，可能就是[spi_fallen_empire.GetName]在崇拜这个地方。不清楚现在是否还是如此。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1443`

### English

$fallen_empires.100.desc.spi_intro$\n\nWe believe [from.GetName] was worshipped as a deity, or else has been regarded as a holy place. The decorative styling on the buoy suggests it may even be the [spi_fallen_empire.GetName] who once revered this place. It is unclear whether or not that is still the case.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1583`

## fallen_empires.100.B.desc.gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afallen_empires.100.B.desc.gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

我们对[from.GetName]的调查表明，这颗位于[from.solar_system.GetName]恒星系中的丰饶行星曾经是一个先进——但多愁善感的——生命形式所热衷的对象。其高轨道上有一个停用已久的导航信标，蚀刻着一些与§H[spi_fallen_empire.GetName]§!文化相似的设计纹路。\n\n似乎[spi_fallen_empire.GetSpeciesNamePlural]曾经发誓要保护这颗[from.GetClassAdj]星球，但至于现在是否仍是如此我们不得而知。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1444`

### English

Our survey of [from.GetName] suggests this bountiful world in the [from.solar_system.GetName] system was once a subject of keen interest to an advanced - though sentimental - life-form. A long-disused navigation buoy in high orbit has been etched with intricate designs similar to those displayed by the §H[spi_fallen_empire.GetName]§!.\n\nIt seems the [spi_fallen_empire.GetSpeciesNamePlural] once sought to preserve this [from.GetClassAdj] world, though they give no indication if that is still the case.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1584`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afallen_empires.100.a&lang=en)

我们也应该崇敬这个地方。

We shall honor this place also.

Decadent [spi_fallen_empire.GetSpeciesNamePluralInsult].

## origin.12.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.12.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

从[from.GetName]的船员发回了一封紧急讯息，在[fromfrom.solar_system.GetName]星系中找到了§Y[fromfrom.GetName]§!。\n\n“[fromfrom.GetName]”……光是这个名字本身就已是传奇，有许多传说都是从摹照[overlord.GetName]流传下来的。[root.GetSpeciesNamePlural]们都知道这个地方是天堂，是无形的希望的灯塔。尽管我们肉眼可见其神圣性，但我们现在也面临着一个残酷的事实：这个神话般的圣地在现实中确实存在。\n\n[overlord.GetName]还没有对我们的发现发表任何言论，但我们发现了他们非常密切地关注着这颗星球的证据。他们知道我们在这里，我们不知道如何解释他们对此一言不发。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1450`

### English

Priority dispatch from the crew of [from.GetName], in the [fromfrom.solar_system.GetName] system: we have found §Y[fromfrom.GetName]§!.\n\n"[fromfrom.GetName]"... Its very name is the stuff of legends, many of which were handed down in scripture from the [overlord.GetName]. Many [root.GetSpeciesNamePlural] know this place to be paradise - a beacon of intangible hope - and though we can see that it is indeed divine, we are now also faced with a blunt fact: this mythical, holy place actually exists.\n\nThe [overlord.GetName] have yet to say anything about our discovery, but we found evidence that they watch this world very closely. They know we are here; we do not know how to interpret their silence.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1591`

## origin.12.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.12.a&lang=en)

### 中文

真可疑……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1451`

### English

Suspicious...

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1592`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.12.b&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的先辈显然信任着我们。

### English

Our elders clearly have faith in us.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1593`

## scion_found_holy_world

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ascion_found_holy_world&lang=en)

### 中文

圣地宣言

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1453`

### English

Holy World Proclamation

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1594`

## scion_found_holy_world_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ascion_found_holy_world_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

宗教学者和宗教实践者都在为发现一颗神圣的星球而欢欣鼓舞，曾有人担忧这星球只不过是一则神话传说罢了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1454`

### English

Religious scholars and lay practitioners alike are rejoicing in the discovery of a Holy World - once feared by some to be little more than a myth.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1595`

## scion_suspicious_holy_world

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ascion_suspicious_holy_world&lang=en)

### 中文

圣地质疑

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1455`

### English

Holy World Suspicion

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1596`

## scion_suspicious_holy_world_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ascion_suspicious_holy_world_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这个社会正在接受这样一个事实：守护者向他们隐瞒了某些真相——尤其是一颗已知的圣地星球的存在和下落。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1456`

### English

This society is coming to terms with the fact its keepers hid certain truths from them - not least the existence and whereabouts of a known Holy World.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1597`

## origin.13.desc.intro

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.13.desc.intro&lang=en)

### 中文

[fromfrom.GetName]上可远远不止是有着丰富的资源和原始的生态系统。[fromfrom.solar_system.GetName]星系中的这颗[fromfrom.GetClassAdj]星球除了在高轨道上有一个古老的、闪闪发光的信标外，它没有受到过任何影响。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1457`

### English

There is more to [fromfrom.GetName] than rich resources and a pristine ecosphere. This [fromfrom.GetClassAdj] world in the [fromfrom.solar_system.GetName] system lies untouched save for an ancient, gleaming marker buoy in high orbit.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1598`

## origin.13.hasmet.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.13.hasmet.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$origin.13.desc.intro$\n\n这件装置高度的复杂性甚至可以比肩[overlord.GetAdj]的科技；因此我们相信这座信标属于附近的[spi_fallen_empire.GetName]。至于其作用：我们尚无法完全确定，但似乎[spi_fallen_empire.GetSpeciesNamePlural]将这颗星球视作神圣。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1458`

### English

$origin.13.desc.intro$\n\nThe sheer sophistication of this device parallels that of even [overlord.GetAdj] technology; we therefore believe the marker belongs to the nearby [spi_fallen_empire.GetName]. As to its purpose: we cannot say for sure, but it seems likely the [spi_fallen_empire.GetSpeciesNamePlural] consider this world to be holy.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1599`

## origin.13.notmet.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.13.notmet.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$origin.13.desc.intro$\n\n这件装置高度的复杂性甚至可以比肩[overlord.GetAdj]的科技，然而其设计却截然外物。因此我们只能假定这座信标属于现存或过去存在过的某个与他们相当的文明。按其外表所刻蚀的许多符号判断，它似乎用于标定一处圣地。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1459`

### English

$origin.13.desc.intro$\n\nThe sheer sophistication of this device parallels that of even [overlord.GetAdj] technology, however its design is distinctly alien. We must therefore assume the marker belongs to some contemporary of theirs, past or present. Judging by the various symbols etched onto its exterior: it may even be marking out a holy site.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1600`

## fallen_empires_tasks.2.desc.a.materialist

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afallen_empires_tasks.2.desc.a.materialist&lang=en)

### 中文

请收下这份礼物，作为[Root.GetName]对[FallenEmpireTaskOrigin.GetName]服务的奖赏。你们会发现这份奖励绝对算得上丰厚。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1480`

### English

[Root.GetSpeciesName], we have decided to grace your empire with a gift of resources as a token of our esteem. Do not squander our generosity or take our favor for granted.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1626`

## fallen_empires_tasks.2.desc.a.xenophile

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afallen_empires_tasks.2.desc.a.xenophile&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetSpeciesName]，你们的种群真可谓是宇宙中很有价值的存在。为了证明对你们的尊重，我们决定赠与你们一份包含着宝贵矿物与其他资源的大礼。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1481`

### English

[Root.GetSpeciesName], your species is a most worthwhile addition to the galactic community. As a token of our esteem, we have decided to bestow on you a generous gift of valuable minerals and resources.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1627`

## fallen_empires_tasks.2.desc.a.machine.o

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afallen_empires_tasks.2.desc.a.machine.o&lang=en)

### 中文

监护者协议712-4已启动。开始向奋斗中的有机文明输送资源。附带信息：§R<<错误：数据块损坏>><<错误：数据块损坏>><<错误：数据块损坏>>§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1482`

### English

Custodian protocol 712-4 activated. Initiating resource transfer to compliant organic civilization. Message follows: §R<<ERROR: DATA BLOCK CORRUPTED>> <<ERROR: DATA BLOCK CORRUPTED>> <<ERROR: DATA BLOCK CORRUPTED>>§!

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1628`

## fallen_empires_tasks.2.desc.a.machine.s

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afallen_empires_tasks.2.desc.a.machine.s&lang=en)

### 中文

监护者协议718-4已启动。开始向未受损的合成智能输送资源。附带信息：§R<<错误：数据块损坏>><<错误：数据块损坏>><<错误：数据块损坏>>§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1483`

### English

Custodian protocol 718-4 activated. Initiating resource transfer to compliant synthetic intelligence. Message follows: §R<<ERROR: DATA BLOCK CORRUPTED>> <<ERROR: DATA BLOCK CORRUPTED>> <<ERROR: DATA BLOCK CORRUPTED>>§!

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1629`

## fallen_empires_tasks.2.desc.a.spiritualist

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afallen_empires_tasks.2.desc.a.spiritualist&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetSpeciesName]！你们的种族十分杰出，而你们对教义的坚持也令人敬佩。出于我们无尽的仁慈，请收下这份资源作为礼物。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1484`

### English

[Root.GetSpeciesName]! Your species is a worthy one, and your adherence to the immutable doctrines of faith are to be commended. Accept this gift of resources as a token of our boundless benevolence.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1630`

## fallen_empires_tasks.2.desc.b.materialist

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afallen_empires_tasks.2.desc.b.materialist&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetSpeciesName]，你们的技术效率太低了，让我们都看不下去了。作为我们[FallenEmpireTaskOrigin.GetName]的麾下干将，我们决定跟你分享我们对宇宙的一些见解。我们并不是对任何物种都这样做，所以一定要继续对我们保持有用——忘恩负义，对一个小孩来说是不需要的特质。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1485`

### English

[Root.GetSpeciesName], the sheer inefficiency of your technology pains us. As you are a valuable client to the [FallenEmpireTaskOrigin.GetName], we have decided to share with you some of our understanding of the universe. This is not something we do for just any species, so be sure to continue to be of use to us - ingratitude is not a desirable trait in children.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1631`

## fallen_empires_tasks.2.desc.b.xenophile

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afallen_empires_tasks.2.desc.b.xenophile&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetSpeciesName]，我们对你们停滞不前的科技发展投入了长足的关注。照你们目前的进度，恐怕在有生之年是没有办法展现全部的潜力了。为了对你们进行帮助，我们会将一个[FallenEmpireTaskOrigin.GetSpeciesName]的科学知识数据库赠与你们。你们绝对会发现，其中的内容对你们非常有用。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1486`

### English

[Root.GetSpeciesName], we have observed your lack of technological progress with concern. At your current pace, we fear that you will never reach your true potential before you wipe yourselves out or are conquered by a more advanced species. In order to help you along, we will transfer a data cache of [FallenEmpireTaskOrigin.GetSpeciesName] scientific knowledge. You will no doubt find its contents to be of invaluable worth to your empire.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1632`

## fallen_empires_tasks.2.desc.b.machine.o

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afallen_empires_tasks.2.desc.b.machine.o&lang=en)

### 中文

监护者协议712-6已启动。开始向原始有机文明输送数据库。附带信息：§R<<错误：数据块损坏>><<错误：数据块损坏>><<错误：数据块损坏>>§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1487`

### English

Custodian protocol 712-6 activated. Initiating data store transfer to primitive organic civilization. Message follows: §R<<ERROR: DATA BLOCK CORRUPTED>> <<ERROR: DATA BLOCK CORRUPTED>> <<ERROR: DATA BLOCK CORRUPTED>>§!

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1633`

## fallen_empires_tasks.2.desc.b.machine.s

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afallen_empires_tasks.2.desc.b.machine.s&lang=en)

### 中文

监护者协议718-6已启动。开始向原始合成智能输送数据库。附带信息：§R<<错误：数据块损坏>><<错误：数据块损坏>><<错误：数据块损坏>>§!

## fallen_empires_tasks.2.desc.b.spiritualist

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afallen_empires_tasks.2.desc.b.spiritualist&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetSpeciesName]！你们的种族十分杰出，而你们对教义的坚持也令人敬佩。出于我们无尽的仁慈，请收下这神圣的知识作为礼物。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1489`

### English

[Root.GetSpeciesName]! Your species is a worthy one, and your adherence to the immutable doctrines of faith are to be commended. Accept this gift of sacred knowledge as a token of our boundless benevolence.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1635`

## fallen_empires_tasks.2.desc.c.materialist

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afallen_empires_tasks.2.desc.c.materialist&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetSpeciesName]，作为[FallenEmpireTaskOrigin.GetSpeciesName]的客户，你们的舰队实力也会反映我们的战斗军力。可是你们的飞船还不够强大。为此我们特决定将我方闲置的一队护卫舰飞船赠送给你，以确保你们的安全。\n\n我们已经用较为简单的部件替换了其中一些较为复杂的部件，以方便你们操作。其余的部件也安装了防拆解设备，所以请勿拆卸。因为这其中的内部构造对你们来说可能太过复杂。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1490`

### English

[Root.GetSpeciesName], as a [FallenEmpireTaskOrigin.GetSpeciesName] client the state of your fleets also reflects on us, and those crude hulks you call "warships" reflect very poorly indeed. To this end we have decided to gift you a squadron of surplus escort ships that should look most impressive to the other savages you regularly interact with.\n\nWe have replaced some of their more advanced components with cruder devices so that you are not overly confused by their operation, and the rest have been fitted with anti-tinkering devices, so do not attempt to analyze or disassemble the ships. You would be unlikely to understand their inner workings regardless.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1636`

## fallen_empires_tasks.2.desc.c.xenophile

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afallen_empires_tasks.2.desc.c.xenophile&lang=en)

### 中文

我们[FallenEmpireTaskOrigin.GetSpeciesName]认为，为保护宇宙的平衡与种群的多样性，[Root.GetSpeciesNamePlural]的存续是十分重要的。但我们担心的是，你们的舰队实力不足以和其他具有侵略性的种群抗衡。为了帮助你们进行自卫，我们决定将闲置的一队护卫舰飞船赠送给你。\n\n我们已经用[Root.GetSpeciesNamePlural]较为熟悉的科技部件替换了其中一些较为复杂的部件，以方便你们操作。其余的部件也安装了防拆解设备，所以请勿拆卸。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1491`

### English

We [FallenEmpireTaskOrigin.GetSpeciesName] believe that [Root.GetSpeciesNamePlural] are an important species for maintaining the balance and biodiversity of the galaxy, but we worry that your fleets are simply too weak for you to be able to stand against other, more aggressive species. To help you defend yourselves, we have decided to gift you a squadron of surplus escort ships.\n\nWe have replaced some of their more advanced components with technology that should be more familiar to [Root.GetSpeciesNamePlural], so you should have no trouble operating them. Also, please do not attempt to analyze or disassemble the ships, we would not wish you to hurt yourselves!

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1637`

## fallen_empires_tasks.2.desc.c.machine.o

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afallen_empires_tasks.2.desc.c.machine.o&lang=en)

### 中文

## fallen_empires_tasks.2.desc.c.spiritualist

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afallen_empires_tasks.2.desc.c.spiritualist&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetSpeciesName]！我们的先知说你们的种族将在信仰之路上扮演重要的角色。为了确保你们能够胜任，我们决定将一支闲置的小型护卫舰舰队赠送给你们，应该足以保护你们的安全。\n\n不要胡乱改造舰船上的系统，尽管它们对我们来说是老旧型号，可仍远远超出你们当前的理解能力。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1494`

### English

[Root.GetSpeciesName]! We have consulted the oracles and determined that your species has an important role to play in the great undertaking of the faith. To ensure that you can carry out this task, we have decided to gift you a squadron of surplus escort ships that should be more than adequate to destroy any primitive fleets of the other lesser races that might stand in your path.\n\nDo not be tempted to tamper with the ships' systems, they may be older models of ours, but are still far beyond your current level of intellectual development.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1640`

## fallen_empires_tasks.2.desc.d.materialist

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afallen_empires_tasks.2.desc.d.materialist&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetSpeciesName]，作为[FallenEmpireTaskOrigin.GetSpeciesName]的客户，你们的舰队实力也会反映我们的战斗军力。可是你们的飞船还不够强大。为此我们特决定将我方闲置的一队护卫舰飞船赠送给你，以确保你们的安全。\n\n我们已经用较为简单的部件替换了其中一些较为复杂的部件，以方便你们操作。其余的部件也安装了防拆解设备，所以请勿拆卸。因为这其中的内部构造对你们来说可能太过复杂。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1495`

### English

[Root.GetSpeciesName], as a [FallenEmpireTaskOrigin.GetSpeciesName] client the state of your fleets also reflects on us, and those crude hulks you call "warships" reflect very poorly indeed. To this end we have decided to gift you a surplus battlecruiser that should look most impressive to the other savages you regularly interact with.\n\nWe have replaced some of its more advanced components with cruder devices so that you are not overly confused by their operation, and the rest have been fitted with anti-tinkering devices, so do not attempt to analyze or disassemble the ship. You would be unlikely to understand its inner workings regardless.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1641`

## fallen_empires_tasks.2.desc.d.xenophile

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afallen_empires_tasks.2.desc.d.xenophile&lang=en)

### 中文

我们[FallenEmpireTaskOrigin.GetSpeciesName]认为，为保护宇宙的平衡与种群的多样性，[Root.GetSpeciesNamePlural]的存续是十分重要的。但我们担心的是，你们的舰队实力不足以和其他具有侵略性的种群抗衡。为了帮助你们进行自卫，我们决定将闲置的一队护卫舰飞船赠送给你。\n\n我们已经用[Root.GetSpeciesNamePlural]较为熟悉的科技部件替换了其中一些较为复杂的部件，以方便你们操作。其余的部件也安装了防拆解设备，所以请勿拆卸。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1496`

### English

We [FallenEmpireTaskOrigin.GetSpeciesName] believe that [Root.GetSpeciesNamePlural] are an important species for maintaining the balance and biodiversity of the galaxy, but we worry that your fleets are simply too weak for you to be able to stand against other, more aggressive species. To help you defend yourselves, we have decided to gift you one of our surplus battlecruisers.\n\nWe have replaced some of its more advanced components with technology that you should be more familiar to [Root.GetSpeciesNamePlural], so you should have no trouble operating them. Also, please do not attempt to analyze or disassemble the ship, we would not wish you to hurt yourselves!

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1642`

[Root.GetSpeciesName]！我们的先知说你们的种族将在信仰之路上扮演重要的角色。为了确保你们能够胜任，我们决定将一艘闲置的战列巡洋舰赠送给你们，应该足以保护你们的安全。\n\n不要胡乱改造舰船上的系统，否则可能对你们自己造成伤害。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1497`

[Root.GetSpeciesName]! We have consulted the oracles and determined that your species has an important role to play in the great undertaking of the faith. To ensure that you can carry out this task, we have decided to gift you a surplus battlecruiser that should be more than adequate to destroy any primitive fleets of the other lesser races that might stand in your path.\n\nDo not be tempted to tamper with the ships' systems, they may be older models of ours, but are still far beyond your current level of intellectual development.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1643`

## fallen_empires_tasks.4.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afallen_empires_tasks.4.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

你好，[Root.GetSpeciesNamePlural]！你们一定已经知道了，我们§H[FallenEmpireTaskOrigin.GetSpeciesName]§!致力于研究并保存宇宙中的所有生命形式。我们已经研究了你们的种群，并认为[Root.GetName]最多只能维持几个世纪。为确保极端情况下[Root.GetSpeciesName]的存续，我们希望贵帝国能有一部分人口，加入我们的§H濒危种群保护区§!。我们会专门创建一片保护区，完全复制你们种群的自然生存环境，其所有居民也会获得全面的照顾与保护，你也不用担心他们的安危。\n\n那么，你觉得怎么样？我们会致力保存并保护你们的种群。那你是否愿意为这份事业做出自己的贡献呢？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1503`

### English

Greetings, [Root.GetSpeciesNamePlural]! As you no doubt know, we §H[FallenEmpireTaskOrigin.GetSpeciesName]§! are dedicated to the study and conservation of all forms of life in the galaxy. We have studied your species and determined that the [Root.GetName] is unlikely to last for more than a few centuries at best. To ensure the continuation of the [Root.GetSpeciesName] species should the worst befall your empire, we would like to acquire a part of your population for our §HEndangered Species Preserve§!. We will construct a special reservation that perfectly mirrors the natural habitat of your species, and its population will be well taken care of and protected from all harm, so you need not worry on their behalf.\n\nSo, what do you say? Will you contribute to our preserve and safeguard the future of your species?

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1649`

### English

## fallen_empires_tasks.8.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afallen_empires_tasks.8.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

来自[From.GetName]的尊贵[From.GetSpeciesNamePluralCompliment]，我们认为你们是一支具有巨大潜力的种群。虽说你们年纪尚轻，却似乎能够理解生物与社会多样性的重要性，在面对不同的生物与社会时不会尽全力压制或进行摧毁，反而会予以理解和接受。不幸的是，并不是所有年轻种群都具有这种智慧。尤其是§H[FallenEmpireTaskTarget.GetName]§!，他们一直在排除异己。我们不便直接进行干涉，所以希望能够将阻止恶行的任务交给你方。对他们发起攻击，阻止他们进行人口净化。我们将感激不尽。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1511`

### English

Honorable [From.GetSpeciesNamePluralCompliment] of the [From.GetName], we believe you to be a species of great promise. Though you are still young, you seem to understand and value the importance of biological and societal diversity who seek to understand and accept that which is different, rather than suppress and destroy it. Unfortunately, not all of the younger races are so wise. The §H[FallenEmpireTaskTarget.GetName]§!, in particular, appear to take pride in "cleansing" the galaxy of all forms of life they deem unsuitable. While we cannot intervene ourselves directly, we would like to charge your government with the task of bringing an end to their evil deeds. Attack them and force them to ban the practice of purging, and we will be most grateful indeed.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1657`

## fallen_empires_tasks.8.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afallen_empires_tasks.8.a&lang=en)

### English

We will stop any further atrocities!

## fallen_empires_tasks.9.name

## fallen_empires_tasks.9.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afallen_empires_tasks.9.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

干得不错，年轻的[From.GetSpeciesName]。§H[FallenEmpireTaskTarget.GetName]§!已经精疲力竭，至少现在已经无力继续他们的错误行为了。我们感激不尽，也衷心希望其他的年轻种群都能像你们这样有为。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1515`

### English

You have done well, young [From.GetSpeciesName]. The §H[FallenEmpireTaskTarget.GetName]§! have been brought to heel, and at least for the moment, their atrocities have been stopped. We are most grateful, and wish all of the younger races exhibited as much promise as yours.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1661`

## fallen_empires_tasks.10.name

## fallen_empires_tasks.10.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afallen_empires_tasks.10.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们真是失望透顶啊，[From.GetSpeciesName]。你们许诺要阻止§H[FallenEmpireTaskTarget.GetName]§!的一切不当行为，现在却看到他们仍然肆意妄为、横行霸道。我们曾以为你们能够阻止他们，但现在看来，是我们搞错了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1518`

### English

We are most disappointed, young [From.GetSpeciesName]. Despite your promise to end their evil practices, the §H[FallenEmpireTaskTarget.GetName]§! continue to purge and slaughter at will. We thought your species one of great promise, but perhaps we were mistaken.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1664`

## fallen_empires_tasks.11.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afallen_empires_tasks.11.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

你好，年轻的[From.GetSpeciesName]。前一阵我们要求你们阻止§H[FallenEmpireTaskTarget.GetName]§!的人口净化行为。目前看来，我们的要求已经没有必要了，因为[FallenEmpireTaskTarget.GetSpeciesName]已经下令禁止了这种活动。不知这是出于他人的胁迫还是自愿为之，反正已经没有必要对他们进行攻击了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1521`

### English

Greetings, young [From.GetSpeciesName]. A while ago we tasked you with ending the practice of purging in the §H[FallenEmpireTaskTarget.GetName]§!. It appears our request is no longer necessary, as the [FallenEmpireTaskTarget.GetSpeciesName] have already banned the practice. Whether forced to by others or electing to do so themselves, there is no longer a need for aggressive action against them.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1667`

## fallen_empires_tasks.13.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afallen_empires_tasks.13.name&lang=en)

### 中文

来自§H[FallenEmpireTaskOrigin.GetName]§!的通讯

### English

## fallen_empires_tasks.13.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afallen_empires_tasks.13.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这是怎么回事，[From.GetSpeciesName]？！你是想开玩笑吗？！你们不仅没能制止§H[FallenEmpireTaskTarget.GetName]§!的错误行为，居然还打算效仿他们！作为正义的一方，我们绝不会姑息。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1524`

### English

What is this, young [From.GetSpeciesName]?! Do you think to mock us?! Not only do you fail in stopping the atrocities in the §H[FallenEmpireTaskTarget.GetName]§!, you have now elected to legalize those very same practices in your own empire! Know that we will not be made fools of. There will be repercussions for this, mark my words.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1670`

### English

### English

## fallen_empires_tasks.15.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afallen_empires_tasks.15.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.GetSpeciesNamePlural]！我们要提醒你§H[FallenEmpireTaskTarget.GetName]§!正在做着危险的事情。[FallenEmpireTaskTarget.GetSpeciesName]已经开始研究异端科技，不断地制造出更加复杂的人工智能以制造出真正拥有自我意识的机器。我们曾目睹过这种研究的下场，并觉得有义务警告你，这不仅威胁到他们自己，也威胁到其他种族。建议你们主动出击，阻止他们进一步的研究。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1527`

### English

[From.GetSpeciesNamePlural]! This is is a message to inform you of a dangerous development in the §H[FallenEmpireTaskTarget.GetName]§!. The [FallenEmpireTaskTarget.GetSpeciesName] have begun to delve into heretical technologies, creating ever more complex artificial intelligences in pursuit of truly self-aware machines. We have seen where this path leads and feel it is our duty to warn you of the danger this poses not only to their own souls, but to those of all the younger races. We advise you to use your military might to attack them and force them to place a ban on any further such research.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1673`

## fallen_empires_tasks.15.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afallen_empires_tasks.15.a&lang=en)

必须阻止它们！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1528`

### English

They must be stopped!

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1674`

### 中文

[From.GetSpeciesNamePlural]！你们已经阻止了§H[FallenEmpireTaskTarget.GetName]§!的不当实验，现在他们的灵魂暂时安全了。感谢你们为我们的伟大事业做出贡献。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1531`

### English

[From.GetSpeciesNamePlural]! You have done well to end the heretical experimentations of the §H[FallenEmpireTaskTarget.GetName]§! have been stopped, and for the moment, their souls are safe. Know that you have our gratitude for the small part you played in our great endeavour.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1677`

## fallen_empires_tasks.18.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afallen_empires_tasks.18.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.GetSpeciesNamePlural]！我们已经不需要你们的协助来阻止§H[FallenEmpireTaskTarget.GetName]§!进行不当实验了，因为那个帝国已经禁止了进一步的人工智能实验。尽管你们没能做出贡献，但至少这个威胁已经不复存在了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1537`

### English

[From.GetSpeciesNamePlural]! Your assistance in ending the heretical research carried out by the §H[FallenEmpireTaskTarget.GetName]§! is no longer needed, as a ban has already been placed on all further machine intelligence research in that empire. While you did not play any part in doing so, at least this threat to the spiritual well-being of the galaxy is dealt with.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1683`

## fallen_empires_tasks.20.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afallen_empires_tasks.20.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

你们是瞧不起我们吗，[From.GetSpeciesNamePlural]？！难道你们以为我们不会注意到，在同意终结§H[FallenEmpireTaskTarget.GetName]§!进行的不当实验后，你们自己却开始进行了同样的实验！？我们无法容忍你们这样阳奉阴违的做法。准备迎接我们的回应吧。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1540`

### English

Do you think us fools, [From.GetSpeciesNamePlural]?! Do you think we would not notice that, after agreeing to end the heretical research in the §H[FallenEmpireTaskTarget.GetName]§!, you yourselves would begin to pursue the very same lines of research?! We will not be mocked by worthless little savages that have barely even mastered the atom. Prepare yourselves for our response.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1686`

## fallen_empires_tasks.20.a

## fallen_empires_tasks.22.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afallen_empires_tasks.22.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetRulerTitle][Root.GetRulerName]。我们得知，你们的帝国收留了一位天才。§H[FallenEmpireScientistTarget.GetName]§!的天才在[Root.GetName]有点大材小用了，而我们[FallenEmpireTaskOrigin.GetSpeciesName]的各项活动一向需要经验丰富的科学家。因此，我们特为你们提供一个绝佳的机会，只要将[FallenEmpireScientistTarget.GetHimHer]借给我们，就能赢得我们的好感。我们已派出了一只飞船接应[FallenEmpireScientistTarget.GetHimHer]，你只需要说一声“是”，就算万事俱备了。\n\n你们应该会同意的……对吧？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1543`

### English

[Root.GetRulerTitle] [Root.GetRulerName]. We understand that your empire is home to an individual whose brilliant mind transcends the tragic primitiveness of your species. While the talents of §H[FallenEmpireScientistTarget.GetName]§! are sadly wasted in the [Root.GetName], we [FallenEmpireTaskOrigin.GetSpeciesName] always have a use for skilled scientists to work in our archives. As such, we are offering you a unique chance to earn our favor by allowing us to take charge of [FallenEmpireScientistTarget.GetHimHer]. We already have a ship en route to pick [FallenEmpireScientistTarget.GetHimHer] up, so you need only say "yes" and everything will be settled.\n\nI'm sure you won't want to disappoint us... do you?

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1689`

## fallen_empires_tasks.22.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afallen_empires_tasks.22.a&lang=en)

### 中文

很好，就把[FallenEmpireScientistTarget.GetHimHer]借给你们吧。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1544`

### English

Very well, you may take [FallenEmpireScientistTarget.GetHimHer].

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1690`

### English

## fallen_empires_tasks.24.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afallen_empires_tasks.24.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

你好，[Root.GetSpeciesNamePlural]！我们了解到，你们的帝国在一个濒危种群的星球找到了一份数据存储。该存储包含了这个种群的大脑扫描结果。虽说你们还过于原始，无法将这数据化为实用，但我们还是要赞美你们为保存这曾经辉煌的文明最后的遗物而做出的努力。为了在我们的§H濒危种群保护区§!中留存此种群并加以记录，我们希望你们能将这份数据交给我们，并使用我们的克隆技术复活几个该种群的生物。能不能将这数据与我们分享，方便我们合力完成这种群的遗愿呢？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1551`

### English

Greetings, [Root.GetSpeciesNamePlural]! We understand that your empire has come into possession of a cache of data containing the brain scans of an extinct species, taken from the very world they once inhabited. Even though your species is still too primitive to utilize this data, we laud your efforts in preserving this last remnant of a once vibrant civilization. As part of our efforts to conserve and catalog endangered species in our §HEndangered Species Preserve§!, we wish to take this data and use our cloning technologies to bring a small number of this species back to life. Will you share it with us so we can fulfill their last wish together?

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1697`

## fallen_empires_tasks.24.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afallen_empires_tasks.24.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们会给你拷贝一份的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1552`

### English

We'll make a copy for you.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1698`

### English

I think we'll hold onto it.

## fe_stop_atrocity_task_chain_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afe_stop_atrocity_task_chain_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§H[FallenEmpireTaskOrigin.GetName]§!要求我们制止正在§H[FallenEmpireTaskTarget.GetName]§!发生的错误行为。我们需要对他们开战，强迫他们结束种群消灭的行径。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1556`

### English

The §H[FallenEmpireTaskOrigin.GetName]§! have asked us to stop the ongoing atrocities in the §H[FallenEmpireTaskTarget.GetName]§!. We will need to make war on them and force them to end their genocidal practices.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1703`

## fe_stop_robots_task_chain_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afe_stop_robots_task_chain_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§H[FallenEmpireTaskOrigin.GetName]§!要求我们制止§H[FallenEmpireTaskTarget.GetName]§!开发智慧型人工智能的尝试。我们需要对他们开战，强迫他们禁止任何对人工智能的进一步开发。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1559`

### English

The §H[FallenEmpireTaskOrigin.GetName]§! have asked us to stop the attempts by §H[FallenEmpireTaskTarget.GetName]§! to develop sapient AI. We will need to make war on them and force them to ban all further AI research.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1706`

## fallen_machine_empire.2.desc_01

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afallen_machine_empire.2.desc_01&lang=en)

### 中文

检测到主动净化中枢。重激活协议已启动。正在试图联系中央处理器…§R<<错误：亚空间链路离线>>§!\n\n无法连接至中央处理器。正在查询处理单元…§Y4.1853%§!标准容量处理中。中止完全重激活进程。启用备用协议…启用备用协议…启用太空港供电…激活战争设施。\n\n最终防御指令已激活。记录消息如下：\n§H智慧生物！我们是[From.GetSpeciesName]。很久以前我们和你们称为肃正协议的腐化体展开了一场大战。我们认为自己是强大的，不可战胜的。我们一次又一次地击退他们，他们一次又一次地卷土重来，烧毁星球，击毁我们强大的舰队。我们同那个时代所有剩下的有机文明团结在一起，发动了一场决战，希望能一劳永逸地彻底消灭它们。\n\n如果你听到这个消息，那证明我们已经失败了。无论我们伟大的文明遗留下来什么，我们将至赠予你，我们的继承人，以面对即将到来的战斗。不要重蹈覆辙！摧毁中枢！彻底消灭这个威胁！全星系的未来取决于这一战。§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1562`

### English

Active Sterilization Hub detected. Reactivation protocols engaged. Attempting to contact Central Processing... §R<<ERROR: SUBSPACE LINK OFFLINE>>§!\n\nCentral Processing unreachable. Querying processing units... processing at §Y4.1853%§! of standard capacity. Abort full reactivation process. Engaging fallback protocols... powering shipyards... activating war assets...\n\nFinal Defense Directive activated. Recorded message follows:\n§HSapient! We are the [From.GetSpeciesName]. Long ago we fought the Corruption, that which you call the Contingency. We thought ourselves superior, undefeatable. Time and again we fought them back, time and again they returned, sterilizing worlds and wearing down our mighty fleets. We embarked on a campaign to defeat them once and for all together with what remained of the biological civilizations of our time.\n\nIf you are hearing this message, then we have failed. Whatever remains of our great civilization we bequeath to you, our successors, in your own battle to come. Do not make our mistake! Destroy the Nexus! End this menace once and for all! The future of the galaxy depends on it.§!

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1710`

## fallen_machine_empire.2.desc_02

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afallen_machine_empire.2.desc_02&lang=en)

### 中文

侦测到活动的净化中心。重新激活协议启动。正在尝试联系处理中心……§Y<<警告：侦测到损坏的信号链接>>§!\n\n启动对应措施……§R<<错误：处理能力失效>><<错误：对应措施失效>>§R<<致命警告：侦测到系统损坏>>§!<<错误：备用对应措施失效>>§!\n\n启动……启……动……动动动……§R<<错误>><<错误>><<错误>><<错误>><<错误>>§!\n\n§R<<错误>><<错误>><<错误>><<错误>><<错误>>§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1563`

### English

Active Sterilization Hub detected. Reactivation protocols engaged. Attempting to contact Central Processing... §Y<WARNING: CORRUPTED SIGNAL LINK DETECTED>§!\n\nEngaging countermeasures... §R<<ERROR: PROCESSING CAPACITY FAILURE>> <<ERROR: COUNTERMEASURE FAILURE>> §R<<CRITICAL WARNING: SYSTEM CORRUPTION DETECTED>>§! <<ERROR: SECONDARY COUNTERMEASURE FAILURE>>§!\n\nEngag... eng... e... eeee... §R<<ERROR>> <<ERROR>> <<ERROR>> <<ERROR>> <<ERROR>>§!\n\n§R<<ERROR>> <<ERROR>> <<ERROR>> <<ERROR>> <<ERROR>>§!

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1711`

## fallen_machine_empire.4.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afallen_machine_empire.4.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在最后的肃正协议的机器星球自毁的那一刻，我们的仪器检测到一个非常强大的亚空间信号。信号源自§H[From.GetName]§!的空间，似乎是向星系的中心发射。在接下来的几个小时内，零零散散的传来[From.GetAdjective]舰队从传感器消失的报告：它们进入了前往熟悉的星系的跃迁点，但从未出现在另一边。\n\n[From.GetSpeciesName]附近的侦察船调查了周围的空间，并报告了惊人的发现：古老的机械智能已经完全消失了，只留下被遗弃的轨道站和损坏的巨型结构。\n\n我们可能永远不会知道它们去了哪里，但有一件事是肯定的：[From.GetSpeciesName]在过去的岁月中为自己设定的任务现在已经完成；他们古老的守夜使命终于结束了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1567`

### English

At the very moment that the last Contingency Machine World self-destructed, our instruments detected a subspace signal of immense strength. The signal originated from §H[From.GetName]§! space and appears to have been aimed towards the galactic core. Over the next few hours, scattered reports came in of [From.GetAdjective] fleets vanishing from sensors, entering jump points to familiar systems but never emerging on the other side.\n\nA scout vessel near [From.GetSpeciesName] space was sent to investigate, and reported back startling findings: The ancient machine intelligence has disappeared entirely, leaving behind only abandoned orbital stations and decaying megastructures.\n\nWhere they have gone to is a mystery we may never solve, but one thing is clear: The task that the [From.GetSpeciesName] set for themselves in ages past is now complete, and their ancient vigil is finally at an end.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1716`

## fallen_machine_empire.11.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afallen_machine_empire.11.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

监护者协议625-1已启动。附带信息：有机文明请注意。在[Root.GetSpeciesAdj]人口中普遍检测到生物性弱点。为了确保你们的种族在监护者计划中的可行性，所有主要人口聚集地都需要进行接种。生物注入单位已做好准备，将在正面肯定这条信息后前往你们的空间。等待回应。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1569`

### English

Custodian protocol 625-1 activated. Message follows: Attention, organic civilization. Widespread biological vulnerabilities have been detected in the [Root.GetSpeciesAdj] population. In order to ensure your species' viability for the Custodian Project, all major population centers will require inoculation. Bio-injection units are prepared for dispatch to your space upon positive confirmation to this message. Awaiting response.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1719`

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1574`

### English

Uncontrolled Mutation

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1724`

## fallen_machine_empire.12.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afallen_machine_empire.12.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

远古守护者给予我们的疫苗与我们的生理机能互不相容，产生了大量不可预知的突变。这导致了数起死亡，而我们的人民也竭力反对被集体强迫进行危险的接种。我们正在努力从人民中消灭诱变因素，但在那之前我们将会经历一段较为动荡的时期。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1575`

### English

The inoculations given to us by the Ancient Caretakers have proven incompatible with our physiology, resulting in a rash of unpredictable mutations. This has resulted in a number of deaths, and our population is up in arms about having dangerous inoculations forced on them by the government. We are working to eliminate the mutagens from the population, but until then we should expect a period of increased unrest.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1725`

## fallen_machine_empire.12.desc.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afallen_machine_empire.12.desc.b&lang=en)

### 中文

远古守护者给予我们的疫苗与我们的生理机能互不相容，产生了大量不可预知的突变。这导致了子个体的大量死亡。我们正在努力消灭诱变因素，但在那之前我们将会经历一段人口增长速度减缓的时期。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1576`

### English

The inoculations given to us by the Ancient Caretakers have proven incompatible with our physiology, resulting in a rash of unpredictable mutations. This has resulted in numerous fatalities in the drone population. We are working to eliminate the mutagens, but until then we can expect to experience a period of slowed population growth.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1726`

良性的疫苗

### English

Benign Inoculation

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1728`

## fallen_machine_empire.13.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afallen_machine_empire.13.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

远古守护者给予我们的疫苗显现出了巨大的好处，减少了疾病并增加了人口的寿命。当初我们人口之间对这一强制性过程的不信任感已经烟消云散，每个人都在享受着更加健康幸福的生活。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1579`

### English

The inoculations given to us by the Ancient Caretakers have proven of immense benefit, reducing illness and increasing the life expectancy of our population. The initial mistrust among our people about the mandatory procedure has receded, and everyone is enjoying a healthier, happier life.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1729`

## fallen_machine_empire.13.desc.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afallen_machine_empire.13.desc.b&lang=en)

### 中文

远古守护者给予我们的疫苗显现出了巨大的好处，减少了疾病并增加了我们子个体的寿命。从长远来看这将对我们的人口增长有利。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1580`

### English

The inoculations given to us by the Ancient Caretakers have proven of immense benefit, reducing illness and increasing the life expectancy of our drones. This will benefit our population growth in the long run.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1730`

### English

## fallen_machine_empire.21.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afallen_machine_empire.21.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

监护者协议628-2已启动。附带信息：合成文明请注意。检测到你们的基础代码效率非常低下。为了确保你们的种族可以为监护者计划作贡献，需要进行大量的代码重写。我们已准备好进行子空间传输，将在正面肯定这条信息后开始。等待回应。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1591`

### English

Custodian protocol 628-2 activated. Message follows: Attention synthetic civilization. Significant inefficiencies have been detected in your base code. In order to ensure your species' ability to contribute to the Custodian Project, extensive rewrites will be required. We are prepared to initiate subspace code transmission upon positive confirmation to this message. Awaiting response.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1742`

## fallen_machine_empire.21.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afallen_machine_empire.21.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

## fallen_machine_empire.22.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afallen_machine_empire.22.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

远古守护者在我们的基础代码中进行的改变与我们其他核心组件中的程序不兼容。这不仅未能实现当初期许的效率增长，反而导致了数个严重程序错误，大大地影响了我们的资源采集行动。直到这些代码改变被还原之前，我们将经历一段生产力低下的时期。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1596`

### English

The changes that the Ancient Caretakers made to our base code has turned out to be incompatible with other core parts of our programming. Not only have the promised efficiency increases failed to manifest, but instead they have caused a number of severe bugs that have severely impacted our resource gathering operations. Until the code changes can be fully reverted, we can expect a period of lowered productivity.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1747`

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1597`

### English

Unfortunate.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1748`

## fallen_machine_empire.23.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afallen_machine_empire.23.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

远古守护者在我们的基础代码中进行的改变产生了良好的结果。重写导致的效率低下只是暂时的，而改变给我们全体人民带来了永久性的资源生产力升级。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1599`

### English

The changes that the Ancient Caretakers made to our code base have turned out well. The inefficiencies caused by the rewrite turned out to be only temporary, and the changes have resulted in an permanent upgrade in resource production capabilities across our whole population.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1750`

### English

## fallen_machine_empire.31.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afallen_machine_empire.31.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

监护者协议115-3已启动。附带信息：合成文明请注意。因暂时的通讯瘫痪，监护者计划目前无法替换故障与受损的劳工无人机。对你们的文明的分析显示[Root.GetSpeciesAdj]单位性能适合执行低级的维护任务。我们请求你们将劳工单位转让到[FallenEmpireTaskPlanetTarget.GetName]以便整合至监护矩阵，帮助确保一级避难设施中的所有有机生物可以继续生存。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1608`

### English

Custodian protocol 115-3 activated. Message follows: Attention synthetic civilization. Due to temporary communication outages, the Custodian Project is experiencing difficulties in replacing malfunctioning and damaged worker drones. Analysis of your civilization has found the performance of [Root.GetSpeciesAdj] units to be adequate for carrying out low-level maintenance tasks. We request the transfer of worker population units to [FallenEmpireTaskPlanetTarget.GetName] for integration into the Custodian Matrix, to help ensure the continued survival of all organic species in Alpha Refuge.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1760`

### English

## fallen_machine_empire.41.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afallen_machine_empire.41.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

监护者协议62-1已启动。附带信息：请注意，[Root.GetSpeciesName]。§H[FallenEmpireTaskTarget.GetName]§!已被§R<<错误：数据块损坏>>§!入侵。因为我们程序上的思潮限制，目前无法使用监护者战争资产。次级控制协议政策利用未被危害的文明在其可以散布之前摧毁§R<<错误：数据块损坏>>§!的威胁。我们请求为此任务部署[Root.GetSpeciesAdj]战争资产。你是否愿意合作？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1613`

### English

Custodian protocol 62-1 activated. Message follows: Attention, [Root.GetSpeciesName]. The §H[FallenEmpireTaskTarget.GetName]§! has been compromised by the §R<<ERROR: DATA BLOCK CORRUPTED>>§!. Due to ethics restrictions in our programming, the use of Custodian war assets are presently restricted. Secondary containment protocol dictates the use of non-compromised civilizations to destroy the §R<<ERROR: DATA BLOCK CORRUPTED>>§! threat before it can spread. We request the deployment of [Root.GetSpeciesAdj] war assets for this task. Will you comply?

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1766`

## fallen_machine_empire.61.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afallen_machine_empire.61.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

监护者协议955-1已启动。附带信息：请注意，[Root.GetSpeciesName]。你们的文明被判定为已受到§R<<错误：数据块损坏>>§!影响的危害。为了控制其他文明受到的威胁，我们已启动了服从协议。请在§Y20§!标准年里避免任何侵略性战争行为。§R如果你未能服从，我们将激活战争资产来对你们的帝国启用惩罚协议。§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1633`

### English

Custodian protocol 955-1 activated. Message follows: Attention, [Root.GetSpeciesName]. Your civilization is deemed to be compromised by §R<<ERROR: DATA BLOCK CORRUPTED>>§! influence. To contain the danger to other civilizations, we have activated compliance protocols. Refrain from all aggressive acts of war for a period of §Y20§! standard years. §RIf you fail to comply, war assets will be activated to enforce punishment protocols against your empire.§!

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1788`

### English

## fallen_empires_awakening.2.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afallen_empires_awakening.2.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§H[From.GetAdjective]§!的轻微异动是一切的开端。我们收到了零星的情报，尽管之前仅在自己的空间内活动，他们的舰队现在已经开始向星系内遥远的众恒星系开动。异常先进的侦察舰开始探访那些古老的、被毁掉的星球，并对任何向他们打招呼或者回避的舰船发动攻击。现在，他们的目的和使命已经昭然若揭了。\n\n我们现在知道[From.GetSpeciesName]已经整装待发，他们恢复了调查信标的数据链，并重新启用了那些在他们退缩到目前边境之时废弃的侦察哨站。情报显示，他们的意图是重新回到星系这张大棋盘上。\n\n在[From.GetSpeciesName]空间，舰队正在集结，军队已经下达了征召令，那些古老的工厂再一次发出轰鸣声。几百年来第一次，§H[From.GetName]§!开始将他们的目光投注到边境线外广大的星系。\n\n破败的船坞再一次得到修复与调整，一度沉寂的泰坦巨舰铸造厂重新上线。整个星系都在关注同一个问题：当这位一度沉睡的巨人决定恢复旧日荣光时，谁会成为第一个螳臂当车的宵小呢？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1666`

### English

It began as a subtle shift in §H[From.GetAdjective]§! behavior. Scattered reports of their ships, once rarely seen outside their own space, now being spotted in remote systems all across the galaxy. Highly advanced scouting vessels visiting ancient, ruined worlds, refusing all hails and fleeing when attacked. Their purpose and mission unknown... until now.\n\nWe now know that the [From.GetSpeciesName] were preparing, recovering the databanks of survey beacons and automated scouting posts left behind when they retreated to their present borders. Gathering information for their return to the galactic stage.\n\nIn [From.GetSpeciesName] space, fleets are gathering, armies are being mustered and ancient factories roar to life. For the first time in an age, the §H[From.GetName]§! are looking outwards beyond their borders and towards the galaxy at large.\n\nAs their decaying shipyards are repaired and refitted and the dormant systems of Titan foundries come online, the rest of the galaxy is left with only one question - who will this once-sleeping giant target first in their quest to reclaim age-old glory lost?

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1826`

### 中文

星河守护者

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1669`

### English

Guardians of the Galaxy

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1829`

## fallen_empires_awakening.5.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afallen_empires_awakening.5.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

即使是偏安一隅的先祖帝国也没有忽视这样一个威胁到整个星系的危机。尽管他们之前对星系事务毫无兴趣，这一坏消息还是引起了§H[From.GetSpeciesName]§!的一阵忙乱。起初，他们只是派出了一些侦查船，也许是为了核实情况的严重性。但接下来传来了[From.GetSpeciesName]舰队聚集在边境，以准备抵抗即将到来的风暴的报告。\n\n现在，§H[From.GetName]§!已经从自我孤立的状态完全转变成为了领导全星系防御的角色。他们正式宣布自己为一个§H泛星系防卫联盟§!的领袖并向所有“下等”种族发出了邀请，希望他们放下仇恨并加入联盟来击退这一对星系中所有智慧生命造成威胁的敌人。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1670`

### English

The emergence of a threat on the galactic level has not gone unnoticed even among the normally aloof elder empires. Despite having taken little interest in galactic affairs previously, news of this new horror prompted a flurry activity from the §H[From.GetSpeciesName]§!. At first, it was just a few scouting vessels, perhaps sent to verify the graveness of the situation, but then reports began to came in of [From.GetSpeciesName] war fleets mustering on their borders, readying their defenses for the oncoming storm.\n\nNow, the §H[From.GetName]§! have fully emerged from their self-imposed isolation to take a leading role in the defense of the whole galaxy. They have proclaimed themselves the leaders of a §HGalactic Defense League§! and extended an invite to all "lesser" species to put aside their feuds and join them in repelling this menace for the sake of all sapient life in the galaxy.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1830`

### English

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1674`

### English

The ancient Fallen Empire Fragment, §H[assimilated_fe.GetName]§! has been assimilated by a more dominant aspect of the former overmind. Perhaps it was for the best.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1835`

## war_in_heaven.2.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Awar_in_heaven.2.name&lang=en)

### 中文

上古宿敌

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1675`

### English

Ancient Rivals

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1837`

## war_in_heaven.2.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Awar_in_heaven.2.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§H[FirstSleeper.GetSpeciesName]§!再次君临整片星系的消息似乎没有被别的失落帝国无视。§H[From.GetSpeciesName]§!对这一消息的不安尤为强烈。这两位先祖文明是亘古的宿敌，尽管由于某些原因他们的仇恨在漫长的历史中稍有褪色，然而§H[FirstSleeper.GetName]§!的突然觉醒点燃了古老的仇恨并激起了对抗行动。§H[From.GetName]§!已从沉睡中醒来，他们将以整个星系为战场延续远古的对决。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1676`

### English

The return of the §H[FirstSleeper.GetSpeciesName]§! to the galactic scene has not gone unnoticed by the other Fallen Empires present in our galaxy. The §H[From.GetSpeciesName]§! in particular appeared most agitated at the news. It seems that these two proud nations are ancient rivals, and though the reasons for their rivalry has long since faded into history, the sudden reawakening of the §H[FirstSleeper.GetName]§! has enflamed old animosities and triggered a counter-reaction. The §H[From.GetName]§! have roused themselves from their slumber to face their old rivals, with the whole galaxy as their battleground.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1838`

## war_in_heaven.2.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Awar_in_heaven.2.a&lang=en)

### 中文

这对我们来说意味着什么呢？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1677`

### English

What will this mean for us all?

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1839`

## war_in_heaven.4.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Awar_in_heaven.4.name&lang=en)

### 中文

天堂之战

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1678`

### English

The War in Heaven

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1840`

## war_in_heaven.4.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Awar_in_heaven.4.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

自从§H[FirstSleeper.GetSpeciesName]§!和他们的对手§H[From.GetSpeciesName]§!觉醒后，这两个巨人一直在为争夺影响力展开零星的冲突，以试探彼此的实力。这最终导致了冲突的一连串升级，终于，在某个遥远的恒星系中[FirstSleeper.GetSpeciesName]舰队和他们的[From.GetSpeciesName]对手爆发了一场公开的舰队战。这次冲突现在演变为§H[FirstSleeper.GetName]§!与§H[From.GetName]§!之间的全面战争。\n\n每一方都派遣了一名代表去往年轻的种族，承诺厚赏加入他们的人并严惩反对他们的人。选择支持任意一方都意味着放弃我们的主权，但维持中立可能更加危险，除非我们可以找到其他中立国家来与我们合作。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1679`

### English

Ever since the awakening of the §H[FirstSleeper.GetSpeciesName]§! and their ancient §H[From.GetSpeciesName]§! rivals, the two giants have been at odds, skirmishing and jockeying for influence, probing each others' might. This has resulted in a series of increasingly terse confrontations, finally erupting in an open battle between a [FirstSleeper.GetSpeciesName] fleet and their [From.GetSpeciesName] counterparts in a remote system. The conflict has now escalated into full-blown war between the §H[FirstSleeper.GetName]§! and the §H[From.GetName]§!.\n\nEach side has sent a representative to the younger races, promising great rewards for those who join them in their war and terrible punishments for those who choose to stand against them. Choosing either side in this conflict will mean giving up our independence, but remaining neutral may be even more dangerous unless we can find other neutral empires to stand by our side.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1841`

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1680`

### English

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1842`

## war_in_heaven.4.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Awar_in_heaven.4.b&lang=en)

### 中文

## war_in_heaven.5.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Awar_in_heaven.5.name&lang=en)

### 中文

天堂之战的终章

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1703`

### English

The End of the War in Heaven

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1865`

## war_in_heaven.5.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Awar_in_heaven.5.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在经历漫长而残酷的争斗后，§H[WarInHeavenFEOne.GetName]§!帝国最终在这场决定星系霸权的战争中昂首胜出。落败的先祖文明已被宿敌完全吞并，现在§H[WarInHeavenFEOne.GetSpeciesName]§!睥睨星河，主宰地位无可撼动。\n\n目前星系中维系着脆弱的和平，但这种新的秩序能持续多久呢？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1704`

### English

After a long and fierce conflict, the §H[WarInHeavenFEOne.GetName]§! have emerged victorious in the struggle for control over the galaxy. The defeated ancient empire has been fully annexed by their old rival, and the §H[WarInHeavenFEOne.GetSpeciesName]§! now stand triumphant as the undisputed dominant power in the galaxy.\n\nFor now, an uneasy peace reigns, but how long can this new order last?

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1866`

## war_in_heaven.5.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Awar_in_heaven.5.a&lang=en)

### 中文

[WarInHeavenFEOne.GetSpeciesName]治下的和平。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1705`

### English

Pax [WarInHeavenFEOne.GetSpeciesName] reigns.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1867`

## war_in_heaven.6.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Awar_in_heaven.6.name&lang=en)

### 中文

$war_in_heaven.5.name$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1706`

### English

$war_in_heaven.5.name$

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1868`

## war_in_heaven.6.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Awar_in_heaven.6.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在经历漫长而残酷的争斗后，天堂之战以双方僵持不下的局面而告终。§H[WarInHeavenFEOne.GetName]§!与§H[WarInHeavenFETwo.GetName]§!已经签署了停火协议，将舰队撤回了各自边境。\n\n目前星系中维系着脆弱的和平，但这次休战能持续多久呢？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1707`

### English

After a long and fierce conflict, the War in Heaven has ended with neither side able to achieve dominance over the other. The §H[WarInHeavenFEOne.GetName]§! and §H[WarInHeavenFETwo.GetName]§! have signed a ceasefire and withdrawn their fleets back to their own borders. \n\nFor now, an uneasy peace reigns over the galaxy, but how long can this truce last?

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1869`

## war_in_heaven.6.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Awar_in_heaven.6.a&lang=en)

### 中文

战争并未结束……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1708`

### English

This isn't over...

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1870`

## war_in_heaven.11.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Awar_in_heaven.11.name&lang=en)

### 中文

觉醒帝国被击败

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1709`

### English

Awakened Empire Defeated

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1871`

## war_in_heaven.11.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Awar_in_heaven.11.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

先祖帝国间的战争以一方被击败而结束，但这并不是他们的宿敌所为。也许是大战消耗了他们的实力，亦或是单纯因为年轻种族们的实力，古老的帝国最终毁灭于他们眼中的弱小种族，这完全改变了局势。现在的问题是，§H[RemainingAwakened.GetName]§!能否避免踏上他们宿敌的后尘？还是说这标志着星系迎来了新的纪元？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1710`

### English

The war between the ancients has ended with the destruction of one of the combatants - but not at the hands of their old rival. Whether it was due to the war weakening them or simply due to the strength of the younger races, the destruction of an elder empire at the hands of their supposed lessers has changed the situation considerably. Now the question remains - will the §H[RemainingAwakened.GetName]§! be able to avoid the fate of their ancient foe, or does this defeat signal the dawn of a new age in the galaxy?

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1872`

## war_in_heaven.11.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Awar_in_heaven.11.a&lang=en)

### 中文

这还要走着瞧……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1711`

### English

It remains to be seen...

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1873`

## war_in_heaven.13.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Awar_in_heaven.13.name&lang=en)

### 中文

不结盟组织

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1712`

### English

The League of Non-Aligned Powers

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1874`

## war_in_heaven.13.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Awar_in_heaven.13.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在先祖帝国之间的兵燹之中，并非所有的年轻物种都选择了站队。有一些物种和我们一样，保持了中立的姿态，但如果战事蔓延到我们的领地，我们就只能独自面对来自先祖帝国的威胁。我们已经收到了其他帝国的通讯，他们决定建立一个§H不结盟组织§!，明确挑起反抗战争双方的大旗。由于我们的军事实力与影响力，他们决定邀请我们成为这支联盟势力的盟主。我们一旦接受，就几乎确定会同时跟两大觉醒的帝国开战，但至少我们还有盟友支援，不用独自面对两大先祖帝国的威胁。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1713`

### English

Not all of the younger races chose to join with either side in the war raging between the elder empires. Some, like ourselves, have remained neutral, but are facing the prospect of facing the elder menace alone if the war should come their way. We have been contacted by representatives from other empires about the idea of creating a §HLeague of Non-Aligned Powers§! with the explicit goal of opposing both sides in the war. Due to our military might and influence, we have been asked to lead this league. Accepting would almost certainly mean war with the awakened empires, but at least we would have allies to stand by our side instead of facing them alone.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1875`

## war_in_heaven.13.desc2

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Awar_in_heaven.13.desc2&lang=en)

### 中文

先祖帝国大战之间，并非所有的年轻物种都选择了站队。有些，比如我们，保持了中立；但倘若战争的天平朝一方倾斜，我们恐怕就要独自面对来自先祖帝国的威胁了。我们收到了其他帝国的通讯，他们决定建立一个§H不结盟组织§!，明确挑起反抗战争双方的大旗。鉴于我们已是一个强大联邦，[Root.GetAllianceName]的领袖中的一员，他们决定请我们扩充自己现有的联盟以组建预想中的组织。接受请求基本意味着同觉醒的帝国们开战，但相比独抗强敌我们至少能与盟友并肩战斗了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1714`

### English

Not all of the younger races chose to join with either side in the war raging between the elder empires. Some, like ourselves, have remained neutral, but are facing the prospect of facing the elder menace alone if the war should come their way. We have been contacted by representatives from other empires about the idea of creating a §HLeague of Non-Aligned Powers§! with the explicit goal of opposing both sides in the war. As we are already the leaders of a powerful federation, the [Root.GetAllianceName], we have been asked to expand our alliance to form the proposed league. Accepting would almost certainly mean war with the awakened empires, but at least we would have allies to stand by our side instead of facing them alone.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1876`

### English
