## war_in_heaven.16.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Awar_in_heaven.16.name&lang=en)

### 中文

与复兴者的战争

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1722`

### English

War with the Awakened

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1884`

## war_in_heaven.16.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Awar_in_heaven.16.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

很快，先祖帝国就意识到了§H不结盟组织§!的存在。双方都派出了代表前往[Root.Capital.GetName]，要求解散联盟，并要求所有成员在这场战争中选边站队。我们自然是拒绝了，而今早，§H[FirstSleeper.GetName]§!与§H[SecondSleeper.GetName]§!都发布了与我们开战的公告。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1723`

### English

It did not take long for the ancients to take notice of the §HLeague of Non-Aligned Powers§!. Representatives from both warring powers have come to [Root.Capital.GetName] demanding the dissolution of the league, and for its members to choose sides in the war. Naturally, we refused, and as of this morning we are now in a state of war with both the §H[FirstSleeper.GetName]§! and the §H[SecondSleeper.GetName]§!.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1885`

## war_in_heaven.16.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Awar_in_heaven.16.a&lang=en)

### 中文

你们两个给老子滚出这个星系！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1724`

### English

Get the hell out of our galaxy, both of you!

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1886`

## war_in_heaven.18.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Awar_in_heaven.18.name&lang=en)

### 中文

复兴者的陨落

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1725`

### English

The Fall of the Awakened

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1887`

## war_in_heaven.18.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Awar_in_heaven.18.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在经历漫长而残酷的冲突后，这场因两个先祖帝国间的摩擦而起的战争以它们的双双覆灭告终。曾经拥有强大古代科技且自负高傲的巨人们沦为了历史尘埃，星系迎来了崭新的黎明，新兴物种将书写他们自己的命运。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1726`

### English

After a long and fierce conflict, the war that begun as a battle between two ancient empires has ended in the destruction of both. For all their ancient technological might, the arrogance and inflexibility of these ancient giants proved their undoing, and now they are gone, reduced to no more than another set of broken ruins of the past. A new age dawns on the galaxy, and from now on the fate of the younger races will be entirely their own to decide.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1888`

## war_in_heaven.18.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Awar_in_heaven.18.a&lang=en)

### 中文

这是属于[Root.GetName]的时代！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1727`

### English

This will be the age of the [Root.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1889`

## war_in_heaven.19.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Awar_in_heaven.19.name&lang=en)

### 中文

不结盟组织

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1728`

### English

The League of Non-Aligned Powers

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1890`

## war_in_heaven.19.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Awar_in_heaven.19.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在先祖帝国之间的大战之中，并非所有的年轻物种都选择了站队。有一些物种和我们一样，保持了中立的姿态，但如果战事蔓延到我们的领地，我们就只能独自面对来自先祖帝国的威胁。在§H[From.GetName]§!的领导下，§H不结盟组织§!成立了，其目标是对抗挑起战火的先驱者帝国，加入这个联盟就意味着要和战争双方觉醒的失落帝国同时开战，但至少加入这个联盟的中立者们不用再孤军奋战了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1729`

### English

Not all of the younger races chose to join with either side in the war raging between the elder empires. Some, like ourselves, have remained neutral, but are facing the prospect of facing the elder menace alone if the war should come their way. A §HLeague of Non-Aligned Powers§! has formed under the leadership of the §H[From.GetName]§! with the explicit goal of opposing both sides in the war. Joining this league would almost certainly mean war with the awakened empires, but at least we would have allies to stand by our side instead of facing them alone.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1891`

## war_in_heaven.19.desc2

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Awar_in_heaven.19.desc2&lang=en)

### 中文

先祖帝国大战之间，并非所有的年轻物种都选择了站队。有些，比如我们，保持了中立；但倘若战争的天平朝一方倾斜，我们恐怕就要独自面对来自先驱帝国的威胁了。§H[From.GetName]§!，我们联邦的主席，已将我们的共同体重新命名为§H不结盟组织§!，挑起了反抗战争双方的大旗。建立这一组织基本意味着同觉醒的帝国们开战，但相比独抗强敌我们至少能与盟友并肩战斗了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1730`

### English

Not all of the younger races chose to join with either side in the war raging between the elder empires. Some, like ourselves, have remained neutral, but are facing the prospect of facing the elder menace alone if the war should come their way. The §H[From.GetName]§!, our federation's President, has renamed our union as the §HLeague of Non-Aligned Powers§! and declared our goal to be opposing both sides in the war. Forming this league will almost certainly mean war with the awakened empires, but at least we have allies to stand by our side instead of facing them alone.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1892`

## war_in_heaven.19.desc3

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Awar_in_heaven.19.desc3&lang=en)

### 中文

先祖帝国大战之间，并非所有的年轻物种都选择了站队。有些，比如我们，保持了中立；但倘若战争的天平朝一方倾斜，我们恐怕就要独自面对来自先驱帝国的威胁了。§H[From.GetName]§!奋勇当先，宣布其联盟将作为§H不结盟组织§!挑起反抗战争双方的大旗，并邀请其他中立国团结在他们的旗帜下。加入这一组织基本意味着同觉醒的失落帝国们开战，但相比独抗强敌我们至少能与盟友并肩战斗了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1731`

### English

Not all of the younger races chose to join with either side in the war raging between the elder empires. Some, like ourselves, have remained neutral, but are facing the prospect of facing the elder menace alone if the war should come their way. The §H[From.GetName]§! has boldly taken the initiative, declaring their alliance a §HLeague of Non-Aligned Powers§! seeking to oppose both sides in the war, and inviting other neutral nations to rally behind their banner. Joining this league would almost certainly mean war with the awakened empires, but at least we would have allies to stand by our side instead of facing them alone.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1893`

## war_in_heaven.21.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Awar_in_heaven.21.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§H[From.GetName]§!拒绝了我们加入§H不结盟组织§!的请求，现在我们只能独自抵抗先祖帝国的威胁了。

### English

Our request to join the §HLeague of Non-Aligned Powers§! was refused by the §H[From.GetName]§!, and now we will have to stand alone against the menace of the elder empires.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1902`

## war_in_heaven.21.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Awar_in_heaven.21.a&lang=en)

这就尴尬了……

### English

## war_in_heaven.22.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Awar_in_heaven.22.name&lang=en)

### 中文

不结盟组织

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1742`

### English

The League of Non-Aligned Powers

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1904`

## war_in_heaven.22.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Awar_in_heaven.22.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在先祖帝国之间的战争中，并非所有的年轻物种都选择了其中一方。比如我们就保持了中立，但这意味着我们要独自面对觉醒帝国的威胁。以在战争中抵抗各边势力为目标，§H不结盟组织§!在§H[From.GetName]§!的领导下成立了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1743`

### English

Not all of the younger races chose to join with either side in the war raging between the elder empires. Some, like ourselves, have remained neutral, but are facing the prospect of facing the awakened menace alone if the war should come their way. A §HLeague of Non-Aligned Powers§! has formed under the leadership of the §H[From.GetName]§! with the explicit goal of opposing both sides in the war.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1905`

## war_in_heaven.22.desc2

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Awar_in_heaven.22.desc2&lang=en)

### 中文

并非所有的年轻物种都选择了先祖帝国的中途中站队。有些，比如我们，保持了中立；但倘若战争的天平朝一方倾斜，我们恐怕就要独自面对来自觉醒帝国的威胁了。§H[From.GetName]§!奋勇当先，宣布其联盟将作为§H不结盟组织§!挑起了反抗战争双方的大旗，并邀请其他中立国团结在他们的旗帜下。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1744`

### English

Not all of the younger races chose to join with either side in the war raging between the elder empires. Some, like ourselves, have remained neutral, but are facing the prospect of facing the awakened menace alone if the war should come their way. The §H[From.GetName]§! has boldly taken the initiative, declaring their alliance a §HLeague of Non-Aligned Powers§! seeking to oppose both sides in the war, and inviting other neutral nations to rally behind their banner.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1906`

## war_in_heaven.22.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Awar_in_heaven.22.a&lang=en)

### 中文

一次豪赌。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1745`

### English

A bold move.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1907`

## war_in_heaven_is_over

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Awar_in_heaven_is_over&lang=en)

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1746`

### English

War in Heaven is Over

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1909`

## namarian.9.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anamarian.9.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

逐浪者舰队上的游牧船员们给我们发来了信息。他们说他们正在从星系边缘离开并向下一个目的地进发。他们将会去哪？他们会找到他们正寻觅的东西吗？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1752`

### English

The galactic nomads aboard the Pursuing Waters fleet have sent us a parting message. They are leaving our galaxy from the outer rim and setting sail towards their next destination. Where will they end up next? Will they ever find what they are searching for?

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1916`

## namarian.9.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anamarian.9.a&lang=en)

### 中文

有人持怀疑态度……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1753`

### English

One wonders...

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1917`

## namarian.9.a.phobe

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anamarian.9.a.phobe&lang=en)

### 中文

谢天谢地。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1754`

### English

Good riddance.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1918`

## namarian.9.c.phile

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anamarian.9.c.phile&lang=en)

### 中文

祝旅程平安。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1755`

### English

Fly safe.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1919`

## namarian.10.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anamarian.10.name&lang=en)

### 中文

星海游牧民

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1756`

### English

Galactic Nomads

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1920`

## namarian.10.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anamarian.10.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

愿凉爽的白天和温暖的夜晚一直伴你左右，[Root.GetAdj][Root.GetRulerTitle]。我们是[contact_speaker_leader.GetName]。我们代表游牧舰队，逐浪者舰队上所有的纳马里安之魂发声。\n\n你不必对我们心存恐惧，我们的舰队无意对天体进行争夺。我们只要求受到尊重，旅程不受到妨碍。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1757`

### English

Cold days and warm nights upon you, [Root.GetAdj] [Root.GetRulerTitle]. We are [contact_speaker_leader.GetName]. We speak on behalf of all the Namarian souls aboard the collected ships of the Pursuing Waters, our nomadic fleet.\n\nYou have nothing to fear from us, as the fleet has no interest in orb-bound affairs. We ask only that you respect our autonomy and do not hinder us on our journey.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1921`

## namarian.21.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anamarian.21.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

你们好，[Root.GetAdj][Root.GetRulerTitle]。我们有个请求。在我们穿过漫漫虚空的旅途中，我们中的一些人民心生倦怠，眼中失去了目标。我们不是暴君，因此不会强迫任何人跟随我们完成旅途。所以我们在此请求：你们可以接收上述船员吗？让他们栖息在你们的[NomadMissionPlanet.GetName]上，可以吗？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1758`

### English

Greetings again, [Root.GetAdj] [Root.GetRulerTitle]. We have a request for you. As we travel the void some of our people grow weary and lose sight of our goal. We are no tyrants, we force no one to see our quest to the end. So we ask of you: will you take some of our people in? To dwell on your orb, the one named [NomadMissionPlanet.GetName]?

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1922`

## namarian.21.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anamarian.21.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们会接收他们的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1759`

### English

We will take them in.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1923`

## namarian.21.a.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anamarian.21.a.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

§H[NomadMissionPlanet.GetName]§!：\n§G+300§!£pop£§H游牧人口§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1760`

### English

§H[NomadMissionPlanet.GetName]§!:\n§G+300§! £pop£ §HNomad Pops§!

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1924`

## namarian.21.a.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anamarian.21.a.response&lang=en)

### 中文

太感谢你们了，[Root.GetSpeciesAdj]。愿我们同你们一道得偿所愿。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1761`

### English

We thank you, [Root.GetSpeciesAdj]. May our people find what they are looking for, in communion with your people.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1925`

## namarian.21.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anamarian.21.b&lang=en)

### 中文

我们不能收留他们。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1762`

### English

We cannot host them.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1926`

## namarian.21.b.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anamarian.21.b.response&lang=en)

### 中文

那真是太不幸了，[Root.GetSpeciesAdj]。希望那些掉队者能再坚持一下。我们马上就启程。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1763`

### English

That is unfortunate, [Root.GetSpeciesAdj]. Hopefully the stragglers will endure for a while longer. We will be on our way.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1927`

## namarian.21.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anamarian.21.c&lang=en)

### 中文

他们因何不满？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1764`

### English

Why are they discontent?

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1928`

## namarian.21.c.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anamarian.21.c.response&lang=en)

### 中文

旅途漫漫，很可能我们一生都无法到达终点。有人想要寻找在有生之年能够到达的目标，而不是在无尽的旅途中耗尽此生。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1765`

### English

The fleet's journey has been a long one. And it will not likely end within our lifetime. The discontent among us want another purpose in their lives other than searching the stars for our goal.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1929`

## namarian.21.d

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anamarian.21.d&lang=en)

### 中文

他们要怎么适应我们的社会？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1766`

### English

How will they adapt to our society?

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1930`

## namarian.21.d.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anamarian.21.d.response&lang=en)

### 中文

他们是一群不仅唯心还追求所谓平等的懦夫，至于他们能否和你们友好相处那就是你们自己的决断了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1767`

### English

They are a spiritual and egalitarian crowd, how well that meshes with your tribe is for you to discern.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1931`

## namarian.30.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anamarian.30.name&lang=en)

### 中文

星海游牧舰队

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1768`

### English

Galactic Nomad Fleet

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1932`

## namarian.30.desc_01

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anamarian.30.desc_01&lang=en)

### 中文

愿凉爽的白天和温暖的夜晚常伴你们左右，[Root.GetAdj][Root.GetRulerTitle]。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1769`

### English

Cold days and warm nights upon you, [Root.GetAdj] [Root.GetRulerTitle].

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1933`

## namarian.30.desc_02

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anamarian.30.desc_02&lang=en)

### 中文

祝福你们，[Root.GetAdj][Root.GetRulerTitle]。希望你们相处愉快。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1770`

### English

Pleasant winds to you, [Root.GetAdj] [Root.GetRulerTitle]. We hope you are doing well.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1934`

## namarian.30.desc_03

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anamarian.30.desc_03&lang=en)

### 中文

你们有什么要求，[Root.GetSpeciesAdj]？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1771`

### English

What do you want, [Root.GetSpeciesAdj]?

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1935`

## namarian.30.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anamarian.30.a&lang=en)

### 中文

再多给我们讲讲你们的帝国。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1772`

### English

Tell us more about your people.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1936`

## namarian.30.a.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anamarian.30.a.response&lang=en)

### 中文

正如我们初步和谈时说过的，我们来自逐浪者舰队。我们世世代代穿梭于恒星间的虚无太空，且将世代延续下去。\n\n舰队由许多个体组成，所有人都必须为了我们的目标倾尽全力。有些人希望脱离出去，在天体上殖民，但大多数人都想穿越下一片虚空，看看之后的景象。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1773`

### English

As we mentioned at our introductory parley, we are the people of the Pursuing Waters fleet. We have traveled the stars and the voids-between for many generations, and will continue to do so for many more.\n\nThe fleet hosts a collection of individuals who all are required to do their best to enable our progress. Some wish to leave and establish a new orb-bound life for themselves, but most wish to see what lies beyond the next void.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1937`

## namarian.30.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anamarian.30.b&lang=en)

### 中文

你们要去哪？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1774`

### English

Where are you headed?

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1938`

## namarian.30.b.response_trust

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anamarian.30.b.response_trust&lang=en)

### 中文

鉴于你是逐浪者信任的盟友，[Root.GetSpeciesAdj]，我将会告诉你一些事：\n\n舰队目前正处于探索之中。我们的先祖曾经不得不离开母星驶向无垠的深空之中。他们希望我们能够找到我们存在的意义，在他们离开满是沙丘的母星千年之后，我们依旧依照着他们的当初的愿景来制定我们的生存之道。\n\n这就是我们想说的。望你安康。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1775`

### English

Being the trusted ally of the Pursuing Waters that you are, [Root.GetSpeciesAdj], we will tell you this:\n\nThe fleet is on a quest. Our ancestors had to leave our birthing orb behind and set sail down the back of night. They wished for us to find the purpose of our people, and we live today by that wish, millennia after they first left the sandy dunes of Home.\n\nThat is all we dare share. May it bring you shade.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1939`

## namarian.30.b.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anamarian.30.b.response&lang=en)

### 中文

我们正沿着多条虚空之河进行探索。这场旅程已经持续千年，但我们仍将继续航行。我们的目标是宇宙的星辰大海。虽然听起来可能有些晦涩难懂，但这充分总结了我们的理想。愿它能为你们遮蔽烈日。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1776`

### English

We are on a journey down the river of the many voids-between. It has been millennia, but we sail on. The destination we seek being the ocean of the universe. It might sound cryptic, but this adequately summarizes what we dare share. May it bring you shade.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1940`

## namarian.30.b.response_no_trust

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anamarian.30.b.response_no_trust&lang=en)

### 中文

请恕我们不愿透露，[Root.GetSpeciesAdj]。还有别的事吗？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1777`

### English

We would rather not say, [Root.GetSpeciesAdj]. Was there anything else?

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1941`

## namarian.30.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anamarian.30.c&lang=en)

### 中文

再见。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1778`

### English

Goodbye.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1942`

## namarian.30.c.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anamarian.30.c.response&lang=en)

### 中文

祝顺利，[Root.GetSpeciesAdj]。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1779`

### English

Favorable winds, [Root.GetSpeciesAdj].

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1943`

## namarian.41.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anamarian.41.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

愿你沐浴和风，[Root.GetAdj][Root.GetRulerTitle]。我们发现你们称之为§Y[NomadMissionPlanet.GetName]§!的球体目前并未被你们占据。我们请求：能否让我们的一支帝国在此安家？\n\n我们知道这是个不小的要求——失去整座星系来给我们一个家。我们等待你的答复，无论你如何决定。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1780`

### English

Pleasant winds to you, [Root.GetAdj] [Root.GetRulerTitle]. We have found that the orb you call §Y[NomadMissionPlanet.GetName]§! is not currently being held by your people. We ask of you: could you let one of our tribes land upon it and make it a new home?\n\nWe realize we ask much of you - to lose an entire system to give our people a home. We await your decision, whatever it may be.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1944`

## namarian.41.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anamarian.41.a&lang=en)

### 中文

这座星系，以及那个“球体”归你们了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1781`

### English

The system, and the "orb" is yours.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1945`

## namarian.41.a.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anamarian.41.a.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

你将§R割让§!§H[NomadMissionPlanet.GetStarName]§!星系的控制权\n\n在§H[NomadMissionPlanet.GetName]§!§!上创建一个拥有§B1.5K£pop£§!的§Y纳马里安族帝国\n\n你与§Y[nomad_fleet_country.GetName]§!以及§Y纳马里安§!帝国的关系得到了§G改善§!\n\n

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1782`

### English

You will §Rcede control§! of the §H[NomadMissionPlanet.GetStarName]§! System\n\nCreates a §YNamarian§! empire with §B1.5K £pop£ Pops§! on §H[NomadMissionPlanet.GetName]§!\n\nYour relation with the §Y[nomad_fleet_country.GetName]§! and the §YNamarian§! empire is §Gimproved§!\n\n

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1946`

## namarian.41.b.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anamarian.41.b.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

与§Y[nomad_fleet_country.GetName]§!的关系§R恶化§!\n\n

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1783`

### English

Your relation with the §Y[nomad_fleet_country.GetName]§! is §Rworsened§!\n\n

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1947`

## namarian.41.a.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anamarian.41.a.response&lang=en)

### 中文

我们衷心感谢你们。在冰冻刺骨的星系中，这样的热情能够驱散一丝寒意。我们游牧舰队衷心祝愿你们以及新生的帝国前途光明。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1784`

### English

We thank your people for their grace. The galaxy is a cold place but today an act of kindness will ripple through its waters. We of the nomadic fleet wish both you and this new empire the best of luck in your affairs.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1948`

## namarian.41.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anamarian.41.b&lang=en)

### 中文

我们不能祝福你们。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1785`

### English

We cannot grant you this wish.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1949`

## namarian.41.b.phobe

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anamarian.41.b.phobe&lang=en)

### 中文

这颗“星球”是我们的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1786`

### English

The "planet" is ours.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1950`

## namarian.41.b.spirit

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anamarian.41.b.spirit&lang=en)

### 中文

不，这是我们不可冒犯的领地。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1787`

### English

No, it is ours by divine right.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1951`

## namarian.41.b.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anamarian.41.b.response&lang=en)

### 中文

这对我们而言是个噩耗，[Root.GetSpeciesAdj]。希望他们能在下一个水坑中找到苦苦搜寻的目标，而不是灼热的风暴。通讯终止。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1788`

### English

That is unfortunate for our people, [Root.GetSpeciesAdj]. We can only hope that they will find what they are looking for at the next watering hole, instead of searing winds. End transmission.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1952`

## namarian.41.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anamarian.41.c&lang=en)

### 中文

为什么对这颗行星感兴趣？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1789`

### English

Why is the planet of interest?

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1953`

## namarian.41.c.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anamarian.41.c.response&lang=en)

### 中文

在虚空之河航行对十分艰难，却只是漫长旅程的一小步。我们的帝国只想寻求栖息之地，而§Y[NomadMissionPlanet.GetName]§!能够满足他们的需求。\n\n[Root.GetAdj][Root.GetRulerTitle]，你们愿意为他们提供栖息之地吗？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1790`

### English

Sailing the voids-between is an arduous journey, and yet only a step on an already strenuous path. Our people simply seek rest, and thought that §Y[NomadMissionPlanet.GetName]§! fulfilled their needs.\n\nWill you grant them this rest, [Root.GetAdj] [Root.GetRulerTitle]?

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1954`

## namarian.100.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anamarian.100.name&lang=en)

### 中文

紧急通讯

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1791`

### English

Urgent Transmission

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1955`

## namarian.100.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anamarian.100.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetSpeciesAdj]，愿灼热的太阳与残酷的干旱常伴你们左右。逐浪者永远不再与你们往来。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1792`

### English

Scorching suns and drought upon you, [Root.GetSpeciesAdj]. The Pursuing Waters shall not deal with your sort ever again.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1956`

## namarian.105.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anamarian.105.name&lang=en)

### 中文

敌意声明

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1793`

### English

Declaration of Hostility

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1957`

## namarian.105.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anamarian.105.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

你当见识怒涛的咆哮，[Root.GetSpeciesAdj]!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1794`

### English

You shall know the fury of the Raging Waters, [Root.GetSpeciesAdj]!

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1958`

## namarian.110.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anamarian.110.name&lang=en)

### 中文

遇险通讯

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1795`

### English

Distress Transmission

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1959`

## namarian.110.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anamarian.110.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

情况十万火急，我们希望寻求帮助，[Root.GetAdj][Root.GetRulerTitle]。舰队遭到了[attacker_country.GetName]的攻击，请求你们支援！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1796`

### English

We come to you with a most urgent request, [Root.GetAdj] [Root.GetRulerTitle]. The fleet is being attacked by [attacker_country.GetName] and it needs your help!

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1960`

## namarian.110.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anamarian.110.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们会支援你们的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1797`

### English

We will aid you.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1961`

## namarian.110.a.reply

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anamarian.110.a.reply&lang=en)

### 中文

舰队铭记你们的恩德，请赶快！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1798`

### English

The fleet is grateful, please hurry!

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1962`

## namarian.110.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anamarian.110.b&lang=en)

### 中文

我们不能插手，很遗憾。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1799`

### English

We sadly cannot intervene.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1963`

## namarian.110.b.reply

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anamarian.110.b.reply&lang=en)

### 中文

……诅咒你的子民与星球永受干旱，[Root.GetSpeciesAdj]。通讯结束。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1800`

### English

... Drought to your orbs and your offspring, [Root.GetSpeciesAdj]. End transmission.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1964`

## namarian.110.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anamarian.110.c&lang=en)

### 中文

我们不会帮忙的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1801`

### English

We won't help you.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1965`

## namarian.500.title

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anamarian.500.title&lang=en)

### 中文

游牧民族贸易提案

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1802`

### English

Nomad Trade Proposal

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1966`

## namarian.500.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anamarian.500.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

很高兴再会，绿洲的[Root.GetRulerTitle]。在周围的行星之间穿行使得我们可以在废弃种群找寻有用的东西。也正因如此我们刚刚建好了我们的新飞船。\n\n事实上我们建造出过量的飞船是为了解决的资源过剩这个问题。我们更希望商队能够保持灵活，不受束缚，所以我们带着希望达成交易的愿景来找到你们。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1803`

### English

Greetings again, [Root.GetRulerTitle] of the oasis. Sailing through the nearby stars have made for some particularly fruitful scavenging. And so we have just finished the construction of new vessels.\n\nIn actuality we build more than we need in order to get rid of the excess resources. We prefer the caravan to stay nimble, unburdened, so we come to you with a transaction in mind.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1967`

## namarian.500.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anamarian.500.a&lang=en)

### 中文

商队？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1804`

### English

The caravan?

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1968`

## namarian.500.a.reply

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anamarian.500.a.reply&lang=en)

### 中文

舰队中的方舟。是我们的建设者工作休息的地方。商队穿梭于宇宙之中需要它来提供维持生存的空间，因此我们需要降低它的负载。\n\n如果飞船组成了舰队的手臂的话，那么商队就是舰队的心脏。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1805`

### English

The arkships of our fleet. Where we construct our contraptions, where our builders sleep. Steering the caravan across the great voids-between requires it to offer living space, hence the need to reduce its load.\n\nIf our military make up the fleet's hands, the caravan is its heart.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1969`

## namarian.500.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anamarian.500.b&lang=en)

### 中文

交易？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1806`

### English

A transaction?

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1970`

## namarian.500.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anamarian.500.c&lang=en)

### 中文

说说吧。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1807`

### English

Out with it.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1971`

## namarian.505.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anamarian.505.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们有一个提案。我们需要能量币和矿物来维持我们的航行。作为交换，我们会交给你们§Y五艘§!我们的新型舰船

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1808`

### English

A proposal, then. We require energy credits and minerals for the next crossing. In exchange, you may have §Yfive§! of our newly-built vessels.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1972`

## namarian.505.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anamarian.505.a&lang=en)

### 中文

只有五艘？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1809`

### English

Only five?

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1973`

## namarian.505.a.reply

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anamarian.505.a.reply&lang=en)

### 中文

我们想卖的就是这么多。毕竟商队需要攻防兼备。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1810`

### English

That is as many as we are willing to part with. The caravan needs its hands to hold both shield and spear after all.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1974`

## namarian.505.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anamarian.505.b&lang=en)

### 中文

请继续。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1811`

### English

Go on.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1975`

## namarian.505.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anamarian.505.c&lang=en)

### 中文

我们不相信纳马里安的工程技术。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1812`

### English

We do not trust Namarian engineering.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1976`

## namarian.510.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anamarian.510.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这就是我们的行事方式，不断的收集资源，随时保持我们的船坞处于运作状态，这不仅仅是我们的传统也是我们必须要做的事。比起拖累我们的舰队我们更希望看到的是我们的造物被其他天体居民买去使用。\n\n我们能够确信我们的设计不会让你失望。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1813`

### English

It is our way, by tradition and necessity, to scavenge resources and activate our internal shipyards when and where we can. We would prefer to see whatever excess we generate is put to use among the orb-dwellers, than have it hamper our journey.\n\nWe believe our designs would not disappoint you.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1977`

## namarian.510.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anamarian.510.a&lang=en)

### 中文

给我介绍介绍这些舰队。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1814`

### English

Tell us about these vessels.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1978`

## namarian.510.a.reply

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anamarian.510.a.reply&lang=en)

### 中文

巡洋舰，你们应该是这么叫的吧。装备有护盾，以及电弧发射器和等离子发射器。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1815`

### English

Cruisers, we suppose you would call them. Shielded and armed with plasma throwers and arc emitters.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1979`

## namarian.510.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anamarian.510.b&lang=en)

### 中文

我们很感兴趣。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1816`

### English

We are interested.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1980`

## namarian.510.c

## namarian.513.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anamarian.513.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们本希望至少能赚回制造成本，但现在只需要§Y1500§!£minerals£或者§Y1500§!£energy£就可以把飞船卖给你们。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1828`

### English

We had hoped to at least make back the manufacturing costs, but we can give you the ships for £minerals£ §Y1500§! or £energy£ §Y1500§!.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1992`

## namarian.514.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anamarian.514.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

舰船将开往你们的中央绿洲——[Root.Capital.GetName]？是的。§Y30§!天内完成交付。\n\n愿你们一路顺风，永远能够得到荫凉的庇佑，[Root.GetRulerName]。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1829`

### English

The ships will be driven to your central oasis - [Root.Capital.GetName]? Yes. To be delivered within §Y30§! days.\n\nMay you always follow the trail and find shade, [Root.GetRulerName].

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1993`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anamarian.514.a&lang=en)

### English

## namarian.520.title

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anamarian.520.title&lang=en)

### 中文

加密通讯

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1832`

### English

Encrypted Transmission

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1996`

## namarian.520.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anamarian.520.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

嗨，天体居民。\n\n我们有消息要告诉你们。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1833`

### English

Hey, orb-dweller.\n\nWe've got some news for your kind.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1997`

## namarian.520.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anamarian.520.a&lang=en)

### 中文

纳马里安人，请表明身份。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1834`

### English

Identify yourself, Namarian.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1998`

## namarian.520.a.reply

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anamarian.520.a.reply&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的身份并无特殊之处。我们是逐浪者舰队中的热心人士。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1835`

### English

We're no one special. A concerned party among the Pursuing Waters fleet, one might call us.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:1999`

## namarian.520.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anamarian.520.b&lang=en)

### 中文

说说吧。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1836`

### English

Out with it.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:2000`

## namarian.521.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anamarian.521.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

你们最近是不是买来了§Y5艘§!我们新制造的舰船，对吧？速度快而且装配着有大口径的武器？\n\n事情是这样的，尽管你们不是……啊算了，我还是老实告诉你们吧。为了维护这片区域的稳定，和我们定下这笔舰船交易的国家可不止你们。你们的邻居，§H[other_empire.GetName]§!同样也和逐浪者舰队谈成了买卖。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1837`

### English

You've recently purchased §Y5§! of the fleet's newly crafted vessels, correct? Swift of speed, with large weapons?\n\nHere's the thing, though, you weren't... ah, given the full story. In the interest of preserving regional stability, your people were not the only one offered this deal. Your neighbors, the §H[other_empire.GetName]§! have also parleyed with the Pursuing Waters.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:2001`

## namarian.521.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anamarian.521.a&lang=en)

### 中文

所以呢？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1838`

### English

So?

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:2002`

## namarian.521.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anamarian.521.b&lang=en)

### 中文

有话直说。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1839`

### English

Get to the point.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:2003`

## namarian.522.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anamarian.522.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

他们谈下了一笔差不多的买卖，又有§Y5艘§!舰船即将被送往[other_empire.GetAdj]的首都[other_empire.Capital.GetName]。但我们并不希望舰队里的这些船最后是为[other_empire.GetName]效力，所以现在想要请你们帮帮忙。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1840`

### English

They accepted a similar offer, and §Y5§! additional craft are set to be shipped to the [other_empire.GetAdj] oasis of [other_empire.Capital.GetName]. We would rather the fleet's ships do not end up in service of the [other_empire.GetName], and we would like your help.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:2004`

## namarian.522.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anamarian.522.b&lang=en)

### 中文

为什么告诉我们这个？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1841`

### English

Why tell us this?

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:2005`

## namarian.522.b.reply

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anamarian.522.b.reply&lang=en)

### 中文

我们不相信[other_empire.GetSpeciesNamePlural]，与我们之前遇到的其他的帝国不同，他们到现在都没有付款，我们怀疑他们在试图拖延付款时间直到舰船送达到他们的首都，然后再拒绝付款。因为他们意识到了我们的舰队领导非常急切的渴望离开这片星域……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1842`

### English

We do not trust the [other_empire.GetSpeciesNamePlural], unlike the rest of the fleet. They have yet to pay, and we suspect they may try to stall until the vessels are delivered and then never send us payment. They realize the fleet leaders are very eager to move on...

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:2006`

## namarian.522.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anamarian.522.c&lang=en)

### 中文

那你们打算怎么做？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1843`

### English

How would you accomplish this?

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:2007`

## namarian.522.c.reply

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anamarian.522.c.reply&lang=en)

### 中文

我们有进入商队船坞自动驾驶设置的权限。只要命令所有舰队在[Root.Capital.GetName]集结，禁止理应交付的那一半飞船发往[other_empire.Capital.GetName]的[other_empire.GetAdj]天体即可。我们不会被逮到的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1844`

### English

We have privileged access to the autopilot configurations in the caravan's shipyards. Simply setting all vessels to rally at [Root.Capital.GetName] - and not sending half of them to the [other_empire.GetAdj] orb of [other_empire.Capital.GetName] - is trivial for us. We wouldn't even get caught.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:2008`

## namarian.522.d

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anamarian.522.d&lang=en)

### 中文

那你想要我们做什么？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1845`

### English

What do you want us to do?

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:2009`

## namarian.522.e

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anamarian.522.e&lang=en)

### 中文

不感兴趣。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1846`

### English

Not interested.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:2010`

## namarian.523.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anamarian.523.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们需要得到实际的利益。就算是能够确保得到£energy£§Y1000§!也比[other_empire.GetAdj]天体居民的承诺靠谱。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1847`

### English

We would need something to show for it. Even £energy£ §Y1000§! would be more than whatever the fleet would get out of the [other_empire.GetAdj] orb-dwellers.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:2011`

## namarian.523.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anamarian.523.c&lang=en)

### 中文

成交。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1848`

### English

Deal.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:2012`

## namarian.523.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anamarian.523.a&lang=en)

### 中文

没门。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1849`

### English

No deal.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:2013`

## namarian.523.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anamarian.523.b&lang=en)

### 中文

我们付不起。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1850`

### English

We cannot afford that.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:2014`

## namarian.524.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anamarian.524.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

愿荫凉庇佑你们，[Root.GetRulerName]。舰船会如约到达目的地——一艘不少。祝顺利。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1851`

### English

Shade upon you, [Root.GetRulerName]. The ships will arrive at the appointed time - all of them. Fair winds.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:2015`

## namarian.524.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anamarian.524.a&lang=en)

结束传输。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1852`

### English

End transmission.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:2016`

## namarian.525.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anamarian.525.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

真是太不幸了。\n\n愿凉爽的白天和温暖的夜晚常伴你们左右，天体居民。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1853`

### English

That is unfortunate.\n\nCold days and warm nights to you, orb-dweller.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:2017`

## namarian.525.desc.poor

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anamarian.525.desc.poor&lang=en)

### 中文

炎热而干旱。那真是不幸。我们原以为[Root.GetName]能够成为我们飞船最好的避风港。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1854`

### English

Heat and drought. That is unfortunate, we think the [Root.GetName] would have made a fine harbor for our vessels.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:2018`

## namarian.525.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anamarian.525.a&lang=en)

### 中文

结束传输。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1855`

### English

End transmission.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:2019`

## namarian.526.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anamarian.526.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们理解。\n\n天体居民，希望将来我们中的任何一方都不会对这个决定感到后悔。\n\n通讯结束。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1856`

### English

We understand.\n\nWe hope neither of us comes to regret your decision, orb-dweller.\n\nEnd transmission.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:2020`

## namarian.526.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anamarian.526.a&lang=en)

### 中文

再见。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1857`

### English

Goodbye.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:2021`

## namarian.530.title

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anamarian.530.title&lang=en)

### 中文

游牧飞船已交付

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1858`

### English

Nomad Ships Delivered

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:2022`

## namarian.530.desc.15ships

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anamarian.530.desc.15ships&lang=en)

### 中文

按照我们与逐浪者游牧舰队的协定，§Y5§!艘武装飞船已经运输到了[Root.Capital.GetStarName]星系，正停驻在我们首都的轨道上。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1859`

### English

As per our agreement with the Pursuing Waters nomadic fleet, §Y5§! military vessels have been delivered to the [Root.Capital.GetStarName] system and are orbiting our capital.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:2023`

## namarian.530.desc.30ships

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anamarian.530.desc.30ships&lang=en)

### 中文

按照我们与逐浪者游牧舰队以及其中的擅入者的合约——两支舰队总共§Y10§!艘武装飞船已经运输到了[Root.Capital.GetStarName]星系，正停驻在我们首都的轨道。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1860`

### English

As per our agreement with the Pursuing Waters nomadic fleet - and the conscientious interloper in its midst - two fleets totaling §Y10§! military vessels have been delivered to the [Root.Capital.GetStarName] system and are orbiting our capital.

Source: `localisation/english/events_2_l_english.yml:2024`
